	public class CxsPerformancTimer : IDisposable
	{
		private static bool _running;

		private static Dictionary<string, double> elapsed = new Dictionary<string, double>();

		private static Dictionary<string, int> hits = new Dictionary<string, int>();

		private static Stopwatch sw = null;

		private TimeSpan? _start;

		private string _section = "unspecificed";

		public static string Logfile { get; set; }

		public static bool Running
		{
			get
			{
				return _running;
			}
			set
			{
				if (sw == null)
				{
					sw = new Stopwatch();
					sw.Start();
				}
				_running = value;
			}
		}

		public static void Reset()
		{
			elapsed.Clear();
			hits.Clear();
		}

		public static void Report()
		{
			string path = ((Logfile == null) ? "CxsPerformancTimer.log" : Logfile);
			try
			{
				TextWriter textWriter = new StreamWriter(path);
				textWriter.WriteLine($"Performace Timer log: {DateTime.Now.ToLongDateString()} {DateTime.Now.ToLongTimeString()}");
				foreach (string key in elapsed.Keys)
				{
					if (hits.ContainsKey(key))
					{
						textWriter.WriteLine($"{key}: hits {hits[key].ToString()} total {elapsed[key].ToString()}");
					}
				}
				textWriter.Flush();
				textWriter.Close();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		public CxsPerformancTimer(string section)
		{
			if (Running)
			{
				if (section != null)
				{
					_section = section;
				}
				if (!elapsed.ContainsKey(_section.ToLower()))
				{
					elapsed.Add(_section.ToLower(), 0.0);
				}
				if (!hits.ContainsKey(_section.ToLower()))
				{
					hits.Add(_section.ToLower(), 0);
				}
				hits[_section.ToLower()] = hits[_section.ToLower()] + 1;
				_start = sw.Elapsed;
			}
		}

		public void Stop()
		{
			if (Running && _start.HasValue)
			{
				TimeSpan value = sw.Elapsed;
				TimeSpan? start = _start;
				double totalMilliseconds = (value - start).Value.TotalMilliseconds;
				elapsed[_section.ToLower()] = elapsed[_section.ToLower()] + totalMilliseconds;
				_start = null;
			}
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Stop();
		}
	}

		}
	}

	[ContentProperty("Display")]
	public class CxvAdvCollectionItem
	{
		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string Display { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string DisplayResourceKey { get; set; }

		public string DisplayLocalized { get; private set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string EnabledPropertyPath { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string Prompt { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string PromptResourceKey { get; set; }

		public string PromptLocalized { get; private set; }

		internal void LocalizeData(ICxvViewDataContainer container)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(DisplayLocalized))
			{
				DisplayLocalized = CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeString(container, Display, DisplayResourceKey);
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(PromptLocalized))
			{
				PromptLocalized = CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeString(container, Prompt, PromptResourceKey);
			}
		}
	}
	[ContentProperty("Items")]
	public class CxvAdvCollectionProperty : CxvStatePropertyBase
	{
		private int _createNewInputMask = 2;

		private string _dataPath;

		private ObservableCollection<CxvAdvCollectionItem> _items = new ObservableCollection<CxvAdvCollectionItem>();

		private Collection<string> _completeVars = new Collection<string>();

		private Collection<string> _enteredVars = new Collection<string>();

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string DataPath
		{
			get
			{
				return _dataPath;
			}
			set
			{
				_dataPath = value;
			}
		}

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public CxvDataFilter Filter { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public bool? UseItemsCollection { get; set; }

		public bool UseItemsCollectionResolved
		{
			get
			{
				if (!UseItemsCollection.HasValue)
				{
					return string.IsNullOrEmpty(DataPath);
				}
				return UseItemsCollection.Value;
			}
		}

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public ObservableCollection<CxvAdvCollectionItem> Items => _items;

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string SelectedItemBinding { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string CreateNewPrompt { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string CreateNewPromptResourceKey { get; set; }

		public string CreateNewPromptLocalized { get; private set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public int? CreateNewMaxLength { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(2)]
		public int CreateNewInputMask
		{
			get
			{
				return _createNewInputMask;
			}
			set
			{
				_createNewInputMask = value;
			}
		}

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public bool? DisplayValues { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(true)]
		public bool? AllowDuplicateDisplayValues { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string Prompt { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string PromptResourceKey { get; set; }

		public string PromptLocalized { get; private set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public bool? Sorted { get; set; }

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public Collection<string> CompletePaths => _completeVars;

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public Collection<string> EnteredPaths => _enteredVars;

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public bool? IsCalculated { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public bool? AllowChange { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public bool? AllowDelete { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public bool? ValidateIds { get; set; }

		protected override object InternalGetPropertyValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			return new CxvAdvCollectionPropertyData(this, dataContainer, DataPath, CreateNewInputMask, CompletePaths, EnteredPaths);
		}

		public override object GetParametricValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (dataContainer == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			string text = DataPath;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && text.Contains("@"))
			{
				text = dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(text, dependencies);
			}
			return text;
		}

		public override void SetParametricValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, object value)
		{
			DataPath = ((value == null) ? "" : value.ToString());
		}

		protected override void LocalizeData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer)
		{
			base.LocalizeData(dataContainer);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(CreateNewPromptLocalized))
			{
				CreateNewPromptLocalized = CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeString(dataContainer, CreateNewPrompt, CreateNewPromptResourceKey);
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(PromptLocalized))
			{
				PromptLocalized = CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeString(dataContainer, Prompt, PromptResourceKey);
			}
			foreach (CxvAdvCollectionItem item in Items)
			{
				item.LocalizeData(dataContainer);
			}
		}
	}
	public class CxvDataFilter
	{
		public event EventHandler UpdateItems;

		public virtual bool Allow(string item)
		{
			return true;
		}

		public virtual void SetContainer(ICxvViewDataContainer container)
		{
		}

		public virtual void ResetValues()
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnUpdateItems()
		{
			if (this.UpdateItems != null)
			{
				this.UpdateItems(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}
	}
	public class CxvSubstreamFilter : CxvDataFilter
	{
		public enum eSubstreamType
		{
			Mixed,
			Conventional,
			NonConventional
		}

		private static string SourcePath = "Top.appModelV8.Setup.Stream-Class.Substreams.Input.TYPE";

		private static List<string> _mixed = new List<string>();

		private static List<string> _conventional = new List<string>();

		private static List<string> _nc = new List<string>();

		private ICxvViewDataContainer _datcontainter;

		private ICxsDataWatch _itemsWatch;

		[DefaultValue(eSubstreamType.Mixed)]
		public eSubstreamType SubstreamType { get; set; }

		public override void ResetValues()
		{
			_mixed.Clear();
			_conventional.Clear();
			_nc.Clear();
			if (_itemsWatch != null)
			{
				_itemsWatch.ValuesChanged -= _itemsWatch_ValuesChanged;
				_itemsWatch = null;
			}
		}

		public override void SetContainer(ICxvViewDataContainer container)
		{
			_datcontainter = container;
		}

		public override bool Allow(string item)
		{
			CxsDataProvider resolvedCxsProvider = _datcontainter.ResolvedCxsProvider;
			if (resolvedCxsProvider?.Case == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			ResetValues();
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(SourcePath, "child_ids");
			object[] array = resolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData);
			if (array[0] == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			string[] array2 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[0]).Split('\n');
			if (_itemsWatch == null)
			{
				_itemsWatch = _datcontainter.CreateCxsWatch();
				if (_itemsWatch == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				string[] array3 = array2;
				foreach (string text in array3)
				{
					_itemsWatch.AddWatch(SourcePath + "." + text, "value");
				}
				object[] values = _itemsWatch.Values;
				if (values != null && values.Length != 0 && values.Length == array2.Length)
				{
					for (int j = 0; j < values.Length; j++)
					{
						string a = values[j] as string;
						if (string.Equals(a, "MIXED"))
						{
							_mixed.Add(array2[j]);
						}
						else if (string.Equals(a, "CISOLID"))
						{
							_conventional.Add(array2[j]);
						}
						else if (string.Equals(a, "NC"))
						{
							_nc.Add(array2[j]);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			_itemsWatch.ValuesChanged += _itemsWatch_ValuesChanged;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(item))
			{
				switch (SubstreamType)
				{
				case eSubstreamType.Conventional:
					return _conventional.Contains(item.ToUpper());
				case eSubstreamType.Mixed:
					return _mixed.Contains(item.ToUpper());
				case eSubstreamType.NonConventional:
					return _nc.Contains(item.ToUpper());
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void _itemsWatch_ValuesChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			OnUpdateItems();
		}
	}

	public class CxvBooleanProperty : CxvStatePropertyBase
	{
		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string Parameter { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string DataPath { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public object TrueValue { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public object FalseValue { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public bool? DefaultValue { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public bool? ReadOnlyIfProxy { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public bool? CanClear { get; set; }

		protected override object InternalGetPropertyValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (dataContainer == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Parameter))
			{
				return new CxvBooleanPropertyData(dataContainer, this, null, Parameter);
			}
			string dataPath = dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(DataPath, dependencies);
			return new CxvBooleanPropertyData(dataContainer, this, dataPath, null);
		}

		public override object GetParametricValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (dataContainer == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			string text = DataPath;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && text.Contains("@"))
			{
				text = dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(text, dependencies);
			}
			return text;
		}

		public override void SetParametricValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, object value)
		{
			DataPath = ((value == null) ? "" : value.ToString());
		}
	}

	public class CxvCustomTooltipHelper
	{
		public static string ResolveDescriptionPath(string variablePath)
		{
			string text = variablePath + ".Input.DESCRIPTION";
			if (text.Contains("Streams"))
			{
				text = variablePath.Replace("Streams", "Setup.ReportOptions.Stream-Names.Input.TEXT");
			}
			if (text.Contains("Flowsheeting Options.Measurement."))
			{
				text = variablePath + ".Input.NAME";
			}
			return text;
		}

		public static string ResolveDataPath(string variablePath, string value)
		{
			string text = variablePath;
			if (variablePath.Contains("Streams.[Value]") && value.Contains("."))
			{
				string text2 = value.Substring(0, value.LastIndexOf("."));
				string text3 = value.Substring(value.LastIndexOf("."));
				text = variablePath.Replace("Streams.[Value]", "Blocks." + text2.Replace(".", ".Data.Blocks.") + ".Data.Streams" + text3);
			}
			else if (!value.Contains("."))
			{
				text = text.Replace("[Value]", value);
			}
			return text;
		}

				"WEGSTEIN" => Resources.rectype_convergence_wegstein, 
				"POINT-DATA" => Resources.rectype_data_set_point_data, 
				"PROFILE-DATA" => Resources.rectype_data_set_profile_data, 
				"MIXTURE" => Resources.rectype_data_mixture, 
				"PURE-COMP" => Resources.rectype_data_pure_comp, 
				_ => strSubobjectType, 
			};
		}

		public static string LocalizeAnalysisType(string strAnalysisType)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(strAnalysisType))
			{
				return strAnalysisType.Replace("GENERIC", Resources.AnalysisType_Generic).Replace("PTENVELOPE", Resources.AnalysisType_Ptenvelope).Replace("RESIDUE", Resources.AnalysisType_Residue)
					.Replace("TERDIAGRAM", Resources.AnalysisType_Terdiagram)
					.Replace("BINARY", Resources.AnalysisType_Binary);
			}
			return strAnalysisType;
		}

		public static string LocalizeRegressionType(string strRegressionType)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(strRegressionType))
			{
				return strRegressionType.Replace("REGRESSION", Resources.Type_Regression);
			}
			return strRegressionType;
		}

		public static string LocalizeDataType(string strDataType)
		{
			string result = string.Empty;
			if (strDataType.Equals("Real"))
			{
				result = Resources.Type_Real;
			}
			else if (strDataType.Equals("Integer"))
			{
				result = Resources.Type_Integer;
			}
			else if (strDataType.Equals("Text"))
			{
				result = Resources.Type_Text;
			}
			else if (strDataType.Equals("Leaf"))
			{
				result = Resources.Type_Leaf;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static string LocalizeDataType1(string strDataType)
		{
			string result = string.Empty;
			if (strDataType.Equals("Calculated"))
			{
				result = Resources.StaType_Calculated;
			}
			else if (strDataType.Equals("Not calculated"))
			{
				result = Resources.StaType_Not_Calculated;
			}
			else if (strDataType.Equals("Fixed"))
			{
				result = Resources.StaType_Fixed;
			}
			else if (strDataType.Equals("Default"))
			{
				result = Resources.StaType_Default;
			}
			else if (strDataType.Equals("Missing"))
			{
				result = Resources.StaType_Missing;
			}
			else if (strDataType.Equals("Specified"))
			{
				result = Resources.StaType_Specified;
			}
			return result;
		}

	public class CxvCustomTooltipProperty : CxvVariableProperty
	{
		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string DescriptionPath { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string ObjectType { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string SubObjectTypePath { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string StatusPath { get; set; }

		protected override object InternalGetPropertyValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (base.ExposeAs == CxvVariableTypeCode.List)
			{
				return new CxvListVariableData(dataContainer, this);
			}
			return new CxvCustomTooltipData(dataContainer, this);
		}
	}
	public class CxvDelegateCommand<T> : ICommand, IActiveAware
	{
		private readonly Action<T> _executeMethod;

		private bool _isActive;

		private object _viewModel;

		private string _canExecutePropertyPath;

		private CxvPropertyWatch _canExecutePropertyWatch;

		private bool? _canExecute;

		private CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection _canExecuteValueChanged;

		private EventHandler _canExecutePropertyPath_ValueChangedHandler;

		private CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection _canExecuteChanged;

		public bool CanExecuteValue
		{
			get
			{
				if (_viewModel == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(_canExecutePropertyPath))
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (_canExecutePropertyWatch == null)
				{
					_canExecutePropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
					_canExecute = _canExecutePropertyWatch.ResolveBoolean(_viewModel, _canExecutePropertyPath);
					_canExecutePropertyWatch.PropertyDependencyChanged += canExecutePropertyPath_ValueChangedHandler;
				}
				if (_canExecute.HasValue)
				{
					return _canExecute.Value;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private EventHandler canExecutePropertyPath_ValueChangedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (_canExecutePropertyPath_ValueChangedHandler == null)
				{
					_canExecutePropertyPath_ValueChangedHandler = delegate
					{
						ResetCanExecuteValue();
					};
				}
				return _canExecutePropertyPath_ValueChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		public bool IsActive
		{
			get
			{
				return _isActive;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_isActive != value)
				{
					_isActive = value;
					OnIsActiveChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler CanExecuteValueChanged
		{
			add
			{
				_canExecuteValueChanged = CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Combine(_canExecuteValueChanged, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_canExecuteValueChanged = CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Remove(_canExecuteValueChanged, value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler CanExecuteChanged
		{
			add
			{
				_canExecuteChanged = CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Combine(_canExecuteChanged, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_canExecuteChanged = CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Remove(_canExecuteChanged, value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler IsActiveChanged;

		public CxvDelegateCommand(Action<T> executeMethod)
		{
			_executeMethod = executeMethod;
		}

		public CxvDelegateCommand(Action<T> executeMethod, object viewModel, string canExecutePropertyPath)
		{
			_executeMethod = executeMethod;
			_viewModel = viewModel;
			_canExecutePropertyPath = canExecutePropertyPath;
		}

		public void ResetCanExecuteValue()
		{
			if (_canExecutePropertyWatch != null)
			{
				_canExecutePropertyWatch.PropertyDependencyChanged -= canExecutePropertyPath_ValueChangedHandler;
				_canExecutePropertyWatch = null;
			}
			OnCanExecuteValueChanged();
		}

		protected virtual void OnCanExecuteValueChanged()
		{
			if (_canExecuteChanged != null)
			{
				CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Notify(_canExecuteChanged, this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
			if (_canExecuteValueChanged != null)
			{
				CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Notify(_canExecuteValueChanged, this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		public bool CanExecute(object parameter)
		{
			return CanExecuteValue;
		}

		public void Execute(object parameter)
		{
			if (_executeMethod != null)
			{
				_executeMethod((T)parameter);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsActiveChanged()
		{
			if (this.IsActiveChanged != null)
			{
				this.IsActiveChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}
	}

	public class CxvFixedVectorProperty : CxvPropertyBase
	{
		private Collection<CxvPropertyBase> _items = new Collection<CxvPropertyBase>();

		public Collection<CxvPropertyBase> Items => _items;

		protected override object InternalGetPropertyValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			List<object> list = new List<object>();
			foreach (CxvPropertyBase item in Items)
			{
				list.Add(item.GetPropertyValue(dataContainer, dependencies));
			}
			return new ReadOnlyCollection<object>(list);
		}
	}

	public static class CxvHelpers
	{
		public const string BasisAttribute = "basis";

		public const string BasisOptionsAttribute = "basis_options";

		public const string ChildCountAttribute = "child_count";

		public const string ChildIdOptionsAttribute = "child_ids_options";

		public const string InsertChildAction = "InsertChild";

		public const string DeleteChildAction = "DeleteChild";

		public const string ReorderChildrenAction = "ReorderChildren";

		public const string DefineChildAction = "DefineChild";

		public const string RenameChildAction = "RenameChild";

		public const string ClearChildAction = "ClearChild ";

		public const string ImportAction = "Import ";

		public static object GetParent(object child)
		{
			return GetParent(child, null);
		}

		public static object GetParent(object child, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (child == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (child is CxvViewModel cxvViewModel)
			{
				dependencies?.AddWatch(cxvViewModel, "Parent");
				return cxvViewModel.Parent;
			}
			PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor = TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(child).Find("Parent", ignoreCase: true);
			if (propertyDescriptor != null)
			{
				dependencies?.AddWatch(child, propertyDescriptor);
				return propertyDescriptor.GetValue(child);
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static string ExpandDataPath(string dataPath, ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(dataPath))
			{
				return dataPath;
			}
			string[] array = CxsPath.Split(dataPath);
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Count(); i++)
			{
				if (array[i].Contains('.'))
				{
					int num = dataPath.IndexOf(array[i], StringComparison.Ordinal);
					if (num > 1 && dataPath[num - 1] == '"')
					{
						array[i] = "\"" + array[i] + "\"";
					}
				}
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.EnsureCapacity(dataPath.Length);
			for (int j = 0; j < array.Length; j++)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				string text = array[j];
				string text2 = string.Empty;
				bool flag2 = false;
				if (text == "@parent")
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				else if (text[0] == '@')
				{
					string text3 = text.Substring(1);
					if (text3.Contains('@'))
					{
						string[] array2 = text3.Split('@');
						StringBuilder stringBuilder2 = new StringBuilder();
						string[] array3 = array2;
						foreach (string parameter in array3)
						{
							string value = CxsConverter.ValueToString(dataContainer.ResolveParameter(parameter, dependencies));
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
							{
								stringBuilder2.Append(value);
							}
						}
						text = stringBuilder2.ToString();
					}
					else
					{
						text2 = text3;
					}
				}
				else if (text[0] == '#')
				{
					string text4 = text.Substring(1);
					string text5 = text4;
					for (int k = 0; k < text5.Length; k++)
					{
						if (!char.IsNumber(text5[k]))
						{
							text2 = text4;
							flag2 = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
					{
						string value2 = CxsPath.ParentPath(stringBuilder.ToString());
						stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
						stringBuilder.Append(value2);
					}
					continue;
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					if (dataContainer == null)
					{
						return null;
					}
					object obj = dataContainer.ResolveParameter(text2, dependencies);
					if (obj == null)
					{
						return null;
					}
					if (flag2)
					{
						int? num2 = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(obj);
						if (!num2.HasValue)
						{
							return null;
						}
						text = "#" + num2.Value.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
					}
					else
					{
						text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(obj);
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
						{
							return null;
						}
						if (text.Contains("@"))
						{
							text = ExpandDataPath(text, dataContainer, dependencies);
						}
					}
				}
				if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append('.');
				}
				stringBuilder.Append(text);
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		public static bool ValuesEquivalent(object value1, object value2)
		{
			return ValuesEquivalent(value1, value2, ignoreCase: false);
		}

		public static bool ValuesEquivalent(object value1, object value2, bool ignoreCase)
		{
			bool flag = value1 == null || value1.GetType() == typeof(DBNull) || (value1.GetType() == typeof(string) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(value1 as string));
			bool flag2 = value2 == null || value2.GetType() == typeof(DBNull) || (value2.GetType() == typeof(string) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(value2 as string));
			bool flag3 = false;
			if (flag && flag2)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (flag || flag2)
			{
				return false;
			}
			string strA = CxsConverter.ValueToString(value1);
			string strB = CxsConverter.ValueToString(value2);
			return string.Compare(strA, strB, ignoreCase ? StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase : StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0;
		}

		public static string GetPropertyPathHead(string path)
		{
			if (path == null)
			{
				return "";
			}
			int num = path.IndexOf('.');
			int num2 = path.IndexOf('[');
			if (num2 >= 0 && (num < 0 || num2 < num))
			{
				num = num2;
			}
			if (num >= 0)
			{
				return path.Substring(0, num);
			}
			return path;
		}

		public static object ResolvePropertyPath(object start, string propertyPath)
		{
			return ResolvePropertyPath(start, propertyPath, null);
		}

		public static string ResolveString(object start, string propertyPath)
		{
			return CxsConverter.ValueToString(ResolvePropertyPath(start, propertyPath, null));
		}

		public static bool? ResolveBoolean(object start, string propertyPath)
		{
			return CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(ResolvePropertyPath(start, propertyPath, null));
		}

		public static int? ResolveInt32(object start, string propertyPath)
		{
			return CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(ResolvePropertyPath(start, propertyPath, null));
		}

		public static double? ResolveDouble(object start, string propertyPath)
		{
			return CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(ResolvePropertyPath(start, propertyPath, null));
		}

		public static object ResolvePropertyPath(object start, string propertyPath, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (start == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(propertyPath))
			{
				return start;
			}
			if (propertyPath.IndexOf('.') < 0 && propertyPath.IndexOf('[') < 0)
			{
				return GetPropertyValue(start, propertyPath, dependencies);
			}
			object obj = start;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = true;
			bool flag3 = false;
			foreach (char c in propertyPath)
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					if (c == ']' && !flag3)
					{
						string text = stringBuilder.ToString();
						stringBuilder.Remove(0, stringBuilder.Length);
						int result = 0;
						if (flag2)
						{
							flag2 = int.TryParse(text, NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result);
						}
						if (flag2)
						{
							IList list = obj as IList;
							obj = ((result < 0 || result >= list.Count) ? null : list[result]);
						}
						else
						{
							IDictionary dictionary = obj as IDictionary;
							try
							{
								obj = dictionary[text];
							}
							catch
							{
								obj = null;
							}
						}
						flag = false;
					}
					else if (flag3)
					{
						if (c == '"')
						{
							flag3 = false;
						}
						else
						{
							stringBuilder.Append(c);
						}
					}
					else if (c == '"' && stringBuilder.Length == 0)
					{
						flag2 = false;
						flag3 = true;
					}
					else
					{
						if (!char.IsDigit(c))
						{
							flag2 = false;
						}
						stringBuilder.Append(c);
					}
				}
				else if (c == '.' || c == '[')
				{
					if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
					{
						string property = stringBuilder.ToString();
						stringBuilder.Remove(0, stringBuilder.Length);
						obj = GetPropertyValue(obj, property, dependencies);
						if (obj == null)
						{
							return obj;
						}
						if (c == '[')
						{
							flag = true;
							flag2 = true;
							flag3 = false;
						}
					}
					else if (c != '.')
					{
						throw new ArgumentException("Invalid syntax in property path", "propertyPath");
					}
				}
				else
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
				}
			}
			if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
			{
				obj = GetPropertyValue(obj, stringBuilder.ToString(), dependencies);
			}
			return obj;
		}

		public static string ResolveString(object start, string propertyPath, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			return CxsConverter.ValueToString(ResolvePropertyPath(start, propertyPath, dependencies));
		}

		public static bool? ResolveBoolean(object start, string propertyPath, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			return CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(ResolvePropertyPath(start, propertyPath, dependencies));
		}

		public static int? ResolveInt32(object start, string propertyPath, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			return CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(ResolvePropertyPath(start, propertyPath, dependencies));
		}

		public static double? ResolveDouble(object start, string propertyPath, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			return CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(ResolvePropertyPath(start, propertyPath, dependencies));
		}

		public static void ResolveUpdatePropertyPath(object start, string path, object value)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(path) || start == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (path.IndexOf('.') < 0 && path.IndexOf('[') < 0)
			{
				SetPropertyValue(start, path, value);
				return;
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = true;
			bool flag3 = false;
			object obj = start;
			int length = path.Length;
			for (int i = 0; i < length; i++)
			{
				char c = path[i];
				bool flag4 = i == length - 1;
				if (!flag)
				{
					if (c != '.' && c != '[')
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(c);
					}
					else
					{
						if (stringBuilder.Length <= 0 && c == '.')
						{
							continue;
						}
						string text = stringBuilder.ToString();
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
						{
							throw new ArgumentException("Invalid syntax in property path");
						}
						stringBuilder.Remove(0, stringBuilder.Length);
						if (flag4)
						{
							SetPropertyValue(obj, text, value);
						}
						else
						{
							obj = GetPropertyValue(obj, text, null);
							if (obj == null)
							{
								return;
							}
						}
						if (c == '[')
						{
							flag = true;
							flag2 = true;
							flag3 = false;
						}
					}
				}
				else if (c == ']' && !flag3)
				{
					string text2 = stringBuilder.ToString();
					stringBuilder.Remove(0, stringBuilder.Length);
					int result = 0;
					if (flag2)
					{
						flag2 = int.TryParse(text2, NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result);
					}
					if (flag2)
					{
						IList list = obj as IList;
						if (flag4)
						{
							list[result] = value;
						}
						else
						{
							obj = list[result];
						}
					}
					else
					{
						IDictionary dictionary = obj as IDictionary;
						if (flag4)
						{
							dictionary[text2] = value;
						}
						else
						{
							obj = dictionary[text2];
						}
					}
					flag = false;
				}
				else if (flag3)
				{
					if (c == '"')
					{
						flag3 = false;
					}
					else
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(c);
					}
				}
				else if (c == '"' && stringBuilder.Length == 0)
				{
					flag2 = false;
					flag3 = true;
				}
				else
				{
					if (!char.IsDigit(c))
					{
						flag2 = false;
					}
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
				}
			}
			if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("Invalid format in property path");
				}
				string property = stringBuilder.ToString();
				SetPropertyValue(obj, property, value);
			}
		}

		public static object GetPropertyValue(object component, string property, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor = TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(component).Find(property, ignoreCase: true);
			if (propertyDescriptor == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			dependencies?.AddWatch(component, propertyDescriptor);
			return propertyDescriptor.GetValue(component);
		}

		public static void SetPropertyValue(object component, string property, object value)
		{
			(TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(component).Find(property, ignoreCase: true) ?? throw new ArgumentException("Property does not exist", "property")).SetValue(component, value);
		}
	}

		public double? DefaultLineWeight { get; set; }

	public enum CxvLogicalOperator
	{
		Equal,
		NotEqual,
		LessThan,
		GreaterThan,
		IsNull,
		IsNotNull,
		IsValidOption,
		IsNotValidOption,
		And,
		Or,
		CanInvokeAction,
		IdExists
	}
	[ContentProperty("SubConditions")]
	public class CxvLogicalTest
	{
		private CxvLogicalTestCollection _subConditions = new CxvLogicalTestCollection();

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string DataPath { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string DataAttribute { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string DataQualifier { get; set; }

		public string DataAttributeResolved
		{
			get
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(DataAttribute))
				{
					return DataAttribute;
				}
				return Operator switch
				{
					CxvLogicalOperator.CanInvokeAction => "action_status", 
					CxvLogicalOperator.IdExists => "id", 
					_ => "value", 
				};
			}
		}

		public string DataQualifierResolved
		{
			get
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(DataQualifier))
				{
					return DataQualifier;
				}
				if (Operator == CxvLogicalOperator.CanInvokeAction)
				{
					return Value.ToString();
				}
				return string.Empty;
			}
		}

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string Parameter { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string PropertyPath { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(true)]
		public object Value { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(CxvLogicalOperator.Equal)]
		public CxvLogicalOperator Operator { get; set; }

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public CxvLogicalTestCollection SubConditions => _subConditions;

		public CxvLogicalTest()
		{
			Operator = CxvLogicalOperator.Equal;
			Value = true;
		}

		public bool? Evaluate(object testValue, ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (_subConditions.Count > 0)
			{
				return _subConditions.EvaluateConditions(Operator, dataContainer, dependencies);
			}
			switch (Operator)
			{
			case CxvLogicalOperator.Equal:
				return TestEqual(testValue);
			case CxvLogicalOperator.NotEqual:
			{
				bool? result = TestEqual(testValue);
				if (!result.HasValue)
				{
					return result;
				}
				return !result.Value;
			}
			case CxvLogicalOperator.LessThan:
				return TestLessThan(testValue);
			case CxvLogicalOperator.GreaterThan:
				return TestGreaterThan(testValue);
			case CxvLogicalOperator.IsNull:
				return testValue == null;
			case CxvLogicalOperator.IsNotNull:
				return testValue != null;
			case CxvLogicalOperator.IsValidOption:
				if (testValue == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return CxsConverter.ValueToOptionsCollection(testValue)?.Contains(Value as string) ?? false;
			case CxvLogicalOperator.IsNotValidOption:
			{
				if (testValue == null)
				{
					return true;
				}
				ReadOnlyCollection<string> readOnlyCollection = CxsConverter.ValueToOptionsCollection(testValue);
				return readOnlyCollection == null || !readOnlyCollection.Contains(Value as string);
			}
			case CxvLogicalOperator.CanInvokeAction:
				if (testValue == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return CxsConverter.ValueToActionStatusDetails(testValue)?.CanInvoke ?? false;
			case CxvLogicalOperator.IdExists:
				if (testValue == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (testValue.GetType() == typeof(int))
				{
					return (int)testValue != -9999;
				}
				return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(testValue.ToString());
			default:
				return null;
			}
		}

		private TypeCode DetermineComparisonBasis(object testValue)
		{
			if (testValue == null)
			{
				return Type.GetTypeCode(Value.GetType());
			}
			if (Value == null)
			{
				return Type.GetTypeCode(testValue.GetType());
			}
			TypeCode typeCode = Type.GetTypeCode(testValue.GetType());
			TypeCode typeCode2 = Type.GetTypeCode(Value.GetType());
			if (typeCode == TypeCode.Boolean || typeCode2 == TypeCode.Boolean)
			{
				return TypeCode.Boolean;
			}
			if (typeCode2 == TypeCode.String)
			{
				switch (typeCode)
				{
				case TypeCode.Boolean:
					return TypeCode.Boolean;
				case TypeCode.Single:
				case TypeCode.Double:
					return TypeCode.Double;
				case TypeCode.Int16:
				case TypeCode.Int32:
					return TypeCode.Int32;
				}
			}
			switch (typeCode2)
			{
			case TypeCode.Boolean:
				return TypeCode.Boolean;
			case TypeCode.Single:
			case TypeCode.Double:
				return TypeCode.Double;
			case TypeCode.Int16:
			case TypeCode.Int32:
				return TypeCode.Int32;
			default:
				return TypeCode.String;
			}
		}

		private bool TestEqual(object testValue)
		{
			if (Value == null && testValue == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (Value == null || testValue == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			switch (DetermineComparisonBasis(testValue))
			{
			case TypeCode.Boolean:
			{
				bool? flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(testValue);
				bool? flag2 = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(Value);
				if (!flag.HasValue || !flag2.HasValue)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return flag.Value == flag2.Value;
			}
			case TypeCode.Int16:
			case TypeCode.Int32:
			{
				int? num3 = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(testValue);
				int? num4 = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(Value);
				if (!num3.HasValue || !num4.HasValue)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return num3.Value == num4.Value;
			}
			case TypeCode.Double:
			{
				double? num = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(testValue);
				double? num2 = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(Value);
				if (!num.HasValue || !num2.HasValue)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return num.Value == num2.Value;
			}
			default:
				return string.Compare(Value.ToString(), testValue.ToString(), StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0;
			}
		}

		private bool TestLessThan(object testValue)
		{
			if (Value == null && testValue == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (Value == null || testValue == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			switch (DetermineComparisonBasis(testValue))
			{
			case TypeCode.Boolean:
			{
				bool? flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(testValue);
				bool? flag2 = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(Value);
				int num5 = ((flag.HasValue && flag.Value) ? 1 : 0);
				int num6 = ((flag2.HasValue && flag2.Value) ? 1 : 0);
				return num5 < num6;
			}
			case TypeCode.Int16:
			case TypeCode.Int32:
			{
				int? num3 = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(testValue);
				int? num4 = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(Value);
				if (!num3.HasValue || !num4.HasValue)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return num3.Value < num4.Value;
			}
			case TypeCode.Double:
			{
				double? num = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(testValue);
				double? num2 = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(Value);
				if (!num.HasValue || !num2.HasValue)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return num.Value < num2.Value;
			}
			default:
				return string.Compare(testValue.ToString(), Value.ToString(), StringComparison.Ordinal) < 0;
			}
		}

		private bool TestGreaterThan(object testValue)
		{
			if (Value == null && testValue == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (Value == null || testValue == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			switch (DetermineComparisonBasis(testValue))
			{
			case TypeCode.Boolean:
			{
				bool? flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(testValue);
				bool? flag2 = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(Value);
				int num5 = ((flag.HasValue && flag.Value) ? 1 : 0);
				int num6 = ((flag2.HasValue && flag2.Value) ? 1 : 0);
				return num5 > num6;
			}
			case TypeCode.Int16:
			case TypeCode.Int32:
			{
				int? num3 = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(testValue);
				int? num4 = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(Value);
				if (!num3.HasValue || !num4.HasValue)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return num3.Value > num4.Value;
			}
			case TypeCode.Double:
			{
				double? num = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(testValue);
				double? num2 = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(Value);
				if (!num.HasValue || !num2.HasValue)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return num.Value > num2.Value;
			}
			default:
				return string.Compare(testValue.ToString(), Value.ToString(), StringComparison.Ordinal) > 0;
			}
		}
	}
	public class CxvLogicalTestCollection : Collection<CxvLogicalTest>
	{
		internal bool? EvaluateConditions(CxvLogicalOperator unionOperator, ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			bool? result = null;
			ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
			if (cxsDataWatch != null)
			{
				int num = 0;
				string[] array = new string[base.Count];
				int num2 = -1;
				using (IEnumerator<CxvLogicalTest> enumerator = GetEnumerator())
				{
					while (enumerator.MoveNext())
					{
						CxvLogicalTest current = enumerator.Current;
						num2++;
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(current.DataPath))
						{
							string text = dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(current.DataPath, dependencies);
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
							{
								array[num2] = text;
								cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, current.DataAttributeResolved, current.DataQualifierResolved);
								num++;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				object[] array2 = null;
				bool flag = true;
				if (num > 0)
				{
					dependencies?.AddWatch(cxsDataWatch);
					array2 = cxsDataWatch.Values;
					flag = array2 != null;
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					int num3 = -1;
					num2 = -1;
					using IEnumerator<CxvLogicalTest> enumerator = GetEnumerator();
					while (enumerator.MoveNext())
					{
						CxvLogicalTest current2 = enumerator.Current;
						num2++;
						object testValue = null;
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(array[num2]))
						{
							num3++;
							testValue = array2[num3];
						}
						else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(current2.Parameter))
						{
							testValue = dataContainer.ResolveParameter(current2.Parameter, dependencies);
						}
						else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(current2.PropertyPath))
						{
							testValue = dataContainer.ResolveViewPropertyPath(current2.PropertyPath, dependencies);
						}
						bool? flag2 = current2.Evaluate(testValue, dataContainer, dependencies);
						if (!flag2.HasValue)
						{
							continue;
						}
						if (flag2.Value)
						{
							result = true;
							if (unionOperator == CxvLogicalOperator.Or)
							{
								break;
							}
						}
						else
						{
							result = false;
							if (unionOperator == CxvLogicalOperator.And)
							{
								break;
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal static bool? EvaluateConditions(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvLogicalOperator unionOperator, params CxvLogicalTest[] testsList)
		{
			CxvLogicalTestCollection cxvLogicalTestCollection = new CxvLogicalTestCollection();
			if (testsList != null)
			{
				foreach (CxvLogicalTest cxvLogicalTest in testsList)
				{
					if (cxvLogicalTest != null)
					{
						cxvLogicalTestCollection.Add(cxvLogicalTest);
					}
				}
			}
			return cxvLogicalTestCollection.EvaluateConditions(unionOperator, dataContainer, null);
		}
	}
	public enum MutualOperator
	{
		Add,
		Multiply,
		Max,
		Min
	}
	public enum SelfOperator
	{
		None,
		Abs,
		Minus,
		Reciprocal,
		Ceiling,
		Floor,
		Power,
		Log,
		Log10,
		Sin,
		Cos
	}
	public class CxvMathOperand
	{
		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string DataPath { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public double? Value { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(SelfOperator.None)]
		public SelfOperator SelfOperator { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public double? Parameter { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string ParameterPath { get; set; }

		public double ResolveValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (dataContainer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("dataContainer");
			}
			if (dependencies == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("dependencies");
			}
			double? selfOperatedValue = GetSelfOperatedValue(dataContainer, dependencies);
			return ResolveSelfOperatedValue(dataContainer, dependencies, selfOperatedValue);
		}

		protected virtual double? GetSelfOperatedValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (Value.HasValue)
			{
				return Value;
			}
			return GetValueFromDataPath(dataContainer, dependencies, DataPath);
		}

		private double ResolveSelfOperatedValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies, double? initialValue)
		{
			if (!initialValue.HasValue)
			{
				return 0.0;
			}
			double value = initialValue.Value;
			switch (SelfOperator)
			{
			case SelfOperator.Abs:
				return Math.Abs(value);
			case SelfOperator.Minus:
				return -1.0 * value;
			case SelfOperator.Reciprocal:
				if (value == 0.0)
				{
					return 0.0;
				}
				return 1.0 / value;
			case SelfOperator.Ceiling:
				return Math.Ceiling(value);
			case SelfOperator.Floor:
				return Math.Floor(value);
			case SelfOperator.Log:
				return Math.Log(value);
			case SelfOperator.Log10:
				return Math.Log10(value);
			case SelfOperator.Sin:
				return Math.Sin(value);
			case SelfOperator.Cos:
				return Math.Cos(value);
			case SelfOperator.Power:
			{
				if (Parameter.HasValue)
				{
					return Math.Pow(value, Parameter.Value);
				}
				double? valueFromDataPath = GetValueFromDataPath(dataContainer, dependencies, ParameterPath);
				if (valueFromDataPath.HasValue)
				{
					return Math.Pow(value, valueFromDataPath.Value);
				}
				return value;
			}
			default:
				return value;
			}
		}

		private static double? GetValueFromDataPath(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies, string dataPath)
		{
			string text = dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(dataPath, dependencies);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return null;
			}
			ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
			cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "value");
			dependencies.AddWatch(cxsDataWatch);
			object[] values = cxsDataWatch.Values;
			if (values != null && values.Length == 1)
			{
				return CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(values[0]);
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	[ContentProperty("Operands")]
	public class CxvMathComplexOperand : CxvMathOperand
	{
		private readonly Collection<CxvMathOperand> _operands = new Collection<CxvMathOperand>();

		[DefaultValue(MutualOperator.Add)]
		public MutualOperator MutualOperator { get; set; }

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public Collection<CxvMathOperand> Operands => _operands;

		public bool ShouldSerializeOperands()
		{
			return _operands.Count > 0;
		}

		protected override double? GetSelfOperatedValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			double? selfOperatedValue = base.GetSelfOperatedValue(dataContainer, dependencies);
			if (selfOperatedValue.HasValue)
			{
				return selfOperatedValue;
			}
			if (_operands.Count == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			List<double> list = new List<double>();
			foreach (CxvMathOperand operand in Operands)
			{
				list.Add(operand.ResolveValue(dataContainer, dependencies));
			}
			return GetMutualOperatedValue(list);
		}

		private double GetMutualOperatedValue(List<double> operatedValues)
		{
			switch (MutualOperator)
			{
			case MutualOperator.Multiply:
			{
				double num4 = 1.0;
				{
					foreach (double operatedValue in operatedValues)
					{
						num4 *= operatedValue;
					}
					return num4;
				}
			}
			case MutualOperator.Max:
			{
				double num3 = double.MinValue;
				{
					foreach (double operatedValue2 in operatedValues)
					{
						num3 = Math.Max(num3, operatedValue2);
					}
					return num3;
				}
			}
			case MutualOperator.Min:
			{
				double num2 = double.MaxValue;
				{
					foreach (double operatedValue3 in operatedValues)
					{
						num2 = Math.Min(num2, operatedValue3);
					}
					return num2;
				}
			}
			default:
			{
				double num = 0.0;
				{
					foreach (double operatedValue4 in operatedValues)
					{
						num += operatedValue4;
					}
					return num;
				}
			}
			}
		}
	}
	[ContentProperty("Operand")]
	public class CxvMathProperty : CxvPropertyBase
	{
		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public CxvMathOperand Operand { get; set; }

		protected override object InternalGetPropertyValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			return new CxvMathPropertyData(dataContainer, this);
		}
	}
	public class CxvObservableObject : INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private CxvWeakPropertyChangedCollection _generalPropertyChangedHandlers;

		private Dictionary<string, CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection> _specificPropertyChangedHandlers;

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged
		{
			add
			{
				_generalPropertyChangedHandlers = CxvWeakPropertyChangedCollection.Combine(_generalPropertyChangedHandlers, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_generalPropertyChangedHandlers = CxvWeakPropertyChangedCollection.Remove(_generalPropertyChangedHandlers, value);
			}
		}

		protected void AddSpecificPropertyEventHandler(string property, EventHandler value)
		{
			CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection value2 = null;
			if (_specificPropertyChangedHandlers != null)
			{
				_specificPropertyChangedHandlers.TryGetValue(property, out value2);
			}
			CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection cxvWeakEventHandlerCollection = CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Combine(value2, value);
			if (cxvWeakEventHandlerCollection != value2)
			{
				if (_specificPropertyChangedHandlers == null)
				{
					_specificPropertyChangedHandlers = new Dictionary<string, CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection>();
				}
				_specificPropertyChangedHandlers[property] = cxvWeakEventHandlerCollection;
			}
		}

		protected void RemoveSpecificPropertyEventHandler(string property, EventHandler value)
		{
			if (_specificPropertyChangedHandlers == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection value2 = null;
			if (!_specificPropertyChangedHandlers.TryGetValue(property, out value2))
			{
				return;
			}
			value2 = CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Remove(value2, value);
			if (value2 == null)
			{
				_specificPropertyChangedHandlers.Remove(property);
				if (_specificPropertyChangedHandlers.Count == 0)
				{
					_specificPropertyChangedHandlers = null;
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			if (_specificPropertyChangedHandlers != null)
			{
				CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection value = null;
				_specificPropertyChangedHandlers.TryGetValue(propertyName, out value);
				value = CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.RemoveDeadReferences(value);
				if (value == null)
				{
					_specificPropertyChangedHandlers.Remove(propertyName);
					if (_specificPropertyChangedHandlers.Count == 0)
					{
						_specificPropertyChangedHandlers = null;
					}
				}
				CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Notify(value, this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
			if (_generalPropertyChangedHandlers != null)
			{
				_generalPropertyChangedHandlers = CxvWeakPropertyChangedCollection.RemoveDeadReferences(_generalPropertyChangedHandlers);
				CxvWeakPropertyChangedCollection.Notify(_generalPropertyChangedHandlers, this, propertyName);
			}
		}

		public static string CxsPathToMoniker(string path)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(path))
			{
				if (!path.StartsWith("Top."))
				{
					path = "Top." + path;
				}
				path = path.Replace("Top.appModelV8", "Top.appModel");
			}
			if (path != null)
			{
				return "cxs:" + path;
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static Collection<string> CxsPathToMonikerCollection(Collection<string> pathCollection)
		{
			Collection<string> collection = new Collection<string>();
			if (pathCollection != null)
			{
				foreach (string item in pathCollection)
				{
					string text = item;
					if (!text.StartsWith("Top."))
					{
						text = "Top." + text;
					}
					text = text.Replace("Top.appModelV8", "Top.appModel");
					collection.Add($"cxs:{text}");
				}
			}
			if (pathCollection != null)
			{
				return collection;
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal void RaisePropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			OnPropertyChanged(propertyName);
		}
	}
	public class CxvParameter : CxvObservableObject
	{
		private object _value;

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string Name { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public object Value
		{
			get
			{
				return _value;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!CxvHelpers.ValuesEquivalent(_value, value))
				{
					_value = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("Value");
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class CxvParameterCollection : ObservableCollection<CxvParameter>
	{
		public CxvParameter Find(string name)
		{
			using (IEnumerator<CxvParameter> enumerator = GetEnumerator())
			{
				while (enumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					CxvParameter current = enumerator.Current;
					if (string.Compare(current.Name, name, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
					{
						return current;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public CxvParameter EnsureParameter(string name)
		{
			return EnsureParameter(name, null);
		}

		public CxvParameter EnsureParameter(string name, object defaultValue)
		{
			CxvParameter cxvParameter = Find(name);
			if (cxvParameter == null)
			{
				cxvParameter = new CxvParameter
				{
					Name = name,
					Value = defaultValue
				};
				Add(cxvParameter);
			}
			return cxvParameter;
		}
	}
	public class CxvParameterProperty : CxvPropertyBase
	{
		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string Parameter { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string PropertyPath { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public object Value { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(false)]
		public bool IsLocal { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(false)]
		public bool IsReadOnly { get; set; }

		public string IsReadOnlyParamter { get; set; }

		public string IsReadOnlyPropertyPath { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(false)]
		public bool IsCalculated { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(false)]
		public bool IsEntered { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public Type ValueType { get; set; }

		public string ParameterResolved
		{
			get
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(Parameter))
				{
					return base.Name;
				}
				return Parameter;
			}
		}

		protected override object InternalGetPropertyValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			return new CxvParameterPropertyData(dataContainer, this);
		}

		public override void RegisterLocalParameters(CxvParameterCollection parameters)
		{
			if (IsLocal && parameters != null)
			{
				string parameterResolved = ParameterResolved;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(parameterResolved))
				{
					parameters.EnsureParameter(parameterResolved, Value);
				}
			}
		}

		public override object GetParametricValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (dataContainer == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			object result = null;
			string parameterResolved = ParameterResolved;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(PropertyPath))
			{
				result = dataContainer.ResolveViewPropertyPath(PropertyPath, dependencies);
			}
			else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(parameterResolved))
			{
				if (parameterResolved.Contains('@'))
				{
					int num = parameterResolved.LastIndexOf('@');
					string parameter = parameterResolved.Substring(num + 1);
					string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(dataContainer.ResolveParameter(parameter));
					string parameter2 = parameterResolved.Substring(0, num) + text;
					result = dataContainer.ResolveParameter(parameter2, Value, IsLocal, dependencies);
				}
				else
				{
					result = dataContainer.ResolveParameter(parameterResolved, Value, IsLocal, dependencies);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public override void SetParametricValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, object value)
		{
			if (dataContainer == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string parameterResolved = ParameterResolved;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(PropertyPath))
			{
				dataContainer.ResolveUpdateViewPropertyPath(PropertyPath, value);
			}
			else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(parameterResolved))
			{
				if (parameterResolved.Contains('@'))
				{
					int num = parameterResolved.LastIndexOf('@');
					string parameter = parameterResolved.Substring(num + 1);
					string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(dataContainer.ResolveParameter(parameter));
					string parameter2 = parameterResolved.Substring(0, num) + text;
					dataContainer.ResolveUpdateParameter(parameter2, value, IsLocal);
				}
				else
				{
					dataContainer.ResolveUpdateParameter(parameterResolved, value, IsLocal);
				}
			}
		}

		public bool GetParametricIsReadOnly(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (dataContainer == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			bool result = false;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(IsReadOnlyPropertyPath))
			{
				result = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(dataContainer.ResolveViewPropertyPath(IsReadOnlyPropertyPath, dependencies)) ?? false;
			}
			else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(IsReadOnlyParamter))
			{
				if (IsReadOnlyParamter.Contains('@'))
				{
					int num = IsReadOnlyParamter.LastIndexOf('@');
					string parameter = IsReadOnlyParamter.Substring(num + 1);
					string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(dataContainer.ResolveParameter(parameter));
					string parameter2 = IsReadOnlyParamter.Substring(0, num) + text;
					result = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(dataContainer.ResolveParameter(parameter2, Value, IsLocal, dependencies)) ?? false;
				}
				else
				{
					result = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(dataContainer.ResolveParameter(IsReadOnlyParamter, Value, IsLocal, dependencies)) ?? false;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	[ContentProperty("Tests")]
	public class CxvLogicalProperty : CxvPropertyBase
	{
		private string _simpleDataPath;

		private string _simpleDataAttribute;

		private string _simpleParameter;

		private string _simplePropertyPath;

		private object _simpleValue = true;

		private CxvLogicalOperator _operator;

		private CxvLogicalTestCollection _tests = new CxvLogicalTestCollection();

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string DataPath
		{
			get
			{
				return _simpleDataPath;
			}
			set
			{
				_simpleDataPath = value;
			}
		}

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string DataAttribute
		{
			get
			{
				return _simpleDataAttribute;
			}
			set
			{
				_simpleDataAttribute = value;
			}
		}

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string Parameter
		{
			get
			{
				return _simpleParameter;
			}
			set
			{
				_simpleParameter = value;
			}
		}

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string PropertyPath
		{
			get
			{
				return _simplePropertyPath;
			}
			set
			{
				_simplePropertyPath = value;
			}
		}

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public object Value
		{
			get
			{
				return _simpleValue;
			}
			set
			{
				_simpleValue = value;
			}
		}

		[DefaultValue(CxvLogicalOperator.And)]
		public CxvLogicalOperator Operator
		{
			get
			{
				return _operator;
			}
			set
			{
				_operator = value;
			}
		}

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public CxvLogicalTestCollection Tests => _tests;

		public CxvLogicalProperty()
		{
			Operator = CxvLogicalOperator.And;
		}

		protected override object InternalGetPropertyValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			CxvLogicalTestCollection cxvLogicalTestCollection = _tests;
			CxvLogicalOperator unionOperator = _operator;
			if (_tests.Count == 0)
			{
				if (_operator == CxvLogicalOperator.And || _operator == CxvLogicalOperator.Or)
				{
					_operator = CxvLogicalOperator.Equal;
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_simpleDataPath))
				{
					cxvLogicalTestCollection = new CxvLogicalTestCollection();
					cxvLogicalTestCollection.Add(new CxvLogicalTest
					{
						DataPath = _simpleDataPath,
						DataAttribute = _simpleDataAttribute,
						Value = _simpleValue,
						Operator = _operator
					});
					unionOperator = CxvLogicalOperator.And;
				}
				else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_simpleParameter))
				{
					cxvLogicalTestCollection = new CxvLogicalTestCollection();
					cxvLogicalTestCollection.Add(new CxvLogicalTest
					{
						Parameter = _simpleParameter,
						Value = _simpleValue,
						Operator = _operator
					});
					unionOperator = CxvLogicalOperator.And;
				}
				else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_simplePropertyPath))
				{
					cxvLogicalTestCollection = new CxvLogicalTestCollection();
					cxvLogicalTestCollection.Add(new CxvLogicalTest
					{
						PropertyPath = _simplePropertyPath,
						Value = _simpleValue,
						Operator = _operator
					});
					unionOperator = CxvLogicalOperator.And;
				}
			}
			return new CxvLogicalPropertyData(dataContainer, this, cxvLogicalTestCollection, unionOperator);
		}
	}
	[ContentProperty("Property")]
	public class CxvPropertyChoice
	{
		private string _simpleDataPath;

		private string _simpleDataAttribute;

		private string _simpleParameter;

		private string _simplePropertyPath;

		private object _simpleValue = true;

		private CxvLogicalOperator _operator;

		private CxvLogicalTestCollection _tests = new CxvLogicalTestCollection();

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public CxvLogicalTestCollection Conditions => _tests;

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string DataPath
		{
			get
			{
				return _simpleDataPath;
			}
			set
			{
				_simpleDataPath = value;
			}
		}

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string DataAttribute
		{
			get
			{
				return _simpleDataAttribute;
			}
			set
			{
				_simpleDataAttribute = value;
			}
		}

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string Parameter
		{
			get
			{
				return _simpleParameter;
			}
			set
			{
				_simpleParameter = value;
			}
		}

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string PropertyPath
		{
			get
			{
				return _simplePropertyPath;
			}
			set
			{
				_simplePropertyPath = value;
			}
		}

		[DefaultValue(true)]
		public object Value
		{
			get
			{
				return _simpleValue;
			}
			set
			{
				_simpleValue = value;
			}
		}

		[DefaultValue(CxvLogicalOperator.Equal)]
		public CxvLogicalOperator Operator
		{
			get
			{
				return _operator;
			}
			set
			{
				_operator = value;
			}
		}

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string TraceData { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(false)]
		public bool IsDefaultChoice { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public CxvPropertyBase Property { get; set; }

		public CxvPropertyChoice()
		{
			IsDefaultChoice = false;
		}

		public bool? Evaluate(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			CxvLogicalTestCollection cxvLogicalTestCollection = _tests;
			CxvLogicalOperator unionOperator = _operator;
			if (_tests.Count == 0)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_simpleDataPath))
				{
					cxvLogicalTestCollection = new CxvLogicalTestCollection();
					cxvLogicalTestCollection.Add(new CxvLogicalTest
					{
						DataPath = _simpleDataPath,
						DataAttribute = _simpleDataAttribute,
						Value = _simpleValue,
						Operator = _operator
					});
					unionOperator = CxvLogicalOperator.And;
				}
				else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_simpleParameter))
				{
					cxvLogicalTestCollection = new CxvLogicalTestCollection();
					cxvLogicalTestCollection.Add(new CxvLogicalTest
					{
						Parameter = _simpleParameter,
						Value = _simpleValue,
						Operator = _operator
					});
					unionOperator = CxvLogicalOperator.And;
				}
				else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_simplePropertyPath))
				{
					cxvLogicalTestCollection = new CxvLogicalTestCollection();
					cxvLogicalTestCollection.Add(new CxvLogicalTest
					{
						PropertyPath = _simplePropertyPath,
						Value = _simpleValue,
						Operator = _operator
					});
					unionOperator = CxvLogicalOperator.And;
				}
			}
			if (cxvLogicalTestCollection == null || cxvLogicalTestCollection.Count <= 0)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return cxvLogicalTestCollection.EvaluateConditions(unionOperator, dataContainer, dependencies);
		}
	}
	[ContentProperty("Choices")]
	public class CxvPropertySelector : CxvPropertyBase
	{
		private Collection<CxvPropertyChoice> _properties = new Collection<CxvPropertyChoice>();

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string TraceMessage { get; set; }

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public Collection<CxvPropertyChoice> Choices => _properties;

		public override object GetParametricValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			CxvPropertyChoice selectedChoice = GetSelectedChoice(dataContainer, dependencies);
			if (selectedChoice != null && selectedChoice.Property is CxvVariableProperty cxvVariableProperty)
			{
				return cxvVariableProperty.DataPath;
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected override object InternalGetPropertyValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			object result = null;
			CxvPropertyChoice selectedChoice = GetSelectedChoice(dataContainer, dependencies);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(TraceMessage))
			{
				string arg = ((selectedChoice != null) ? selectedChoice.TraceData : "<no choice selected>");
				Trace.WriteLine(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, TraceMessage, arg));
			}
			if (selectedChoice != null && selectedChoice.Property != null)
			{
				result = selectedChoice.Property.GetPropertyValue(dataContainer, dependencies);
			}
			return result;
		}

		private CxvPropertyChoice GetSelectedChoice(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			CxvPropertyChoice result = null;
			foreach (CxvPropertyChoice choice in Choices)
			{
				if (choice.IsDefaultChoice)
				{
					result = choice;
					continue;
				}
				bool? flag = choice.Evaluate(dataContainer, dependencies);
				if (!flag.HasValue || !flag.Value)
				{
					continue;
				}
				result = choice;
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public sealed class CxvPropertyWatch : IWeakEventListener
	{
		private class WatchProperty
		{
			public object _watchedObject;

			public PropertyDescriptor _watchedProperty;
		}

		private class WatchItem
		{
			public INotifyPropertyChanged _watchedObject;

			public List<string> _watchedProperties;
		}

		private EventHandler _watchedProperty_OnValueChangedHandler;

		private PropertyChangedEventHandler _watchedItem_OnPropertyChangedHandler;

		private CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection _propertyDependencyChangedHandlers;

		private List<WatchItem> _watchedItems = new List<WatchItem>();

		private List<WatchProperty> _watchedProperties = new List<WatchProperty>();

		public object Tag { get; set; }

		public bool IsEmpty
		{
			get
			{
				if (_watchedItems == null || _watchedItems.Count == 0)
				{
					if (_watchedProperties != null)
					{
						return _watchedProperties.Count == 0;
					}
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private EventHandler watchedProperty_OnValueChangedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (_watchedProperty_OnValueChangedHandler == null)
				{
					_watchedProperty_OnValueChangedHandler = watchedProperty_OnValueChanged;
				}
				return _watchedProperty_OnValueChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		private PropertyChangedEventHandler watchedItem_OnPropertyChangedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (_watchedItem_OnPropertyChangedHandler != null)
				{
					_watchedItem_OnPropertyChangedHandler = watchedItem_OnPropertyChanged;
				}
				return _watchedItem_OnPropertyChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler PropertyDependencyChanged
		{
			add
			{
				if (_propertyDependencyChangedHandlers == null)
				{
					foreach (WatchItem watchedItem in _watchedItems)
					{
						if (watchedItem != null)
						{
							PropertyChangedEventManager.AddListener(watchedItem._watchedObject, this, string.Empty);
						}
					}
					foreach (WatchProperty watchedProperty in _watchedProperties)
					{
						watchedProperty?._watchedProperty.AddValueChanged(watchedProperty._watchedObject, watchedProperty_OnValueChangedHandler);
					}
				}
				_propertyDependencyChangedHandlers = CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Combine(_propertyDependencyChangedHandlers, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_propertyDependencyChangedHandlers = CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Remove(_propertyDependencyChangedHandlers, value);
				if (_propertyDependencyChangedHandlers != null)
				{
					return;
				}
				foreach (WatchItem watchedItem in _watchedItems)
				{
					if (watchedItem != null && watchedItem._watchedObject != null)
					{
						PropertyChangedEventManager.RemoveListener(watchedItem._watchedObject, this, string.Empty);
					}
				}
				foreach (WatchProperty watchedProperty in _watchedProperties)
				{
					watchedProperty?._watchedProperty.RemoveValueChanged(watchedProperty._watchedObject, watchedProperty_OnValueChangedHandler);
				}
			}
		}

		public void AddWatch(object dataSource, PropertyDescriptor property)
		{
			if (property == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (dataSource is INotifyPropertyChanged propChange)
			{
				AddNotifyPropertyChangedWatch(propChange, property.Name);
				return;
			}
			foreach (WatchProperty watchedProperty in _watchedProperties)
			{
				if (watchedProperty._watchedObject == dataSource && watchedProperty._watchedProperty == property)
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			WatchProperty watchProperty = new WatchProperty();
			watchProperty._watchedObject = dataSource;
			watchProperty._watchedProperty = property;
			_watchedProperties.Add(watchProperty);
			if (_propertyDependencyChangedHandlers != null)
			{
				watchProperty._watchedProperty.AddValueChanged(watchProperty._watchedObject, watchedProperty_OnValueChangedHandler);
			}
		}

		public void AddWatch(object dataSource, string dataMember)
		{
			PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor = TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(dataSource).Find(dataMember, ignoreCase: false);
			if (propertyDescriptor != null)
			{
				AddWatch(dataSource, propertyDescriptor);
			}
			else
			{
				AddNotifyPropertyChangedWatch(dataSource as INotifyPropertyChanged, dataMember);
			}
		}

		public void AddNotifyPropertyChangedWatch(INotifyPropertyChanged propChange, string dataMember)
		{
			if (propChange == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			WatchItem watchItem = null;
			foreach (WatchItem watchedItem in _watchedItems)
			{
				if (watchedItem._watchedObject == propChange)
				{
					watchItem = watchedItem;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (watchItem == null)
			{
				watchItem = new WatchItem();
				watchItem._watchedObject = propChange;
				if (_propertyDependencyChangedHandlers != null)
				{
					PropertyChangedEventManager.AddListener(propChange, this, string.Empty);
				}
				_watchedItems.Add(watchItem);
			}
			if (watchItem._watchedProperties == null)
			{
				watchItem._watchedProperties = new List<string>();
				watchItem._watchedProperties.Add(dataMember);
			}
			else if (!watchItem._watchedProperties.Contains(dataMember))
			{
				watchItem._watchedProperties.Add(dataMember);
			}
		}

		public void AddWatch(ICxsDataWatch watch)
		{
			AddWatch(watch, "Values");
		}

		public void AddWatch(CxvParameter param)
		{
			AddWatch(param, "Value");
		}

		public object ResolvePropertyPath(object start, string propertyPath)
		{
			return CxvHelpers.ResolvePropertyPath(start, propertyPath, this);
		}

		public string ResolveString(object start, string propertyPath)
		{
			return CxsConverter.ValueToString(ResolvePropertyPath(start, propertyPath));
		}

		public bool? ResolveBoolean(object start, string propertyPath)
		{
			return CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(ResolvePropertyPath(start, propertyPath));
		}

		public int? ResolveInt32(object start, string propertyPath)
		{
			return CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(ResolvePropertyPath(start, propertyPath));
		}

		public double? ResolveDouble(object start, string propertyPath)
		{
			return CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(ResolvePropertyPath(start, propertyPath));
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			foreach (WatchItem watchedItem in _watchedItems)
			{
				if (watchedItem != null)
				{
					watchedItem._watchedObject.PropertyChanged -= watchedItem_OnPropertyChangedHandler;
				}
			}
			_watchedItems.Clear();
			foreach (WatchProperty watchedProperty in _watchedProperties)
			{
				watchedProperty?._watchedProperty.RemoveValueChanged(watchedProperty._watchedObject, watchedProperty_OnValueChangedHandler);
			}
			_watchedProperties.Clear();
		}

		private void watchedProperty_OnValueChanged(object sender, EventArgs args)
		{
			_propertyDependencyChangedHandlers = CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.RemoveDeadReferences(_propertyDependencyChangedHandlers);
			CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Notify(_propertyDependencyChangedHandlers, this, args);
		}

		bool IWeakEventListener.ReceiveWeakEvent(Type managerType, object sender, EventArgs args)
		{
			if (managerType == typeof(PropertyChangedEventManager))
			{
				watchedItem_OnPropertyChanged(sender, (PropertyChangedEventArgs)args);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void watchedItem_OnPropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (_propertyDependencyChangedHandlers == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (WatchItem watchedItem in _watchedItems)
			{
				if (watchedItem._watchedObject == sender)
				{
					if (watchedItem._watchedProperties != null && watchedItem._watchedProperties.Contains(args.PropertyName))
					{
						CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Notify(_propertyDependencyChangedHandlers, this, args);
					}
					break;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	[ContentProperty("Items")]
	public class CxvReactionListProperty : CxvPropertyBase

	public class CxvSharedParameters
	{
		public CxvParameterCollection Parameters { get; private set; }

		public CxvSharedParameters()
		{
			Parameters = new CxvParameterCollection();
		}

		public CxvParameter EnsureParameter(string name)
		{
			return EnsureParameter(name, null);
		}

		public CxvParameter EnsureParameter(string name, object defaultValue)
		{
			CxvParameter cxvParameter = Parameters.Find(name);
			if (cxvParameter == null)
			{
				cxvParameter = new CxvParameter
				{
					Name = name,
					Value = defaultValue
				};
				Parameters.Add(cxvParameter);
			}
			return cxvParameter;
		}
	}

	public class CxvTabItemProperty : CxvStatePropertyBase
	{
		private readonly Collection<string> _completeDataPaths = new Collection<string>();

		private readonly Collection<string> _accessibleDataPaths = new Collection<string>();

		private readonly Collection<string> _enteredDataPaths = new Collection<string>();

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public Collection<string> CompleteDataPaths => _completeDataPaths;

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public Collection<string> AccessibleDataPaths => _accessibleDataPaths;

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public Collection<string> EnteredDataPaths => _enteredDataPaths;

		protected override object InternalGetPropertyValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
