			return new CxvTabItemPropertyData(dataContainer, this);
		}
	}
	public enum CxvTabStateOperator
	{
		Complete,
		Accessible,
		Entered
	}
	public class CxvTabStateProperty : CxvPropertyBase
	{
		public CxvTabStateOperator Operator { get; set; }

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public Collection<string> DataPaths { get; private set; }

		public CxvTabStateProperty()
		{
			DataPaths = new Collection<string>();
			Operator = CxvTabStateOperator.Complete;
		}

		protected override object InternalGetPropertyValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			CxvLogicalTestCollection tabStateLogicalTestCollection = GetTabStateLogicalTestCollection(DataPaths, Operator);
			CxvLogicalOperator unionOperator = GetUnionOperator(Operator);
			return new CxvLogicalPropertyData(dataContainer, this, tabStateLogicalTestCollection, unionOperator);
		}

		internal static CxvLogicalTestCollection GetTabStateLogicalTestCollection(IEnumerable<string> dataPaths, CxvTabStateOperator tabStateOperator)
		{
			if (dataPaths == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("dataPaths");
			}
			CxvLogicalTestCollection cxvLogicalTestCollection = new CxvLogicalTestCollection();
			switch (tabStateOperator)
			{
			case CxvTabStateOperator.Complete:
				foreach (string dataPath in dataPaths)
				{
					cxvLogicalTestCollection.Add(new CxvLogicalTest
					{
						Operator = CxvLogicalOperator.Or,
						SubConditions = 
						{
							new CxvLogicalTest
							{
								DataPath = dataPath,
								DataAttribute = "tabstate",
								DataQualifier = "complete",
								Operator = CxvLogicalOperator.IsNull
							},
							new CxvLogicalTest
							{
								DataPath = dataPath,
								DataAttribute = "tabstate",
								DataQualifier = "complete",
								Operator = CxvLogicalOperator.Equal,
								Value = 1
							}
						}
					});
				}
				break;
			case CxvTabStateOperator.Accessible:
				foreach (string dataPath2 in dataPaths)
				{
					if (dataPath2.Contains(".SharedData."))
					{
						cxvLogicalTestCollection.Add(new CxvLogicalTest
						{
							DataPath = dataPath2,
							DataAttribute = "value",
							DataQualifier = "",
							Operator = CxvLogicalOperator.Equal,
							Value = true
						});
					}
					else
					{
						cxvLogicalTestCollection.Add(new CxvLogicalTest
						{
							DataPath = dataPath2,
							DataAttribute = "tabstate",
							DataQualifier = "accessible",
							Operator = CxvLogicalOperator.Equal,
							Value = 1
						});
					}
				}
				break;
			case CxvTabStateOperator.Entered:
				foreach (string dataPath3 in dataPaths)
				{
					cxvLogicalTestCollection.Add(new CxvLogicalTest
					{
						DataPath = dataPath3,
						DataAttribute = "tabstate",
						DataQualifier = "entered",
						Operator = CxvLogicalOperator.Equal,
						Value = 1
					});
				}
				break;
			}
			return cxvLogicalTestCollection;
		}

		internal static CxvLogicalOperator GetUnionOperator(CxvTabStateOperator tabStateOperator)
		{
			if (tabStateOperator == CxvTabStateOperator.Complete)
			{
				return CxvLogicalOperator.And;
			}
			return CxvLogicalOperator.Or;
		}
	}
	[CLSCompliant(false)]
	public static class CxvUnityContainerExtensions
	{
		public static T TryResolve<T>(this IUnityContainer container)
		{
			object obj = container.TryResolve(typeof(T));
			if (obj != null)
			{
				return (T)obj;
			}
			return default(T);
		}

		public static object TryResolve(this IUnityContainer container, Type typeToResolve)
		{
			if (container == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			try
			{
				return container.Resolve(typeToResolve);
			}
			catch
			{
				return null;
			}
		}
	}
	[TypeDescriptionProvider(typeof(VMTypeDescriptionProvider))]
	public class CxvViewModel : CxvObservableObject, ICxvViewDataContainer, IViewDataSerializable, ICxvPropertyData
	{
		private bool _notifyUpdates = true;

		private IUnityContainer _unityContainer;

		private EventHandler _parent_OnResolvedCxsProviderChangedHandler;

		private object _parent;

		private CxvParameterCollection _localParameters = new CxvParameterCollection();

		private CxvSharedParameters _sharedParameters;

		private bool _unloaded;

		private EventHandler _propertyDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;

		private Dictionary<int, object> _propertyValues = new Dictionary<int, object>();

		private Dictionary<int, CxvPropertyWatch> _propertyDependencies = new Dictionary<int, CxvPropertyWatch>();

		private Dictionary<int, CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection> _propertyValueChangedHandlers;

		private EventHandler _resolvedCxsProvider_UpdateDataCompletedHandler;

		private CxsDataProvider _resolvedCxsProvider;

		private bool _localParametersRegistered;

		private IDictionary _descriptionProviderCache;

		public bool NotifyUpdates
		{
			get
			{
				return _notifyUpdates;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == _notifyUpdates)
				{
					return;
				}
				_notifyUpdates = value;
				if (_notifyUpdates)
				{
					if (_resolvedCxsProvider != null)
					{
						_resolvedCxsProvider.UpdateDataCompleted += resolvedCxsProvider_UpdateDataCompletedHandler;
					}
				}
				else if (_resolvedCxsProvider != null)
				{
					_resolvedCxsProvider.UpdateDataCompleted -= resolvedCxsProvider_UpdateDataCompletedHandler;
				}
			}
		}

		[Microsoft.Practices.Unity.Dependency]
		public IUnityContainer UnityContainer
		{
			get
			{
				return _unityContainer;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _unityContainer)
				{
					_unityContainer = value;
					OnUnityContainerChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public ICxvResourceProvider ResourceProvider { get; set; }

		public ResourceManager ResourceManager { get; set; }

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public object Parent
		{
			get
			{
				return _parent;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_parent != value)
				{
					if (_parent is CxvViewModel cxvViewModel)
					{
						cxvViewModel.ResolvedCxsProviderChanged -= parent_OnResolvedCxsProviderChangedHandler;
					}
					_parent = value;
					if (_parent is CxvViewModel cxvViewModel2)
					{
						cxvViewModel2.ResolvedCxsProviderChanged += parent_OnResolvedCxsProviderChangedHandler;
					}
					OnPropertyChanged("Parent");
				}
			}
		}

		private EventHandler parent_OnResolvedCxsProviderChangedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (_parent_OnResolvedCxsProviderChangedHandler == null)
				{
					_parent_OnResolvedCxsProviderChangedHandler = delegate
					{
						ResolvedCxsProvider = null;
					};
				}
				return _parent_OnResolvedCxsProviderChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public CxvParameterCollection LocalParameters => _localParameters;

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public CxvParameterCollection Parameters => _localParameters;

		[Microsoft.Practices.Unity.Dependency]
		public CxvSharedParameters SharedParameters
		{
			get
			{
				return _sharedParameters;
			}
			set
			{
				_sharedParameters = value;
			}
		}

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string Name { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string SpecifiedMainPath { get; set; }

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[Bindable(true)]
		public object ViewData
		{
			get
			{
				if (ResolvedCxsProvider == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return this;
			}
		}

		public bool IsUnloaded => _unloaded;

		private EventHandler propertyDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (_propertyDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler == null)
				{
					_propertyDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler = OnPropertyDeclarationValueDependencyChanged;
				}
				return _propertyDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[Microsoft.Practices.Unity.Dependency]
		public CxsDataProvider ResolvedCxsProvider
		{
			get
			{
				if (_resolvedCxsProvider == null && _unityContainer != null)
				{
					CxsDataProvider cxsDataProvider = _unityContainer.Resolve<CxsDataProvider>(Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
					if (cxsDataProvider != null && NotifyUpdates)
					{
						_resolvedCxsProvider = cxsDataProvider;
						_resolvedCxsProvider.UpdateDataCompleted += resolvedCxsProvider_UpdateDataCompletedHandler;
					}
				}
				return _resolvedCxsProvider;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _resolvedCxsProvider)
				{
					if (_resolvedCxsProvider != null && NotifyUpdates)
					{
						_resolvedCxsProvider.UpdateDataCompleted -= resolvedCxsProvider_UpdateDataCompletedHandler;
					}
					_resolvedCxsProvider = value;
					if (_resolvedCxsProvider != null && NotifyUpdates)
					{
						_resolvedCxsProvider.UpdateDataCompleted += resolvedCxsProvider_UpdateDataCompletedHandler;
					}
					OnPropertyChanged("ResolvedCxsProvider");
					IsInitialUpdate = true;
					ResetViewData();
					if (_resolvedCxsProvider != null)
					{
						UpdateData();
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private EventHandler resolvedCxsProvider_UpdateDataCompletedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (_resolvedCxsProvider_UpdateDataCompletedHandler == null)
				{
					_resolvedCxsProvider_UpdateDataCompletedHandler = delegate
					{
						UpdateData();
					};
				}
				return _resolvedCxsProvider_UpdateDataCompletedHandler;
			}
		}

		internal (CxvVectorPropertyData Vector, int RowIndex) ParentVectorInfo { get; set; }

		public bool IsInitialUpdate { get; set; }

		public event EventHandler ViewDataChanged
		{
			add
			{
				AddSpecificPropertyEventHandler("ViewData", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				RemoveSpecificPropertyEventHandler("ViewData", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler ResolvedCxsProviderChanged
		{
			add
			{
				AddSpecificPropertyEventHandler("ResolvedCxsProvider", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				RemoveSpecificPropertyEventHandler("ResolvedCxsProvider", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<SubPropertyDataChangingEventArgs> SubPropertyDataChanging;

		public event EventHandler<SubPropertyDataChangedEventArgs> SubPropertyDataChanged;

		public CxvViewModel()
		{
			IsInitialUpdate = true;
			NotifyUpdates = true;
		}

		public virtual void UpdatePathParameters()
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnUnityContainerChanged()
		{
		}

		public static CxvPropertyDeclaration DeclarationFromProperty(PropertyDescriptor descriptor)
		{
			if (descriptor is VMStaticPropertyDescriptor vMStaticPropertyDescriptor)
			{
				return vMStaticPropertyDescriptor.PropertyDeclaration;
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static CxvPropertyBase MetadataFromProperty(PropertyDescriptor descriptor)
		{
			if (descriptor is VMStaticPropertyDescriptor vMStaticPropertyDescriptor)
			{
				return vMStaticPropertyDescriptor.PropertyDeclaration.Metadata;
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void ResetViewData()
		{
			OnPropertyChanged("ViewData");
		}

		public virtual void Unload()
		{
			if (IsUnloaded)
			{
				return;
			}
			_unloaded = true;
			foreach (object value in _propertyValues.Values)
			{
				CxvPropertyData cxvPropertyData = value as CxvPropertyData;
				if (value is CxvVariableData cxvVariableData)
				{
					cxvVariableData.Unload();
				}
				if (cxvPropertyData != null)
				{
					CxvDataAttributeResolver.RemovePropertyData(cxvPropertyData);
					CxvPropertyValueResolver.RemovePropertyData(cxvPropertyData);
				}
				if (value is ICxvPropertyData cxvPropertyData2)
				{
					cxvPropertyData2.Unload();
				}
			}
			CxvDataAttributeResolver.UnloadPropertyDataInViewModel(this);
			foreach (CxvPropertyWatch value2 in _propertyDependencies.Values)
			{
				value2.PropertyDependencyChanged -= propertyDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				value2.Clear();
			}
			_propertyValues.Clear();
			_propertyDependencies.Clear();
			_unityContainer = null;
			if (_resolvedCxsProvider != null && NotifyUpdates)
			{
				_resolvedCxsProvider.UpdateDataCompleted -= resolvedCxsProvider_UpdateDataCompletedHandler;
			}
			_resolvedCxsProvider = null;
		}

		public virtual object GetValue(CxvPropertyDeclaration property)
		{
			if (property == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("property");
			}
			if (IsUnloaded)
			{
				return null;
			}
			object value = null;
			if (!_propertyValues.TryGetValue(property.GlobalIndex, out value))
			{
				CxvPropertyWatch cxvPropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
				value = property.Metadata.GetPropertyValue(this, cxvPropertyWatch);
				UpdatePropertyDeclarationValueDependencies(property, cxvPropertyWatch);
				if (value != null)
				{
					_propertyValues[property.GlobalIndex] = value;
				}
			}
			return value;
		}

		public void SetValue(CxvPropertyDeclaration propertyDeclaration, object value)
		{
			if (propertyDeclaration == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("propertyDeclaration");
			}
			if (propertyDeclaration.IsReadOnly)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, Resources.PropertyCannotChange, propertyDeclaration.Name));
			}
		}

		public void ClearValue(CxvPropertyDeclaration propertyDeclaration)
		{
			if (propertyDeclaration == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("propertyDeclaration");
			}
			if (propertyDeclaration.IsReadOnly)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, Resources.PropertyCannotChange, propertyDeclaration.Name));
			}
			SetValue(propertyDeclaration, null);
		}

		internal bool ShouldSerializePropertyValue(CxvPropertyDeclaration propertyName)
		{
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual void OnPropertyDeclarationValueChanged(CxvPropertyDeclaration propertyDeclaration)
		{
			if (propertyDeclaration == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_propertyValueChangedHandlers != null)
			{
				CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection value = null;
				if (_propertyValueChangedHandlers.TryGetValue(propertyDeclaration.GlobalIndex, out value) && value != null)
				{
					value = CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.RemoveDeadReferences(value);
					if (value != null)
					{
						CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Notify(value, this, EventArgs.Empty);
					}
					else
					{
						_propertyValueChangedHandlers.Remove(propertyDeclaration.GlobalIndex);
					}
				}
			}
			OnPropertyChanged(propertyDeclaration.Name);
			OnSubPropertyDataChanged(propertyDeclaration, string.Empty);
		}

		public void AddPropertyDeclarationValueChanged(CxvPropertyDeclaration propertyDeclaration, EventHandler handler)
		{
			if (propertyDeclaration == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection value = null;
			if (_propertyValueChangedHandlers != null)
			{
				_propertyValueChangedHandlers.TryGetValue(propertyDeclaration.GlobalIndex, out value);
			}
			CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection cxvWeakEventHandlerCollection = CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Combine(value, handler);
			if (cxvWeakEventHandlerCollection != value)
			{
				if (_propertyValueChangedHandlers == null)
				{
					_propertyValueChangedHandlers = new Dictionary<int, CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection>();
				}
				_propertyValueChangedHandlers[propertyDeclaration.GlobalIndex] = cxvWeakEventHandlerCollection;
			}
		}

		public void RemovePropertyDeclarationValueChanged(CxvPropertyDeclaration propertyDeclaration, EventHandler handler)
		{
			if (propertyDeclaration == null || _propertyValueChangedHandlers == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection value = null;
			if (_propertyValueChangedHandlers.TryGetValue(propertyDeclaration.GlobalIndex, out value) && value != null && CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Remove(value, handler) == null)
			{
				_propertyValueChangedHandlers.Remove(propertyDeclaration.GlobalIndex);
				if (_propertyValueChangedHandlers.Count == 0)
				{
					_propertyValueChangedHandlers = null;
				}
			}
		}

		protected void UpdatePropertyDeclarationValueDependencies(CxvPropertyDeclaration propertyDeclaration, CxvPropertyWatch newDependencies)
		{
			if (propertyDeclaration != null)
			{
				CxvPropertyWatch value = null;
				if (_propertyDependencies.TryGetValue(propertyDeclaration.GlobalIndex, out value))
				{
					value.PropertyDependencyChanged -= propertyDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
					value.Clear();
					value.Tag = null;
				}
				if (newDependencies != null && !newDependencies.IsEmpty)
				{
					newDependencies.Tag = propertyDeclaration;
					newDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += propertyDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
					_propertyDependencies[propertyDeclaration.GlobalIndex] = newDependencies;
				}
				else
				{
					_propertyDependencies.Remove(propertyDeclaration.GlobalIndex);
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnPropertyDeclarationValueDependencyChanged(object sender, EventArgs args)
		{
			CxvPropertyDeclaration propertyDeclaration = (sender as CxvPropertyWatch).Tag as CxvPropertyDeclaration;
			ResetProperty(propertyDeclaration);
		}

		public void ResetProperty(CxvPropertyDeclaration propertyDeclaration)
		{
			if (propertyDeclaration == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_propertyValues.TryGetValue(propertyDeclaration.GlobalIndex, out var value))
			{
				if (value is ICxvPropertyData cxvPropertyData)
				{
					cxvPropertyData.Unload();
				}
				_propertyValues.Remove(propertyDeclaration.GlobalIndex);
			}
			UpdatePropertyDeclarationValueDependencies(propertyDeclaration, null);
			OnPropertyDeclarationValueChanged(propertyDeclaration);
		}

		public ICxsDataWatch CreateCxsWatch()
		{
			return ResolvedCxsProvider?.CreateWatch();
		}

		public object ExpandDataPathAndGet(string dataPath, string attribute)
		{
			return ExpandDataPathAndGet(dataPath, attribute, null);
		}

		public object ExpandDataPathAndGet(string dataPath, string attribute, string units)
		{
			CxsDataProvider resolvedCxsProvider = ResolvedCxsProvider;
			if (resolvedCxsProvider == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			string text = ExpandDataPath(dataPath, null);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return resolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(text, attribute, units);
		}

		public object ExpandDataPathAndGet(string dataPath, string attribute, string units, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (dependencies == null)
			{
				return ExpandDataPathAndGet(dataPath, attribute, units);
			}
			ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = CreateCxsWatch();
			if (ResolvedCxsProvider == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			string text = ExpandDataPath(dataPath, dependencies);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return null;
			}
			cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, attribute, units);
			if (cxsDataWatch.Values != null)
			{
				dependencies.AddWatch(cxsDataWatch);
				if (cxsDataWatch.Values.Length == 0)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return cxsDataWatch.Values[0];
			}
			return null;
		}

		public bool ExpandDataPathAndSet(string dataPath, string attribute, object value)
		{
			return ExpandDataPathAndSet(dataPath, attribute, null, value);
		}

		public bool ExpandDataPathAndSet(string dataPath, string attribute, string units, object value)
		{
			CxsDataProvider resolvedCxsProvider = ResolvedCxsProvider;
			if (resolvedCxsProvider == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			string text = ExpandDataPath(dataPath, null);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return resolvedCxsProvider.BatchSet(text, attribute, units, value);
		}

		public string ExpandDataPath(string dataPath)
		{
			if (SpecifiedMainPath == null || !dataPath.StartsWith("@main"))
			{
				return ExpandDataPath(dataPath, this, null);
			}
			return SpecifiedMainPath + dataPath.Substring(5);
		}

		public string ExpandDataPath(string dataPath, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (SpecifiedMainPath == null || !dataPath.StartsWith("@main"))
			{
				return ExpandDataPath(dataPath, this, dependencies);
			}
			return SpecifiedMainPath + dataPath.Substring(5);
		}

		public object ResolveParameter(string parameter)
		{
			return ResolveParameter(parameter, null);
		}

		public object ResolveParameter(string parameter, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			return ResolveParameter(parameter, null, localOnly: false, dependencies);
		}

		public CxvActionResult InvokeAction(string path, string action, bool refresh, params object[] arguments)
		{
			string path2 = ExpandDataPath(path);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(path))
			{
				return new CxvActionResult
				{
					Succeeded = false,
					Error = "Failed to expand path"
				};
			}
			CxsDataProvider resolvedCxsProvider = ResolvedCxsProvider;
			if (resolvedCxsProvider == null)
			{
				return new CxvActionResult
				{
					Succeeded = false,
					Error = "No connection to case"
				};
			}
			return resolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(path2, action, refresh, arguments);
		}

		private void RegisterLocalParameters()
		{
			if (_localParametersRegistered)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (PropertyDescriptor property in TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(this))
			{
				MetadataFromProperty(property)?.RegisterLocalParameters(LocalParameters);
			}
			_localParametersRegistered = true;
		}

		public object ResolveParameter(string parameter, object defaultValue, bool localOnly, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			RegisterLocalParameters();
			CxvParameter cxvParameter = null;
			for (object obj = this; obj != null; obj = CxvHelpers.GetParent(obj))
			{
				cxvParameter = ((obj is CxvViewModel cxvViewModel) ? cxvViewModel.LocalParameters.Find(parameter) : null);
				if (cxvParameter != null || localOnly)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			if (cxvParameter == null && !localOnly && _sharedParameters != null)
			{
				cxvParameter = _sharedParameters.Parameters.Find(parameter);
			}
			if (cxvParameter != null)
			{
				dependencies?.AddWatch(cxvParameter);
				return cxvParameter.Value;
			}
			for (object obj2 = this; obj2 != null; obj2 = CxvHelpers.GetParent(obj2))
			{
				PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor = TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(obj2).Find(parameter, ignoreCase: true);
				if (propertyDescriptor != null)
				{
					CxvPropertyBase cxvPropertyBase = MetadataFromProperty(propertyDescriptor);
					if (cxvPropertyBase != null)
					{
						if (cxvPropertyBase is CxvParameterProperty cxvParameterProperty && string.Compare(propertyDescriptor.Name, cxvParameterProperty.ParameterResolved, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
						{
							break;
						}
						return cxvPropertyBase.GetParametricValue(obj2 as CxvViewModel, dependencies);
					}
					if (dependencies == null)
					{
						break;
					}
					dependencies.AddWatch(obj2, propertyDescriptor);
					return propertyDescriptor.GetValue(obj2);
				}
				if (localOnly)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			cxvParameter = ((!localOnly && _sharedParameters != null) ? _sharedParameters.EnsureParameter(parameter, defaultValue) : LocalParameters.EnsureParameter(parameter, defaultValue));
			dependencies?.AddWatch(cxvParameter);
			return cxvParameter.Value;
		}

		public void ResolveUpdateParameter(string parameter, object value)
		{
			ResolveUpdateParameter(parameter, value, localOnly: false);
		}

		public void ResolveUpdateParameter(string parameter, object value, bool localOnly)
		{
			RegisterLocalParameters();
			CxvParameter cxvParameter = null;
			for (object obj = this; obj != null; obj = CxvHelpers.GetParent(obj))
			{
				cxvParameter = ((obj is CxvViewModel cxvViewModel) ? cxvViewModel.LocalParameters.Find(parameter) : null);
				if (cxvParameter != null || localOnly)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			if (cxvParameter == null && !localOnly && _sharedParameters != null)
			{
				cxvParameter = _sharedParameters.Parameters.Find(parameter);
			}
			if (cxvParameter != null)
			{
				cxvParameter.Value = value;
				return;
			}
			for (object obj2 = this; obj2 != null; obj2 = CxvHelpers.GetParent(obj2))
			{
				PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor = TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(obj2).Find(parameter, ignoreCase: true);
				if (propertyDescriptor != null)
				{
					CxvPropertyBase cxvPropertyBase = MetadataFromProperty(propertyDescriptor);
					if (cxvPropertyBase != null)
					{
						if (cxvPropertyBase is CxvParameterProperty cxvParameterProperty && string.Compare(propertyDescriptor.Name, cxvParameterProperty.ParameterResolved, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
						{
							break;
						}
						cxvPropertyBase.SetParametricValue(obj2 as CxvViewModel, value);
					}
					else
					{
						propertyDescriptor.SetValue(obj2, value);
					}
					return;
				}
				if (localOnly)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			cxvParameter = ((!localOnly && _sharedParameters != null) ? _sharedParameters.EnsureParameter(parameter) : LocalParameters.EnsureParameter(parameter));
			cxvParameter.Value = value;
		}

		public object ResolveViewPropertyPath(string path, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(path))
			{
				return null;
			}
			string propertyPathHead = CxvHelpers.GetPropertyPathHead(path);
			return CxvHelpers.ResolvePropertyPath(SelectViewDataWithProperty(propertyPathHead, dependencies), path, dependencies);
		}

		public void ResolveUpdateViewPropertyPath(string path, object value)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(path))
			{
				string propertyPathHead = CxvHelpers.GetPropertyPathHead(path);
				CxvHelpers.ResolveUpdatePropertyPath(SelectViewDataWithProperty(propertyPathHead, null), path, value);
			}
		}

		public static bool CheckMessageBoxResults(MessageBoxResult? result)
		{
			if (!result.HasValue)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (result.Value == MessageBoxResult.OK || result.Value == MessageBoxResult.Yes)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

				UnityContainer.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<GetUserInputEvent>().Publish(args);
			}
		}

		public virtual object ResolveResource(string key)
		{
			ICxvResourceProvider resourceProvider = ResourceProvider;
			if (resourceProvider != null)
			{
				object obj = resourceProvider.TryResolveResource(key);
				if (obj != null)
				{
					return obj;
				}
			}
			if (ResourceManager != null)
			{
				object obj2 = ResourceManager.GetObject(key);
				if (obj2 != null)
				{
					return obj2;
				}
			}
			if (UnityContainer != null)
			{
				foreach (ICxvResourceProvider item in UnityContainer.ResolveAll<ICxvResourceProvider>(Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()))
				{
					object obj3 = item.TryResolveResource(key);
					if (obj3 != null)
					{
						return obj3;
					}
				}
				ICxvResourceProvider cxvResourceProvider = UnityContainer.TryResolve<ICxvResourceProvider>();
				if (cxvResourceProvider != null)
				{
					object obj4 = cxvResourceProvider.TryResolveResource(key);
					if (obj4 != null)
					{
						return obj4;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public virtual object ResolveResource(string key, string resourceType)
		{
			ICxvResourceProvider resourceProvider = ResourceProvider;
			if (resourceProvider != null)
			{
				object obj = resourceProvider.TryResolveResource(key, resourceType);
				if (obj != null)
				{
					return obj;
				}
			}
			if (UnityContainer != null)
			{
				foreach (ICxvResourceProvider item in UnityContainer.ResolveAll<ICxvResourceProvider>(Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()))
				{
					object obj2 = item.TryResolveResource(key, resourceType);
					if (obj2 != null)
					{
						return obj2;
					}
				}
				ICxvResourceProvider cxvResourceProvider = UnityContainer.TryResolve<ICxvResourceProvider>();
				if (cxvResourceProvider != null)
				{
					object obj3 = cxvResourceProvider.TryResolveResource(key, resourceType);
					if (obj3 != null)
					{
						return obj3;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		void ICxvViewDataContainer.OnSubPropertyDataChanged(CxvPropertyDeclaration pd, string subPropertyName)
		{
			OnSubPropertyDataChanged(pd, subPropertyName);
		}

		void ICxvViewDataContainer.OnSubPropertyDataChanging(SubPropertyDataChangingEventArgs args)
		{
			OnSubPropertyDataChanging(args);
		}

		public void UpdateData()
		{
			OnUpdateData();
			IsInitialUpdate = false;
		}

		protected virtual void OnUpdateData()
		{
			foreach (object value in _propertyValues.Values)
			{
				if (!(value is CxvVectorPropertyData cxvVectorPropertyData))
				{
					continue;
				}
				foreach (object item in cxvVectorPropertyData)
				{
					if (item is CxvViewModel { NotifyUpdates: false } cxvViewModel)
					{
						cxvViewModel.OnUpdateData();
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public string DumpData(bool saveFile)
		{
			try
			{
				XElement xElement = CxvInternalHelpers.WriteDataToXElement(this);
				string caseFullFilePath = UnityContainer.Resolve<CxsCase>(Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).CaseFullFilePath;
				string directoryName = Path.GetDirectoryName(caseFullFilePath);
				string directoryName2 = Path.GetDirectoryName(directoryName);
				string text = null;
				bool flag = false;
				if (directoryName.EndsWith("mm\\WpfForms\\SharedTestData", StringComparison.Ordinal))
				{
					flag = true;
					text = directoryName2;
				}
				else if (directoryName.EndsWith("\\TestData", StringComparison.Ordinal) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(directoryName2))
				{
					string directoryName3 = Path.GetDirectoryName(directoryName2);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(directoryName3) && directoryName3.EndsWith("mm\\WpfForms", StringComparison.Ordinal))
					{
						flag = true;
						text = directoryName3;
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					int num = caseFullFilePath.IndexOf("mm\\WpfForms", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
					xElement.SetAttributeValue("TestCasePartialPath", caseFullFilePath.Substring(num + 3));
					string text2 = null;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						DirectoryInfo[] directories = new DirectoryInfo(text).GetDirectories(GetType().Assembly.GetName().Name, SearchOption.AllDirectories);
						if (directories.Length != 0)
						{
							text2 = directories[0].Parent.FullName;
						}
					}
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
					{
						throw new CxsException("Cannot find the wpf project that contains this type of ViewModel.");
					}
					if (saveFile)
					{
						string text3 = Path.Combine(text2, "TestData");
						if (!Directory.Exists(text3))
						{
							Directory.CreateDirectory(text3);
						}
						string text4 = Path.Combine(text3, "DumpedViewModels");
						if (!Directory.Exists(text4))
						{
							Directory.CreateDirectory(text4);
						}
						string[] array = ExpandDataPath("@main").Split('.');
						string text5 = string.Empty;
						if (array.Length > 2)
						{
							for (int i = 2; i < array.Length; i++)
							{
								text5 = text5 + array[i] + ".";
							}
						}
						if (text5.EndsWith(".", StringComparison.Ordinal))
						{
							text5 = text5.Remove(text5.Length - 1);
						}
						string text6 = GetType().Name;
						if (text6.EndsWith("_ViewModel", StringComparison.Ordinal) && text6.Length > 10)
						{
							text6 = text6.Substring(0, text6.Length - 10);
						}
						string text7 = Path.Combine(text4, Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(UnityContainer.Resolve<CxsCase>(Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).Id) + "_(" + text5 + ")_" + text6 + ".xml");
						if (File.Exists(text7))
						{
							ShowMessageBoxEventArgs e = new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
							{
								Message = Resources.OverrideWarning,
								Title = Resources.ConfirmOverride,
								MsgBoxButton = MessageBoxButton.YesNo
							};
							ShowMessageBox(e);
							switch (e.DialogResult)
							{
							case MessageBoxResult.Yes:
								File.SetAttributes(text7, FileAttributes.Normal);
								xElement.Save(text7, SaveOptions.None);
								break;
							}
						}
						else
						{
							xElement.Save(text7, SaveOptions.None);
						}
					}
					return xElement.ToString();
				}
				throw new CxsException("The TestData should be from WpfForms\\SharedTestData or WpfForms\\[project name]\\TestData folder.");
			}
			catch (CxsException)
			{
				return null;
			}
		}

		public string DumpData(string fileName)
		{
			XElement xElement = CxvInternalHelpers.WriteDataToXElement(this);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(fileName))
			{
				if (File.Exists(fileName))
				{
					ShowMessageBoxEventArgs e = new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
					{
						Message = Resources.OverrideWarning,
						Title = Resources.ConfirmOverride,
						MsgBoxButton = MessageBoxButton.YesNo
					};
					ShowMessageBox(e);
					switch (e.DialogResult)
					{
					case MessageBoxResult.Yes:
						File.SetAttributes(fileName, FileAttributes.Normal);
						xElement.Save(fileName, SaveOptions.None);
						break;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					xElement.Save(fileName, SaveOptions.None);
				}
			}
			return xElement.ToString();
		}

		private object SelectViewDataWithProperty(string property, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			for (object obj = this; obj != null; obj = CxvHelpers.GetParent(obj, dependencies))
			{
				if (TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(obj).Find(property, ignoreCase: true) != null)
				{
					return obj;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static string ExpandDataPath(string dataPath, ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			return CxvHelpers.ExpandDataPath(dataPath, dataContainer, dependencies);
		}

		internal IDictionary GetDescriptionProviderCache(bool createIfMissing)
		{
			if (_descriptionProviderCache == null && createIfMissing)
			{
				_descriptionProviderCache = new Hashtable();
			}
			return _descriptionProviderCache;
		}

		protected virtual void OnSubPropertyDataChanged(CxvPropertyDeclaration pd, string subPropertyName)
		{
			ParentVectorInfo.Vector?.OnItemPropertyChanged(new CxvVectorItemPropertyChangedEventArgs(ParentVectorInfo.RowIndex, pd.Metadata, subPropertyName));
			this.SubPropertyDataChanged?.Invoke(this, new SubPropertyDataChangedEventArgs(pd?.Metadata, subPropertyName));
		}

		protected virtual void OnSubPropertyDataChanging(SubPropertyDataChangingEventArgs args)
		{
			if (ParentVectorInfo.Vector != null)
			{
				CxvVectorItemPropertyChangingEventArgs e = new CxvVectorItemPropertyChangingEventArgs(ParentVectorInfo.RowIndex, args.Property, args.SubPropertyName, args.OldValue, args.NewValue);
				ParentVectorInfo.Vector.OnItemPropertyChanging(e);
				if (e.Cancel)
				{
					args.Cancel = true;
					return;
				}
			}
			this.SubPropertyDataChanging?.Invoke(this, args);
		}

		XElement IViewDataSerializable.WriteXElement()
		{
			List<object> list = new List<object>();
			if (SharedParameters != null && SharedParameters.Parameters.Count > 0)
			{
				List<object> list2 = new List<object>();
				foreach (CxvParameter parameter in SharedParameters.Parameters)
				{
					if (string.Compare(parameter.Name, "main", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) != 0 && string.Compare(parameter.Name, "extraComments_path", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) != 0 && string.Compare(parameter.Name, "extraDescription_path", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) != 0 && parameter.Name != null && parameter.Value != null)
					{
						list2.Add(new XElement("Parameter", new XAttribute("Name", parameter.Name), new XAttribute("Value", parameter.Value)));
					}
				}
				list.Add(new XElement("SharedParameters", list2));
			}
			PropertyDescriptorCollection properties = TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(ViewData);
			if (properties != null && properties.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (PropertyDescriptor item in properties)
				{
					CxvPropertyDeclaration cxvPropertyDeclaration = DeclarationFromProperty(item);
					if (cxvPropertyDeclaration == null)
					{
						if (item.PropertyType != null && item.PropertyType.IsValueType)
						{
							object value = item.GetValue(ViewData);
							string name = item.Name;
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(name) && value != null && string.Compare(name, "IsUnloaded", StringComparison.Ordinal) != 0 && string.Compare(name, "IsInitialUpdate", StringComparison.Ordinal) != 0)
							{
								list.Add(new XElement("Property", new XAttribute("Name", name), new XAttribute("Value", value)));
							}
						}
						continue;
					}
					object value2 = GetValue(cxvPropertyDeclaration);
					if (value2 == null || value2.GetType() == typeof(CxvInfoTabItemPropertyData))
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (value2 is IViewDataSerializable)
					{
						XElement xElement = CxvInternalHelpers.WriteDataToXElement(value2);
						xElement.SetAttributeValue("Name", cxvPropertyDeclaration.Name);
						list.Add(xElement);
						continue;
					}
					List<object> list3 = new List<object>();
					if (value2 is ICollection collection)
					{
						foreach (object item2 in collection)
						{
							list3.Add(CxvInternalHelpers.WriteDataToXElement(item2));
						}
						XElement xElement2 = new XElement((cxvPropertyDeclaration.Metadata != null) ? cxvPropertyDeclaration.Metadata.GetType().Name : cxvPropertyDeclaration.Name, list3);
						xElement2.SetAttributeValue("Name", cxvPropertyDeclaration.Name);
						list.Add(xElement2);
						continue;
					}
					throw new NotImplementedException("Data cannot be serialized. Type: " + value2.GetType().Name);
				}
			}
			return new XElement(GetType().Name, list);
		}

			};

		}

	[ContentProperty("Values")]
	public class CxvMultiMapOption
	{
		private CxvMultiMapValueCollection _values = new CxvMultiMapValueCollection();

		private string _display;

		private object _value;

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string Display
		{
			get
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_display))
				{
					return _display;
				}
				if (_value == null)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				return _value.ToString();
			}
			set
			{
				_display = value;
			}
		}

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string DisplayResourceKey { get; set; }

		public string DisplayLocalized { get; private set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public object Value
		{
			get
			{
				if (_value == null)
				{
					return _display;
				}
				return _value;
			}
			set
			{
				_value = value;
			}
		}

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string Prompt { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string PromptResourceKey { get; set; }

		public string PromptLocalized { get; private set; }

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public CxvMultiMapValueCollection Values => _values;

		public bool ShouldSerializeValue()
		{
			return _value == null;
		}

		internal void LocalizeData(ICxvViewDataContainer container)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(DisplayLocalized))
			{
				DisplayLocalized = CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeString(container, Display, DisplayResourceKey);
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(PromptLocalized))
			{
				string text = CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeString(container, Prompt, PromptResourceKey);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					PromptLocalized = text.Replace("\r\n", " ");
				}
				else
				{
					PromptLocalized = text;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class CxvMultiMapOptionCollection : ObservableCollection<CxvMultiMapOption>
	{
	}
	[ContentProperty("Options")]
	public class CxvMultiMapProperty : CxvStatePropertyBase
	{
		private CxvMultiMapVariableCollection _variables = new CxvMultiMapVariableCollection();

		private CxvMultiMapOptionCollection _options = new CxvMultiMapOptionCollection();

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public CxvMultiMapVariableCollection Variables => _variables;

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public CxvMultiMapOptionCollection Options => _options;

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string IsReadOnlyPropertyPath { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public bool? IsReadOnly { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string Prompt { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string PromptResourceKey { get; set; }

		public string PromptLocalized { get; private set; }

		protected override object InternalGetPropertyValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			return new CxvMultiMapPropertyData(dataContainer, this);
		}

		protected override void LocalizeData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer)
		{
			base.LocalizeData(dataContainer);
			foreach (CxvMultiMapOption option in Options)
			{
				option.LocalizeData(dataContainer);
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(PromptLocalized))
			{
				string text = CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeString(dataContainer, Prompt, PromptResourceKey);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					PromptLocalized = text.Replace("\r\n", " ");
				}
				else
				{
					PromptLocalized = text;
				}
			}
		}

		public CxvMultiMapVariable FindVariable(string name)
		{
			foreach (CxvMultiMapVariable variable in Variables)
			{
				if (variable.Name == name)
				{
					return variable;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public CxvMultiMapOption FindOptionByDisplayName(string name)
		{
			CxvMultiMapOption result = null;
			foreach (CxvMultiMapOption option in Options)
			{
				if (option.Display == name || option.DisplayLocalized == name)
				{
					result = option;
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public CxvMultiMapOption FindOptionByValue(object value)
		{
			CxvMultiMapOption result = null;
			foreach (CxvMultiMapOption option in Options)
			{
				if (CxvHelpers.ValuesEquivalent(option.Value, value))
				{
					result = option;
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public abstract class CxvPropertyBase
	{
		private IValueConverter _valueConverter;

		public CxvPropertyDeclaration PropertyDeclaration { get; internal set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string Name { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string Label { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string LabelResourceKey { get; set; }

		public string LabelLocalized { get; private set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public Type Converter { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string ToolTipText { get; set; }

		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string ToolTipTextResourceKey { get; set; }

		public string ToolTipTextLocalized { get; private set; }

		public IValueConverter ValueConverter(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer)
		{
			if (_valueConverter != null)
			{
				return _valueConverter;
			}
			if (dataContainer == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (Converter == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return dataContainer.UnityContainer.Resolve(Converter) as IValueConverter;
		}

		public virtual Type GetPropertyType()
		{
			return typeof(object);
		}

		public object GetPropertyValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (dataContainer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("dataContainer");
			}
			LocalizeData(dataContainer);
			return InternalGetPropertyValue(dataContainer, dependencies);
		}

		protected virtual void LocalizeData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer)
		{
			if (dataContainer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("dataContainer");
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(LabelLocalized))
			{
				LabelLocalized = CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeString(dataContainer, Label, LabelResourceKey);
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(ToolTipTextLocalized))
			{
				ToolTipTextLocalized = CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeString(dataContainer, ToolTipText, ToolTipTextResourceKey);
			}
		}

		protected abstract object InternalGetPropertyValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies);

		public virtual void RegisterLocalParameters(CxvParameterCollection parameters)
		{
		}

		public virtual object GetParametricValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			return null;
		}

		public virtual void SetParametricValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, object value)
		{
		}
	}

		RealId

					return DataAttribute;
				}
				CxvVariableTypeCode exposeAs = ExposeAs;
				if ((uint)(exposeAs - 6) <= 4u)
				{
					return "display_name";
				}
				return "value";
			}
		}

		public string UnitAttributeResolved
		{

		[DefaultValue(false)]
		public bool CallConverterEvenWhenValueIsNull { get; set; }

		public CxvVariableProperty()
		{
			ExposeAs = CxvVariableTypeCode.Unknown;
		}

		protected override object InternalGetPropertyValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (ExposeAs == CxvVariableTypeCode.List)
			{
				return new CxvListVariableData(dataContainer, this);
			}
			return new CxvVariableData(dataContainer, this);
		}

		public override object GetParametricValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (dataContainer == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			string text = DataPath;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && text.Contains("@"))
			{
				text = dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(text, dependencies);
			}
			return text;
		}

		public override void SetParametricValue(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, object value)
		{
			DataPath = ((value == null) ? "" : value.ToString());
		}

		protected override void LocalizeData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer)
		{
			base.LocalizeData(dataContainer);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(CreateNewPromptLocalized))
			{
				CreateNewPromptLocalized = CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeString(dataContainer, CreateNewPrompt, CreateNewPromptResourceKey);
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(CreateNewTitleLocalized))
			{
				CreateNewTitleLocalized = CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeString(dataContainer, CreateNewTitle, CreateNewTitleResourceKey);
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(VariablePromptLocalized))
			{
				VariablePromptLocalized = CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeString(dataContainer, VariablePrompt, VariablePromptResourceKey);
			}
		}
	}

	public class CxsDataProvider : IDisposable, INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private class CxsDataWatch : ICxsDataWatch
		{
			private CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection _valuedChanged;

			private CxsDataProvider _dataProvider;

			private CxsBatchRequestData _request;

			public object[] Values
			{
				get
				{
					if (_dataProvider == null)
					{
						return null;
					}
					return _dataProvider.BatchGet(_request);
				}
			}

			public bool IsEmpty
			{
				get
				{
					if (_request == null)
					{
						return true;
					}
					return _request.RequestsCount == 0;
				}
			}

			public bool HasEventListeners
			{
				get
				{
					if (_valuedChanged != null)
					{
						return _valuedChanged.Count > 0;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}

			public bool ChangedNotificationPending { get; set; }

			public event EventHandler ValuesChanged
			{
				add
				{
					_valuedChanged = CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Combine(_valuedChanged, value);
				}
				remove
				{
					_valuedChanged = CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Remove(_valuedChanged, value);
				}
			}

			public CxsDataWatch(CxsDataProvider provider)
			{
				_dataProvider = provider;
			}

			public void AddWatch(string path, string attribute)
			{
				AddWatch(path, attribute, null);
			}

			public void AddWatch(string path, string attribute, string units)
			{
				if (_request == null)
				{
					_request = new CxsBatchRequestData();
				}
				_request.AddRequest(path, attribute, units);
				_dataProvider.OnAddWatchedPath(this, path);
			}

			public void Unload()
			{
				if (_dataProvider != null && !IsEmpty)
				{
					_dataProvider.ForgetWatch(this, _request.CanonicalPaths);
				}
				_dataProvider = null;
				_request = null;
			}

			public void RaiseChangedNotifications()
			{
				ChangedNotificationPending = false;
				CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Notify(_valuedChanged, this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}

			public bool IsWatching(string path, string attribute, string units)
			{
				bool result = false;
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(attribute))
				{
					attribute = "value";
				}
				int canonicalPathIndex = _request.GetCanonicalPathIndex(path);
				if (canonicalPathIndex >= 0)
				{
					string text = _request.CanonicalPaths[canonicalPathIndex];
					foreach (CxsBatchRequest request in _request.Requests)
					{
						if ((object)request.Path != text)
						{
							continue;
						}
						string text2 = request.Attribute;
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
						{
							text2 = "value";
						}
						if (!(attribute != text2))
						{
							string units2 = request.Units;
							if (units2 == units || (string.IsNullOrEmpty(units2) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(units)))
							{
								result = true;
								break;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		private class NodeCache
		{
			private readonly Dictionary<string, object> _cachedAttributeData = new Dictionary<string, object>();

			private readonly List<WeakReference> _watchList = new List<WeakReference>();

			public string Path { get; private set; }

			public IEnumerable<CxsDataWatch> ActiveDataWatches
			{
				get
				{
					foreach (WeakReference watch in _watchList)
					{
						if (watch.IsAlive)
						{
							yield return watch.Target as CxsDataWatch;
						}
					}
					RemoveDeadWatches();
				}
			}

			public NodeCache(string path)
			{
				Path = path;
			}

			private static string AttributeKey(string attribute, string units)
			{
				string result = attribute;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(units))
				{
					StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(attribute.Length + units.Length + 1);
					stringBuilder.Append(attribute);
					stringBuilder.Append('#');
					stringBuilder.Append(units);
					result = stringBuilder.ToString();
				}
				return result;
			}

			public bool TryGetAttribute(string attribute, string units, out object value)
			{
				string key = AttributeKey(attribute, units);
				return _cachedAttributeData.TryGetValue(key, out value);
			}

			public void StoreAttribute(string attribute, string units, object value)
			{
				string key = AttributeKey(attribute, units);
				_cachedAttributeData[key] = value;
			}

			public void AddToRefreshRequest(CxsBatchRequestData getRequests)
			{
				int num = -1;
				foreach (string key in _cachedAttributeData.Keys)
				{
					string text = key;
					string units = string.Empty;
					if (text.Contains('#'))
					{
						string[] array = text.Split('#');
						text = array[0];
						units = array[1];
					}
					if (num >= 0)
					{
						getRequests.AddTaggedRequest(num, text, units, this);
						continue;
					}
					getRequests.AddTaggedRequest(Path, text, units, this);
					num = getRequests.GetCanonicalPathIndex(Path);
				}
			}

			public void StoreResultFromRefreshRequest(CxsBatchRequest request, object value, List<CxsDataWatch> watchesToNotify)
			{
				string key = AttributeKey(request.Attribute, request.Units);
				if (watchesToNotify != null && _watchList != null)
				{
					object value2 = null;
					if (_cachedAttributeData.ContainsKey(key))
					{
						value2 = _cachedAttributeData[key];
					}
					if (!CxvHelpers.ValuesEquivalent(value2, value))
					{
						foreach (CxsDataWatch activeDataWatch in ActiveDataWatches)
						{
							if (!activeDataWatch.ChangedNotificationPending && activeDataWatch.HasEventListeners && activeDataWatch.IsWatching(request.Path, request.Attribute, request.Units))
							{
								activeDataWatch.ChangedNotificationPending = true;
								watchesToNotify.Add(activeDataWatch);
							}
						}
					}
				}
				_cachedAttributeData[key] = value;
			}

			public void RegisterWatch(CxsDataWatch watch)
			{
				RemoveDeadWatches();
				if (!_watchList.Exists((WeakReference x) => x.Target == watch))
				{
					_watchList.Add(new WeakReference(watch));
				}
			}

			public void ForgetWatch(CxsDataWatch watch)
			{
				_watchList.RemoveAll((WeakReference x) => !x.IsAlive || x.Target == watch);
			}

			public void RemoveDeadWatches()
			{
				_watchList.RemoveAll((WeakReference x) => !x.IsAlive);
			}
		}

		private const uint FI_E_Consistency = 2147790850u;

		private const uint FI_S_Refresh = 2147790851u;

		private static int objectCounter;

		private int objectNumber = -1;

		private int _suspendedCount;

		private bool _force;

		private bool _ccAsError;

		private bool _needsUpdate;

		private CxsBatchRequestData _suspendedRequests = new CxsBatchRequestData();

		private Collection<string> _dirtyRequestsInCache = new Collection<string>();

		private bool _isBatchPaste;

		private bool _isErrorOccurredInBatchPaste;

		private CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection _updateDataStarted;

		private CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection _updateDataCompleted;

		private int recursing2;

		private const int bigset = 2000;

		private const int minStepsToShowProgressDialog = 3;

		private int _lastPurge;

		private Dictionary<string, NodeCache> _nodeCache = new Dictionary<string, NodeCache>();

		private int _dataCacheUpdateFlag;

		[Microsoft.Practices.Unity.Dependency]
		public IUnityContainer UnityContainer { get; set; }

		[Microsoft.Practices.Unity.Dependency]
		public CxsCase Case { get; set; }

		public bool IsConnected
		{
			get
			{
				if (Case != null)
				{
					return Case.IsConnected;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool Force
		{
			get
			{
				return _force;
			}
			set
			{
				_force = value;
			}
		}

		public bool CCAsError
		{
			get
			{
				return _ccAsError;
			}
			set
			{
				_ccAsError = value;
			}
		}

		public CxsBatchRequestData DataCacheRequestData
		{
			get
			{
				CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
				foreach (NodeCache value in _nodeCache.Values)
				{
					value.AddToRefreshRequest(cxsBatchRequestData);
				}
				return cxsBatchRequestData;
			}
		}

		public double PurgeAt { get; set; }

		public event EventHandler UpdateDataStarted
		{
			add
			{
				_updateDataStarted = CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Combine(_updateDataStarted, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_updateDataStarted = CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Remove(_updateDataStarted, value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler UpdateDataCompleted
		{
			add
			{
				_updateDataCompleted = CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Combine(_updateDataCompleted, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_updateDataCompleted = CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Remove(_updateDataCompleted, value);
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public CxsDataProvider()
		{
			objectNumber = objectCounter++;
			Case = new CxsCase();
			PurgeAt = 1.2;
		}

		protected virtual void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing && Case != null && UnityContainer == null)
			{
				Case.Dispose();
			}
			Case = null;
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		public void AttachToRunningCase(string applicationName, object runningCase)
		{
			if (Case == null)
			{
				if (UnityContainer != null)
				{
					Case = UnityContainer.Resolve<CxsCase>(Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
				}
				else
				{
					Case = new CxsCase();
				}
			}
			Case.ApplicationRequirement = applicationName;
			Case.AttachToRunningCase(runningCase);
		}

		public void Disconnect()
		{
			if (Case != null)
			{
				Case.Dispose();
				Case = null;
			}
		}

		public ICxsDataWatch CreateWatch()
		{
			return new CxsDataWatch(this);
		}

		public object[] BatchGet(CxsBatchRequestData requests)
		{
			if (requests == null)
			{
				return new object[0];
			}
			if (!IsConnected)
			{
				return new object[requests.RequestsCount];
			}
			IEnumerable<string> enumerable = requests.CanonicalPaths.Where((string p) => _dirtyRequestsInCache.Contains(p));
			if (enumerable != null && enumerable.Any())
			{
				if (_dataCacheUpdateFlag > 0)
				{
					UpdateDataCache(enumerable);
				}
				else
				{
					UpdateDataCache();
				}
			}
			object[] array = new object[requests.RequestsCount];
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			List<int> list = new List<int>();
			List<NodeCache> list2 = new List<NodeCache>();
			foreach (string canonicalPath in requests.CanonicalPaths)
			{
				NodeCache value = null;
				bool flag = _nodeCache.TryGetValue(canonicalPath, out value);
				if (!flag)
				{
					value = new NodeCache(canonicalPath);
					if (!canonicalPath.StartsWith("Top."))
					{
						return array;
					}
					_nodeCache[canonicalPath] = value;
				}
				int num = 0;
				foreach (CxsBatchRequest request in requests.Requests)
				{
					if ((object)request.Path == canonicalPath)
					{
						if (flag && value.TryGetAttribute(request.Attribute, request.Units, out var value2))
						{
							array[num] = value2;
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(canonicalPath) && canonicalPath.Contains("Top.appModelV8.Properties.Molecular Structure.") && request.Attribute == "child_ids" && (value2 == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(value2.ToString())))
							{
								cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(request);
								list.Add(num);
								list2.Add(value);
							}
						}
						else
						{
							cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(request);
							list.Add(num);
							list2.Add(value);
						}
					}
					num++;
				}
			}
			if (cxsBatchRequestData.RequestsCount > 0)
			{
				try
				{
					object[] array2 = Case.BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData);
					if (array2 != null)
					{
						for (int num2 = 0; num2 < array2.Length; num2++)
						{
							array[list[num2]] = array2[num2];
							CxsBatchRequest cxsBatchRequest = cxsBatchRequestData.Requests[num2];
							list2[num2].StoreAttribute(cxsBatchRequest.Attribute, cxsBatchRequest.Units, array2[num2]);
						}
					}
				}
				catch (CxsException ex)
				{
					Console.WriteLine("Random BatchGet error occurred. " + ex.ToString());
				}
			}
			return array;
		}

		public object BatchGet(string path, string attribute, string units)
		{
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(path, attribute, units);
			object[] array = BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData);
			if (array == null || array.Length == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return array[0];
		}

		public void ShowMessageBox(ShowMessageBoxEventArgs args)
		{
			UnityContainer.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<ShowMessageBoxEvent>().Publish(args);
		}

		public bool BatchSet(CxsBatchRequestData requests)
		{
			CxsException cxsUpdateException;
			return BatchSet(requests, out cxsUpdateException);
		}

		public bool BatchSet(CxsBatchRequestData requests, out CxsException cxsUpdateException)
		{
			cxsUpdateException = null;
			if (!IsConnected)
			{
				throw new CxvException("Data Provider is not connected");
			}
			if (requests == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			try
			{
				foreach (string canonicalPath in requests.CanonicalPaths)
				{
					if (!_dirtyRequestsInCache.Contains(canonicalPath))
					{
						_dirtyRequestsInCache.Add(canonicalPath);
					}
				}
				Case.BatchSet(requests);
			}
			catch (CxsException ex)
			{
				COMException ex2 = (cxsUpdateException = ex).InnerException as COMException;
				if (ex2 != null && ex2.ErrorCode == -2147176445)
				{
					if (_suspendedCount == 0)
					{
						OnDataChanged(requests);
						return true;
					}
					_suspendedRequests.AddRequests(requests.Requests);
					_needsUpdate = true;
					return false;
				}
				if (ex2 == null || ex2.ErrorCode != -2147176446 || _ccAsError)
				{
					if (ex2 != null && ex2.Message != null && ex2.Message == "cancel change")
					{
						return false;
					}
					if (ex2 != null && ex2.Message != null && ex2.Message.Length > 0 && ex2.ErrorCode != -2147467259)
					{
						if (!_isBatchPaste)
						{
							ShowMessageBoxEventArgs payload = new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
							{
								Title = GetI18nApplicationName(),
								Message = ex2.Message,
								MsgBoxButton = MessageBoxButton.OK,
								MsgBoxImage = MessageBoxImage.Exclamation,
								DialogResult = MessageBoxResult.OK
							};
							UnityContainer.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<ShowMessageBoxEvent>().Publish(payload);
						}
						else
						{
							_isErrorOccurredInBatchPaste = true;
						}
					}
					return false;
				}
				string message = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, Resources.ConsistencyError, ex2.Message);
				ShowMessageBoxEventArgs e = new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
				{
					Title = Resources.ConsistencyErrorTitle,
					Message = message,
					MsgBoxButton = MessageBoxButton.YesNo,
					MsgBoxImage = MessageBoxImage.Question,
					DialogResult = MessageBoxResult.Yes
				};
				if (!_force)
				{
					UnityContainer.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<ShowMessageBoxEvent>().Publish(e);
				}
				if (e.DialogResult != MessageBoxResult.Yes)
				{
					return false;
				}
				CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
				cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(requests.CommonBasePath, "ignore_errors", null, 1);
				cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequests(requests.Requests);
				try
				{
					Case.BatchSet(cxsBatchRequestData);
				}
				catch (CxsException ex3)
				{
					if (ex3.InnerException is COMException { ErrorCode: -2147176445 })
					{
						if (_suspendedCount == 0)
						{
							OnDataChanged(requests);
						}
						else
						{
							_suspendedRequests.AddRequests(requests.Requests);
							_needsUpdate = true;
						}
					}
					return false;
				}
			}
			if (_suspendedCount == 0)
			{
				OnDataChanged(requests);
			}
			else
			{
				_suspendedRequests.AddRequests(requests.Requests);
				_needsUpdate = true;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public string GetI18nApplicationName()
		{
			string text = "";
			try
			{
				string[] array = Case.ApplicationName.Split(' ');
				foreach (string text2 in array)
				{
					text += text2;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			text = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString(text);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return Case.ApplicationName;
			}
			return text;
		}

		public void SuspendNotifications()
		{
			_suspendedCount++;
		}

		public void ResumeNotifications()
		{
			if (_suspendedCount > 0)
			{
				_suspendedCount--;
			}
			if (_suspendedCount == 0 && _needsUpdate)
			{
				_needsUpdate = false;
				if (_suspendedRequests.Requests.Count > 0)
				{
					OnDataChanged(_suspendedRequests);
					_suspendedRequests = new CxsBatchRequestData();
				}
				else
				{
					OnDataChanged(null);
				}
			}
		}

		public void StartBatchPaste()
		{
			_isBatchPaste = true;
			_isErrorOccurredInBatchPaste = false;
		}

		public void BatchPasteError()
		{
			_isErrorOccurredInBatchPaste = true;
		}

		public void EndBatchPaste()
		{
			_isBatchPaste = false;
			if (_isErrorOccurredInBatchPaste)
			{
				_isErrorOccurredInBatchPaste = false;
				ShowMessageBoxEventArgs payload = new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
				{
					Title = GetI18nApplicationName(),
					Message = Resources.ErrorsOccurredDuringPaste,
					MsgBoxButton = MessageBoxButton.OK,
					MsgBoxImage = MessageBoxImage.Exclamation,
					DialogResult = MessageBoxResult.OK
				};
				UnityContainer.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<ShowMessageBoxEvent>().Publish(payload);
			}
		}

		public bool BatchSetNoRefresh(CxsBatchRequestData requests)
		{
			if (!IsConnected)
			{
				throw new CxvException("Data Provider is not connected");
			}
			if (requests == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			try
			{
				Case.BatchSet(requests);
			}
			catch (CxsException)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected virtual void OnDataChanged(CxsBatchRequestData requests)
		{
			UnityContainer.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<BatchSetCompletedEvent>().Publish(new BatchSetCompletedEventArgs(requests));
		}

		public bool BatchSet(string path, string attribute, string units, object value)
		{
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(path, attribute, units, value);
			return BatchSet(cxsBatchRequestData);
		}

		private void OnAddWatchedPath(CxsDataWatch watch, string path)
		{
			NodeCache value = null;
			if (!_nodeCache.TryGetValue(path, out value))
			{
				if (!path.StartsWith("Top."))
				{
					return;
				}
				value = new NodeCache(path);
				_nodeCache[path] = value;
			}
			value.RegisterWatch(watch);
		}

		private void ForgetWatch(CxsDataWatch watch, ReadOnlyCollection<string> watchedPaths)
		{
			if (watchedPaths == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (string watchedPath in watchedPaths)
			{
				NodeCache value = null;
				if (_nodeCache.TryGetValue(watchedPath, out value))
				{
					value.ForgetWatch(watch);
				}
			}
		}

		public virtual void UpdateDataCache()
		{
			CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Notify(_updateDataStarted, this, EventArgs.Empty);
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			foreach (NodeCache value in _nodeCache.Values)
			{
				value.AddToRefreshRequest(cxsBatchRequestData);
			}
			UpdateDataCache(cxsBatchRequestData);
			CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Notify(_updateDataCompleted, this, EventArgs.Empty);
		}

		public virtual void UpdateDataCacheSelective(CxsBatchRequestData requests)
		{
			CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Notify(_updateDataStarted, this, EventArgs.Empty);
			UpdateDataCache(requests);
			CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection.Notify(_updateDataCompleted, this, EventArgs.Empty);
		}

		public void UpdateDataCache(string path)
		{
			if (path != null)
			{
				CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
				NodeCache value = null;
				if (_nodeCache.TryGetValue(path, out value))
				{
					value.AddToRefreshRequest(cxsBatchRequestData);
				}
				UpdateDataCache(cxsBatchRequestData);
			}
		}

		public void UpdateDataCache(IEnumerable<string> paths)
		{
			if (paths == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			foreach (string path in paths)
			{
				NodeCache value = null;
				if (_nodeCache.TryGetValue(path, out value))
				{
					value.AddToRefreshRequest(cxsBatchRequestData);
				}
			}
			UpdateDataCache(cxsBatchRequestData);
		}

		public object[] LargeBatchGet(CxsBatchRequestData requests, bool showProgressIndicator = false)
		{
			object[] array = null;
			if ((double)requests.Requests.Count() > 3000.0)
			{
				array = new object[requests.Requests.Count()];
				int num = requests.Requests.Count() / 2000 + 1;
				if (num >= 3 && showProgressIndicator)
				{
					MMProgressWindow.Show(num - 1);
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
				{
					int num2 = 0;
					num2 = ((i != num - 1) ? 2000 : (requests.Requests.Count() - 2000 * i));
					if (num2 > 0)
					{
						CxsBatchRequest[] array2 = new CxsBatchRequest[num2];
						Array.Copy(requests.Requests.ToArray(), i * 2000, array2, 0, num2);
						object[] array3 = Case.BatchGet(array2);
						if (array3 == null)
						{
							return null;
						}
						Array.Copy(array3, 0, array, i * 2000, num2);
					}
					if (num >= 3 && showProgressIndicator)
					{
						MMProgressWindow.StepProgress();
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				array = Case.BatchGet(requests);
			}
			return array;
		}

		public void UpdateDataCache(CxsBatchRequestData requests)
		{
			if (requests == null || requests.RequestsCount == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			recursing2++;
			object[] array = LargeBatchGet(requests);
			if (array == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<CxsDataWatch> list = new List<CxsDataWatch>();
			int num = -1;
			foreach (CxsBatchRequest request in requests.Requests)
			{
				num++;
				NodeCache value = request.Tag as NodeCache;
				if (value == null)
				{
					_nodeCache.TryGetValue(request.Path, out value);
				}
				if (value != null)
				{
					value.StoreResultFromRefreshRequest(request, array[num], list);
					if (_dirtyRequestsInCache.Contains(value.Path))
					{
						_dirtyRequestsInCache.Remove(value.Path);
					}
				}
			}
			recursing2--;
			foreach (CxsDataWatch item in list)
			{
				item.RaiseChangedNotifications();
			}
		}

		public void PurgeDataCache()
		{
			if ((double)_nodeCache.Count < (double)_lastPurge * PurgeAt)
			{
				return;
			}
			Dictionary<string, NodeCache> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, NodeCache>();
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, NodeCache> item in _nodeCache)
			{
				if (item.Value.ActiveDataWatches.Count() > 0)
				{
					dictionary[item.Key] = item.Value;
				}
			}
			_nodeCache = dictionary;
			_lastPurge = _nodeCache.Count;
		}

		public CxvActionResult InvokeAction(string path, string action, bool refresh, params object[] arguments)
		{
			if (!IsConnected)
			{
				return new CxvActionResult
				{
					Succeeded = false,
					Error = "CxsDataProvider is not connected"
				};
			}
			CxvActionResult cxvActionResult = new CxvActionResult();
			try
			{
				cxvActionResult.Result = Case.InvokeAction(path, action, arguments);
				cxvActionResult.Succeeded = true;
			}
			catch (CxsException ex)
			{
				cxvActionResult.Succeeded = false;
				cxvActionResult.Error = ex.Message;
			}
			catch (COMException ex2)
			{
				cxvActionResult.Succeeded = false;
				cxvActionResult.Error = ex2.Message;
			}
			if (refresh)
			{
				if (_suspendedCount == 0)
				{
					OnDataChanged(null);
				}
				else
				{
					_needsUpdate = true;
				}
			}
			return cxvActionResult;
		}

		protected void OnPropertyChanged(string name)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(name));
			}
		}

		public void SuspendUpdateAllDataCache()
		{
			_dataCacheUpdateFlag++;
		}

		public void ResumeUpdateAllDataCache()
		{
			if (_dataCacheUpdateFlag > 0)
			{
				_dataCacheUpdateFlag--;
			}
		}
	}
	public class CxvPropertyDeclaration
	{
		private class NameTypeKey
		{
			private int _hashCode;

			private string _name;

			private Type _ownerType;

			public NameTypeKey(string name, Type ownerType)
			{
				_name = name;
				ReplaceOwnerType(ownerType);
			}

			public override bool Equals(object o)
			{
				if (o is NameTypeKey key)
				{
					return Equals(key);
				}
				return false;
			}

			public bool Equals(NameTypeKey key)
			{
				if (_name.Equals(key._name))
				{
					return _ownerType == key._ownerType;
				}
				return false;
			}

			public override int GetHashCode()
			{
				return _hashCode;
			}

			public void ReplaceOwnerType(Type value)
			{
				_ownerType = value;
				_hashCode = _name.GetHashCode() ^ _ownerType.GetHashCode();
			}
		}

		internal static int GlobalIndexCount = 1;

		private static Dictionary<NameTypeKey, CxvPropertyDeclaration> _propertyDict = new Dictionary<NameTypeKey, CxvPropertyDeclaration>();

		public string Name { get; private set; }

		public Type OwnerType { get; private set; }

		public bool IsReadOnly => true;

		public Type PropertyType => Metadata.GetPropertyType();

		internal int GlobalIndex { get; private set; }

		public CxvPropertyBase Metadata { get; private set; }

		private CxvPropertyDeclaration()
		{
		}

		public static CxvPropertyDeclaration Register(string name, Type ownerType, CxvPropertyBase metadata)
		{
			GlobalIndexCount++;
			CxvPropertyDeclaration cxvPropertyDeclaration = new CxvPropertyDeclaration
			{
				Name = name,
				OwnerType = ownerType,
				Metadata = metadata,
				GlobalIndex = GlobalIndexCount
			};
			if (metadata != null)
			{
				metadata.Name = name;
				metadata.PropertyDeclaration = cxvPropertyDeclaration;
			}
			_propertyDict.Add(new NameTypeKey(name, ownerType), cxvPropertyDeclaration);
			if (metadata is CxvPropertySelector && metadata is CxvPropertySelector { Choices: not null } cxvPropertySelector)
			{
				foreach (CxvPropertyChoice choice in cxvPropertySelector.Choices)
				{
					if (choice.Property != null)
					{
						choice.Property.PropertyDeclaration = cxvPropertyDeclaration;
					}
				}
			}
			return cxvPropertyDeclaration;
		}

		public static CxvPropertyDeclaration FromName(string name, Type ownerType)
		{
			CxvPropertyDeclaration value = null;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(name))
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("name");
			}
			if (ownerType == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("ownerType");
			}
			NameTypeKey nameTypeKey = new NameTypeKey(name, ownerType);
			while (value == null && ownerType != null)
			{
				RuntimeHelpers.RunClassConstructor(ownerType.TypeHandle);
				nameTypeKey.ReplaceOwnerType(ownerType);
				if (_propertyDict.TryGetValue(nameTypeKey, out value))
				{
					break;
				}
				ownerType = ownerType.BaseType;
			}
			return value;
		}
	}
	public class CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection : Collection<DelegateReference>
	{
		public static CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection Combine(CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection handlers, EventHandler value)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return handlers;
			}
			if (handlers == null)
			{
				handlers = new CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection();
			}
			handlers.Add(new DelegateReference(value, keepReferenceAlive: false));
			return handlers;
		}

		public static CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection Remove(CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection handlers, EventHandler value)
		{
			if (handlers == null)
			{
				return handlers;
			}
			for (int num = handlers.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				Delegate target = handlers[num].Target;
				if ((object)target == null)
				{
					handlers.RemoveAt(num);
				}
				if (target == value)
				{
					handlers.RemoveAt(num);
				}
			}
			if (handlers.Count == 0)
			{
				handlers = null;
			}
			return handlers;
		}

		public static CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection RemoveDeadReferences(CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection handlers)
		{
			if (handlers == null)
			{
				return handlers;
			}
			for (int num = handlers.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				if ((object)handlers[num].Target == null)
				{
					handlers.RemoveAt(num);
				}
			}
			if (handlers.Count == 0)
			{
				handlers = null;
			}
			return handlers;
		}

		public static void Notify(CxvWeakEventHandlerCollection handlers, object sender, EventArgs args)
		{
			if (handlers == null || handlers.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			int count = handlers.Count;
			EventHandler[] array = new EventHandler[count];
			for (int num = count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				if (handlers[num].Target is EventHandler eventHandler)
				{
					array[num] = eventHandler;
				}
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				array[i]?.Invoke(sender, args);
			}
		}
	}
	public class CxvWeakPropertyChangedCollection : Collection<DelegateReference>
	{
		public static CxvWeakPropertyChangedCollection Combine(CxvWeakPropertyChangedCollection handlers, PropertyChangedEventHandler value)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return handlers;
			}
			if (handlers == null)
			{
				handlers = new CxvWeakPropertyChangedCollection();
			}
			handlers.Add(new DelegateReference(value, keepReferenceAlive: false));
			return handlers;
		}

		public static CxvWeakPropertyChangedCollection Remove(CxvWeakPropertyChangedCollection handlers, PropertyChangedEventHandler value)
		{
			if (handlers == null)
			{
				return handlers;
			}
			for (int num = handlers.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				Delegate target = handlers[num].Target;
				if ((object)target == null)
				{
					handlers.RemoveAt(num);
				}
				if (target == value)
				{
					handlers.RemoveAt(num);
				}
			}
			if (handlers.Count == 0)
			{
				handlers = null;
			}
			return handlers;
		}

		public static CxvWeakPropertyChangedCollection RemoveDeadReferences(CxvWeakPropertyChangedCollection handlers)
		{
			if (handlers == null)
			{
				return handlers;
			}
			for (int num = handlers.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				if ((object)handlers[num].Target == null)
				{
					handlers.RemoveAt(num);
				}
			}
			if (handlers.Count == 0)
			{
				handlers = null;
			}
			return handlers;
		}

		public static void Notify(CxvWeakPropertyChangedCollection handlers, object sender, string propertyName)
		{
			if (handlers != null)
			{
				Notify(handlers, sender, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
			}
		}

		public static void Notify(CxvWeakPropertyChangedCollection handlers, object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (handlers == null || handlers.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			int count = handlers.Count;
			Delegate[] array = new Delegate[count];
			for (int num = count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				if (handlers[num].Target is PropertyChangedEventHandler propertyChangedEventHandler)
				{
					array[num] = propertyChangedEventHandler;
				}
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				Delegate obj = array[i];
				if ((object)obj != null)
				{
					((PropertyChangedEventHandler)obj)(sender, args);
				}
			}
		}
	}
