	public class MMProgressWindow : Window, IComponentConnector
	{
		internal static object instanceLock = new object();

		internal static MMProgressWindow instance = null;

		internal Dispatcher winDispatcher;

		private Stopwatch timer;

		private bool allowClose;

		private bool _showETA;

		internal ProgressBar progressBar;

		internal TextBlock txbTimeRemaining;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		private bool showETA
		{
			get
			{
				return _showETA;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _showETA)
				{
					_showETA = value;
					if (value)
					{
						txbTimeRemaining.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					}
					else
					{
						txbTimeRemaining.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private double progressValue
		{
			get
			{
				return progressBar.Value;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(value > 0.0))
				{
					return;
				}
				progressBar.Value = value;
				updateETA();
				if (value >= progressBar.Maximum)
				{
					Task.Run(delegate
					{
						Thread.Sleep(1000);
						CloseWindow();
						return 0;
					});
				}
			}
		}

		public static void Show(double maximumProgress, bool showETA = true, string title = null)
		{
			lock (instanceLock)
			{
				if (instance == null)
				{
					if (maximumProgress <= 0.0)
					{
						maximumProgress = 100.0;
					}
					MMProgressWindow win = null;
					CountdownEvent cde = new CountdownEvent(1);
					Thread thread = new Thread((ThreadStart)delegate
					{
						SynchronizationContext.SetSynchronizationContext(new DispatcherSynchronizationContext(Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher));
						win = new MMProgressWindow(maximumProgress, showETA, title);
						win.Show();
						win.winDispatcher = Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher;
						cde.Signal();
						Dispatcher.Run();
					});
					thread.SetApartmentState(ApartmentState.STA);
					thread.Start();
					cde.Wait();
					cde.Dispose();
					instance = win;
				}
			}
		}

		public static void SetProgressValue(double ProgressValue)
		{
			lock (instanceLock)
			{
				instance?.winDispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					if (instance != null)
					{
						instance.progressValue = ProgressValue;
					}
				});
			}
		}

		public static void SetShowETA(bool ShowETA)
		{
			lock (instanceLock)
			{
				instance?.winDispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					if (instance != null)
					{
						instance.showETA = ShowETA;
					}
				});
			}
		}

		public static void SetMaximumProgress(double MaximumProgress)
		{
			lock (instanceLock)
			{
				instance?.winDispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					if (instance != null)
					{
						instance.progressBar.Maximum = MaximumProgress;
					}
				});
			}
		}

		public static void StepProgress(double? steps = null)
		{
			lock (instanceLock)
			{
				instance?.winDispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					if (instance != null)
					{
						instance._step(steps);
					}
				});
			}
		}

		public static void CloseWindow()
		{
			lock (instanceLock)
			{
				instance?.winDispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					if (instance != null)
					{
						instance.InternalCloseWindow();
					}
				});
			}
		}

		internal void InternalCloseWindow()
		{
			MMProgressWindow mMProgressWindow = null;
			lock (instanceLock)
			{
				mMProgressWindow = instance;
				instance = null;
			}
			allowClose = true;
			mMProgressWindow.Close();
		}

		internal MMProgressWindow(double maxValue, bool showETA = true, string title = null)
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			progressBar.Maximum = maxValue;
			progressBar.Value = 0.0;
			timer = new Stopwatch();
			timer.Start();
			if (title != null)
			{
				base.Title = title;
			}
			else
			{
				base.Title = CXSResources.MMProgressDlg_Title;
			}
			base.Topmost = true;
			base.Closed += win_Closed;
			base.Closing += MMProgressWindow_Closing;
		}

		private void MMProgressWindow_Closing(object sender, CancelEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!allowClose && sender == this)
			{
				e.Cancel = true;
			}
		}

		private static void win_Closed(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			MMProgressWindow obj = sender as MMProgressWindow;
			obj.timer.Stop();
			obj.Dispatcher.BeginInvokeShutdown(DispatcherPriority.Background);
		}

		internal void _step(double? steps)
		{
			if (steps.HasValue)
			{
				progressValue += steps.Value;
			}
			else
			{
				progressValue++;
			}
		}

		internal void updateETA()
		{
			if (progressBar.Value == 0.0)
			{
				txbTimeRemaining.Text = string.Empty;
			}
			TimeSpan timeSpan = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds((progressBar.Maximum - progressBar.Value) / progressBar.Value * (double)timer.ElapsedMilliseconds);
			txbTimeRemaining.Text = CXSResources.MMProgressDlg_ETA + " " + timeSpan.ToString("hh\\:mm\\:ss");
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/AspenTech.AspenCxs.CxsViewModel;component/mmprogresswindow.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				progressBar = (ProgressBar)target;
				break;
			case 2:
				txbTimeRemaining = (TextBlock)target;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}
	}

	public abstract class NotifiablePropertiesObject : INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private readonly Dictionary<string, object> _properties = new Dictionary<string, object>();

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		protected T GetValue<T>(T defaultValue = default(T), [CallerMemberName] string propertyName = "")
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(propertyName) && _properties.ContainsKey(propertyName))
			{
				return (T)_properties[propertyName];
			}
			return defaultValue;
		}

		protected void SetValue<T>(T value, [CallerMemberName] string propertyName = null)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(propertyName))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_properties.ContainsKey(propertyName))
			{
				if (object.Equals((T)_properties[propertyName], value))
				{
					_properties[propertyName] = value;
					OnPropertyChanged(propertyName);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				_properties.Add(propertyName, value);
				OnPropertyChanged(propertyName);
			}
		}

		public string Save(Func<string, object, string> propertyValueSerializer = null)
		{
			XElement xElement = new XElement("root");
			foreach (string key in _properties.Keys)
			{
				object obj = _properties[key];
				if (propertyValueSerializer == null)
				{
					xElement.SetElementValue(key, obj);
				}
				else
				{
					xElement.SetElementValue(key, propertyValueSerializer(key, obj));
				}
			}
			return xElement.ToString(SaveOptions.DisableFormatting);
		}

		public void TryParse(string text, Func<string, string, object> propertyValueDeserializer = null)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
			{
				return;
			}
			XElement xElement = null;
			xElement = XElement.Parse(text);
			if (xElement == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Type type = GetType();
			foreach (XElement item in xElement.Elements())
			{
				string localName = item.Name.LocalName;
				string value = item.Value;
				if (propertyValueDeserializer == null)
				{
					PropertyInfo property = type.GetProperty(localName);
					if (property != null)
					{
						TypeConverter converter = TypeDescriptor.GetConverter(property.PropertyType);
						if (converter != null)
						{
							SetValue(converter.ConvertFromString(value), localName);
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					SetValue(propertyValueDeserializer(localName, value), localName);
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			this.PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
		}
	}

namespace AspenTech.AspenCxs.CxsViewModel.ViewData

	public class CxvSecondLevelChildCountIndexBasisData : CxvChildCountIndexBasisData
	{
		public CxvSecondLevelChildCountIndexBasisData(CxvChildCountIndexBasis indexBasis, ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer)
			: base(indexBasis, dataContainer)
		{
		}

		protected override void RefreshChildIds()
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			if (base.IndexBasis.ProxyRow.HasValue)
			{
				base.HasProxyRow = base.IndexBasis.ProxyRow.Value;
			}
			CxvPropertyWatch cxvPropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
			string text2 = base.DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(base.IndexBasis.DataPath, cxvPropertyWatch);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
			{
				ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = base.DataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
				if (cxsDataWatch != null)
				{
					cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text2, "child_ids");
					cxvPropertyWatch.AddWatch(cxsDataWatch);
					object[] values = cxsDataWatch.Values;
					text = ((values != null) ? CxsConverter.ValueToString(values[0]) : string.Empty);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						string[] array = text.Split('\n');
						if (array.Length != 0)
						{
							text2 = base.DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(base.IndexBasis.DataPath + "." + array[0], cxvPropertyWatch);
							cxsDataWatch = base.DataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
							cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text2, "child_ids");
							if (!base.IndexBasis.ProxyRow.HasValue)
							{
								cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text2, "action_status", "InsertChild");
							}
							cxvPropertyWatch.AddWatch(cxsDataWatch);
							values = cxsDataWatch.Values;
							text = ((values != null) ? CxsConverter.ValueToString(values[0]) : string.Empty);
							if (!base.IndexBasis.ProxyRow.HasValue && values != null && values[1] != null)
							{
								base.HasProxyRow = CxsConverter.ValueToActionStatusDetails(values[1])?.CanInvoke ?? false;
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			SetChildIds(text, cxvPropertyWatch);
		}

		public override void Insert(int index, object value)
		{
			if (base.DataContainer != null)
			{
				try
				{
					string path = base.DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(base.IndexBasis.DataPath) + ".#0";
					base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(path, "InsertChild", true, index);
				}
				catch (CxsException)
				{
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class CxvSecondLevelChildCountIndexBasisDataNew : CxvChildCountIndexBasisData
	{
		public CxvSecondLevelChildCountIndexBasisDataNew(CxvChildCountIndexBasis indexBasis, ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer)
			: base(indexBasis, dataContainer)
		{
		}

		protected override void RefreshChildIds()
		{
			string childIds = string.Empty;
			base.HasProxyRow = true;
			CxvPropertyWatch cxvPropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
			string text = base.DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(base.IndexBasis.DataPath, cxvPropertyWatch);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = base.DataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
				if (cxsDataWatch != null)
				{
					cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "child_ids");
					cxvPropertyWatch.AddWatch(cxsDataWatch);
					object[] values = cxsDataWatch.Values;
					string text2 = ((values != null) ? CxsConverter.ValueToString(values[0]) : string.Empty);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
					{
						int num = 0;
						string[] array = text2.Split('\n');
						foreach (string text3 in array)
						{
							ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch2 = base.DataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
							string path = base.DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(base.IndexBasis.DataPath + "." + text3, cxvPropertyWatch);
							cxsDataWatch2.AddWatch(path, "child_ids");
							cxvPropertyWatch.AddWatch(cxsDataWatch2);
							if (cxsDataWatch2.Values == null)
							{
								continue;
							}
							string text4 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(cxsDataWatch2.Values[0]);
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
							{
								int num2 = text4.Split('\n').Length;
								if (num2 > num)
								{
									num = num2;
									childIds = text4;
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			SetChildIds(childIds, cxvPropertyWatch);
		}
	}
	public class CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem : CxvObservableObject, IEquatable<CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem>, IViewDataSerializable
	{
		private string _display;

		private string _id;

		private bool? _complete;

		private bool? _entered;

		public string Display
		{
			get
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_display))
				{
					return _display;
				}
				return _id;
			}
		}

		public string Id => _id;

		public bool? Complete
		{
			get
			{
				return _complete;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _complete)
				{
					_complete = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("Complete");
				}
			}
		}

		public bool? Entered
		{
			get
			{
				return _entered;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _entered)
				{
					_entered = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("Entered");
				}
			}
		}

		public string HelpFile { get; set; }

		public int? HelpId { get; set; }

		public CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem(string id, string display)
		{
			_id = id;
			_display = display;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			if (_display != null)
			{
				return _display;
			}
			return _id;
		}

		public bool Equals(CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem other)
		{
			if (other == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return other._id == _id;
		}

		XElement IViewDataSerializable.WriteXElement()
		{
			List<XElement> list = new List<XElement>();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Display))
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("Display", Display));
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Id))
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("Id", Id));
			}
			if (Complete.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("Complete", Complete.Value));
			}
			if (Entered.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("Entered", Entered.Value));
			}
			if (list.Count <= 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return new XElement(GetType().Name, list);
		}
	}
	public class CxvAdvCollectionPropertyData : CxvStatePropertyData, ICxvPropertyData, ICxvObjectVerbs, IViewDataSerializable, ICxvCompletableObject
	{
		private bool? _isCalculated;

		private string _variablePrompt;

		private EventHandler _pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;

		private EventHandler _itemsDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;

		private EventHandler _itemsWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;

		private EventHandler _completeWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;

		private EventHandler _selectedItemPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;

		private CxvAdvCollectionProperty _property;

		private ICxvViewDataContainer _dataContainer;

		private string _dataPath;

		private string _resolvedDataPath;

		private ICxsDataWatch _itemsWatch;

		private ICxsDataWatch _itemsValueWatch;

		private bool _idWatchItemsAdded;

		private CxvPropertyWatch _selectedItemPropertyWatch;

		private CxvPropertyWatch _dataPathDependencies;

		private ICxsDataWatch _completeValuesWatch;

		private ObservableCollection<CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem> _items;

		private string _selectedItem;

		private ReadOnlyCollection<string> _validAttributes;

		private ReadOnlyCollection<string> _currentChildren;

		private ReadOnlyCollection<string> _currentChildrenDisplay;

		private ReadOnlyCollection<string> _childIdOptions;

		private ReadOnlyCollection<string> _childIdHelpFiles;

		private ReadOnlyCollection<string> _childIdHelpIds;

		private ObservableCollection<CxvAdvCollectionItem> _sourceItems;

		private CxvPropertyWatch _itemsDependencies;

		private bool _canEditCollection;

		private bool _canInsertCollection;

		private bool _canRenameChild;

		private bool _canDeleteChild;

		private bool _canClearChild;

		private Collection<CxvVerbDescriptor> _verbs;

		private bool _inNew;

		private int _textmask;

		private IList<string> _complete;

		private Collection<string> _resolvedComplete;

		private IList<string> _entered;

		private Collection<string> _resolvedEntered;

		private bool _isSystemOrderArray;

		private bool? _isArray;

		private bool _isArrayOfValues;

		private bool _indexOrdered;

		private Dictionary<string, string> _optionPrompts;

		public ObservableCollection<CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem> Items
		{
			get
			{
				if (_items == null)
				{
					RefreshItems();
				}
				if ((_property.Sorted ?? false) && _items != null)
				{
					return new ObservableCollection<CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem>(_items.OrderBy((CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem i) => i.Display));
				}
				return _items;
			}
		}

		public string SelectedItem
		{
			get
			{
				if (_selectedItem == null)
				{
					RefreshSelectedItem();
				}
				_inNew = false;
				return _selectedItem;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(_selectedItem != value))
				{
					return;
				}
				if (value == null)
				{
					if (_canClearChild || _canDeleteChild)
					{
						DoVerb("Delete", null);
					}
					return;
				}
				Dictionary<string, string> optionPrompts = _optionPrompts;
				if (optionPrompts != null && optionPrompts.ContainsKey(value))
				{
					_variablePrompt = _optionPrompts[value];
					OnPropertyChanged("VariablePrompt");
				}
				ChangeSelectedItem(value);
				if (_selectedItem != value)
				{
					OnPropertyChanged("SelectedItem");
					OnPropertyChanged("SelectedObject");
					if (this is CxvAdvCollectionTooltipData)
					{
						OnPropertyChanged("VariablePrompt");
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public string DefaultNewName { get; set; }

		public bool? IsReadOnly => false;

		public bool? IsCalculated
		{
			get
			{
				if (_property.IsCalculated.HasValue)
				{
					return _property.IsCalculated;
				}
				return _isCalculated ?? false;
			}
		}

		public bool? IsEntered
		{
			get
			{
				if (_items == null)
				{
					RefreshItems();
				}
				if (_items == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (_items.Count > 1)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (_items.Count == 1 && _items[0].Display != Resources.CreateNewMenuItem)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public object SelectedObject
		{
			get
			{
				if (_selectedItem == null)
				{
					RefreshSelectedItem();
				}
				_inNew = false;
				if (_items == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _items.Where((CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem p) => p.Display == _selectedItem).FirstOrDefault();
			}
			set
			{
				CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem = value as CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem;
				if ((cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem == null && _selectedItem != null) || (cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem != null && cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem.Display != _selectedItem))
				{
					SelectedItem = cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem?.Display;
				}
			}
		}

		public string CxsItemMoniker => CxvObservableObject.CxsPathToMoniker(_resolvedDataPath);

		public override string VariablePrompt
		{
			get
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.PromptLocalized))
				{
					return _property.PromptLocalized;
				}
				return _variablePrompt;
			}
		}

		public virtual Dictionary<string, string> OptionPrompts => _optionPrompts;

		private EventHandler pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler == null)
				{
					_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler = delegate
					{
						ResetDataPath();
					};
				}
				return _pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		private EventHandler itemsDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (_itemsDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler == null)
				{
					_itemsDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler = delegate
					{
						RefreshItems();
					};
				}
				return _itemsDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		private EventHandler itemsWatch_ValuesChangedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (_itemsWatch_ValuesChangedHandler == null)
				{
					_itemsWatch_ValuesChangedHandler = delegate
					{
						RefreshItems();
					};
				}
				return _itemsWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		private EventHandler completeWatch_ValuesChangedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (_completeWatch_ValuesChangedHandler == null)
				{
					_completeWatch_ValuesChangedHandler = delegate
					{
						OnCompleteValueChange();
					};
				}
				return _completeWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		private EventHandler selectedItemPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (_selectedItemPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler == null)
				{
					_selectedItemPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler = delegate
					{
						RefreshSelectedItem();
					};
				}
				return _selectedItemPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		public bool IsUnloaded => _dataContainer == null;

		public bool IsComplete
		{
			get
			{
				foreach (CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem item in Items)
				{
					if (item.Complete.HasValue && !item.Complete.Value)
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public CxvAdvCollectionPropertyData(CxvAdvCollectionProperty property, ICxvViewDataContainer dataProvider, string dataPath, int newItemMask, IList<string> complete)
			: base(dataProvider, property)
		{
			if (property == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("property cannot be null.");
			}
			_property = property;
			_dataContainer = dataProvider;
			_dataPath = dataPath;
			_textmask = newItemMask;
			_complete = complete;
			_entered = null;
			if (_property.Filter != null)
			{
				_property.Filter.SetContainer(dataProvider);
				_property.Filter.UpdateItems += Filter_UpdateItems;
			}
		}

		public CxvAdvCollectionPropertyData(CxvAdvCollectionProperty property, ICxvViewDataContainer dataProvider, string dataPath, int newItemMask, IList<string> complete, IList<string> entered)
			: base(dataProvider, property)
		{
			if (property == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("property cannot be null.");
			}
			_property = property;
			_dataContainer = dataProvider;
			_dataPath = dataPath;
			_textmask = newItemMask;
			_complete = complete;
			_entered = entered;
			if (_property.Filter != null)
			{
				_property.Filter.SetContainer(dataProvider);
				_property.Filter.UpdateItems += Filter_UpdateItems;
			}
		}

		private void Filter_UpdateItems(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			RefreshItems();
		}

		private string EnsureDataPath()
		{
			if (_resolvedDataPath == null && _dataContainer != null && _dataPath != null)
			{
				ResolveDataPath();
			}
			return _resolvedDataPath;
		}

		private void ResetDataPath()
		{
			if (_dataPathDependencies != null)
			{
				_dataPathDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				_dataPathDependencies = null;
			}
			_resolvedDataPath = null;
			_items = null;
			_selectedItem = null;
			if (_itemsWatch != null)
			{
				_itemsWatch.ValuesChanged -= itemsWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;
				_itemsWatch = null;
				_idWatchItemsAdded = false;
			}
			if (_itemsValueWatch != null)
			{
				_itemsValueWatch.ValuesChanged -= itemsWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;
				_itemsValueWatch = null;
			}
			ResetCompletePaths();
			if (_property.Filter != null)
			{
				_property.Filter.ResetValues();
			}
			OnPropertyChanged("Items");
			OnPropertyChanged("SelectedItem");
			OnPropertyChanged("SelectedObject");
			OnPropertyChanged("CxsItemMoniker");
			OnPropertyChanged("IsEntered");
			OnPropertyChanged("VariablePrompt");
			OnPropertyChanged("OptionPrompts");
		}

		private void ResetCompletePaths()
		{
			if ((_complete != null && _complete.Count != 0) || (_entered != null && _entered.Count != 0))
			{
				if (_completeValuesWatch != null)
				{
					_completeValuesWatch.ValuesChanged -= completeWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;
					_completeValuesWatch = null;
				}
				_resolvedComplete = null;
				_resolvedEntered = null;
			}
		}

		private void ResolveDataPath()
		{
			if (_dataPathDependencies != null)
			{
				_dataPathDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				_dataPathDependencies = null;
			}
			CxvPropertyWatch cxvPropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.DataPath))
			{
				_resolvedDataPath = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.DataPath, cxvPropertyWatch);
			}
			ResolveCompletePaths(cxvPropertyWatch);
			ResolveEnteredPaths(cxvPropertyWatch);
			if (!cxvPropertyWatch.IsEmpty)
			{
				_dataPathDependencies = cxvPropertyWatch;
				_dataPathDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		public void RefreshItems()
		{
			if (!IsUnloaded)
			{
				ObservableCollection<CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem> observableCollection = null;
				observableCollection = (_property.UseItemsCollectionResolved ? RefreshItemsFromCollection() : ((!_property.DisplayValues.HasValue || !_property.DisplayValues.Value) ? RefreshItemsFromDataPath() : RefreshItemsFromDataPathUsingValues()));
				bool flag = _items != observableCollection;
				object oldSelectedObject = null;
				if (_items != null && _selectedItem != null)
				{
					oldSelectedObject = SelectedObject;
				}
				_items = ((observableCollection != null) ? observableCollection : new ObservableCollection<CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem>());
				if (!_property.UseItemsCollectionResolved)
				{
					RefreshCompleteValues();
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					OnPropertyChanged("Items");
					OnPropertyChanged("IsEntered");
				}
				if (_selectedItem == null || flag || (_property.UseItemsCollectionResolved && !_items.Contains(new CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem(_selectedItem, null))) || (!_property.UseItemsCollectionResolved && (_currentChildren == null || (_currentChildrenDisplay != null && !_currentChildrenDisplay.Contains(_selectedItem)) || (_currentChildrenDisplay == null && !_currentChildren.Contains(_selectedItem)))))
				{
					RefreshSelectedItem(oldSelectedObject);
				}
			}
		}

		private ObservableCollection<CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem> RefreshItemsFromCollection()
		{
			if (_sourceItems != _property.Items)
			{
				_sourceItems = _property.Items;
			}
			if (_itemsDependencies != null)
			{
				_itemsDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= itemsDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				_itemsDependencies = null;
			}
			ObservableCollection<CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem>();
			CxvPropertyWatch cxvPropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
			if (_sourceItems != null)
			{
				Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
				foreach (CxvAdvCollectionItem sourceItem in _sourceItems)
				{
					bool? flag = true;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sourceItem.EnabledPropertyPath))
					{
						flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(_dataContainer.ResolveViewPropertyPath(sourceItem.EnabledPropertyPath, cxvPropertyWatch));
					}
					if (flag.HasValue && flag.Value)
					{
						observableCollection.Add(new CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem(sourceItem.DisplayLocalized, null));
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sourceItem.PromptLocalized))
					{
						dictionary.Add(sourceItem.DisplayLocalized, sourceItem.PromptLocalized);
					}
				}
				if (dictionary.Count > 0)
				{
					_optionPrompts = dictionary;
					OnPropertyChanged("OptionPrompts");
				}
			}
			if (!cxvPropertyWatch.IsEmpty)
			{
				_itemsDependencies = cxvPropertyWatch;
				_itemsDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += itemsDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
			return observableCollection;
		}

		private void OnCompleteValueChange()
		{
			RefreshCompleteValues();
			OnPropertyChanged("Items");
			OnPropertyChanged("SelectedItem");
			OnPropertyChanged("SelectedObject");
		}

		private void RefreshCompleteValues()
		{
			if (_items == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_complete == null || _complete.Count == 0)
			{
				foreach (CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem item in _items)
				{
					if (item.Display != Resources.CreateNewMenuItem)
					{
						item.Complete = true;
					}
					if (_currentChildren == null || !_currentChildren.Contains(item.Id))
					{
						item.Complete = null;
					}
				}
			}
			if (_entered == null || _entered.Count == 0)
			{
				foreach (CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem item2 in _items)
				{
					if (item2.Display != Resources.CreateNewMenuItem && (_currentChildren == null || !_currentChildren.Contains(item2.Id)))
					{
						item2.Entered = false;
					}
				}
			}
			if ((_complete == null || _complete.Count == 0) && (_entered == null || _entered.Count == 0))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_completeValuesWatch != null)
			{
				_completeValuesWatch.ValuesChanged -= completeWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;
				_completeValuesWatch = null;
			}
			ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = _dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
			if (cxsDataWatch == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch2 = _dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
			if (_resolvedComplete != null)
			{
				foreach (string item3 in _resolvedComplete)
				{
					cxsDataWatch2.AddWatch(item3, "isleaf", null);
				}
			}
			if (_resolvedEntered != null)
			{
				foreach (string item4 in _resolvedEntered)
				{
					cxsDataWatch2.AddWatch(item4, "isleaf", null);
				}
			}
			object[] values = cxsDataWatch2.Values;
			int num = 0;
			if (_isSystemOrderArray)
			{
				if (_currentChildren != null)
				{
					foreach (string currentChild in _currentChildren)
					{
						num = 0;
						if (_resolvedComplete != null)
						{
							foreach (string item5 in _resolvedComplete)
							{
								if (values != null && num < values.Length)
								{
									if (CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(values[num]) ?? false)
									{
										cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(item5, "tabstate", "complete");
									}
									else
									{
										cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(item5 + "." + currentChild, "tabstate", "complete");
									}
									num++;
								}
								else
								{
									cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(item5 + "." + currentChild, "tabstate", "complete");
								}
							}
						}
						if (_resolvedEntered == null)
						{
							continue;
						}
						foreach (string item6 in _resolvedEntered)
						{
							if (values != null && num < values.Length)
							{
								if (CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(values[num]) ?? false)
								{
									cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(item6, "tabstate", "entered");
								}
								else
								{
									cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(item6 + "." + currentChild, "tabstate", "entered");
								}
								num++;
							}
							else
							{
								cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(item6 + "." + currentChild, "tabstate", "entered");
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				foreach (CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem item7 in _items)
				{
					if (!(item7.Display != Resources.CreateNewMenuItem))
					{
						continue;
					}
					num = 0;
					if (_resolvedComplete != null)
					{
						foreach (string item8 in _resolvedComplete)
						{
							if (values != null && num < values.Length)
							{
								if (CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(values[num]) ?? false)
								{
									cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(item8, "tabstate", "complete");
								}
								else
								{
									cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(item8 + "." + item7.Id, "tabstate", "complete");
								}
								num++;
							}
							else
							{
								cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(item8 + "." + item7.Id, "tabstate", "complete");
							}
						}
					}
					if (_resolvedEntered == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					foreach (string item9 in _resolvedEntered)
					{
						if (values != null && num < values.Length)
						{
							if (CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(values[num]) ?? false)
							{
								cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(item9, "tabstate", "entered");
							}
							else
							{
								cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(item9 + "." + item7.Id, "tabstate", "entered");
							}
							num++;
						}
						else
						{
							cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(item9 + "." + item7.Id, "tabstate", "entered");
						}
					}
				}
			}
			object[] values2 = cxsDataWatch.Values;
			if (values2 == null)
			{
				foreach (CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem item10 in _items)
				{
					item10.Complete = null;
					item10.Entered = null;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				int num2 = 0;
				if (_isSystemOrderArray)
				{
					if (_items != null)
					{
						foreach (CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem item11 in _items)
						{
							item11.Complete = null;
						}
					}
					foreach (string childId in _currentChildrenDisplay)
					{
						bool value = true;
						if (_resolvedComplete == null)
						{
							continue;
						}
						foreach (string item12 in _resolvedComplete)
						{
							_ = item12;
							bool? flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(values2[num2]);
							if (flag.HasValue && !flag.Value)
							{
								value = false;
							}
							num2++;
						}
						if (_items != null)
						{
							CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem = _items.Where((CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem p) => p.Display == childId).FirstOrDefault();
							if (cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem != null)
							{
								cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem.Complete = value;
							}
						}
						bool value2 = false;
						if (_resolvedEntered == null)
						{
							continue;
						}
						foreach (string item13 in _resolvedEntered)
						{
							_ = item13;
							bool? flag2 = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(values2[num2]);
							if (flag2.HasValue && flag2.Value)
							{
								value2 = true;
							}
							num2++;
						}
						if (_items != null)
						{
							CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem2 = _items.Where((CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem p) => p.Display == childId).FirstOrDefault();
							if (cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem2 != null)
							{
								cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem2.Entered = value2;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					foreach (CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem item14 in _items)
					{
						if (!(item14.Display != Resources.CreateNewMenuItem))
						{
							continue;
						}
						bool value3 = true;
						if (_resolvedComplete != null)
						{
							foreach (string item15 in _resolvedComplete)
							{
								_ = item15;
								bool? flag3 = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(values2[num2]);
								if (flag3.HasValue && !flag3.Value)
								{
									value3 = false;
								}
								num2++;
							}
							if (_currentChildren == null || !_currentChildren.Contains(item14.Id))
							{
								item14.Complete = null;
							}
							else
							{
								item14.Complete = value3;
							}
						}
						bool value4 = false;
						if (_resolvedEntered == null)
						{
							continue;
						}
						foreach (string item16 in _resolvedEntered)
						{
							_ = item16;
							bool? flag4 = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(values2[num2]);
							if (flag4.HasValue && flag4.Value)
							{
								value4 = true;
							}
							num2++;
						}
						if (_currentChildren == null || !_currentChildren.Contains(item14.Id))
						{
							item14.Entered = false;
						}
						else
						{
							item14.Entered = value4;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (!cxsDataWatch.IsEmpty)
			{
				_completeValuesWatch = cxsDataWatch;
				_completeValuesWatch.ValuesChanged += completeWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		private void ResolveCompletePaths(CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (_complete == null || _complete.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			_resolvedComplete = new Collection<string>();
			foreach (string item in _complete)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(item))
				{
					string dataPath = item;
					if (item[0] != '@')
					{
						dataPath = "@main.CC Nodes." + item;
					}
					string text = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(dataPath, dependencies);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						_resolvedComplete.Add(text);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void ResolveEnteredPaths(CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (_entered == null || _entered.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			_resolvedEntered = new Collection<string>();
			foreach (string item in _entered)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(item))
				{
					string dataPath = item;
					if (item[0] != '@')
					{
						dataPath = "@main.CC Nodes." + item;
					}
					string text = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(dataPath, dependencies);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						_resolvedEntered.Add(text);
					}
				}
			}
		}

			{
				_canEditCollection = false;
				_canInsertCollection = false;
			}
			else

				CxsActionStatusDetails cxsActionStatusDetails = CxsConverter.ValueToActionStatusDetails(array[1]);

		public void RefreshSelectedItem(object oldSelectedObject = null)
		{
			if (IsUnloaded)
			{
				return;
			}
			ObservableCollection<CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem> items = Items;
			string text = null;
			string selectedItemBoundValue = null;
			string selectedItemBinding = _property.SelectedItemBinding;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(selectedItemBinding))
			{
				if (_selectedItemPropertyWatch != null)
				{
					_selectedItemPropertyWatch.PropertyDependencyChanged -= selectedItemPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				}
				if (_selectedItemPropertyWatch == null)
				{
					_selectedItemPropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
				}
				_selectedItemPropertyWatch.Clear();
				selectedItemBoundValue = CxsConverter.ValueToString(_dataContainer.ResolveViewPropertyPath(selectedItemBinding, _selectedItemPropertyWatch));
				if (!_selectedItemPropertyWatch.IsEmpty)
				{
					_selectedItemPropertyWatch.PropertyDependencyChanged += selectedItemPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				}
				else
				{
					_selectedItemPropertyWatch = null;
				}
				selectedItemBoundValue = InvariantValueToLocalizedValue(selectedItemBoundValue);
				text = (_property.UseItemsCollectionResolved ? (items.Contains(new CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem(selectedItemBoundValue, null)) ? selectedItemBoundValue : null) : ((_isSystemOrderArray || (_currentChildren != null && _currentChildren.Contains(selectedItemBoundValue))) ? items.Where((CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem p) => p.Id == selectedItemBoundValue).FirstOrDefault()?.Display : null));
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				if (_property.UseItemsCollectionResolved || _property.Filter != null)
				{
					if (items != null && items.Count > 0)
					{
						text = items[0].Display;
					}
				}
				else if (_isSystemOrderArray)
				{
					if (_currentChildrenDisplay != null && _currentChildrenDisplay.Count > 0)
					{
						text = _currentChildrenDisplay[0];
					}
				}
				else if (_currentChildrenDisplay != null && _currentChildrenDisplay.Count > 0)
				{
					text = _currentChildrenDisplay[0];
				}
				else if (_currentChildren != null && _currentChildren.Count > 0)
				{
					text = _currentChildren[0];
				}
			}
			if (!CxvHelpers.ValuesEquivalent(_selectedItem, text))
			{
				_selectedItem = text;
				OnPropertyChanged("SelectedItem");
				OnPropertyChanged("SelectedObject");
			}
			else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem = oldSelectedObject as CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem;
				CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem2 = SelectedObject as CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem;
				if (cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem != null && cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem2 != null && (cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem.Entered != cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem2.Entered || cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem.Complete != cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem2.Complete))
				{
					OnPropertyChanged("SelectedItem");
					OnPropertyChanged("SelectedObject");
				}
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(selectedItemBinding))
			{
				return;
			}
			string text2 = null;
			text2 = ((_currentChildrenDisplay != null) ? items.Where((CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem p) => p.Display == _selectedItem).FirstOrDefault()?.Id : _selectedItem);
			if (!CxvHelpers.ValuesEquivalent(text2, selectedItemBoundValue))
			{
				string value = LocalizedValueToInvariantValue(text2);
				if (_selectedItemPropertyWatch != null)
				{
					_selectedItemPropertyWatch.PropertyDependencyChanged -= selectedItemPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				}
				_dataContainer.ResolveUpdateViewPropertyPath(selectedItemBinding, value);
				if (_selectedItemPropertyWatch != null && !_selectedItemPropertyWatch.IsEmpty)
				{
					_selectedItemPropertyWatch.PropertyDependencyChanged += selectedItemPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				}
			}
		}

		private string LocalizedValueToInvariantValue(string value)
		{
			if (!_property.UseItemsCollectionResolved)
			{
				return value;
			}
			foreach (CxvAdvCollectionItem item in _property.Items)
			{
				if (item.DisplayLocalized == value)
				{
					return item.Display;
				}
			}
			return value;
		}

		private string InvariantValueToLocalizedValue(string value)
		{
			if (!_property.UseItemsCollectionResolved)
			{
				return value;
			}
			foreach (CxvAdvCollectionItem item in _property.Items)
			{
				if (item.Display == value)
				{
					return item.DisplayLocalized;
				}
			}
			return value;
		}

		private void ChangeSelectedItem(string newSelectedItem)
		{
			if (_currentChildren == null && !_isSystemOrderArray)
			{
				RefreshItems();
			}
			if (!_property.UseItemsCollectionResolved && !_isSystemOrderArray && (_currentChildren == null || newSelectedItem == Resources.CreateNewMenuItem || (_currentChildrenDisplay != null && !_currentChildrenDisplay.Contains(newSelectedItem) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(newSelectedItem) && newSelectedItem[0] == '#' && !_currentChildren.Contains(newSelectedItem)) || (_currentChildrenDisplay == null && !_currentChildren.Contains(newSelectedItem))))
			{
				string text = newSelectedItem;
				if (text == Resources.CreateNewMenuItem)
				{
					if (_inNew)
					{
						_inNew = false;
						return;
					}
					_inNew = true;
					if ((_isArray ?? false) && !_isArrayOfValues)
					{
						text = null;
					}
					else
					{
						if (_itemsWatch != null && !_idWatchItemsAdded)
						{
							_itemsWatch.AddWatch(_resolvedDataPath, "default_name");
							_itemsWatch.AddWatch(_resolvedDataPath, "create_id_string");

								string.Empty,
								string.Empty,
								string.Empty,
								string.Empty,
								string.Empty,
								string.Empty
							};
						}

							string text3 = null;
							string text4 = _property.CreateNewPromptLocalized;
							if (text4 == null)
							{
								text2 = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, Resources.CreateNewNameTitle, Resources.CreateNewNamePrompt_Item);
								text3 = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, Resources.CreateNewNamePrompt, Resources.CreateNewNamePrompt_Item);
							}
							else
							{
								if (text4.EndsWith(":", StringComparison.CurrentCulture))
								{
									text4 = text4.TrimEnd(':');
								}
								text2 = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, Resources.CreateNewNameTitle, text4);

							}
