							GetUserInputEventArgs e = new GetUserInputEventArgs();
							e.Title = text2;
							e.Message = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[num]);
							if (e.Message == null || e.Message.Length == 0)
							{
								e.Message = text3;
							}
							int num2 = array.Length - 2;
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(DefaultNewName))
							{
								e.InputValue = DefaultNewName;
							}
							else
							{
								e.InputValue = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[num2]);
							}
							e.InputMask = _textmask;
							if (_property.CreateNewMaxLength.HasValue)
							{
								e.MaxTextLength = _property.CreateNewMaxLength.Value;
							}
							_dataContainer.GetUserInput(e);
							if (!e.DialogResult.HasValue || !e.DialogResult.Value)
							{
								RefreshItems();
								return;
							}
							text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(e.InputValue);
							if (_currentChildrenDisplay != null && _currentChildrenDisplay.Contains(text))
							{
								_dataContainer.ShowMessageBox(new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
								{
									Title = Resources.AspenPlus,
									Message = Resources.CreateNewIDExsists,
									MsgBoxButton = MessageBoxButton.OK,
									MsgBoxImage = MessageBoxImage.Exclamation
								});
								RefreshItems();
								return;
							}
							if ((_childIdOptions != null && _childIdOptions.Contains(text)) || (_currentChildren != null && _currentChildren.Contains(text)))
							{
								_dataContainer.ShowMessageBox(new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
								{
									Title = Resources.AspenPlus,
									Message = Resources.CreateNewIDExsists,
									MsgBoxButton = MessageBoxButton.OK,
									MsgBoxImage = MessageBoxImage.Exclamation
								});
							}
							else
							{
								flag = false;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				CxsDataProvider resolvedCxsProvider = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider;
				string newChildId = null;
				CxvActionResult cxvActionResult = null;
				try
				{
					if (_isArrayOfValues)
					{
						cxvActionResult = resolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(_resolvedDataPath, "InsertChild", true, -1);
						resolvedCxsProvider.UpdateDataCache();
						if (cxvActionResult.Succeeded && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && _currentChildren != null)
						{
							bool num3 = resolvedCxsProvider.BatchSet(_resolvedDataPath + "." + _currentChildren.Last(), "value", null, text);
							resolvedCxsProvider.UpdateDataCache();
							if (!num3)
							{
								resolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(_resolvedDataPath, "DeleteChild", true, _currentChildren.Count - 1);
								return;
							}
							newChildId = _currentChildren.Last();
						}
					}
					else
					{
						cxvActionResult = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text)) ? resolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(_resolvedDataPath, "InsertChild", true, -1, text) : resolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(_resolvedDataPath, "InsertChild", true, -1));
						resolvedCxsProvider.UpdateDataCache();
						if (cxvActionResult.Succeeded && _currentChildren != null)
						{
							newChildId = _currentChildren.Last();
						}
					}
				}
				catch (CxsException ex)
				{
					_dataContainer.ShowMessageBox(new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
					{
						Message = ex.Message
					});
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(newChildId))
				{
					if (cxvActionResult != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(cxvActionResult.Error))
					{
						_dataContainer.ShowMessageBox(new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
						{
							Title = Resources.AspenPlus,
							Message = cxvActionResult.Error,
							MsgBoxButton = MessageBoxButton.OK,
							MsgBoxImage = MessageBoxImage.Exclamation
						});
						RefreshItems();
						return;
					}
					if (!(_isArray ?? false) || _isSystemOrderArray || _currentChildren == null)
					{
						return;
					}
					newChildId = _currentChildren.Last();
				}
				resolvedCxsProvider.UpdateDataCache(_resolvedDataPath);
				if (newSelectedItem != newChildId)
				{
					if (!_items.Contains(new CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem(newChildId, null)))
					{
						_dataContainer.ShowMessageBox(new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
						{
							Message = Resources.UnableToAccessNewObject
						});
						return;
					}
					CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem = _items.Where((CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem p) => p.Id == newChildId).FirstOrDefault();
					newSelectedItem = cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem.Display;
				}
			}
			if (_isSystemOrderArray)
			{
				if (_currentChildren == null || !_currentChildrenDisplay.Contains(newSelectedItem))
				{
					CxsDataProvider resolvedCxsProvider2 = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider;
					int num4 = ((_currentChildren != null) ? _currentChildren.Count : 0);
					if (resolvedCxsProvider2.InvokeAction(_resolvedDataPath, "InsertChild", false, -1).Succeeded)
					{
						try
						{
							resolvedCxsProvider2.CCAsError = true;
							if (!resolvedCxsProvider2.BatchSet(_resolvedDataPath + ".#" + num4.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture), "value", null, newSelectedItem))
							{
								resolvedCxsProvider2.InvokeAction(_resolvedDataPath, "DeleteChild", false, num4);
								newSelectedItem = _selectedItem;
							}
							resolvedCxsProvider2.CCAsError = false;
						}
						catch (CxsException)
						{
							newSelectedItem = _selectedItem;
							resolvedCxsProvider2.CCAsError = false;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else if (_indexOrdered && (_currentChildren == null || !_currentChildrenDisplay.Contains(newSelectedItem)))
			{
				CxsDataProvider resolvedCxsProvider3 = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider;
				if (_currentChildren != null)
				{
					_ = _currentChildren.Count;
				}
				CxvActionResult cxvActionResult2 = resolvedCxsProvider3.InvokeAction(_resolvedDataPath, "InsertChild", true, -1, newSelectedItem);
				if (cxvActionResult2.Succeeded)
				{
					if (_entered == null || _entered.Count == 0)
					{
						CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem2 = _items.Where((CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem p) => p.Id == newSelectedItem).FirstOrDefault();
						if (cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem2 != null)
						{
							cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem2.Entered = true;
						}
					}
					resolvedCxsProvider3.UpdateDataCache(_resolvedDataPath);
				}
				else
				{
					if (cxvActionResult2 != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(cxvActionResult2.Error))
					{
						_dataContainer.ShowMessageBox(new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
						{
							Title = Resources.AspenPlus,
							Message = cxvActionResult2.Error,
							MsgBoxButton = MessageBoxButton.OK,
							MsgBoxImage = MessageBoxImage.Exclamation
						});
					}
					newSelectedItem = _selectedItem;
				}
			}
			_selectedItem = newSelectedItem;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.SelectedItemBinding))
			{
				string text5 = null;
				text5 = ((_currentChildrenDisplay != null || _isSystemOrderArray) ? _items.Where((CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem p) => p.Display == _selectedItem).FirstOrDefault()?.Id : _selectedItem);
				if (_selectedItemPropertyWatch != null)
				{
					_selectedItemPropertyWatch.PropertyDependencyChanged -= selectedItemPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				}
				string value = LocalizedValueToInvariantValue(text5);
				_dataContainer.ResolveUpdateViewPropertyPath(_property.SelectedItemBinding, value);
				if (_selectedItemPropertyWatch != null && !_selectedItemPropertyWatch.IsEmpty)
				{
					_selectedItemPropertyWatch.PropertyDependencyChanged += selectedItemPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				}
			}
			OnPropertyChanged("SelectedItem");
			OnPropertyChanged("SelectedObject");
			if (this is CxvAdvCollectionTooltipData)
			{
				OnPropertyChanged("VariablePrompt");
			}
		}

		public void Unload()
		{
			if (_dataContainer != null)
			{
				if (_itemsDependencies != null)
				{
					_itemsDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= itemsDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
					_itemsDependencies.Clear();
					_itemsDependencies = null;
				}
				if (_property != null && _property.Filter != null)
				{
					_property.Filter.ResetValues();
					_property.Filter.UpdateItems -= Filter_UpdateItems;
				}
				if (_selectedItemPropertyWatch != null)
				{
					_selectedItemPropertyWatch.PropertyDependencyChanged -= selectedItemPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
					_selectedItemPropertyWatch.Clear();
					_selectedItemPropertyWatch = null;
				}
				if (_itemsWatch != null)
				{
					_itemsWatch.ValuesChanged -= itemsWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;
					_itemsWatch.Unload();
					_itemsWatch = null;
					_idWatchItemsAdded = false;
				}
				if (_itemsValueWatch != null)
				{
					_itemsValueWatch.ValuesChanged -= itemsWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;
					_itemsValueWatch.Unload();
					_itemsValueWatch = null;
				}
				if (_completeValuesWatch != null)
				{
					_completeValuesWatch.ValuesChanged -= completeWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;
					_completeValuesWatch.Unload();
					_completeValuesWatch = null;
				}
				_dataContainer = null;
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<CxvVerbDescriptor> EnumerateVerbs()
		{
			if (_verbs == null)
			{
				_verbs = new Collection<CxvVerbDescriptor>();
				_verbs.Add(new CxvVerbDescriptor
				{
					Verb = "Create",
					VerbDisplayName = Resources.CreateVerb,
					Enabled = _canInsertCollection
				});
				_verbs.Add(new CxvVerbDescriptor
				{
					Verb = "Rename",
					VerbDisplayName = Resources.RenameVerb,
					Enabled = (_selectedItem != null && _canRenameChild)
				});
				_verbs.Add(new CxvVerbDescriptor
				{
					Verb = "Delete",
					VerbDisplayName = Resources.DeleteVerb,
					Enabled = (_selectedItem != null && _canDeleteChild)
				});
				_verbs.Add(new CxvVerbDescriptor
				{
					Verb = "Clear",
					VerbDisplayName = Resources.ClearVerb,
					Enabled = (_selectedItem != null && _canClearChild)
				});
			}
			else
			{
				_verbs[0].Enabled = _canInsertCollection;
				_verbs[1].Enabled = _selectedItem != null && _canRenameChild;
				_verbs[2].Enabled = _selectedItem != null && _canDeleteChild;
				_verbs[3].Enabled = _selectedItem != null && _canClearChild;
			}
			return new ReadOnlyCollection<CxvVerbDescriptor>(_verbs);
		}

		public CxvVerbDescriptor FindVerb(string verb)
		{
			EnumerateVerbs();
			foreach (CxvVerbDescriptor verb2 in _verbs)
			{
				if (verb2.Verb == verb)
				{
					return verb2;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public object DoVerb(string verb, params object[] args)
		{
			string text = _selectedItem;
			if (args != null && args.Count() > 0)
			{
				text = ((args[0] == null) ? null : args[0].ToString());
			}
			switch (verb)
			{
			case "Create":
				SelectedItem = Resources.CreateNewMenuItem;
				break;
			case "Rename":
			{
				if (_itemsWatch != null && !_idWatchItemsAdded)
				{
					_itemsWatch.AddWatch(_resolvedDataPath, "default_name");
					_itemsWatch.AddWatch(_resolvedDataPath, "create_id_string");
					_idWatchItemsAdded = true;
				}
				object[] array = _itemsWatch.Values;
				if (array == null)
				{
					array = new object[9]
					{
						string.Empty,
						string.Empty,
						string.Empty,
						string.Empty,
						string.Empty,
						string.Empty,
						string.Empty,
						string.Empty,
						string.Empty
					};
				}
				string text2 = null;
				string text3 = _property.CreateNewPromptLocalized;
				if (text3 == null)
				{
					text2 = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, Resources.RenameItemPrompt, "item");
				}
				else
				{
					if (text3.EndsWith(":", StringComparison.CurrentCulture))
					{
						text3 = text3.TrimEnd(':');
					}
					text2 = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, Resources.RenameItemPrompt, text3);
				}
				GetUserInputEventArgs e2 = new GetUserInputEventArgs();
				e2.Title = Resources.RenameVerb;
				e2.Message = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[array.Length - 1]);
				if (e2.Message == null || e2.Message.Length == 0)
				{
					e2.Message = text2;
				}
				string value = ((args[0] is CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem) ? ((CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem)args[0]).Display : args[0].ToString());
				e2.InputValue = CxsConverter.ValueToString(value);
				e2.InputMask = _textmask;
				_dataContainer.GetUserInput(e2);
				if (!e2.DialogResult.HasValue || !e2.DialogResult.Value)
				{
					return null;
				}
				string text4 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(e2.InputValue);
				CxsDataProvider resolvedCxsProvider2 = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider;
				try
				{
					if (_isArrayOfValues)
					{
						int index = _currentChildrenDisplay.IndexOf(text);
						string text5 = _currentChildren[index];
						if (!resolvedCxsProvider2.BatchSet(_resolvedDataPath + "." + text5, "value", null, text4))
						{
							return null;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						CxvActionResult cxvActionResult2 = null;
						cxvActionResult2 = resolvedCxsProvider2.InvokeAction(_resolvedDataPath, "RenameChild", true, text, text4);
						if (cxvActionResult2 != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(cxvActionResult2.Error))
						{
							resolvedCxsProvider2.ShowMessageBox(new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
							{
								Title = Resources.AspenPlus,
								Message = cxvActionResult2.Error,
								MsgBoxButton = MessageBoxButton.OK,
								MsgBoxImage = MessageBoxImage.Exclamation
							});
							return null;
						}
					}
				}
				catch (CxvException)
				{
				}
				resolvedCxsProvider2.UpdateDataCache();
				if (SelectedItem == text4)
				{
					OnPropertyChanged("SelectedItem");
					OnPropertyChanged("SelectedObject");
				}
				else
				{
					SelectedItem = text4;
				}
				break;
			}
			case "Delete":
			case "Clear":
			{
				if (text == Resources.CreateNewMenuItem)
				{
					return null;
				}
				if (verb == "Delete")
				{
					ShowMessageBoxEventArgs e = new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
					{
						Title = Resources.DeleteConfirmTitle,
						Message = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, Resources.DeleteConfirmPrompt, text),
						MsgBoxButton = MessageBoxButton.YesNo,
						MsgBoxImage = MessageBoxImage.Exclamation
					};
					_dataContainer.ShowMessageBox(e);
					if (e.DialogResult != MessageBoxResult.Yes)
					{
						return null;
					}
				}
				CxvActionResult cxvActionResult = null;
				CxsDataProvider resolvedCxsProvider = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider;
				if (_isSystemOrderArray)
				{
					if (_currentChildrenDisplay != null && _currentChildrenDisplay.Contains(text))
					{
						try
						{
							cxvActionResult = resolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(_resolvedDataPath, "DeleteChild", true, _currentChildrenDisplay.IndexOf(text));
						}
						catch (CxvException)
						{
						}
					}
				}
				else if (_isArrayOfValues)
				{
					if (_currentChildrenDisplay != null && _currentChildrenDisplay.Contains(text))
					{
						int num = _currentChildrenDisplay.IndexOf(text);
						if (num >= 0)
						{
							try
							{
								cxvActionResult = resolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(_resolvedDataPath, "DeleteChild", true, num);
							}
							catch (CxvException)
							{
							}
						}
					}
				}
				else if (_currentChildrenDisplay != null && _currentChildrenDisplay.Contains(text))
				{
					int num2 = _currentChildrenDisplay.IndexOf(text);
					if (num2 >= 0)
					{
						try
						{
							cxvActionResult = resolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(_resolvedDataPath, "DeleteChild", true, num2);
						}
						catch (CxvException)
						{
						}
					}
				}
				if (cxvActionResult != null && !cxvActionResult.Succeeded)
				{
					ShowMessageBoxEventArgs args2 = new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
					{
						Title = Resources.AspenPlus,
						Message = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, Resources.DeleteError, cxvActionResult.Error),
						MsgBoxButton = MessageBoxButton.OK,
						MsgBoxImage = MessageBoxImage.Exclamation
					};
					_dataContainer.ShowMessageBox(args2);
				}
				resolvedCxsProvider.UpdateDataCache(_resolvedDataPath);
				_selectedItem = null;
				string selectedItemBinding = _property.SelectedItemBinding;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(selectedItemBinding))
				{
					if (_selectedItemPropertyWatch != null)
					{
						_selectedItemPropertyWatch.PropertyDependencyChanged -= selectedItemPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
					}
					_dataContainer.ResolveUpdateViewPropertyPath(selectedItemBinding, null);
					if (_selectedItemPropertyWatch != null && !_selectedItemPropertyWatch.IsEmpty)
					{
						_selectedItemPropertyWatch.PropertyDependencyChanged += selectedItemPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
					}
				}
				RefreshItems();
				OnPropertyChanged("SelectedItem");
				OnPropertyChanged("SelectedObject");
				break;
			}
			}
			return null;
		}

		XElement IViewDataSerializable.WriteXElement()
		{
			List<XElement> list = new List<XElement>();
			string selectedItem = SelectedItem;
			bool? isReadOnly = IsReadOnly;
			bool? isCalculated = IsCalculated;
			bool isComplete = IsComplete;
			bool? isEntered = IsEntered;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(selectedItem))
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("SelectedItem", selectedItem));
			}
			ObservableCollection<CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem> items = Items;
			if (items != null)
			{
				List<object> list2 = new List<object>();
				foreach (CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem item in items)
				{
					list2.Add(CxvInternalHelpers.WriteDataToXElement(item));
				}
				list.Add(new XElement("Items", list2, new XAttribute("Count", items.Count)));
			}
			if (isReadOnly.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("IsReadOnly", isReadOnly.Value));
			}
			if (isEntered.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("IsEntered", isEntered.Value));
			}
			if (isCalculated.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("IsCalculated", isCalculated.Value));
			}
			list.Add(new XElement("IsComplete", isComplete));
			object selectedObject = SelectedObject;
			if (selectedObject != null)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("SelectedObject", selectedObject.ToString()));
			}
			return new XElement(GetType().Name, list);
		}

		public int ShowNextIncomplete()
		{
			if (_selectedItem != null)
			{
				CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem = Items.Where((CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem p) => p.Display == _selectedItem).FirstOrDefault();
				if (cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem != null && cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem.Complete.HasValue && !cxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem.Complete.Value)
				{
					return 1;
				}
			}
			foreach (CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem item in Items)
			{
				if (item != null && item.Complete.HasValue && !item.Complete.Value)
				{
					SelectedItem = item.Display;
					OnPropertyChanged("SelectedItem");
					OnPropertyChanged("SelectedObject");
					return 0;
				}
			}
			return -1;
		}
	}
	public class CxvAdvCollectionTooltipData : CxvAdvCollectionPropertyData
	{
		private ICxsDataWatch _tooltipWatch;

		private EventHandler _tooltipWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;

		public override Dictionary<string, string> OptionPrompts
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.Property is CxvAdvCollectionTooltipProperty cxvAdvCollectionTooltipProperty && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(cxvAdvCollectionTooltipProperty.LongNamePath))
				{
					Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
					{
						foreach (CxvAdvCollectionPropertyDataItem item in base.Items)
						{
							string id = item.Id;
							string streamPath = CxvCustomTooltipHelper.ResolveDataPath(cxvAdvCollectionTooltipProperty.LongNamePath, id);
							string longNameForStream = GetLongNameForStream(streamPath);
							dictionary.Add(id, longNameForStream);
						}
						return dictionary;
					}
				}
				return base.OptionPrompts;
			}
		}

		public override string VariablePrompt
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.Property is CxvAdvCollectionTooltipProperty cxvAdvCollectionTooltipProperty && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(cxvAdvCollectionTooltipProperty.LongNamePath))
				{
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.SelectedItem))
					{
						return null;
					}
					string text = CxvCustomTooltipHelper.ResolveDataPath(cxvAdvCollectionTooltipProperty.LongNamePath, base.SelectedItem);
					string text2 = text.Replace("Top.appModelV8.", string.Empty).Replace("Blocks.", "Block ").Replace("Streams.", "Stream ")
						.Replace(".Data.", " (Hierarchy) ")
						.Replace(".", " ");
					string path = CxvCustomTooltipHelper.ResolveDescriptionPath(text);
					string text3 = null;
					string text4 = null;
					string text5 = null;
					try
					{
						ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = base.DataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
						cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(path, "value");
						cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "type");
						cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "status");
						UpdateTooltipWatch(cxsDataWatch);
						object[] values = cxsDataWatch.Values;
						text4 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(values[0]);
						text3 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(values[1]);
						text5 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(values[2]);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
					return text2 + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3) ? string.Empty : (" (" + text3 + ")")) + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4) ? string.Empty : ("\n" + text4)) + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text5) ? string.Empty : ("\n" + text5));
				}
				return base.VariablePrompt;
			}
		}

		public CxvAdvCollectionTooltipData(CxvAdvCollectionProperty property, ICxvViewDataContainer dataProvider, string dataPath, int newItemMask, IList<string> complete, IList<string> entered)
			: base(property, dataProvider, dataPath, newItemMask, complete, entered)
		{
		}

		private string GetLongNameForStream(string streamPath)
		{
			string text = streamPath.Replace("Top.appModelV8.", string.Empty).Replace("Blocks.", "Block ").Replace("Streams.", "Stream ")
				.Replace(".Data.", " (Hierarchy) ")
				.Replace(".", " ");
			string path = CxvCustomTooltipHelper.ResolveDescriptionPath(streamPath);
			string text2 = null;
			string text3 = null;
			string text4 = null;
			try
			{
				text3 = base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(path, "value", string.Empty) as string;
				text2 = base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(streamPath, "type", string.Empty) as string;
				text4 = base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(streamPath, "status", string.Empty) as string;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			return text + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2) ? string.Empty : (" (" + text2 + ")")) + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3) ? string.Empty : ("\n" + text3)) + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4) ? string.Empty : ("\n" + text4));
		}

		private void UpdateTooltipWatch(ICxsDataWatch tooltipWatch)
		{
			if (tooltipWatch == _tooltipWatch)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_tooltipWatch != null && _tooltipWatch_ValuesChangedHandler != null)
			{
				_tooltipWatch.ValuesChanged -= _tooltipWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;
				_tooltipWatch.Unload();
				_tooltipWatch = null;
			}
			if (tooltipWatch == null || tooltipWatch.IsEmpty)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_tooltipWatch_ValuesChangedHandler == null)
			{
				_tooltipWatch_ValuesChangedHandler = delegate
				{
					OnPropertyChanged("OptionPrompts");
					OnPropertyChanged("VariablePrompt");
				};
			}
			_tooltipWatch = tooltipWatch;
			_tooltipWatch.ValuesChanged += _tooltipWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;
		}
	}
	public class CxvBooleanPropertyData : CxvStatePropertyData, IViewDataSerializable, ICxvPropertyData, IQuickValueReadable
	{
		private readonly string _parameter;

		private readonly string _dataPath;

		private readonly CxvBooleanProperty _property;

		private bool? _value;

		private bool? _isProxy;

		private bool _settingValue;

		private bool? _defaultCxsValue;

		private string _dataPathResolved;

		private bool _unresolvedPath;

		private CxvPropertyWatch _dataPathDependencies;

		private EventHandler _pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;

		private ICxsDataWatch _watch;

		private EventHandler _dataWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;

		private IHappDataAccessor _happDataAccessor;

		public bool? Value
		{
			get
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_parameter))
				{
					return GetBooleanValue(base.DataContainer.ResolveParameter(_parameter));
				}
				EnsureData();
				return _value;
			}
			set
			{
				if (Value == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_parameter))
				{
					base.DataContainer.ResolveUpdateParameter(_parameter, GetCxsValue(value, defaultResetsValue: false));
					return;
				}
				_settingValue = true;
				if (base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchSet(EnsureDataPath(), "value", string.Empty, GetCxsValue(value, defaultResetsValue: true)))
				{
					_value = value;
				}
				_settingValue = false;
				if (!_value.HasValue)
				{
					UpdateWatch(null);
					EnsureData();
				}
				OnPropertyChangedAccordingly("Value", _value == value);
			}
		}

		public bool? IsEntered
		{
			get
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_parameter))
				{
					return true;
				}
				return CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(GetDataAttributeValue("IsEntered", _dataPath, "user_entered"));
			}
		}

		public bool? IsCalculated
		{
			get
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_parameter))
				{
					return true;
				}
				return CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(GetDataAttributeValue("IsCalculated", _dataPath, "calculated"));
			}
		}

		public bool? CanEnter
		{
			get
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_parameter))
				{
					return true;
				}
				return CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(GetDataAttributeValue("CanEnter", _dataPath, "can_enter"));
			}
		}

		public bool? CanClear => _property.CanClear;

		public bool? IsReadOnly
		{
			get
			{
				bool? flag = CanEnter.HasValue && !CanEnter.Value;
				if (!(flag ?? false) && (_property.ReadOnlyIfProxy ?? false) && (_isProxy ?? false))
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				return flag;
			}
		}

		public string CxsItemMoniker => CxvObservableObject.CxsPathToMoniker(_dataPath);

		public override string VariablePrompt => CxvInternalHelpers.ValueToStringRemoveXml(GetDataAttributeValue("VariablePrompt", _dataPath, "description"));

		public bool IsUnloaded { get; set; }

		object IQuickValueReadable.QuickValue
		{
			get
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_parameter))
				{
					return GetBooleanValue(base.DataContainer.ResolveParameter(_parameter));
				}
				string text = base.DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.DataPath);
				if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
				{
					return GetBooleanValue(null);
				}
				IHappDataNode happDataNode = FindHappDataNode(text);
				if (happDataNode != null)
				{
					return GetBooleanValue(happDataNode.Value);
				}
				CxsCase cxsCase = base.DataContainer?.ResolvedCxsProvider?.Case;
				if (cxsCase != null)
				{
					cxsCase.BatchGet(text, "value");
					return GetBooleanValue(happDataNode.Value);
				}
				return Value;
			}
		}

		private IHappDataAccessor ResolvedHappDataAccessor => _happDataAccessor ?? (_happDataAccessor = base.DataContainer?.UnityContainer?.TryResolve<IHappDataAccessor>());

		private void EnsureData()
		{
			if (_value.HasValue || _watch != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = EnsureDataPath();
			if (text == null)
			{
				_value = GetBooleanValue(null);
				return;
			}
			ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = base.DataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
			if (cxsDataWatch != null)
			{
				cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "value", null);
				cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "is_proxy", null);
				cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "default", null);
				object[] values = cxsDataWatch.Values;
				_value = GetBooleanValue(values[0]);
				_isProxy = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(values[1]);
				_defaultCxsValue = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(values[2]);
				UpdateWatch(cxsDataWatch);
			}
		}

		public void RefreshData()
		{
			if (!_settingValue)
			{
				UpdateWatch(null);
				_value = null;
				OnPropertyChanged("Value");
			}
		}

		private string EnsureDataPath()
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dataPathResolved) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.DataPath))
			{
				ResolveDataPath();
			}
			return _dataPathResolved;
		}

		private void ResolveDataPath()
		{
			if (!IsUnloaded && (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dataPathResolved) || !_unresolvedPath))
			{
				UpdateDataPathDependencies(null);
				CxvPropertyWatch cxvPropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.DataPath))
				{
					_dataPathResolved = base.DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.DataPath, cxvPropertyWatch);
				}
				UpdateDataPathDependencies(cxvPropertyWatch);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dataPathResolved))
				{
					_unresolvedPath = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateDataPathDependencies(CxvPropertyWatch dataPathDependencies)
		{
			if (dataPathDependencies == _dataPathDependencies)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_dataPathDependencies != null)
			{
				if (_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler != null)
				{
					_dataPathDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= _pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				}
				_dataPathDependencies.Clear();
				_dataPathDependencies = null;
			}
			if (dataPathDependencies == null || dataPathDependencies.IsEmpty)
			{
				return;
			}
			_dataPathDependencies = dataPathDependencies;
			if (_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler == null)
			{
				_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler = delegate
				{
					ResetDataPath();
				};
			}
			_dataPathDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += _pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
		}

		private void ResetDataPath()
		{
			UpdateDataPathDependencies(null);
			_dataPathResolved = null;
			_unresolvedPath = false;
			_value = null;
			OnPropertyChanged("Value");
			UpdateDataAttributeValue("VariablePrompt");
			OnPropertyChanged("CxsItemMoniker");
		}

		private void UpdateWatch(ICxsDataWatch watch)
		{
			if (watch == _watch)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_watch != null && _dataWatch_ValuesChangedHandler != null)
			{
				_watch.ValuesChanged -= _dataWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;
				_watch.Unload();
				_watch = null;
			}
			if (watch == null || watch.IsEmpty)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_dataWatch_ValuesChangedHandler == null)
			{
				_dataWatch_ValuesChangedHandler = delegate
				{
					RefreshData();
				};
			}
			_watch = watch;
			_watch.ValuesChanged += _dataWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;
		}

		protected override void OnPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			base.OnPropertyChanged(propertyName);
			if (propertyName == "CanEnter")
			{
				OnPropertyChanged("IsReadOnly");
			}
		}

		public CxvBooleanPropertyData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvBooleanProperty property, string dataPath, string parameter)
			: base(dataContainer, property)
		{
			_property = property;
			_dataPath = dataPath;
			_parameter = parameter;
		}

		private bool? GetBooleanValue(object cxsValue)
		{
			if (_property.TrueValue == null && _property.FalseValue == null)
			{
				return CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(cxsValue);
			}
			if (CxvHelpers.ValuesEquivalent(cxsValue, _property.TrueValue))
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (CxvHelpers.ValuesEquivalent(cxsValue, _property.FalseValue))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (cxsValue == null || cxsValue.GetType() == typeof(DBNull))
			{
				return _property.DefaultValue;
			}
			return null;
		}

		private object GetCxsValue(bool? booleanValue, bool defaultResetsValue)
		{
			if (!booleanValue.HasValue)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (defaultResetsValue && _defaultCxsValue.HasValue && booleanValue.Value == _defaultCxsValue.Value)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (_property.TrueValue == null && _property.FalseValue == null)
			{
				return booleanValue.Value;
			}
			if (booleanValue.Value)
			{
				return _property.TrueValue;
			}
			return _property.FalseValue;
		}

		XElement IViewDataSerializable.WriteXElement()
		{
			List<XElement> list = new List<XElement>();
			bool? value = Value;
			if (value.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("Value", value.Value));
			}
			bool? isReadOnly = IsReadOnly;
			bool? isCalculated = IsCalculated;
			bool? isEntered = IsEntered;
			bool? canEnter = CanEnter;
			if (isReadOnly.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("IsReadOnly", isReadOnly.Value));
			}
			if (isEntered.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("IsEntered", isEntered.Value));
			}
			if (isCalculated.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("IsCalculated", isCalculated.Value));
			}
			if (canEnter.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("CanEnter", canEnter.Value));
			}
			return new XElement(GetType().Name, list);
		}

		public void Unload()
		{
			UpdateWatch(null);
			UpdateDataPathDependencies(null);
		}

		object IQuickValueReadable.GetQuickValue(out bool? canEnter, out bool? isDefaulted)
		{
			canEnter = (isDefaulted = null);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_parameter))
			{
				return GetBooleanValue(base.DataContainer.ResolveParameter(_parameter));
			}
			string text = base.DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.DataPath);
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
			{
				return GetBooleanValue(null);
			}
			IHappDataNode happDataNode = FindHappDataNode(text);
			if (happDataNode != null)
			{
				canEnter = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(happDataNode.GetAttributeValue(HappDataNodeAttribute.Enterable));
				isDefaulted = false;
				return GetBooleanValue(happDataNode.Value);
			}
			CxsCase cxsCase = base.DataContainer?.ResolvedCxsProvider?.Case;
			if (cxsCase != null)
			{
				cxsCase.BatchGet(text, "value");
				return GetBooleanValue(happDataNode.Value);
			}
			return Value;
		}

		private IHappDataNode FindHappDataNode(string dataPath)
		{
			return ResolvedHappDataAccessor?.GetHappNode(dataPath);
		}
	}
	public class CxvChildCountIndexBasisData : CxvIndexBasisBaseData
	{
		private bool? _isNotJagged;

		private EventHandler _childIdDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;

		private CxvPropertyWatch _childIdDependencies;

		private string[] _childIdsVector;

		private string[] _childValuesVector;

		private ICxvViewDataContainer _dataContainer;

		private CxvChildCountIndexBasis _indexBasis;

		private bool _hasProxyRow;

		private bool _canDelete;

		private bool? _canReorder = false;

		public override int Count
		{
			get
			{
				if (_childIdsVector == null)
				{
					RefreshChildIds();
				}
				if (_childIdsVector == null)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				return _childIdsVector.Length;
			}
		}

		public override bool? IsNotJagged
		{
			get
			{
				if (_childIdsVector == null)
				{
					RefreshChildIds();
				}
				if (!_isNotJagged.HasValue && _indexBasis.IsNotJagged.HasValue)
				{
					return _indexBasis.IsNotJagged;
				}
				return _isNotJagged;
			}
		}

		private EventHandler childIdDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (_childIdDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler == null)
				{
					_childIdDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler = delegate
					{
						ResetChildIds();
					};
				}
				return _childIdDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		public string[] ChildIdsVector => _childIdsVector;

		public string[] ChildValuesVector => _childIdsVector;

		protected ICxvViewDataContainer DataContainer
		{
			get
			{
				return _dataContainer;
			}
			set
			{
				_dataContainer = value;
			}
		}

		public CxvChildCountIndexBasis IndexBasis
		{
			get
			{
				return _indexBasis;
			}
			set
			{
				_indexBasis = value;
			}
		}

		public bool HasProxyRow
		{
			get
			{
				return _hasProxyRow;
			}
			protected set
			{
				_hasProxyRow = value;
			}
		}

		public override bool AllowRemove => _canDelete;

		public override bool AllowNew => _hasProxyRow;

		public override bool AllowReorder => _canReorder ?? false;

		public CxvChildCountIndexBasisData(CxvChildCountIndexBasis indexBasis, ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer)
		{
			_indexBasis = indexBasis;
			_dataContainer = dataContainer;
		}

		public override object GetIndexSubstitution(int index, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (_childIdsVector == null)
			{
				RefreshChildIds();
			}
			dependencies?.AddWatch(this, "Count");
			object obj = null;
			if (_indexBasis.UseChildValue && _childIdsVector.Length != 0)
			{
				return _childValuesVector[index];
			}
			if (_indexBasis.UseChildId && _childIdsVector != null && index >= 0 && index < _childIdsVector.Length)
			{
				return _childIdsVector[index];
			}
			return "#" + index.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
		}

		private void ResetChildIds()
		{
			_childIdsVector = null;
			UpdateChildIdDependencies(null);
			OnCountChanged();
		}

		protected virtual void RefreshChildIds()
		{
			string childIds = string.Empty;
			CxvPropertyWatch cxvPropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
			string text = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_indexBasis.DataPath, cxvPropertyWatch);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = _dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
				if (cxsDataWatch != null)
				{
					cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "child_ids");
					cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "is_not_jagged");
					cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "action_status", "DeleteChild");
					cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "action_status", "ReorderChildren");
					if (_indexBasis.ProxyRow.HasValue)
					{
						_hasProxyRow = _indexBasis.ProxyRow.Value;
					}
					else
					{
						cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "action_status", "InsertChild");
					}
					cxvPropertyWatch.AddWatch(cxsDataWatch);
					object[] values = cxsDataWatch.Values;
					childIds = ((values != null) ? CxsConverter.ValueToString(values[0]) : string.Empty);
					if (values != null && values[1] != null)
					{
						_isNotJagged = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(values[1]);
					}
					else
					{
						_isNotJagged = null;
					}
					if (values != null && values[2] != null)
					{
						_canDelete = CxsConverter.ValueToActionStatusDetails(values[2])?.CanInvoke ?? false;
					}
					if (values != null && values[3] != null)
					{
						_canReorder = CxsConverter.ValueToActionStatusDetails(values[3])?.CanInvoke ?? false;
					}
					if (!_indexBasis.ProxyRow.HasValue && values != null && values[4] != null)
					{
						_hasProxyRow = CxsConverter.ValueToActionStatusDetails(values[4])?.CanInvoke ?? false;
					}
				}
			}
			SetChildIds(childIds, cxvPropertyWatch);
		}

		protected virtual void SetChildIds(string childIds, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			_childIdsVector = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(childIds) ? new string[0] : childIds.Split('\n'));
			if (_hasProxyRow)
			{
				int num = _childIdsVector.Length;
				Array.Resize(ref _childIdsVector, num + 1);
				_childIdsVector[num] = "#" + num.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < _childIdsVector.Length; i++)
			{
				if (_childIdsVector[i].Contains('.'))
				{
					_childIdsVector[i] = "\"" + _childIdsVector[i] + "\"";
				}
			}
			if (_indexBasis.MinimumRows.HasValue)
			{
				for (int j = _childIdsVector.Length; j < _indexBasis.MinimumRows; j++)
				{
					Array.Resize(ref _childIdsVector, j + 1);
					_childIdsVector[j] = "#" + j.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				}
			}
			if (IndexBasis.MaximumRows.HasValue && _childIdsVector.Length > IndexBasis.MaximumRows.Value)
			{
				Array.Resize(ref _childIdsVector, IndexBasis.MaximumRows.Value);
			}
			if (_indexBasis.UseChildValue)
			{
				string text = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_indexBasis.DataPath, dependencies);
				ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = _dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
				if (cxsDataWatch != null)
				{
					int num2 = _childIdsVector.Length;
					if (_hasProxyRow)
					{
						num2--;
					}
					for (int k = 0; k < num2; k++)
					{
						cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text + "." + _childIdsVector[k], "value");
					}
					dependencies.AddWatch(cxsDataWatch);
					object[] values = cxsDataWatch.Values;
					if (values != null)
					{
						_childValuesVector = new string[_childIdsVector.Length];
						for (int l = 0; l < values.Length; l++)
						{
							_childValuesVector[l] = CxsConverter.ValueToString(values[l]);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			UpdateChildIdDependencies(dependencies);
		}

		protected void UpdateChildIdDependencies(CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (_childIdDependencies != null)
			{
				_childIdDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= childIdDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				_childIdDependencies = null;
			}
			if (dependencies != null && !dependencies.IsEmpty)
			{
				_childIdDependencies = dependencies;
				_childIdDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += childIdDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		public override void Unload()
		{
			base.Unload();
			if (_dataContainer != null)
			{
				UpdateChildIdDependencies(null);
				_dataContainer = null;
			}
		}

		public override bool AllowRemoveAt(int index)
		{
			if (!_canDelete || _dataContainer == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (index >= Count || index < 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (_hasProxyRow && index == Count - 1)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public override void RemoveAt(int index)
		{
			CxvActionResult cxvActionResult = null;
			if (_dataContainer != null)
			{
				if (index >= Count || index < 0)
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException();
				}
				if (_hasProxyRow && index == Count - 1)
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException("This row is used for adding new record, it can't be deleted.");
				}
				try
				{
					string text = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_indexBasis.DataPath);
					if (_indexBasis.LinkedPaths != null)
					{
						foreach (string linkedPath in _indexBasis.LinkedPaths)
						{
							string text2 = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(linkedPath);
							if (!(text2 == text) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
							{
								cxvActionResult = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(text2, "DeleteChild", false, index);
							}
						}
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						cxvActionResult = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(text, "DeleteChild", true, index);
					}
				}
				catch (CxsException)
				{
				}
			}
			if (cxvActionResult == null)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Unable to delete row");
			}
			if (!cxvActionResult.Succeeded)
			{
				throw new CxsException(cxvActionResult.Error);
			}
		}

		public override void RemoveAt(List<int> indices)
		{
			if (indices == null || indices.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			object[] array = new object[indices.Count];
			int num = 0;
			CxvActionResult cxvActionResult = null;
			bool flag = false;
			if (_dataContainer != null)
			{
				foreach (int index in indices)
				{
					if (index >= Count || index < 0)
					{
						throw new InvalidOperationException();
					}
					if (_hasProxyRow && index == Count - 1)
					{
						if (indices.Count == 1)
						{
							throw new InvalidOperationException("This row is used for adding new record, it can't be deleted.");
						}
						flag = true;
					}
					else
					{
						array[num] = index;
						num++;
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					Array.Resize(ref array, indices.Count - 1);
				}
				try
				{
					if (_indexBasis.LinkedPaths != null)
					{
						foreach (string linkedPath in _indexBasis.LinkedPaths)
						{
							string text = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(linkedPath);
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
							{
								cxvActionResult = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(text, "DeleteChild", refresh: false, array);
							}
						}
					}
					string text2 = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_indexBasis.DataPath);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
					{
						cxvActionResult = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(text2, "DeleteChild", refresh: true, array);
					}
				}
				catch (CxsException)
				{
				}
			}
			if (cxvActionResult == null)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Unable to delete row");
			}
			if (cxvActionResult.Succeeded)
			{
				return;
			}
			throw new CxsException(cxvActionResult.Error);
		}

		public override void Insert(int index, object value)
		{
			CxvActionResult cxvActionResult = null;
			if (_dataContainer != null)
			{
				try
				{
					if (_indexBasis.LinkedPaths != null)
					{
						foreach (string linkedPath in _indexBasis.LinkedPaths)
						{
							string text = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(linkedPath);
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
							{
								cxvActionResult = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(text, "InsertChild", false, index);
							}
						}
					}
					string text2 = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_indexBasis.DataPath);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
					{
						cxvActionResult = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(text2, "InsertChild", true, index);
					}
				}
				catch (CxsException)
				{
				}
			}
			if (cxvActionResult == null)
			{
				throw new CxsException("Unable to insert row");
			}
			if (!cxvActionResult.Succeeded)
			{
				throw new CxsException(cxvActionResult.Error);
			}
		}
	}

	public class CxvComponentSubstrmPropertyData : CxvObservableObject, ICxvPropertyData, IViewDataSerializable

		public string SelectedItem
		{
			get
			{
				if (_selectedItem == null)
				{
					RefreshSelectedItem();
				}
				return _selectedItem;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(_selectedItem != value))
				{
					return;
				}
				if (value == null)
				{
					if (Clear(_selectedItem))
					{
						_selectedItem = value;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					_selectedItem = value;
					RefreshSelectedItem();
				}
				if (!CxvHelpers.ValuesEquivalent(_selectedItem, value))
				{
					Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action<string>(OnPropertyChanged), "SelectedItem");
				}
			}
		}

		public string VariablePrompt => Resources.ComponentSubstreamPrompt;

		public ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string> Items
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureData();
				if ((_property.Sorted ?? false) && _options != null)
				{
					return new ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string>(new ObservableCollection<string>(_options.OrderBy((string i) => i)));
				}
				return _options;
			}
		}

		public bool? IsCalculated => false;

		public bool? IsEntered => true;

		public bool? IsReadOnly => false;

		private bool SetSubstreamChildId(string sCompId)

				text = sCompId.Substring(0, _selectedItem.LastIndexOf('(')).TrimEnd();
				text2 = sCompId.Substring(_selectedItem.LastIndexOf('(') + 1);
				text2 = text2.TrimEnd(')');

					value2 = text2;

		private void RefreshSelectedItem()
		{
			if (IsUnloaded)

			}

			{
				cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(text, "display_name");
			}
			else
			{
				cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(text, "value");
			}
			CxsDataProvider resolvedCxsProvider2 = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider;
			CxsCase cxsCase = resolvedCxsProvider2?.Case;
			if (cxsCase == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			object[] array2 = resolvedCxsProvider2.BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData);
			string text2 = null;
			if (array2 != null)
			{
				text2 = ((array2 != null) ? CxsConverter.ValueToString(array2[0]) : string.Empty);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2) && !Items.Contains(text2))
				{
					object obj = null;
					try
					{
						obj = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dataSubstreamPathResolved)) ? resolvedCxsProvider2.BatchGet(_dataSubstreamPathResolved, "value", null) : resolvedCxsProvider2.BatchGet(_dataStoicPathResolved + ".#0", "display_name", null));
					}
					catch (CxsException)
					{
					}
					if (obj != null && obj.GetType() != typeof(DBNull))
					{
						text2 = text2 + " (" + obj.ToString() + ")";
					}
				}
			}
			if (_selectedItem == null)
			{
				_selectedItem = text2;
				return;
			}
			cxsBatchRequestData.Clear();
			string text3 = null;
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = 0;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dataSubstreamPathResolved))
			{
				int num3 = text.LastIndexOf(".");
				if (num3 != -1)
				{
					text3 = text.Substring(0, num3);
					array2 = null;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3))
					{
						cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(text3, "child_count");
						cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(text3, "child_ids");
						array2 = resolvedCxsProvider2.BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData);
						if (array2 != null)
						{
							int? num4 = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(array2[0]);
							if (num4.HasValue)
							{
								num = num4.Value;
							}
						}
					}
					string text4 = text.Substring(num3 + 1);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
					{
						if (text4[0] == '#')
						{
							num2 = Convert.ToInt32(text.Substring(num3 + 2), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
						}
						else
						{
							ReadOnlyCollection<string> readOnlyCollection = CxsConverter.ValueToOptionsCollection(array2[1]);
							int num5 = 0;
							foreach (string item in readOnlyCollection)
							{
								if (item == text4)
								{
									num2 = num5;
									break;
								}
								num5++;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if (num2 + 1 > num)
				{
					try
					{
						string text5 = null;
						text5 = ((array != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_selectedItem) && array.Contains(_selectedItem)) ? _selectedItem : ((!_selectedItem.Contains('(')) ? _selectedItem : _selectedItem.Substring(0, _selectedItem.LastIndexOf('(')).TrimEnd()));
						CxvActionResult cxvActionResult = resolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(text3, "InsertChild", false, -1, text5);
						if (!cxvActionResult.Succeeded)
						{
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cxvActionResult.Error))
							{
								_dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.ShowMessageBox(new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs

					}
					catch (CxsException ex2)
					{
						_dataContainer.ShowMessageBox(new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
						{
							Message = ex2.Message
						});
					}
					if (!SetSubstreamChildId(_selectedItem))
					{
						_selectedItem = text2;
						resolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(text3, "DeleteChild", true, num2);
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			if (CxvHelpers.ValuesEquivalent(_selectedItem, text2) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(selectedItemBinding))
			{
				return;
			}
			string text6 = null;
			text6 = ((array != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_selectedItem) && array.Contains(_selectedItem)) ? _selectedItem : ((!_selectedItem.Contains('(')) ? _selectedItem : _selectedItem.Substring(0, _selectedItem.LastIndexOf('(')).TrimEnd()));
			try
			{
				CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData2 = new CxsBatchRequestData();
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dataSubstreamPathResolved))
				{
					cxsBatchRequestData2.AddRequest(text, "display_name", null, text6);
					UpdateStoicPath(_selectedItem, text2);
				}
				else
				{
					cxsBatchRequestData2.AddRequest(text, "value", null, text6);
				}
				if (resolvedCxsProvider2.BatchSet(cxsBatchRequestData2))
				{
					resolvedCxsProvider.UpdateDataCache(_dataStoicPathResolved);
					if (!SetSubstreamChildId(_selectedItem))
					{
						_selectedItem = text2;
						cxsBatchRequestData2.Clear();
						text6 = ((!text2.Contains('(')) ? text2 : text2.Substring(0, text2.IndexOf('(') - 1));
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dataSubstreamPathResolved))
						{
							cxsBatchRequestData2.AddRequest(text, "display_name", null, text6);
							UpdateStoicPath(text2, _selectedItem);
						}
						else

							cxsBatchRequestData2.AddRequest(text, "value", null, text6);
						}
						SetSubstreamChildId(text2);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					_selectedItem = text2;
				}
			}
			catch (CxsException)
			{
				_selectedItem = text2;
			}
		}

		private bool Clear(string oldValue)
		{
			if (IsUnloaded)
			{
				return false;
			}
			CxsDataProvider resolvedCxsProvider = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dataSubstreamPathResolved) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.SubstreamDataPath))
			{
				_dataSubstreamPathResolved = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.SubstreamDataPath, null);
			}
			string selectedItemBinding = _property.SelectedItemBinding;
			string text = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(selectedItemBinding, null);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dataSubstreamPathResolved))
			{
				int num = text.LastIndexOf(".");
				if (num != -1)
				{
					int num2 = 0;
					int num3 = 0;
					string text2 = text.Substring(0, num);
					string text3 = text.Substring(num + 1);
					CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
					object[] array = null;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
					{
						cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(text2, "child_count");
						cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(text2, "child_ids");
						array = resolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData);
						if (array != null)
						{
							int? num4 = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(array[0]);
							if (num4.HasValue)
							{
								num2 = num4.Value;
							}
						}
					}
					Collection<int> collection = new Collection<int>();
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3))
					{
						if (text3[0] == '#')
						{
							num3 = Convert.ToInt32(text.Substring(num + 2), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
						}
						else
						{
							ReadOnlyCollection<string> readOnlyCollection = CxsConverter.ValueToOptionsCollection(array[1]);
							int num5 = 0;
							foreach (string item in readOnlyCollection)
							{
								if (item == text3)
								{
									num3 = num5;
									collection.Add(num3);
								}
								num5++;
							}
						}
					}
					if (collection.Count > 1)
					{
						string text4 = null;
						if (oldValue.Contains('('))
						{
							text4 = oldValue.Substring(oldValue.IndexOf('(') + 1);
							text4 = text4.TrimEnd(')');
						}
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
						{
							num3 = collection[0];
						}
						else
						{
							foreach (int item2 in collection)
							{
								cxsBatchRequestData.Clear();
								string path = text2 + ".#" + item2.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
								cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(path, "child_count");
								cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(path, "child_ids");
								array = resolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData);
								if (array != null && (CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(array[0]) ?? 0) > 0)
								{
									string[] array2 = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(array[1]);
									if (array2 != null && array2.Length != 0 && array2[0] == text4)
									{
										num3 = item2;
										break;
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					if (num3 < num2)
					{
						CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData2 = new CxsBatchRequestData();
						string text5 = text2 + ".#" + num3;
						cxsBatchRequestData2.AddRequest(text5 + ".#0", "display_name", null, "");
						cxsBatchRequestData2.AddRequest(text5, "display_name", null, "");
						return resolvedCxsProvider.BatchSet(cxsBatchRequestData2);
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData3 = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			cxsBatchRequestData3.AddRequest(_dataSubstreamPathResolved, "value", null, null);
			cxsBatchRequestData3.AddRequest(text, "value", null, null);
			return resolvedCxsProvider.BatchSet(cxsBatchRequestData3);
		}

		private void UpdateWatch(ICxsDataWatch watch)
		{
			if (_watch != null)
			{
				if (_watch_ValuesChangedHandler != null)
				{
					_watch.ValuesChanged -= _watch_ValuesChangedHandler;
				}
				_watch.Unload();
			}
			_watch = watch;
			if (_watch == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_watch_ValuesChangedHandler == null)
			{
				_watch_ValuesChangedHandler = delegate
				{
					RefreshData();
				};
			}
			_watch.ValuesChanged += _watch_ValuesChangedHandler;
		}

		private void RefreshOptions(ReadOnlyCollection<string> value)
		{
			if (value == _options)
			{
				return;
			}
			ObservableCollection<string> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<string>();
			foreach (string item in value)
			{
				observableCollection.Add(item);
			}
			ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string> options = new ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string>(observableCollection);
			_options = options;
			OnPropertyChanged("Items");
		}

		private void ResetDataPath()
		{
			UpdateDataPathDependencies(null);
			_dataStoicPathResolved = null;
			_selectedItem = null;
			if (_watch != null)
			{
				UpdateWatch(null);
				RefreshData();
			}
			OnPropertyChanged("SelectedItem");
			OnPropertyChanged("CxsItemMoniker");
		}

		private string EnsureDataPath()
		{
			ResolveDataPath();
			return _dataComponentOptionPathResolved;
		}

		private void ResolveDataPath()
		{
			if (!IsUnloaded)
			{
				UpdateDataPathDependencies(null);
				CxvPropertyWatch cxvPropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.StoicDataPath))
				{
					_dataStoicPathResolved = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.StoicDataPath, cxvPropertyWatch);
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.SubstreamDataPath))
				{
					_dataSubstreamPathResolved = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.SubstreamDataPath, cxvPropertyWatch);
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.ComponentOptionPath))
				{
					_dataComponentOptionPathResolved = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.ComponentOptionPath, cxvPropertyWatch);
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.SubstreamOptionPath))
				{
					_dataSubstreamOptionPathPathResolved = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.SubstreamOptionPath, cxvPropertyWatch);
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.SelectedItemBinding))
				{
					_dataSelectedItemPathResolved = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.SelectedItemBinding, cxvPropertyWatch);
				}
				UpdateDataPathDependencies(cxvPropertyWatch);
			}
		}

		private void AddDataToWatch(ICxsDataWatch watch)
		{
			if (_dataComponentOptionPathResolved != null)
			{
				watch.AddWatch(_dataComponentOptionPathResolved, "option_list");
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dataSelectedItemPathResolved))
			{
				watch.AddWatch(_dataSelectedItemPathResolved, "value");
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dataSubstreamPathResolved))
			{
				watch.AddWatch(_dataSubstreamPathResolved, "value");
			}
		}

		public void RefreshData()
		{
			if (IsUnloaded)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_watch == null && _dataContainer != null)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(EnsureDataPath()))
				{
					return;
				}
				ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = _dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
				if (cxsDataWatch == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				AddDataToWatch(cxsDataWatch);
				UpdateWatch(cxsDataWatch);
			}
			if (_watch == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			object[] array = _watch.Values;
			if (array == null)
			{
				array = new object[4];
			}
			_compsList = CxsConverter.ValueToOptionsCollection(array[0]);
			_validSubstreams = new Dictionary<string, ReadOnlyCollection<string>>();
			Collection<string> collection = new Collection<string>();
			string text = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.ComponentOptionPath, null);
			_dataSubstreamOptionPathPathResolved = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.SubstreamOptionPath, null);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dataSubstreamOptionPathPathResolved))
			{
				IUnityContainer container = _dataContainer.UnityContainer.CreateChildContainer();
				container.RegisterType(typeof(CxsDataProvider), new ContainerControlledLifetimeManager());
				CxvViewModel cxvViewModel = container.Resolve<CxvViewModel>(Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
				foreach (string comps in _compsList)
				{
					CxsDataProvider resolvedCxsProvider = cxvViewModel.ResolvedCxsProvider;
					if (resolvedCxsProvider == null)
					{
						return;
					}
					CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
					cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(text, "value", null, comps);
					if (!resolvedCxsProvider.BatchSetNoRefresh(cxsBatchRequestData))
					{
						break;
					}
					resolvedCxsProvider.UpdateDataCache();
					ReadOnlyCollection<string> readOnlyCollection = CxsConverter.ValueToOptionsCollection(resolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(_dataSubstreamOptionPathPathResolved, "option_list", null));
					_validSubstreams.Add(comps, readOnlyCollection);
					if (readOnlyCollection == null || readOnlyCollection.Count == 0 || readOnlyCollection.Count == 1)
					{
						collection.Add(comps);
						continue;
					}
					foreach (string item in readOnlyCollection)
					{
						collection.Add(comps + " (" + item + ")");
					}
				}
				_compsList = new ReadOnlyCollection<string>(collection);
			}
			if (_compsList != null)
			{
				RefreshOptions(_compsList);
			}
			string text2 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[1]);
			string text3 = ((array.Length > 2) ? CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[2]) : null);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
			{
				_selectedItem = text2;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3))
				{
					_selectedItem = _selectedItem + " (" + text3 + ")";
				}
				OnPropertyChanged("SelectedItem");
			}
		}

		private void UpdateDataPathDependencies(CxvPropertyWatch dataPathDependencies)
		{
			if (dataPathDependencies == _dataPathDependencies)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_dataPathDependencies != null)
			{
				if (_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler != null)
				{
					_dataPathDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= _pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				}
				_dataPathDependencies.Clear();
				_dataPathDependencies = null;
			}
			if (dataPathDependencies == null || dataPathDependencies.IsEmpty)
			{
				return;
			}
			_dataPathDependencies = dataPathDependencies;
			if (_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler == null)
			{
				_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler = delegate
				{
					ResetDataPath();
				};
			}
			_dataPathDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += _pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
		}

		private void UpdateStoicPath(string newId, string oldId)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dataStoicPathResolved) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(oldId) && _dataStoicPathResolved.EndsWith(oldId))
			{
				int length = _dataStoicPathResolved.Length - oldId.Length;
				_dataStoicPathResolved = _dataStoicPathResolved.Substring(0, length) + newId;
			}
		}

		public void Unload()
		{
			_property = null;
			_dataContainer = null;
			UpdateDataPathDependencies(null);
			if (_watch != null)
			{
				_watch.Unload();
			}
		}

		XElement IViewDataSerializable.WriteXElement()
		{
			List<XElement> list = new List<XElement>();
			string selectedItem = SelectedItem;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(selectedItem))
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("SelectedItem", selectedItem));
			}
			ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string> items = Items;
			if (items != null)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("Items", items.Select((string item) => new XElement("string", item)), new XAttribute("Count", items.Count)));
			}
			if (list.Count <= 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return new XElement(GetType().Name, list);
		}
	}
	public class CxvDescriptionPropertyData : CxvStatePropertyData, IViewDataSerializable
	{
		private readonly CxvDescriptionProperty _property;

		private bool? _truncate;

		private CxvPropertyWatch _dependencies;

		private static List<string> _objPaths = new List<string>
		{
			"Blocks", "Streams", "Utilities", "Flowsheeting Options.Calculator", "Flowsheeting Options.Design-Spec", "Flowsheeting Options.Transfer", "Flowsheeting Options.Balance", "Flowsheeting Options.Pres-Relief", "Flowsheeting Options.Measurement", "Model Analysis Tools.Sensitivity",
			"Model Analysis Tools.Optimization", "Model Analysis Tools.Data-Fit.Regression", "Model Analysis Tools.Contraint", "Convergence.Convergence", "Convergence.Sequence", "EO Configuration.Objective", "EO Configuration.Ports", "Controllers", "Sensors", "Properties.Analysis",
			"Components.Assay/Blend", "Components.PetroCharacterization.Generation", "Components.PetroCharacterization.Analysis Options", "Properties.Estimation", "Properties.Regression"
		};

		public string Value
		{
			get
			{
				return GetText(base.DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.DataPath));
			}
			set
			{
				SetText(base.DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.DataPath), value);
			}
		}

		public override string VariablePrompt => CxvInternalHelpers.ValueToStringRemoveXml(GetDataAttributeValue("VariablePrompt", _property.DataPath, "description"));

		public bool IsReadOnly
		{
			get
			{
				if (_property.IsReadOnly.HasValue)
				{
					return _property.IsReadOnly.Value;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool? IsEntered => true;

		public bool? IsCalculated
		{
			get
			{
				if (_property.IsCalculated.HasValue)
				{
					return _property.IsCalculated.Value;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public CxvDescriptionPropertyData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvDescriptionProperty property, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
			: base(dataContainer, property)
		{
			_property = property;
			if (dependencies == null)
			{
				_dependencies = new CxvPropertyWatch();
			}
			else
			{
				_dependencies = dependencies;
			}
			_dependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += OnDependenciesChanged;
		}

		private Dictionary<string, string> ExistingIds(string path)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(path))
			{
				return null;
			}
			string text = CxsPath.LeafId(path);
			string text2 = null;
			switch (text)
			{
			case "Blocks":
			case "Utilities":
				text2 = CxsPath.ParentPath(path);
				break;
			case "Regression":
				text2 = CxsPath.ParentPath(CxsPath.ParentPath(CxsPath.ParentPath(path)));
				break;
			default:
				text2 = CxsPath.ParentPath(CxsPath.ParentPath(path));
				break;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
			{
				return null;
			}
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			foreach (string objPath in _objPaths)
			{
				cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(new CxsBatchRequest(CxsPath.CombinePaths(text2, objPath), "child_ids"));
			}
			object[] array = null;
			try
			{
				array = base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData);
			}
			catch (CxsException)
			{
			}
			if (array == null || array.Count() == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				string[] array2 = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(array[i]);
				if (array2 == null || array2.Count() == 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				string[] array3 = array2;
				foreach (string key in array3)
				{
					if (!dictionary.ContainsKey(key))
					{
						dictionary.Add(key, _objPaths[i]);
					}
				}
			}
			return dictionary;
		}

		private string GetErrorObjectsDescription(string s, bool isBatchConvergence)
		{
			string[] array = s.Trim().Split(' ');
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			if (array != null)
			{
				string text = "Top.appModelV8.Blocks";
				Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = ExistingIds(text);
				string[] array2 = array;
				foreach (string text2 in array2)
				{
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (text2.Contains('.'))
					{
						string[] array3 = text2.Trim().Split('.');
						string text3 = text;
						for (int j = 0; j < array3.Length - 1; j++)
						{
							text3 += ".";
							text3 += array3[j];
							text3 += ".Data.Blocks";
						}
						Dictionary<string, string> dictionary2 = ExistingIds(text3);
						string text4 = array3[array3.Length - 1];
						if (dictionary2.ContainsKey(text4))
						{
							string recordType = GetRecordType(text3 + "." + text4);
							string value = $"<ErrorObjects Type=\"{dictionary2[text4]}\" RecordType=\"{recordType}\">{text2}</ErrorObjects>\t";
							stringBuilder.Append(value);
						}
						else if (isBatchConvergence && text4.StartsWith("$"))
						{
							string value2 = string.Format("<ErrorObjects Type=\"{0}\" RecordType=\"{1}\">{2}</ErrorObjects>\t", "Convergence.Batch-Options", "Convergence.Batch-Options", text2);
							stringBuilder.Append(value2);
						}
					}
					else if (dictionary.ContainsKey(text2))
					{
						string recordType2 = GetRecordType(text + "." + text2);
						string value3 = $"<ErrorObjects Type=\"{dictionary[text2]}\" RecordType=\"{recordType2}\">{text2}</ErrorObjects>\t";
						stringBuilder.Append(value3);
					}
					else
					{
						if (!isBatchConvergence || !text2.StartsWith("$"))
						{
							return null;
						}
						string value4 = string.Format("<ErrorObjects Type=\"{0}\" RecordType=\"{1}\">{2}</ErrorObjects>\t", "Convergence.Batch-Options", "Convergence.Batch-Options", text2);
						stringBuilder.Append(value4);
					}
				}
				return stringBuilder.ToString();
			}
			return null;
		}

		private string GetRecordType(string dataPath)
		{
			string result = null;
			try
			{
				result = CxsConverter.ValueToString(base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(dataPath, "type", null));
			}
			catch (CxsException)
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		private string GetText(string dataPath)
		{
			if (IsReadOnly)
			{
				int? num = base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(dataPath, "child_count", null) as int?;
				_dependencies.Clear();
				if (num.HasValue)
				{
					string text = null;
					List<string> list = new List<string>();
					StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
					for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
					{
						string path = dataPath + ".#" + i.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
						ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.CreateWatch();
						cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(path, "value", null);
						if (cxsDataWatch.Values != null)
						{
							_dependencies.AddWatch(cxsDataWatch);
						}
						string text2 = ((cxsDataWatch.Values.Length != 0) ? cxsDataWatch.Values[0].ToString() : string.Empty);
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
						{
							list.Add(text2);
							bool isBatchConvergence = list.Count > 2 && list[0] == "The following Batch Convergence blocks were" && (list[1] == "completed with errors:" || list[1] == "completed with warnings:");
							if (list.Count > 2)
							{
								list.Clear();
							}
							string errorObjectsDescription = GetErrorObjectsDescription(text2, isBatchConvergence);
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(errorObjectsDescription) && !text.EndsWith("never executed:") && !text.EndsWith("FOLLOWING STAGES:"))
							{
								text2 = errorObjectsDescription;
							}
							text = text2;
						}
						if (i == num - 1)
						{
							stringBuilder.Append(text2);
						}
						else
						{
							stringBuilder.AppendLine(text2);
						}
					}
					return stringBuilder.ToString();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				int? num2 = base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(dataPath, "child_count", null) as int?;
				_dependencies.Clear();
				if (num2.HasValue)
				{
					StringBuilder stringBuilder2 = new StringBuilder();
					for (int j = 0; j < num2; j++)
					{
						string path2 = dataPath + ".#" + j.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
						ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch2 = base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.CreateWatch();
						cxsDataWatch2.AddWatch(path2, "value", null);
						if (cxsDataWatch2.Values != null)
						{
							_dependencies.AddWatch(cxsDataWatch2);
						}
						string value = ((cxsDataWatch2.Values.Length != 0) ? cxsDataWatch2.Values[0].ToString() : string.Empty);
						if (j == num2 - 1)
						{
							stringBuilder2.Append(value);
						}
						else
						{
							stringBuilder2.AppendLine(value);
						}
					}
					return stringBuilder2.ToString();
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private void SetText(string dataPath, string value)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.SuspendNotifications();
			int? num = GetDataAttributeValue("Text", dataPath, "child_count") as int?;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
			{
				string[] array = value.Replace("\r\n", "\n").Split('\n');
				int num2 = array.Length;
				if (_property.LineLimit.HasValue && num2 > _property.LineLimit.Value)
				{
					if (!_truncate.HasValue)
					{
						ShowMessageBoxEventArgs e = new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
						{
							Title = base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.GetI18nApplicationName(),
							Message = string.Format(Resources.TruncateWarning, _property.LineLimit.Value, array.Length),
							MsgBoxButton = MessageBoxButton.YesNo,
							MsgBoxImage = MessageBoxImage.Exclamation,
							DialogResult = MessageBoxResult.Yes
						};
						base.DataContainer.UnityContainer.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<ShowMessageBoxEvent>().Publish(e);
						_truncate = e.DialogResult == MessageBoxResult.Yes;
					}
					if (_truncate.Value)
					{
						num2 = _property.LineLimit.Value;
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < num2; i++)
				{
					string value2 = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(array[i]) ? " " : array[i]);
					if (i < array.Length - 1 || !string.IsNullOrEmpty(value2))
					{
						if (num.HasValue && i < num.Value)
						{
							SaveData(dataPath + ".#" + i.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture), "value", "preserve_space", value2);
							continue;
						}
						base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(dataPath, "InsertChild", true, i);
						SaveData(dataPath + ".#" + i.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture), "value", "preserve_space", value2);
					}
				}
				if (num.HasValue)
				{
					for (int num3 = num.Value - 1; num3 >= num2; num3--)
					{
						DeleteChild(dataPath, num3);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (num.HasValue)
			{
				for (int num4 = num.Value - 1; num4 >= 0; num4--)
				{
					DeleteChild(dataPath, num4);
				}
			}
			base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.UpdateDataCache();
			base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.ResumeNotifications();
			if (flag)
			{
				Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action<string>(OnPropertyChanged), "Value");
			}
		}

		XElement IViewDataSerializable.WriteXElement()
		{
			string value = Value;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
			{
				return new XElement(GetType().Name, new XElement("Value", value));
			}
			return null;
		}

		private void OnDependenciesChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			OnPropertyChanged("Value");
		}
	}
	public class CxvExpanderPropertyData : CxvStatePropertyData
	{
		private readonly CxvExpanderProperty _property;

		public bool? Entered => GetPropertyWatchedValue("Entered", GetIsEntered) as bool?;

		public bool? Complete => GetPropertyWatchedValue("Complete", GetIsComplete) as bool?;

		public bool? Accessible => GetPropertyWatchedValue("Accessible", GetIsAccessible) as bool?;

		public override string VariablePrompt
		{
			get
			{
				if (_property.AccessibleDataPaths.Count >= 1 && !(Accessible ?? true))
				{
					return CxvInternalHelpers.ValueToStringRemoveXml(GetDataAttributeValue("VariablePrompt", _property.AccessibleDataPaths[0], "description"));
				}
				return base.VariablePrompt;
			}
		}

		public string CompletionMessages
		{
			get
			{
				ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = base.DataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
				foreach (string completeDataPath in _property.CompleteDataPaths)
				{
					string path = base.DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(completeDataPath);
					cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(path, "tabstate", "complete");
				}
				object[] values = cxsDataWatch.Values;
				cxsDataWatch = base.DataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
				string text = null;
				if (values != null)
				{
					int num = 0;
					object[] array = values;
					for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
					{
						bool? flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(array[i]);
						if (flag.HasValue && !flag.Value)
						{
							string path2 = base.DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.CompleteDataPaths[num]);
							cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(path2, "completion_message");
						}
						num++;
					}
					array = cxsDataWatch.Values;
					for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
					{
						string text2 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[i]);
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
						{
							text = ((text != null) ? (text + text2) : text2);
						}
					}
				}
				return text;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			base.OnPropertyChanged(propertyName);
			if (propertyName == "Accessible")
			{
				RaisePropertyChanged("VariablePrompt");
			}
		}

		public CxvExpanderPropertyData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvExpanderProperty property)
			: base(dataContainer, property)
		{
			_property = property;
		}

		private object GetIsEntered(CxvPropertyWatch watcher)
		{
			CxvLogicalTestCollection tabStateLogicalTestCollection = CxvTabStateProperty.GetTabStateLogicalTestCollection(_property.EnteredDataPaths, CxvTabStateOperator.Entered);
			CxvLogicalOperator unionOperator = CxvTabStateProperty.GetUnionOperator(CxvTabStateOperator.Entered);
			return tabStateLogicalTestCollection.EvaluateConditions(unionOperator, base.DataContainer, watcher);
		}

		private object GetIsComplete(CxvPropertyWatch watcher)
		{
			CxvLogicalTestCollection tabStateLogicalTestCollection = CxvTabStateProperty.GetTabStateLogicalTestCollection(_property.CompleteDataPaths, CxvTabStateOperator.Complete);
			CxvLogicalOperator unionOperator = CxvTabStateProperty.GetUnionOperator(CxvTabStateOperator.Complete);
			return tabStateLogicalTestCollection.EvaluateConditions(unionOperator, base.DataContainer, watcher);
		}

		private object GetIsAccessible(CxvPropertyWatch watcher)
		{
			CxvLogicalTestCollection tabStateLogicalTestCollection = CxvTabStateProperty.GetTabStateLogicalTestCollection(_property.AccessibleDataPaths, CxvTabStateOperator.Accessible);
			CxvLogicalOperator unionOperator = CxvTabStateProperty.GetUnionOperator(CxvTabStateOperator.Accessible);
			return tabStateLogicalTestCollection.EvaluateConditions(unionOperator, base.DataContainer, watcher);
		}
	}
