	public class CxvExtraChildCountIndexBasisData : CxvChildCountIndexBasisData
	{
		private int _extraCount;

		public override int Count
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.ChildIdsVector == null)
				{
					RefreshChildIds();
				}
				if (base.ChildIdsVector == null)
				{
					return 1;
				}
				return base.ChildIdsVector.Length + 1;
			}
		}

		public CxvExtraChildCountIndexBasisData(CxvExtraChildCountIndexBasis indexBasis, ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer)
			: base(indexBasis, dataContainer)
		{
			_extraCount = indexBasis.ExtraCount;
		}

		public override object GetIndexSubstitution(int index, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (index < _extraCount)
			{
				return "#" + (-(index + 1)).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
			}
			index -= _extraCount;
			if (base.ChildIdsVector == null)
			{
				RefreshChildIds();
			}
			dependencies?.AddWatch(this, "Count");
			object obj = null;
			if (base.IndexBasis.UseChildValue && base.ChildIdsVector.Length != 0)
			{
				return base.ChildValuesVector[index];
			}
			if (base.IndexBasis.UseChildId && base.ChildIdsVector != null && index >= 0 && index < base.ChildIdsVector.Length)
			{
				return base.ChildIdsVector[index];
			}
			return "#" + index.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
		}
	}
	public class CxvIndexBasisBaseData
	{
		public virtual int Count => 0;

		public virtual bool? IsNotJagged => null;

		public virtual bool AllowRemove => false;

		public virtual bool AllowNew => false;

		public virtual bool AllowReorder => false;

		public event EventHandler CountChanged;

		protected void OnCountChanged()
		{
			if (this.CountChanged != null)
			{
				this.CountChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		public virtual object GetIndexSubstitution(int index, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			return index;
		}

		public virtual void Unload()
		{
		}

		public virtual bool AllowRemoveAt(int index)
		{
			return false;
		}

		public virtual void RemoveAt(int index)
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException();
		}

		public virtual void RemoveAt(List<int> indices)
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException();
		}

		public virtual void Insert(int index, object value)
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException();
		}
	}
	public class CxvInfoTabItemPropertyData : CxvTabItemPropertyData
	{
		public CxvInfoTabItemPropertyData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvInfoTabItemProperty property)
			: base(dataContainer, property)
		{
		}

		protected override object GetIsEntered(CxvPropertyWatch watcher)
		{
			return new CxvLogicalTestCollection
			{
				new CxvLogicalTest
				{
					Operator = CxvLogicalOperator.And,
					SubConditions = 
					{
						new CxvLogicalTest
						{
							DataPath = "@main.@extraDescription_path",
							Operator = CxvLogicalOperator.IsNotNull
						},
						new CxvLogicalTest
						{
							DataPath = "@main.@extraDescription_path",
							Operator = CxvLogicalOperator.NotEqual,
							Value = ""
						}
					}
				},
				new CxvLogicalTest
				{
					Operator = CxvLogicalOperator.And,
					SubConditions = 
					{
						new CxvLogicalTest
						{
							DataPath = "@main.@extraComments_path",
							Operator = CxvLogicalOperator.IsNotNull
						},
						new CxvLogicalTest
						{
							DataPath = "@main.@extraComments_path",
							DataAttribute = "child_count",
							Operator = CxvLogicalOperator.GreaterThan,
							Value = 0
						}
					}
				}
			}.EvaluateConditions(CxvLogicalOperator.Or, base.DataContainer, watcher);
		}
	}
	public class CxvLabelPropertyData : CxvEnablePromptPropertyData, IViewDataSerializable
	{
		public CxvLabelPropertyData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvLabelProperty property)
			: base(dataContainer, property)
		{
		}

		XElement IViewDataSerializable.WriteXElement()
		{
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class CxvLineFormatData : CxvPropertyData, ICxvPropertyData
	{
		private ICxvViewDataContainer _dataContainer;

		private CxvLineFormatProperty _property;

		private string _dataPathResolved;

		private CxvPropertyWatch _dataPathDependencies;

		private ICxsDataWatch _watch;

		private object _value;

		private static Color[] _definedColors = new Color[16]
		{
			Colors.Black,
			Colors.Navy,
			Colors.Green,
			Colors.Teal,
			Colors.Brown,
			Colors.Purple,
			Colors.DarkOliveGreen,
			Colors.Gray,
			Colors.DarkGray,
			Colors.Blue,
			Colors.LightGreen,
			Colors.SkyBlue,
			Colors.Red,
			Colors.Fuchsia,
			Colors.Yellow,
			Colors.LightGray
		};

		private EventHandler _pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;

		private EventHandler _watch_ValuesChangedHandler;

		public object Value
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureData();
				return _value;
			}
			set
			{
			}
		}

		public Color LineColor
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureData();
				int num = 0;
				string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(_value);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					string[] array = text.Split(';');
					if (array.Length >= 3)
					{
						if (array[0].StartsWith("#"))
						{
							try
							{
								return (Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString(array[0]);
							}
							catch (FormatException)
							{
							}
						}
						else
						{
							int? num2 = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(array[0]);
							if (num2.HasValue)
							{
								num = num2.Value;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if (num < 0 || num > _definedColors.Length - 1)
				{
					num = 0;
				}
				return _definedColors[num];
			}
		}

		public int ColorIndex
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureData();
				int num = 0;
				string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(_value);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && !text.StartsWith("#"))
				{
					string[] array = text.Split(';');
					if (array.Length >= 3)
					{
						int? num2 = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(array[0]);
						if (num2.HasValue)
						{
							num = num2.Value;
						}
					}
				}
				if (num < 0 || num > _definedColors.Length - 1)
				{
					num = 0;
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		public double LineWeight
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureData();
				string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(_value);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					string[] array = text.Split(';');
					if (array.Length >= 3)
					{
						double? num = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(array[1]);
						if (num.HasValue)
						{
							return num.Value;
						}
					}
				}
				if (_property.DefaultLineWeight.HasValue)
				{
					return _property.DefaultLineWeight.Value;
				}
				return 1.0;
			}
		}

		public int LineStyle
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureData();
				string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(_value);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					string[] array = text.Split(';');
					if (array.Length >= 3)
					{
						int? num = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(array[2]);
						if (num.HasValue)
						{
							return num.Value;
						}
					}
				}
				return 0;
			}
		}

		public bool IsUnloaded => _dataContainer == null;

		public CxvLineFormatData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvLineFormatProperty property, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
			: base(dataContainer, property)
		{
			_dataContainer = dataContainer;
			_property = property;
		}

		private void EnsureData()
		{
			if (_watch == null && _dataContainer != null)
			{
				RefreshData();
			}
		}

		private void RefreshData()
		{
			if (IsUnloaded)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = EnsureDataPath();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_watch == null)
			{
				ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = _dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
				if (cxsDataWatch == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "value");
				UpdateWatch(cxsDataWatch);
			}
			object[] values = _watch.Values;
			RefreshValue(values[0]);
		}

		private void RefreshValue(object value, bool notify = true)
		{
			if (_value != value || value == null)
			{
				_value = value;
				if (notify)
				{
					OnPropertyChanged("Value");
					OnPropertyChanged("LineColor");
					OnPropertyChanged("LineWeight");
					OnPropertyChanged("LineStyle");
				}
			}
		}

		private string EnsureDataPath()
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dataPathResolved))
			{
				ResolveDataPath();
			}
			return _dataPathResolved;
		}

		private void ResolveDataPath()
		{
			if (!IsUnloaded)
			{
				UpdateDataPathDependencies(null);
				CxvPropertyWatch cxvPropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.DataPath))
				{
					_dataPathResolved = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.DataPath, cxvPropertyWatch);
				}
				UpdateDataPathDependencies(cxvPropertyWatch);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateDataPathDependencies(CxvPropertyWatch dataPathDependencies)
		{
			if (dataPathDependencies == _dataPathDependencies)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_dataPathDependencies != null)
			{
				if (_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler != null)
				{
					_dataPathDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= _pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				}
				_dataPathDependencies.Clear();
				_dataPathDependencies = null;
			}
			if (dataPathDependencies == null || dataPathDependencies.IsEmpty)
			{
				return;
			}
			_dataPathDependencies = dataPathDependencies;
			if (_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler == null)
			{
				_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler = delegate
				{
					ResetDataPath();
				};
			}
			_dataPathDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += _pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
		}

		private void ResetDataPath()
		{
			UpdateDataPathDependencies(null);
			_dataPathResolved = null;
			UpdateWatch(null);
			RefreshData();
			OnPropertyChanged("CxsItemMoniker");
		}

		private void UpdateWatch(ICxsDataWatch watch)
		{
			if (_watch != null)
			{
				if (_watch_ValuesChangedHandler != null)
				{
					_watch.ValuesChanged -= _watch_ValuesChangedHandler;
				}
				_watch.Unload();
			}
			_watch = watch;
			if (_watch == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_watch_ValuesChangedHandler == null)
			{
				_watch_ValuesChangedHandler = delegate
				{
					RefreshData();
				};
			}
			_watch.ValuesChanged += _watch_ValuesChangedHandler;
		}

		public void Unload()
		{
			_property = null;
			_dataContainer = null;
		}
	}
	public class CxvListVariableData : CxvPropertyData, ICxvEditableObject, IEditableObject, ICxvPropertyData, IViewDataSerializable
	{
		private bool? _readOnly = false;

		private EventHandler _watch_ValuesChangedHandler;

		private EventHandler _pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;

		private ICxvViewDataContainer _dataContainer;

		private CxvVariableProperty _property;

		private string _dataPathResolved;

		private CxvPropertyWatch _dataPathDependencies;

		private ICxsDataWatch _watch;

		private ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string> _options;

		private Dictionary<string, string> _optionPrompts;

		private string _optionPromptsString;

		private ObservableCollection<string> _value = new ObservableCollection<string>();

		private Collection<string> _previousValue = new Collection<string>();

		private ReadOnlyCollection<string> _childIds;

		private bool? _canReorder;

		private bool? _canDefine;

		private bool _inUpdate;

		private bool _inNew;

		private bool _inEditMode;

		private bool _itemsChanged;

		private string _focusProperty;

		public ObservableCollection<string> Value
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureData();
				return _value;
			}
		}

		public ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string> Options
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureData();
				return _options;
			}
		}

		public Dictionary<string, string> OptionPrompts
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureData();
				return _optionPrompts;
			}
		}

		public string VariablePrompt => CxvInternalHelpers.ValueToStringRemoveXml(GetDataAttributeValue("VariablePrompt", EnsureDataPath(), "description"));

		public bool? IsReadOnly
		{
			get
			{
				return _readOnly;
			}
			private set
			{
				if (value != _readOnly)
				{
					_readOnly = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("IsReadOnly");
				}
			}
		}

		public bool? IsCalculated => false;

		public bool? IsEntered => _value != null && _value.Count > 0;

		public bool? CanReorder
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureData();
				return _canReorder;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_canReorder != value)
				{
					_canReorder = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("CanReorder");
				}
			}
		}

		public bool? CanDefine
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureData();
				return _canDefine;
			}
		}

		public string CxsItemMoniker => CxvObservableObject.CxsPathToMoniker(_dataPathResolved);

		public bool IsUnloaded => _dataContainer == null;

		public bool InEditMode => _inEditMode;

		public string FocusProperty
		{
			get
			{
				return _focusProperty;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(_focusProperty != value))
				{
					return;
				}
				_focusProperty = value;
				if (_inEditMode && string.IsNullOrEmpty(_focusProperty))
				{
					try
					{
						EndEdit();
					}
					catch (CxsException)
					{
						CancelEdit();
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public CxvListVariableData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataProvider, CxvVariableProperty property)
			: base(dataProvider, property)
		{
			_dataContainer = dataProvider;
			_property = property;
		}

		private void EnsureData()
		{
			if (_watch == null)
			{
				RefreshData();
			}
		}

		public void RefreshData()
		{
			string text = EnsureDataPath();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				if (_options != null && _options.Count > 0)
				{
					RefreshOptions(new ReadOnlyCollection<string>(new List<string>()), notify: true);
					RefreshOptionPrompts("", notify: true);
				}
				if (_value != null && _value.Count > 0)
				{
					RefreshValue(new ReadOnlyCollection<string>(new List<string>()));
				}
				IsReadOnly = true;
				return;
			}
			IsReadOnly = false;
			_inNew = false;
			if (_watch == null)
			{
				ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = _dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
				if (cxsDataWatch == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "child_ids");
				cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "option_list");
				cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "option_prompts");
				cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "action_status", "ReorderChildren");
				cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "action_status", "DefineChild");
				UpdateWatch(cxsDataWatch);
			}
			object[] array = _watch.Values;
			if (array == null)
			{
				array = new object[5];
			}
			string optionsString = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[1]);
			string optionPomptsString = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[2]);
			CxvInternalHelpers.ReviseOptionAndOptionPrompt(ref optionsString, ref optionPomptsString);
			RefreshOptions(CxsConverter.ValueToOptionsCollection(optionsString), notify: false);
			RefreshOptionPrompts(CxsConverter.ValueToString(optionPomptsString), notify: false);
			_childIds = CxsConverter.ValueToOptionsCollection(array[0]);
			if (!_inUpdate)
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				if (_childIds != null)
				{
					ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch2 = _dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
					foreach (string childId in _childIds)
					{
						cxsDataWatch2.AddWatch(text + "." + childId, "value");
						_watch.AddWatch(text + "." + childId, "value");
					}
					object[] values = cxsDataWatch2.Values;
					foreach (object obj in values)
					{
						list.Add(obj.ToString());
					}
				}
				RefreshValue(new ReadOnlyCollection<string>(list));
				if (_property.AllowSort.HasValue && !_property.AllowSort.Value)
				{
					_canReorder = false;
				}
				else if (array[3] == null)
				{
					_canReorder = false;
				}
				else
				{
					CanReorder = CxsConverter.ValueToActionStatusDetails(array[3])?.CanInvoke ?? false;
				}
				if (array[4] == null)
				{
					_canDefine = false;
				}
				else
				{
					_canDefine = CxsConverter.ValueToActionStatusDetails(array[4])?.CanInvoke ?? false;
				}
			}
			OnPropertyChanged("Options");
			OnPropertyChanged("OptionPrompts");
		}

		private void UpdateWatch(ICxsDataWatch watch)
		{
			if (_watch != null)
			{
				if (_watch_ValuesChangedHandler != null)
				{
					_watch.ValuesChanged -= _watch_ValuesChangedHandler;
				}
				_watch.Unload();
			}
			_watch = watch;
			if (_watch == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_watch_ValuesChangedHandler == null)
			{
				_watch_ValuesChangedHandler = delegate
				{
					RefreshData();
				};
			}
			_watch.ValuesChanged += _watch_ValuesChangedHandler;
		}

		private void RefreshValue(ReadOnlyCollection<string> value)
		{
			try
			{
				bool flag = _value.Count != value.Count;
				if (!flag)
				{
					for (int i = 0; i < value.Count; i++)
					{
						if (_value[i] != value[i])
						{
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					_value.CollectionChanged -= _value_CollectionChanged;
					_value.Clear();
					foreach (string item in value)
					{
						_value.Add(item);
					}
					_value.CollectionChanged += _value_CollectionChanged;
				}
				OnPropertyChanged("Value");
				OnPropertyChanged("IsEntered");
				_previousValue.Clear();
				foreach (string item2 in _value)
				{
					_previousValue.Add(item2);
				}
			}
			catch (InvalidOperationException)
			{
			}
			finally
			{
				_value.CollectionChanged -= _value_CollectionChanged;
				_value.CollectionChanged += _value_CollectionChanged;
			}
		}

		private void _value_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_inEditMode)
			{
				OnSetValue();
			}
			else
			{
				_itemsChanged = true;
			}
		}

		private void OnSetValue()
		{
			string text = EnsureDataPath();
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = false;
			bool flag3 = false;
			bool flag4 = false;
			int num = -1;
			if (_value.Count > _previousValue.Count)
			{
				flag3 = true;
				for (int i = 0; i < _previousValue.Count; i++)
				{
					if (_value[i] != _previousValue[i])
					{
						flag3 = false;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (_value.Count == _previousValue.Count - 1)
			{
				int num2 = 0;
				flag4 = true;
				for (int j = 0; j < _previousValue.Count; j++)
				{
					if (num2 == _value.Count || _value[num2] != _previousValue[j])
					{
						if (num != -1)
						{
							flag4 = false;
							break;
						}
						num = j;
					}
					else
					{
						num2++;
					}
				}
			}
			if (flag4)
			{
				_inUpdate = true;
				CxvActionResult cxvActionResult = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(text, "DeleteChild", true, num);
				_inUpdate = false;
				if (cxvActionResult.Succeeded)
				{
					_previousValue.Clear();
					if (_value != null)
					{
						foreach (string item in _value)
						{
							_previousValue.Add(item);
						}
					}
					if ((_property.RestoreDefaultOnClear ?? false) && (_value == null || _value.Count() == 0))
					{
						Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(RefreshData));
					}
				}
				OnPropertyChanged("Value");
				OnPropertyChanged("IsEntered");
				if (!cxvActionResult.Succeeded)
				{
					OnPropertyChanged("Options");
				}
				return;
			}
			if (_value.Count != _previousValue.Count)
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			else
			{
				for (int k = 0; k < _value.Count; k++)
				{
					if (_value[k] != _previousValue[k])
					{
						flag = true;
						flag2 = true;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (flag2 && _canReorder.HasValue && _canReorder.Value)
			{
				object[] array = new object[_value.Count];
				object obj = array;
				for (int l = 0; l < _value.Count; l++)
				{
					bool flag5 = false;
					for (int m = 0; m < _previousValue.Count; m++)
					{
						if (_value[l] == _previousValue[m])
						{
							flag5 = true;
							array[l] = m;
							break;
						}
					}
					if (!flag5)
					{
						flag2 = false;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (flag2)
				{
					CxvActionResult cxvActionResult2 = ((!(_property.SkipSemanticOnReorder ?? false)) ? _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(text, "ReorderChildren", true, obj) : _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(text, "ReorderChildren", true, obj, 1));
					if (cxvActionResult2.Succeeded)
					{
						_dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.UpdateDataCache(text);
						_previousValue.Clear();
						{
							foreach (string item2 in _value)
							{
								_previousValue.Add(item2);
							}
							return;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (_childIds == null)
			{
				if (_watch != null)
				{
					_inUpdate = true;
					_dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.UpdateDataCache(text);
					RefreshData();
					_inUpdate = false;
				}
				if (_childIds == null)
				{
					_childIds = new ReadOnlyCollection<string>(new List<string>());
				}
			}
			_inUpdate = true;
			for (int num3 = _childIds.Count; num3 > _value.Count; num3--)
			{
				_dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(text, "DeleteChild", num3 == _value.Count + 1, num3 - 1);
			}
			Collection<int> collection = new Collection<int>();
			_dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.SuspendNotifications();
			if (_childIds == null)
			{
				_childIds = new ReadOnlyCollection<string>(new List<string>());
			}
			for (int n = _childIds.Count; n < _value.Count; n++)
			{
				_dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(text, "InsertChild", n == _value.Count - 1, -1);
				collection.Add(n);
			}
			_inUpdate = false;
			if (_watch != null)
			{
				_inUpdate = true;
				_dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.UpdateDataCache(text);
				RefreshData();
				_inUpdate = false;
			}
			if (_value == null || _value.Count == 0)
			{
				OnPropertyChanged("Value");
				OnPropertyChanged("IsEntered");
				if ((_property.RestoreDefaultOnClear ?? false) && (_value == null || _value.Count() == 0))
				{
					Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(RefreshData));
				}
				_previousValue.Clear();
				_dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.ResumeNotifications();
				return;
			}
			if (_childIds != null && !flag3)
			{
				CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
				for (int num4 = 1; num4 < _childIds.Count; num4++)
				{
					if (_value != null && _value.Count > num4)
					{
						cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(text + "." + _childIds[num4], "value", null, null);
					}
				}
				_dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchSet(cxsBatchRequestData);
			}
			bool flag6 = true;
			if (_childIds != null)
			{
				CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData2 = new CxsBatchRequestData();
				if (flag3)
				{
					for (int num5 = _previousValue.Count; num5 < _childIds.Count; num5++)
					{
						if (_value != null && _value.Count > num5)
						{
							cxsBatchRequestData2.AddRequest(text + "." + _childIds[num5], "value", null, _value[num5]);
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					for (int num6 = 0; num6 < _childIds.Count; num6++)
					{
						if (_value != null && _value.Count > num6)
						{
							cxsBatchRequestData2.AddRequest(text + "." + _childIds[num6], "value", null, _value[num6]);
						}
					}
				}
				if (!_dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchSet(cxsBatchRequestData2))
				{
					if (collection.Count > 0)
					{
						foreach (int item3 in collection.Reverse())
						{
							_dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(text, "DeleteChild", (item3 == collection.First()) ? true : false, item3);
						}
					}
					Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(RefreshData));
					_dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.ResumeNotifications();
					return;
				}
			}
			_dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.ResumeNotifications();
			if (flag6)
			{
				_previousValue.Clear();
				foreach (string item4 in _value)
				{
					_previousValue.Add(item4);
				}
				Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(RefreshData));
			}
			OnPropertyChanged("Value");
			OnPropertyChanged("IsEntered");
			if (!flag6)
			{
				OnPropertyChanged("Options");
			}
		}

		private void RefreshOptions(ReadOnlyCollection<string> value, bool notify)
		{
			if (value == _options)
			{
				return;
			}
			ObservableCollection<string> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<string>();
			if (value != null)
			{
				foreach (string item in value)
				{
					observableCollection.Add(item);
				}
			}
			ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string> options = new ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string>(observableCollection);
			_options = options;
			if (notify)
			{
				OnPropertyChanged("Options");
			}
		}

		private void RefreshOptionPrompts(string value, bool notify)
		{
			if (!(value != _optionPromptsString))
			{
				return;
			}
			_optionPromptsString = value;
			Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_optionPromptsString))
			{
				int num = 0;
				string[] array = _optionPromptsString.Split('\n');
				foreach (string value2 in array)
				{
					if (_options != null && _options.Count > num && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_options[num]))
					{
						if (EnsureDataPath().Contains("Pure Databanks.Input.FILE_SYM_NAM"))
						{
							dictionary[_options[num]] = CxvCustomTooltipHelper.LocalizationLDBToolTip(_options[num]);
						}
						else
						{
							dictionary[_options[num]] = value2;
						}
					}
					num++;
				}
			}
			_optionPrompts = dictionary;
			if (notify)
			{
				OnPropertyChanged("OptionPrompts");
			}
		}

		public void OnNewItem()
		{
			if (!_canDefine.HasValue || !_canDefine.Value)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_inNew)
			{
				_inNew = false;
				return;
			}
			_inNew = true;
			ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = _dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
			if (cxsDataWatch == null)
			{
				_inNew = false;
				return;
			}
			cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(_dataPathResolved, "default_name");
			cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(_dataPathResolved, "create_id_string");
			object[] array = cxsDataWatch.Values;
			if (array == null)
			{
				array = new object[2]
				{
					string.Empty,
					string.Empty
				};
			}
			if (1 == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			GetUserInputEventArgs e = new GetUserInputEventArgs();
			e.Title = _property.CreateNewTitleLocalized;
			if (e.Title == null)
			{
				e.Title = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, Resources.CreateNewNameTitle, "Item");
			}
			e.Message = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[1]);
			if (e.Message == null || e.Message.Length == 0)
			{
				e.Message = _property.CreateNewPromptLocalized;
				if (e.Message == null)
				{
					e.Message = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, Resources.CreateNewNamePrompt, "item");
				}
			}
			e.InputValue = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[0]);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.InputValue as string))
			{
				string createNewPrefix = _property.CreateNewPrefix;
				if (createNewPrefix != null)
				{
					int num = 1;
					string text = createNewPrefix + num.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
					while (_options != null && _options.Contains(text))
					{
						num++;
						text = createNewPrefix + num.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
					}
					e.InputValue = text;
				}
			}
			e.InputMask = 2;
			_dataContainer.GetUserInput(e);
			if (!e.DialogResult.HasValue || !e.DialogResult.Value)
			{
				CancelEdit();
				_inNew = false;
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				_value.Add(CxsConverter.ValueToString(e.InputValue.ToString().ToUpper(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture)));
			}
			catch (CxsException)
			{
			}
			RefreshData();
		}

		private string EnsureDataPath()
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dataPathResolved))
			{
				ResolveDataPath();
			}
			return _dataPathResolved;
		}

		private void ResolveDataPath()
		{
			if (!IsUnloaded)
			{
				UpdateDataPathDependencies(null);
				CxvPropertyWatch cxvPropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.DataPath))
				{
					_dataPathResolved = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.DataPath, cxvPropertyWatch);
				}
				UpdateDataPathDependencies(cxvPropertyWatch);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateDataPathDependencies(CxvPropertyWatch dataPathDependencies)
		{
			if (dataPathDependencies == _dataPathDependencies)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_dataPathDependencies != null)
			{
				if (_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler != null)
				{
					_dataPathDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= _pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				}
				_dataPathDependencies.Clear();
				_dataPathDependencies = null;
			}
			if (dataPathDependencies == null || dataPathDependencies.IsEmpty)
			{
				return;
			}
			_dataPathDependencies = dataPathDependencies;
			if (_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler == null)
			{
				_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler = delegate
				{
					ResetDataPath();
				};
			}
			_dataPathDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += _pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
		}

		private void ResetDataPath()
		{
			UpdateDataPathDependencies(null);
			_dataPathResolved = null;
			UpdateWatch(null);
			RefreshData();
			OnPropertyChanged("CxsItemMoniker");
		}

		public void Unload()
		{
			_property = null;
			_dataContainer = null;
			UpdateDataPathDependencies(null);
			if (_watch != null)
			{
				_watch.Unload();
			}
		}

		public void BeginEdit()
		{
			_inEditMode = true;
			_itemsChanged = false;
		}

		public void CancelEdit()
		{
			if (!_itemsChanged)
			{
				_value.CollectionChanged -= _value_CollectionChanged;
				_value.Clear();
				foreach (string item in _previousValue)
				{
					_value.Add(item);
				}
				_value.CollectionChanged += _value_CollectionChanged;
			}
			_itemsChanged = false;
			_inEditMode = false;
		}

		public void EndEdit()
		{
			if (_itemsChanged)
			{
				OnSetValue();
			}
			_itemsChanged = false;
			_inEditMode = false;
		}

		XElement IViewDataSerializable.WriteXElement()
		{
			List<XElement> list = new List<XElement>();
			ObservableCollection<string> value = Value;
			if (value != null)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("Value", value.Select((string content) => new XElement("string", content)), new XAttribute("Count", value.Count)));
			}
			ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string> options = Options;
			if (options != null)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("Options", options.Select((string option) => new XElement("string", option)), new XAttribute("Count", options.Count)));
			}
			Dictionary<string, string> optionPrompts = OptionPrompts;
			if (optionPrompts != null)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("OptionPrompts", optionPrompts.Select((KeyValuePair<string, string> prompt) => new XElement("string", prompt)), new XAttribute("Count", optionPrompts.Count)));
			}
			bool? isReadOnly = IsReadOnly;
			bool? isCalculated = IsCalculated;
			bool? isEntered = IsEntered;
			bool? canReorder = CanReorder;
			bool? canDefine = CanDefine;
			if (isReadOnly.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("IsReadOnly", isReadOnly.Value));
			}
			if (isEntered.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("IsEntered", isEntered.Value));
			}
			if (isCalculated.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("IsCalculated", isCalculated.Value));
			}
			if (canReorder.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("CanReorder", canReorder.Value));
			}
			if (canDefine.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("CanDefine", canDefine.Value));
			}
			if (list.Count <= 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return new XElement(GetType().Name, list);
		}
	}
	public class CxvLogicalPropertyData : CxvPropertyData, IViewDataSerializable
	{
		private EventHandler _dependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;

		private bool? _value;

		private CxvPropertyWatch _dependencies;

		private ICxvViewDataContainer _dataContainer;

		private CxvLogicalTestCollection _tests;

		private CxvLogicalOperator _unionOperator;

		public bool Value
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_value.HasValue)
				{
					Evaluate();
				}
				if (!_value.HasValue)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return _value.Value;
			}
		}

		public Visibility ValueAsVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				if (!Value)
				{
					return Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				return Visibility.Visible;
			}
		}

		private EventHandler dependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (_dependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler == null)
				{
					_dependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler = delegate
					{
						ResetValue();
					};
				}
				return _dependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		public CxvLogicalPropertyData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyBase property, CxvLogicalTestCollection tests, CxvLogicalOperator unionOperator)
			: base(dataContainer, property)
		{
			_dataContainer = dataContainer;
			_tests = tests;
			_unionOperator = unionOperator;
		}

		private void Evaluate()
		{
			CxvPropertyWatch cxvPropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
			_value = _tests.EvaluateConditions(_unionOperator, _dataContainer, cxvPropertyWatch);
			UpdateDependencies(cxvPropertyWatch);
		}

		private void ResetValue()
		{
			_value = null;
			OnPropertyChanged("Value");
			OnPropertyChanged("ValueAsVisibility");
		}

		private void UpdateDependencies(CxvPropertyWatch dep)
		{
			if (_dependencies != null)
			{
				_dependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= dependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
			if (dep != null && !dep.IsEmpty)
			{
				_dependencies = dep;
				_dependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += dependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		XElement IViewDataSerializable.WriteXElement()
		{
			return new XElement(GetType().Name, new XElement("Value", Value), new XElement("ValueAsVisibility", ValueAsVisibility));
		}
	}

		private bool? _IsEntered;

				return _value;

			get

				return _IsVisible;
			}
			set

				if (value != _IsVisible)
				{
					_IsVisible = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("IsVisible");
				}
			}
		}

		public bool? IsEntered
		{
			get

	public class CxvMathPropertyData : CxvPropertyData, IViewDataSerializable
	{
		private readonly ICxvViewDataContainer _dataContainer;

		private readonly CxvMathProperty _cxvProperty;

		private CxvPropertyWatch _dependencies;

		private double? _value;

		private EventHandler _dependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;

		public double Value
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_value.HasValue)
				{
					Evaluate();
				}
				if (_value.HasValue)
				{
					return _value.Value;
				}
				return 0.0;
			}
		}

		private EventHandler dependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (_dependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler == null)
				{
					_dependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler = delegate
					{
						ResetValue();
					};
				}
				return _dependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		public CxvMathPropertyData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvMathProperty cxvProperty)
			: base(dataContainer, cxvProperty)
		{
			if (dataContainer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("dataContainer");
			}
			if (cxvProperty == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("cxvProperty");
			}
			_dataContainer = dataContainer;
			_cxvProperty = cxvProperty;
		}

		private void Evaluate()
		{
			CxvPropertyWatch dependencies = new CxvPropertyWatch();
			_value = ResolveValue(_cxvProperty.Operand, dependencies);
			UpdateDependencies(dependencies);
		}

		private double ResolveValue(CxvMathOperand cxvMathOperand, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			return cxvMathOperand?.ResolveValue(_dataContainer, dependencies) ?? 0.0;
		}

		private void UpdateDependencies(CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (_dependencies != null)
			{
				_dependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= dependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
			if (dependencies != null)
			{
				_dependencies = dependencies;
				_dependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += dependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		private void ResetValue()
		{
			_value = null;
			OnPropertyChanged("Value");
		}

		XElement IViewDataSerializable.WriteXElement()
		{
			return new XElement(GetType().Name, new XElement("Value", Value));
		}
	}
	public class CxvParameterIndexBasisData : CxvIndexBasisBaseData
	{
		private int? _count;

		private ICxvViewDataContainer _dataContainer;

		private CxvParameterIndexBasis _indexBasis;

		private EventHandler _parameterDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;

		private CxvPropertyWatch _parameterDependencies;

		public override int Count
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureCount();
				if (!_count.HasValue)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				return _count.Value;
			}
		}

		protected ICxvViewDataContainer DataContainer
		{
			get
			{
				return _dataContainer;
			}
			set
			{
				_dataContainer = value;
			}
		}

		protected CxvParameterIndexBasis IndexBasis
		{
			get
			{
				return _indexBasis;
			}
			set
			{
				_indexBasis = value;
			}
		}

		private EventHandler childIdDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (_parameterDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler == null)
				{
					_parameterDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler = delegate
					{
						ResetCount();
					};
				}
				return _parameterDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		public CxvParameterIndexBasisData(CxvParameterIndexBasis indexBasis, ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer)
		{
			_indexBasis = indexBasis;
			_dataContainer = dataContainer;
		}

		public override object GetIndexSubstitution(int index, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			dependencies?.AddWatch(this, "Count");
			return "#" + index.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
		}

		private void EnsureCount()
		{
			if (!_count.HasValue)
			{
				if (_indexBasis == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(_indexBasis.Parameter))
				{
					_count = 0;
					return;
				}
				UpdateParameterDependencies(null);
				CxvPropertyWatch dependencies = new CxvPropertyWatch();
				object value = _dataContainer.ResolveParameter(_indexBasis.Parameter, dependencies);
				_count = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(value);
				UpdateParameterDependencies(dependencies);
			}
		}

		private void ResetCount()
		{
			_count = null;
			UpdateParameterDependencies(null);
			OnCountChanged();
		}

		public override void Unload()
		{
			base.Unload();
			if (_dataContainer != null)
			{
				UpdateParameterDependencies(null);
				_dataContainer = null;
			}
		}

		protected void UpdateParameterDependencies(CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (_parameterDependencies != null)
			{
				_parameterDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= childIdDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				_parameterDependencies = null;
			}
			if (dependencies != null && !dependencies.IsEmpty)
			{
				_parameterDependencies = dependencies;
				_parameterDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += childIdDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
		}
	}
	public class CxvParameterPropertyData : CxvPropertyData, IViewDataSerializable, IQuickValueReadable, IQuickValueEditable
	{
		private bool? _isEntered;

		private EventHandler _dependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;

		private ICxvViewDataContainer _dataContainer;

		private CxvParameterProperty _property;

		private object _value;

		private CxvPropertyWatch _dependencies;

		public object Value
		{
			get
			{
				if (_value == DBNull.Value)
				{
					RefreshValue();
				}
				if (_property.Converter != null)
				{
					IValueConverter valueConverter = _property.ValueConverter(_dataContainer);
					if (valueConverter != null)
					{
						return valueConverter.Convert(_value, typeof(object), null, null);
					}
				}
				if (_property.ValueType == typeof(int))
				{
					return CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(_value);
				}
				return _value;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_property.Converter != null)
				{
					IValueConverter valueConverter = _property.ValueConverter(_dataContainer);
					if (valueConverter != null)
					{
						_property.SetParametricValue(_dataContainer, valueConverter.ConvertBack(value, typeof(object), null, null));
						return;
					}
				}
				_property.SetParametricValue(_dataContainer, value);
			}
		}

		public Type ValueType
		{
			get
			{
				Type type = _property.ValueType;
				if (type == null)
				{
					object value = Value;
					type = ((value != null) ? value.GetType() : typeof(object));
				}
				return type;
			}
		}

		public bool IsReadOnly
		{
			get
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.IsReadOnlyParamter) || !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.IsReadOnlyPropertyPath))
				{
					CxvPropertyWatch dependencies = new CxvPropertyWatch();
					return _property.GetParametricIsReadOnly(_dataContainer, dependencies);
				}
				return _property.IsReadOnly;
			}
		}

		public bool IsCalculated => _property.IsCalculated;

		public bool? IsEntered
		{
			get
			{
				if (_isEntered.HasValue)
				{
					return _isEntered;
				}
				return _property.IsEntered;
			}
			set
			{
				_isEntered = value;
			}
		}

		private EventHandler dependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (_dependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler == null)
				{
					_dependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler = delegate
					{
						ResetValue();
					};
				}
				return _dependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		public string VariablePrompt => _property.ToolTipText;

		public object QuickValue => Value;

		public CxvParameterPropertyData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvParameterProperty property)
			: base(dataContainer, property)
		{
			_dataContainer = dataContainer;
			_property = property;
			_value = DBNull.Value;
		}

		private void RefreshValue()
		{
			CxvPropertyWatch dependencies = new CxvPropertyWatch();
			_value = _property.GetParametricValue(_dataContainer, dependencies);
			UpdateDependencies(dependencies);
		}

		private void ResetValue()
		{
			_value = DBNull.Value;
			OnPropertyChanged("Value");
			OnPropertyChanged("QuickValue");
		}

		private void UpdateDependencies(CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (_dependencies != null)
			{
				_dependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= dependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				_dependencies = null;
			}
			if (dependencies != null && !dependencies.IsEmpty)
			{
				_dependencies = dependencies;
				_dependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += dependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		XElement IViewDataSerializable.WriteXElement()
		{
			List<XElement> list = new List<XElement>();
			object value = Value;
			Type valueType = ValueType;
			bool isReadOnly = IsReadOnly;
			if (value != null)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("Value", value.ToString()));
			}
			if (valueType != null)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("ValueType", valueType));
			}
			list.Add(new XElement("IsReadOnly", isReadOnly));
			return new XElement(GetType().Name, list);
		}

		object IQuickValueReadable.GetQuickValue(out bool? canEnter, out bool? isDefaulted)
		{
			canEnter = (isDefaulted = null);
			return Value;
		}

		void IQuickValueEditable.QuickUpdate(object value)
		{
			Value = value;
		}
	}
	public class CxvMultiMapPropertyData : CxvStatePropertyData, IViewDataSerializable, ICxvPropertyData, IQuickValueReadable, IQuickValueEditable
	{
		private readonly Dictionary<string, string> _optionPrompts = new Dictionary<string, string>();

		private readonly Dictionary<string, string> _variablePrompts = new Dictionary<string, string>();

		private bool? _isEntered;

		private bool? _isCalculated;

		private EventHandler _selectedItemDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;

		private bool? _isReadOnly;

		private ICxsDataWatch _readOnlyDataWatch;

		private EventHandler _isReadOnlyDependencies_EventHandler;

		private CxvPropertyWatch _isReadOnlyDependencies;

		private ICxvViewDataContainer _dataContainer;

		private CxvMultiMapProperty _mapProperty;

		private CxvMultiMapOption _selectedItem;

		private ObservableCollection<string> _items;

		private CxvPropertyWatch _selectedItemDependencies;

		private bool _inNotify;

		public string SelectedItem
		{
			get
			{
				if (_dataContainer == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				if (_selectedItem == null)
				{
					RefreshSelectedItem();
				}
				if (_selectedItem == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _selectedItem.DisplayLocalized;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_dataContainer == null || _inNotify)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (value == null && _selectedItem != null)
				{
					OnResetVariables();
				}
				else if (value != ((_selectedItem != null) ? _selectedItem.DisplayLocalized : null))
				{
					CxvMultiMapOption cxvMultiMapOption = _mapProperty.FindOptionByDisplayName(value);
					if (cxvMultiMapOption != _selectedItem)
					{
						OnSelectedItemChanged(cxvMultiMapOption, resetOnFailure: true);
					}
				}
				if (value != ((_selectedItem != null) ? _selectedItem.DisplayLocalized : null))
				{
					Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action<string>(OnPropertyChanged), "SelectedItem");
				}
			}
		}

		public object Value
		{
			get
			{
				if (_selectedItem == null)
				{
					RefreshSelectedItem();
				}
				if (_selectedItem == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _selectedItem.Value;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == null && _selectedItem != null)
				{
					OnResetVariables();
				}
				else if (_selectedItem == null || value != _selectedItem.Value)
				{
					CxvMultiMapOption cxvMultiMapOption = _mapProperty.FindOptionByValue(value);
					if (cxvMultiMapOption != _selectedItem)
					{
						OnSelectedItemChanged(cxvMultiMapOption, resetOnFailure: true);
					}
					if (_selectedItem == null || !CxvHelpers.ValuesEquivalent(_selectedItem.Value, value))
					{
						Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action<string>(OnPropertyChanged), "Value");
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public override string VariablePrompt
		{
			get
			{
				if (_mapProperty == null)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_mapProperty.PromptLocalized))
				{
					return _mapProperty.PromptLocalized;
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_mapProperty.ToolTipTextLocalized))
				{
					return _mapProperty.ToolTipTextLocalized;
				}
				if (_selectedItem != null)
				{
					string displayLocalized = _selectedItem.DisplayLocalized;
					if (_optionPrompts.ContainsKey(displayLocalized))
					{
						return _optionPrompts[displayLocalized];
					}
					if (_selectedItem.Values.Count == 1 && _variablePrompts.ContainsKey(_selectedItem.Values[0].Variable))
					{
						return _variablePrompts[_selectedItem.Values[0].Variable];
					}
				}
				else if (_variablePrompts != null && _variablePrompts.Count == 1)
				{
					return _variablePrompts.ElementAt(0).Value;
				}
				return string.Empty;
			}
		}

		public Dictionary<string, string> OptionPrompts => _optionPrompts;

		public bool IsReadOnly => EnsureIsReadOnly();

		public bool? IsEntered
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureIsReadOnly();
				return _isEntered;
			}
		}

		public bool? IsCalculated
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureIsReadOnly();
				return _isCalculated;
			}
		}

		public ObservableCollection<string> Items
		{
			get
			{
				InitializeItemsCollection();
				return _items;
			}
		}

		public ObservableCollection<string> ValueOptions => _items;

		private EventHandler selectedItemDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (_selectedItemDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler == null)
				{
					_selectedItemDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler = delegate
					{
						RefreshSelectedItem();
					};
				}
				return _selectedItemDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		public string CxsItemMoniker
		{
			get
			{
				if (_mapProperty != null && _mapProperty.Variables.Count > 0)
				{
					return CxvObservableObject.CxsPathToMoniker(_dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_mapProperty.Variables[0].DataPath));
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		public bool IsUnloaded => _dataContainer == null;

		public object QuickValue => SelectedItem;

		public CxvMultiMapPropertyData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvMultiMapProperty mapProperty)
			: base(dataContainer, mapProperty)
		{
			_dataContainer = dataContainer;
			_mapProperty = mapProperty;
		}

		protected override void OnPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			_inNotify = true;
			base.OnPropertyChanged(propertyName);
			if (propertyName == "SelectedItem")
			{
				OnPropertyChanged("VariablePrompt");
			}
			if (propertyName == "Items")
			{
				OnPropertyChanged("OptionPrompts");
			}
			_inNotify = false;
		}

		private void RefreshSelectedItem()
		{
			if (_mapProperty == null || _mapProperty.Variables.Count == 0 || _mapProperty.Options.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_selectedItemDependencies != null)
			{
				_selectedItemDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= selectedItemDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
			if (_selectedItemDependencies != null)
			{
				_selectedItemDependencies.Clear();
			}
			else
			{
				_selectedItemDependencies = new CxvPropertyWatch();
			}
			ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = null;
			ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch2 = null;
			List<object> list = new List<object>();
			List<object> list2 = new List<object>();
			foreach (CxvMultiMapVariable variable in _mapProperty.Variables)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(variable.Parameter))
				{
					list.Add(_dataContainer.ResolveParameter(variable.Parameter));
					continue;
				}
				string text = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(variable.DataPath, _selectedItemDependencies);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					if (!_selectedItemDependencies.IsEmpty)
					{
						_selectedItemDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += selectedItemDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
					}
					else
					{
						_selectedItemDependencies = null;
					}
					if (_selectedItem != null)
					{
						_selectedItem = null;
						RaiseSelectedItemPropertyChanged();
					}
					return;
				}
				if (cxsDataWatch == null)
				{
					cxsDataWatch = _dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
				}
				if (cxsDataWatch2 == null)
				{
					cxsDataWatch2 = _dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
				}
				cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "value");
				cxsDataWatch2.AddWatch(text, "description");
			}
			if (cxsDataWatch != null)
			{
				_selectedItemDependencies.AddWatch(cxsDataWatch, "Values");
				_selectedItemDependencies.AddWatch(cxsDataWatch2, "VariablePrompts");
				list.AddRange(cxsDataWatch.Values);
				list2.AddRange(cxsDataWatch2.Values);
			}
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			_variablePrompts.Clear();
			int num = 0;
			foreach (CxvMultiMapVariable variable2 in _mapProperty.Variables)
			{
				dictionary[variable2.Name] = list[num];
				if (list2.Count > num)
				{
					_variablePrompts[variable2.Name] = CxvInternalHelpers.ValueToStringRemoveXml(list2[num]);
				}
				num++;
			}
			CxvMultiMapOption cxvMultiMapOption = null;
			foreach (CxvMultiMapOption option in _mapProperty.Options)
			{
				bool flag = true;
				foreach (CxvMultiMapValue value3 in option.Values)
				{
					object value = value3.Value;
					object value2 = dictionary[value3.Variable];
					if (!CxvHelpers.ValuesEquivalent(value, value2))
					{
						flag = false;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					cxvMultiMapOption = option;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!_selectedItemDependencies.IsEmpty)
			{
				_selectedItemDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += selectedItemDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
			else
			{
				_selectedItemDependencies = null;
			}
			if (cxvMultiMapOption != _selectedItem)
			{
				_selectedItem = cxvMultiMapOption;
				RaiseSelectedItemPropertyChanged();
			}
			InitializeItemsCollection();
		}

		private void UpdatePrompts()
		{
			ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = null;
			List<object> list = new List<object>();
			if (_mapProperty != null)
			{
				foreach (CxvMultiMapVariable variable in _mapProperty.Variables)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(variable.Parameter))
					{
						continue;
					}
					string text = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(variable.DataPath);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						if (cxsDataWatch == null)
						{
							cxsDataWatch = _dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
						}
						cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "description");
					}
				}
			}
			if (cxsDataWatch == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			list.AddRange(cxsDataWatch.Values);
			int num = 0;
			if (_mapProperty == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (CxvMultiMapVariable variable2 in _mapProperty.Variables)
			{
				if (list.Count > num)
				{
					_variablePrompts[variable2.Name] = CxvInternalHelpers.ValueToStringRemoveXml(list[num]);
				}
				num++;
			}
		}

		private void OnSelectedItemChanged(CxvMultiMapOption newSelecton, bool resetOnFailure)
		{
			if (newSelecton == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxvMultiMapOption selectedItem = _selectedItem;
			foreach (var item in GetValuesToChange(selectedItem, newSelecton))
			{
				if (ShouldCancelPropertyChanging(item.Variable, item.OldValue, item.NewValue))
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData2 = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			foreach (CxvMultiMapValue value2 in newSelecton.Values)
			{
				CxvMultiMapVariable cxvMultiMapVariable = _mapProperty.FindVariable(value2.Variable);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cxvMultiMapVariable.Parameter))
				{
					_dataContainer.ResolveUpdateParameter(cxvMultiMapVariable.Parameter, value2.Value);
					continue;
				}
				string text = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(cxvMultiMapVariable.DataPath, null);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					if (value2 == newSelecton.Values.Last())
					{
						cxsBatchRequestData2.AddRequest(text, "value", null, value2.Value);
					}
					else
					{
						cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(text, "value", null, value2.Value);
					}
				}
			}
			bool flag = true;
			if (_selectedItemDependencies != null)
			{
				_selectedItemDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= selectedItemDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
			try
			{
				if (cxsBatchRequestData.RequestsCount > 0)
				{
					CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData3 = new CxsBatchRequestData();
					cxsBatchRequestData3.AddRequest(cxsBatchRequestData.CommonBasePath, "ignore_errors", null, 1);
					cxsBatchRequestData3.AddRequests(cxsBatchRequestData.Requests);
					flag = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchSet(cxsBatchRequestData3);
				}
				if (cxsBatchRequestData2.RequestsCount > 0)
				{
					bool flag2 = false;
					if (flag)
					{
						if (!resetOnFailure && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(cxsBatchRequestData2.CommonBasePath))
						{
							object obj = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData2.CommonBasePath, "value", null);
							object value = cxsBatchRequestData2.Requests[0].Value;
							if (obj != null && obj.ToString() == value.ToString())
							{
								flag2 = true;
							}
						}
						if (!flag2)
						{
							flag = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchSet(cxsBatchRequestData2);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			catch (CxsException)
			{
				flag = false;
			}
			if (_selectedItemDependencies != null)
			{
				_selectedItemDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += selectedItemDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				_selectedItem = newSelecton;
				UpdatePrompts();
				RaiseSelectedItemPropertyChanged();
			}
			else if (resetOnFailure)
			{
				OnSelectedItemChanged(selectedItem, resetOnFailure: false);
			}
		}

		private IEnumerable<(string Variable, object OldValue, object NewValue)> GetValuesToChange(CxvMultiMapOption oldSelection, CxvMultiMapOption newSelection)
		{
			if (oldSelection == null || newSelection == null)
			{
				yield break;
			}
			foreach (CxvMultiMapValue oldValue in oldSelection.Values)
			{
				CxvMultiMapValue cxvMultiMapValue = newSelection.Values.FirstOrDefault((CxvMultiMapValue x) => x.Variable == oldValue.Variable);
				yield return (Variable: oldValue.Variable, OldValue: oldValue.Value, NewValue: cxvMultiMapValue?.Value);
			}
		}

		private void OnResetVariables()
		{
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			foreach (CxvMultiMapVariable variable in _mapProperty.Variables)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(variable.Parameter))
				{
					string text = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(variable.DataPath, null);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(text, "value", null, null);
					}
				}
			}
			try
			{
				_dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchSet(cxsBatchRequestData);
			}
			catch (CxsException)
			{
			}
			_selectedItem = null;
			RaiseSelectedItemPropertyChanged();
			ResetIsReadOnly();
		}

		private void InitializeItemsCollection()
		{
			if (_items != null && _items.Count == _mapProperty.Options.Count)
			{
				return;
			}
			ObservableCollection<string> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<string>();
			foreach (CxvMultiMapOption option in _mapProperty.Options)
			{
				observableCollection.Add(option.DisplayLocalized);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(option.PromptLocalized))
				{
					_optionPrompts[option.DisplayLocalized] = option.PromptLocalized;
				}
				else
				{
					if (option.Values.Count <= 0)
					{
						continue;
					}
					CxvMultiMapVariable cxvMultiMapVariable = _mapProperty.FindVariable(option.Values[0].Variable);
					string path = base.DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(cxvMultiMapVariable.DataPath);
					string value = string.Empty;
					string text = option.Values[0].Value as string;
					string resourceOptionKey = CxvInternalHelpers.GetResourceOptionKey(_dataContainer, path, text);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(resourceOptionKey))
					{
						value = CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeOptionPrompt(_dataContainer, text, resourceOptionKey + "_prompt", "ComboItemPrompt");
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
					{
						_optionPrompts[option.DisplayLocalized] = value;
						continue;
					}
					string[] array = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(ReadData(cxvMultiMapVariable.DataPath, "option_list", null));
					string[] array2 = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(ReadData(cxvMultiMapVariable.DataPath, "option_prompts", null));
					if (array2 != null && array2.Length > array.Length && string.IsNullOrEmpty(array2[0]))
					{
						List<string> list = new List<string>(array2);
						list.RemoveAt(0);
						array2 = list.ToArray();
					}
					if (array != null && array2 != null && array.Contains(text) && array.Length == array2.Length)
					{
						for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
						{
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(array[i]) && array[i].Equals(text))
							{
								value = array2[i];
							}
						}
					}
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
					{
						string resourceOptionKey2 = CxvInternalHelpers.GetResourceOptionKey(_dataContainer, path);
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(resourceOptionKey2))
						{
							value = CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeOptionPrompt(_dataContainer, string.Empty, resourceOptionKey2 + "_prompt", "ComboItemPrompt");
						}
					}
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
					{
						value = CxsConverter.ValueToString(ReadData(cxvMultiMapVariable.DataPath, "description", null));
					}
					_optionPrompts[option.DisplayLocalized] = value;
				}
			}
			_items = observableCollection;
			OnPropertyChanged("Items");
			OnPropertyChanged("ValueOptions");
		}

		private void RaiseSelectedItemPropertyChanged()
		{
			OnPropertyChanged("SelectedItem");
			OnPropertyChanged("Value");
			OnPropertyChanged("QuickValue");
		}

		private bool EnsureIsReadOnly()
		{
			if (!_isReadOnly.HasValue)
			{
				RefreshIsReadOnly();
			}
			if (!_isReadOnly.HasValue)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return _isReadOnly.Value;
		}

		private void RefreshIsReadOnly()
		{
			if (_mapProperty == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxvPropertyWatch dependencies = new CxvPropertyWatch();
			ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = null;
			foreach (CxvMultiMapVariable variable in _mapProperty.Variables)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(variable.Parameter))
				{
					string text = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(variable.DataPath, dependencies);
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						break;
					}
					if (cxsDataWatch == null)
					{
						cxsDataWatch = _dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
					}
					cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "can_enter");
					cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "user_entered");
					cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "calculated");
				}
			}
			UpdateIsReadOnlyDataWatch(cxsDataWatch);
			if (cxsDataWatch == null)
			{
				_isReadOnly = false;
				_isEntered = null;
				_isCalculated = null;
				UpdateIsReadOnlyDependencies(dependencies);
				return;
			}
			object[] values = cxsDataWatch.Values;
			if (values == null)
			{
				_isReadOnly = true;
				_isEntered = null;
				_isCalculated = null;
			}
			else
			{
				_isReadOnly = true;
				_isEntered = null;
				_isCalculated = null;
				for (int i = 0; i < values.Length; i += 3)
				{
					bool? flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(values[i]);
					if (flag.HasValue && flag.Value)
					{
						_isReadOnly = false;
					}
					bool? flag2 = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(values[i + 1]);
					if (flag2.HasValue)
					{
						if (flag2.Value)
						{
							_isEntered = true;
						}
						else if (!_isEntered.HasValue)
						{
							_isEntered = false;
						}
					}
					bool? flag3 = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(values[i + 2]);
					if (flag3.HasValue)
					{
						if (!flag3.Value)
						{
							_isCalculated = false;
						}
						else if (!_isCalculated.HasValue)
						{
							_isCalculated = true;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (_mapProperty.IsReadOnly.HasValue)
			{
				_isReadOnly = _mapProperty.IsReadOnly.Value;
			}
			else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_mapProperty.IsReadOnlyPropertyPath))
			{
				object value = _dataContainer.ResolveViewPropertyPath(_mapProperty.IsReadOnlyPropertyPath, dependencies);
				_isReadOnly = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(value);
			}
			UpdateIsReadOnlyDependencies(dependencies);
		}

		private void UpdateIsReadOnlyDataWatch(ICxsDataWatch dataWatch)
		{
			if (dataWatch == _readOnlyDataWatch)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_readOnlyDataWatch != null)
			{
				if (_isReadOnlyDependencies_EventHandler != null)
				{
					_readOnlyDataWatch.ValuesChanged -= _isReadOnlyDependencies_EventHandler;
				}
				_readOnlyDataWatch.Unload();
				_readOnlyDataWatch = null;
			}
			if (dataWatch == null || dataWatch.IsEmpty)
			{
				return;
			}
			_readOnlyDataWatch = dataWatch;
			if (_isReadOnlyDependencies_EventHandler == null)
			{
				_isReadOnlyDependencies_EventHandler = delegate
				{
					ResetIsReadOnly();
				};
			}
			dataWatch.ValuesChanged += _isReadOnlyDependencies_EventHandler;
		}

		private void UpdateIsReadOnlyDependencies(CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (_isReadOnlyDependencies != null)
			{
				if (_isReadOnlyDependencies_EventHandler != null)
				{
					_isReadOnlyDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= _isReadOnlyDependencies_EventHandler;
				}
				_isReadOnlyDependencies = null;
			}
			if (dependencies == null || dependencies.IsEmpty)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_isReadOnlyDependencies_EventHandler == null)
			{
				_isReadOnlyDependencies_EventHandler = delegate
				{
					ResetIsReadOnly();
				};
			}
			_isReadOnlyDependencies = dependencies;
			_isReadOnlyDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += _isReadOnlyDependencies_EventHandler;
		}

		private void ResetIsReadOnly()
		{
			UpdateIsReadOnlyDependencies(null);
			UpdateIsReadOnlyDataWatch(null);
			_isReadOnly = null;
			OnPropertyChanged("IsReadOnly");
			OnPropertyChanged("IsCalculated");
			OnPropertyChanged("IsEntered");
		}

		XElement IViewDataSerializable.WriteXElement()
		{
			List<XElement> list = new List<XElement>();
			string selectedItem = SelectedItem;
			object value = Value;
			if (value != null)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("Value", value.ToString()));
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(selectedItem))
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("SelectedItem", selectedItem));
			}
			ObservableCollection<string> items = Items;
			if (items != null)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("Items", items.Select((string item) => new XElement("string", item)), new XAttribute("Count", items.Count)));
			}
			Dictionary<string, string> optionPrompts = OptionPrompts;
			if (optionPrompts != null)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("OptionPrompts", optionPrompts.Select(delegate(KeyValuePair<string, string> item)
				{
					XName name = "string";
					KeyValuePair<string, string> keyValuePair = item;
					string key = keyValuePair.Key;
					keyValuePair = item;
					return new XElement(name, key + ", " + keyValuePair.Value);
				}), new XAttribute("Count", optionPrompts.Count)));
			}
			bool isReadOnly = IsReadOnly;
			bool? isCalculated = IsCalculated;
			bool? isEntered = IsEntered;
			list.Add(new XElement("IsReadOnly", isReadOnly));
			if (isEntered.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("IsEntered", isEntered.Value));
			}
			if (isCalculated.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("IsCalculated", isCalculated.Value));
			}
			return new XElement(GetType().Name, list);
		}

		public void Unload()
		{
			if (_dataContainer != null)
			{
				UpdateIsReadOnlyDependencies(null);
				if (_selectedItemDependencies != null)
				{
					_selectedItemDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= selectedItemDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
					_selectedItemDependencies.Clear();
					_selectedItemDependencies = null;
				}
				_mapProperty = null;
				_dataContainer = null;
			}
		}

		object IQuickValueReadable.GetQuickValue(out bool? canEnter, out bool? isDefaulted)
		{
			canEnter = (isDefaulted = null);
			return SelectedItem;
		}

		void IQuickValueEditable.QuickUpdate(object value)
		{
			SelectedItem = value as string;
		}
	}
	public class CxvFixedCountIndexBasisData : CxvIndexBasisBaseData
	{
		private int _fixedCount;

		private CxvFixedCountIndexBasis _indexBasis;

		public override int Count => _fixedCount;

		public CxvFixedCountIndexBasisData(CxvFixedCountIndexBasis indexBasis)
		{
			_indexBasis = indexBasis;
			_fixedCount = indexBasis?.FixedCount ?? 0;
		}

		public override object GetIndexSubstitution(int index, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (_indexBasis != null)
			{
				if (_indexBasis.UseGradeIndex)
				{
					return "#" + _indexBasis.StartIndex + index;
				}
				return _indexBasis.StartIndex + index;
			}
			return index;
		}
	}
	public abstract class CxvPropertyData : CxvObservableObject, IPropertyData, INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		public ICxvViewDataContainer DataContainer { get; private set; }

		public CxvPropertyBase Property { get; private set; }

		protected CxvPropertyData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyBase property)
		{
			if (dataContainer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("dataContainer");
			}
			if (property == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("property");
			}
			DataContainer = dataContainer;
			Property = property;
		}

		protected virtual bool ShouldCancelPropertyChanging(string propertyName, object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			SubPropertyDataChangingEventArgs e = new SubPropertyDataChangingEventArgs(Property, propertyName, oldValue, newValue);
			DataContainer?.OnSubPropertyDataChanging(e);
			return e.Cancel;
		}

		protected override void OnPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			if (Property.PropertyDeclaration != null)
			{
				DataContainer.OnSubPropertyDataChanged(Property.PropertyDeclaration, propertyName);
			}
			base.OnPropertyChanged(propertyName);
		}

		protected virtual void OnPropertyChangedAccordingly(string propertyName, bool changeSucceeded)
		{
			if (changeSucceeded)
			{
				OnPropertyChanged(propertyName);
				return;
			}
			Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action<string>(OnPropertyChanged), propertyName);
		}

		protected object GetPropertyWatchedValue(string watchedProperty, Func<CxvPropertyWatch, object> propertyValueCallback)
		{
			return CxvPropertyValueResolver.ResolvePropertyValue(this, watchedProperty, propertyValueCallback);
		}

		protected void UpdatePropertyWatchedValue(string watchedProperty)
		{
			CxvPropertyValueResolver.UpdatePropertyValue(this, watchedProperty);
		}

		protected object GetDataAttributeValue(string watchedProperty, string dataPath, string datAttribute)
		{
			return CxvDataAttributeResolver.ResolvePropertyValue(this, watchedProperty, DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(dataPath), datAttribute);
		}

		protected void UpdateDataAttributeValue(string watchedProperty)
		{
			CxvDataAttributeResolver.UpdatePropertyValue(this, watchedProperty);
		}

		public object ReadData(string dataPath, string dataAttribute, string units)
		{
			string text = DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(dataPath);
			if (text != null)
			{
				return DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(text, dataAttribute, units);
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void SaveData(string dataPath, string dataAttribute, string units, object value)
		{
			if (DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(dataPath) != null)
			{
				CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
				cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(dataPath), dataAttribute, units, value);
				DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchSet(cxsBatchRequestData);
			}
		}

		public void AddChild(string dataPath, int index, object value)
		{
			string text = DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(dataPath);
			try
			{
				DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(text, "InsertChild", true, index);
				SaveData(text + ".#" + index.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture), "value", null, value);
			}
			catch (CxsException)
			{
			}
		}

		public void DeleteChild(string dataPath, int index)
		{
			DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.InvokeAction(DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(dataPath), "DeleteChild", true, index);
		}
	}
	public interface IStateData
	{
		bool? IsEnabled { get; }

		bool? IsVisible { get; }

		string DisabledPrompt { get; }

		string VariablePrompt { get; }
	}
	public abstract class CxvStatePropertyData : CxvPropertyData, IStateData, IStatePropertyData, IPropertyData, INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private readonly CxvStatePropertyBase _stateProperty;

		private bool? _IsEnabled;

		private string _disabledPrompt;

		private ICxsDataWatch _disabledValue;

		public bool? IsEnabled
		{
			get
			{
				if (_IsEnabled.HasValue)
				{
					return _IsEnabled;
				}
				return GetPropertyWatchedValue("IsEnabled", GetIsEnabledValue) as bool?;
			}
			set
			{
				_IsEnabled = value;
			}
		}

		public bool? IsVisible => GetPropertyWatchedValue("IsVisible", GetIsVisibleValue) as bool?;

		public virtual string DisabledPrompt
		{
			get
			{
				if (_stateProperty.DisabledPrompt != null)
				{
					return _stateProperty.DisabledPrompt;
				}
				if (_stateProperty.IsEnabledCondition != null)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_stateProperty.IsEnabledCondition.DataPath))
					{
						if (_disabledPrompt == null)
						{
							_disabledPrompt = CxvInternalHelpers.ValueToStringRemoveXml(GetDataAttributeValue("DisabledPrompt", _stateProperty.IsEnabledCondition.DataPath, "description"));
						}
						bool flag = true;
						if (_disabledValue != null)
						{
							_disabledValue.ValuesChanged -= _disabledValue_ValuesChanged;
							_disabledValue.Unload();
							_disabledValue = null;
						}
						if (base.DataContainer != null && base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider != null)
						{
							string text = base.DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_stateProperty.IsEnabledCondition.DataPath);
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
							{
								_disabledValue = base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.CreateWatch();
								_disabledValue.AddWatch(text, "tabstate", "complete");
								_disabledValue.AddWatch(text, "description");
								flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(_disabledValue.Values[0]) ?? true;
								_disabledPrompt = CxsConverter.ValueToString(_disabledValue.Values[1]);
								_disabledValue.ValuesChanged += _disabledValue_ValuesChanged;
							}
						}
						if (!flag)
						{
							return _disabledPrompt;
						}
					}
					else if (_stateProperty.IsEnabledCondition.SubConditions != null)
					{
						if (_disabledValue != null)
						{
							_disabledValue.ValuesChanged -= _disabledValue_ValuesChanged;
							_disabledValue.Unload();
							_disabledValue = null;
						}
						if (base.DataContainer != null && base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider != null)
						{
							_disabledValue = base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.CreateWatch();
							_disabledValue.ValuesChanged += _disabledValue_ValuesChanged;
						}
						for (int i = 0; i < _stateProperty.IsEnabledCondition.SubConditions.Count; i++)
						{
							CxvLogicalTest cxvLogicalTest = _stateProperty.IsEnabledCondition.SubConditions[i];
							if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(cxvLogicalTest.DataPath))
							{
								continue;
							}
							bool flag2 = true;
							if (base.DataContainer != null && base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider != null)
							{
								string text2 = base.DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(cxvLogicalTest.DataPath);
								if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
								{
									_disabledValue.AddWatch(text2, "tabstate", "complete");
									_disabledValue.AddWatch(text2, "description");
									flag2 = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(_disabledValue.Values[i * 2]) ?? true;
									_disabledPrompt = CxsConverter.ValueToString(_disabledValue.Values[i * 2 + 1]);
								}
							}
							if (!flag2 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_disabledPrompt))
							{
								return _disabledPrompt;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		public virtual string VariablePrompt => string.Empty;

		private void _disabledValue_ValuesChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			OnPropertyChanged("DisabledPrompt");
		}

		protected CxvStatePropertyData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvStatePropertyBase property)
			: base(dataContainer, property)
		{
			_stateProperty = property;
		}

		private object GetIsEnabledValue(CxvPropertyWatch watcher)
		{
			CxvLogicalTestCollection cxvLogicalTestCollection = new CxvLogicalTestCollection();
			if (_stateProperty.IsEnabledCondition != null)
			{
				cxvLogicalTestCollection.Add(_stateProperty.IsEnabledCondition);
			}
			return cxvLogicalTestCollection.EvaluateConditions(CxvLogicalOperator.And, base.DataContainer, watcher);
		}

		private object GetIsVisibleValue(CxvPropertyWatch watcher)
		{
			CxvLogicalTestCollection cxvLogicalTestCollection = new CxvLogicalTestCollection();
			if (_stateProperty.IsVisibleCondition != null)
			{
				cxvLogicalTestCollection.Add(_stateProperty.IsVisibleCondition);
			}
			return cxvLogicalTestCollection.EvaluateConditions(CxvLogicalOperator.And, base.DataContainer, watcher);
		}
	}
	public abstract class CxvEnablePromptPropertyData : CxvStatePropertyData
	{
		private readonly CxvStatePropertyBase _property;

		public override string DisabledPrompt
		{
			get
			{
				if (_property.DisabledPrompt != null)
				{
					return _property.DisabledPrompt;
				}
				if (_property.IsEnabledCondition != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.IsEnabledCondition.DataPath))
				{
					return CxvInternalHelpers.ValueToStringRemoveXml(GetDataAttributeValue("DisabledPrompt", _property.IsEnabledCondition.DataPath, "description"));
				}
				return base.DisabledPrompt;
			}
		}

		protected CxvEnablePromptPropertyData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvStatePropertyBase property)
			: base(dataContainer, property)
		{
			_property = property;
		}
	}
	public class CxvReactionListPropertyData : CxvObservableObject, ICxvPropertyData, IViewDataSerializable
	{
		private EventHandler _selectedItemPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;

		private EventHandler _watch_ValuesChangedHandler;

		private EventHandler _pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;

		private ICxvViewDataContainer _dataContainer;

		private CxvReactionListProperty _property;

		private string _dataPathResolved;

		private CxvPropertyWatch _dataPathDependencies;

		private CxvPropertyWatch _selectedItemPropertyWatch;

		private CxvPropertyWatch _filterPropertyWatch;

		private string _selectedItem;

		private ICxsDataWatch _watch;

		private ObservableCollection<string> _allrxnsList = new ObservableCollection<string>();

		private ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string> _options;

		private ObservableCollection<string> _value = new ObservableCollection<string>();

		private ReadOnlyCollection<string> _childIds;

		private bool bInSideRefreshSelItem;

		public ObservableCollection<string> Value
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureData();
				return _value;
			}
		}

		public string SelectedItem
		{
			get
			{
				if (_options == null || _options.Count == 0)
				{
					_selectedItem = null;
				}
				else if (_selectedItem == null)
				{
					RefreshSelectedItem();
				}
				return _selectedItem;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_selectedItem != value)
				{
					_selectedItem = value;
					RefreshSelectedItem();
				}
			}
		}

		public ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string> Items
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureData();
				return _options;
			}
		}

		public string CxsItemMoniker => CxvObservableObject.CxsPathToMoniker(_dataPathResolved);

		public bool? IsReadOnly
		{
			get
			{
				if (_property != null && _property.IsReadOnly.HasValue)
				{
					return _property.IsReadOnly;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private EventHandler selectedItemPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (_selectedItemPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler == null)
				{
					_selectedItemPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler = delegate
					{
						RefreshSelectedItem();
					};
				}
				return _selectedItemPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		public bool IsUnloaded => _dataContainer == null;

		public CxvReactionListPropertyData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataProvider, CxvReactionListProperty property)
		{
			_dataContainer = dataProvider;
			_property = property;
		}

		private void EnsureData()
		{
			if (_watch == null)
			{
				RefreshData();
			}
		}

		private void RefreshSelectedItem()
		{
			if (IsUnloaded || bInSideRefreshSelItem)
			{
				return;
			}
			string selectedItemBinding = _property.SelectedItemBinding;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(selectedItemBinding))
			{
				if (_selectedItemPropertyWatch == null)
				{
					_selectedItemPropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
					_selectedItemPropertyWatch.PropertyDependencyChanged += selectedItemPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				}
				_selectedItemPropertyWatch.Clear();
			}
			string text = null;
			string selectedIntItemValue = CxsConverter.ValueToString(_dataContainer.ResolveViewPropertyPath(selectedItemBinding, _selectedItemPropertyWatch));
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(selectedIntItemValue) && _options != null && _options.Count > 0)
			{
				text = _options.FirstOrDefault((string p) => p.StartsWith(selectedIntItemValue));
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_selectedItem))
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					_selectedItem = text;
					text = null;
				}
				else if (_options != null && _options.Count > 0)
				{
					_selectedItem = _options[0];
				}
			}
			if (!CxvHelpers.ValuesEquivalent(_selectedItem, text))
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(selectedItemBinding) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_selectedItem))
				{
					bInSideRefreshSelItem = true;
					string value = _selectedItem.Substring(0, _selectedItem.IndexOf(")", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
					_dataContainer.ResolveUpdateViewPropertyPath(selectedItemBinding, value);
					bInSideRefreshSelItem = false;
				}
			}
			else if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_selectedItem) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(selectedIntItemValue) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(selectedItemBinding))
			{
				bInSideRefreshSelItem = true;
				_dataContainer.ResolveUpdateViewPropertyPath(selectedItemBinding, null);
				bInSideRefreshSelItem = false;
			}
			OnPropertyChanged("SelectedItem");
		}

		public void RefreshData()
		{
			string text = EnsureDataPath();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return;
			}
			UpdateWatch(null);
			_allrxnsList.Clear();
			if (_dataContainer == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_watch == null)
			{
				ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = _dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
