				if (cxsDataWatch == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "child_ids");
				UpdateWatch(cxsDataWatch);
			}
			object[] array = _watch.Values;
			if (array == null)
			{
				array = new object[4];
			}
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			if (_filterPropertyWatch != null)
			{
				_filterPropertyWatch.PropertyDependencyChanged -= _filterPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChanged;
			}
			if (_filterPropertyWatch != null)
			{
				_filterPropertyWatch.Clear();
			}
			else
			{
				_filterPropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
			}
			_childIds = CxsConverter.ValueToOptionsCollection(array[0]);
			if (_childIds != null)
			{
				string text2 = "@main.Custom.Reactions";
				foreach (string childId in _childIds)
				{
					string text3 = text2;
					string filterDataPath = _property.FilterDataPath;
					CxsDataProvider resolvedCxsProvider = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider;
					int num = 0;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(filterDataPath))
					{
						filterDataPath = filterDataPath + "." + childId;
						if (resolvedCxsProvider != null)
						{
							ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch2 = resolvedCxsProvider.CreateWatch();
							string text4 = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(filterDataPath, _filterPropertyWatch);
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
							{
								cxsDataWatch2.AddWatch(text4, "value", null);
								if (cxsDataWatch2.Values != null)
								{
									_filterPropertyWatch.AddWatch(cxsDataWatch2);
									if (cxsDataWatch2.Values.Length == 1 && cxsDataWatch2.Values[0] != null)
									{
										if (int.TryParse(cxsDataWatch2.Values[0].ToString(), out var result))
										{
											num = result;
										}
									}
									else
									{
										num = 1;
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					else
					{
						num = 1;
					}
					text3 = text3 + "." + childId;
					string path = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(text3, _dataPathDependencies);
					_watch.AddWatch(path, "value");
					object obj = resolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(path, "value", null);
					if (obj != null)
					{
						string text5 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(obj);
						if (_property.ReactionType != CxvReactionListProperty.eReactionType.Forward)
						{
							text5 = text5.Replace(" --> ", " <--> ");
						}
						text5 = childId + ") " + text5;
						_allrxnsList.Add(text5);
						if (num != 0)
						{
							list.Add(text5);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			RefreshOptions(new ReadOnlyCollection<string>(list));
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_selectedItem))
			{
				RefreshSelectedItem();
			}
			else if (!list.Contains(_selectedItem))
			{
				if (_options != null && _options.Count > 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_options[0]))
				{
					bInSideRefreshSelItem = true;
					_selectedItem = null;
					_dataContainer.ResolveUpdateViewPropertyPath(_property.SelectedItemBinding, _options[0].Substring(0, _options[0].IndexOf(')')));
					bInSideRefreshSelItem = false;
				}
				RefreshSelectedItem();
			}
			if (!_filterPropertyWatch.IsEmpty)
			{
				_filterPropertyWatch.PropertyDependencyChanged += _filterPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChanged;
			}
			else
			{
				_filterPropertyWatch = null;
			}
		}

		private void _filterPropertyWatch_PropertyDependencyChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			RefreshData();
		}

		private void UpdateWatch(ICxsDataWatch watch)
		{
			if (_watch != null)
			{
				if (_watch_ValuesChangedHandler != null)
				{
					_watch.ValuesChanged -= _watch_ValuesChangedHandler;
				}
				_watch.Unload();
			}
			_watch = watch;
			if (_watch != null)
			{
				_watch_ValuesChangedHandler = delegate
				{
					RefreshData();
				};
				_watch.ValuesChanged += _watch_ValuesChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		private void RefreshOptions(ReadOnlyCollection<string> value)
		{
			if (value == _options)
			{
				return;
			}
			ObservableCollection<string> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<string>();
			foreach (string item in value)
			{
				observableCollection.Add(item);
			}
			ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string> options = new ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string>(observableCollection);
			_options = options;
			OnPropertyChanged("Items");
		}

		private string EnsureDataPath()
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dataPathResolved))
			{
				ResolveDataPath();
			}
			return _dataPathResolved;
		}

		private void ResolveDataPath()
		{
			if (!IsUnloaded)
			{
				UpdateDataPathDependencies(null);
				CxvPropertyWatch cxvPropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.DataPath))
				{
					_dataPathResolved = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.DataPath, cxvPropertyWatch);
				}
				UpdateDataPathDependencies(cxvPropertyWatch);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateDataPathDependencies(CxvPropertyWatch dataPathDependencies)
		{
			if (dataPathDependencies == _dataPathDependencies)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_dataPathDependencies != null)
			{
				if (_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler != null)
				{
					_dataPathDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= _pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				}
				_dataPathDependencies.Clear();
				_dataPathDependencies = null;
			}
			if (dataPathDependencies == null || dataPathDependencies.IsEmpty)
			{
				return;
			}
			_dataPathDependencies = dataPathDependencies;
			if (_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler == null)
			{
				_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler = delegate
				{
					ResetDataPath();
				};
			}
			_dataPathDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += _pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
		}

		private void ResetDataPath()
		{
			UpdateDataPathDependencies(null);
			_dataPathResolved = null;
			if (_watch != null)
			{
				UpdateWatch(null);
				RefreshData();
			}
			OnPropertyChanged("CxsItemMoniker");
		}

		public void Unload()
		{
			_property = null;
			_dataContainer = null;
			UpdateDataPathDependencies(null);
			_watch.Unload();
		}

		XElement IViewDataSerializable.WriteXElement()
		{
			List<object> list = new List<object>();
			ObservableCollection<string> value = Value;
			if (value != null)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("Value", value.Select((string content) => new XElement("string", content)), new XAttribute("Count", value.Count)));
			}
			string selectedItem = SelectedItem;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(selectedItem))
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("SelectedItem", selectedItem));
			}
			ReadOnlyObservableCollection<string> items = Items;
			if (items != null)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("Items", items.Select((string item) => new XElement("string", item)), new XAttribute("Count", items.Count)));
			}
			if (list.Count <= 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return new XElement(GetType().Name, list);
		}
	}
	public class CxvSpecPropertyDataItem : CxvObservableObject, IViewDataSerializable, IStateData, ICxvPropertyData
	{
		private EventHandler _dataDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;

		private CxvSpecPropertyData _controller;

		private string _selectedItem;

		private ObservableCollection<string> _items = new ObservableCollection<string>();

		private object _variable;

		private CxvPropertyWatch _dataDependencies;

		public ObservableCollection<string> Items
		{
			get
			{
				if (_items == null)
				{
					_controller.UpdateItems();
				}
				return _items;
			}
		}

		public bool? IsReadOnly => false;

		public bool? IsCalculated => false;

		public bool? IsEntered
		{
			get
			{
				if (_variable != null)
				{
					if (_variable is CxvVariableData)
					{
						return (_variable as CxvVariableData).IsEntered;
					}
					return null;
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		public string SelectedItem
		{
			get
			{
				if (_selectedItem == null)
				{
					_controller.UpdateSelectedItem();
				}
				return _selectedItem;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _selectedItem)
				{
					_selectedItem = value;
					_controller.OnSelectedItemChanged(this);
					if (_selectedItem != value)
					{
						_controller.RefreshOptions();
					}
					OnPropertyChanged("SelectedItem");
					if (_selectedItem != value)
					{
						Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action<string>(OnPropertyChanged), "SelectedItem");
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public object Variable
		{
			get
			{
				if (_variable == null)
				{
					CreateVariable();
				}
				return _variable;
			}
		}

		private EventHandler dataDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (_dataDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler == null)
				{
					_dataDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler = delegate
					{
						ResetVariable(clearValue: false);
					};
				}
				return _dataDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		public bool? IsEnabled => _controller.IsEnabled;

		public bool? IsVisible => _controller.IsVisible;

		public string DisabledPrompt => _controller.DisabledPrompt;

		public string VariablePrompt
		{
			get
			{
				if (SelectedItem != null && OptionPrompts.ContainsKey(SelectedItem))
				{
					return OptionPrompts[SelectedItem];
				}
				if (Items == null || Items.Count == 0)
				{
					return Resources.NoSpecPrompt;
				}
				return string.Empty;
			}
		}

		public Dictionary<string, string> OptionPrompts => _controller.OptionPrompts;

		public bool IsUnloaded { get; set; }

		public CxvSpecPropertyDataItem(CxvSpecPropertyData controller)
		{
			_controller = controller;
		}

		public void RaiseIsEnteredChanged()
		{
			OnPropertyChanged("IsEntered");
		}

		internal string PeekSelectedItem()
		{
			return _selectedItem;
		}

		internal void SilentlySetSelectedItem(string value)
		{
			if (_selectedItem != value)
			{
				_selectedItem = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("SelectedItem");
				ResetVariable(clearValue: false);
			}
		}

		internal void SilentlySetSelectedItemNR(string value)
		{
			if (_selectedItem != value)
			{
				_selectedItem = value;
				ResetVariable(clearValue: false);
			}
		}

		public void UpdateItems(ObservableCollection<string> value)
		{
			_items = value;
			OnPropertyChanged("Items");
		}

		private void CreateVariable()
		{
			CxvPropertyWatch cxvPropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
			_variable = _controller.CreateSpecVariable(this, cxvPropertyWatch);
			if (_variable != null)
			{
				OnPropertyChanged("Variable");
			}
			if (_variable is INotifyPropertyChanged notifyPropertyChanged)
			{
				notifyPropertyChanged.PropertyChanged += variable_PropertyChanged;
			}
			UpdateItemDependencies(cxvPropertyWatch);
		}

		public void ResetVariable(bool clearValue)
		{
			UpdateItemDependencies(null);
			if (clearValue)
			{
				if (_variable is CxvVariableData cxvVariableData)
				{
					cxvVariableData.Value = null;
				}
				_controller.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.UpdateDataCache();
			}
			if (_variable is INotifyPropertyChanged notifyPropertyChanged)
			{
				notifyPropertyChanged.PropertyChanged -= variable_PropertyChanged;
			}
			_variable = null;
			OnPropertyChanged("Variable");
		}

		private void UpdateItemDependencies(CxvPropertyWatch newDependencies)
		{
			if (_dataDependencies != null)
			{
				_dataDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= dataDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
			if (newDependencies != null && !newDependencies.IsEmpty)
			{
				_dataDependencies = newDependencies;
				_dataDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += dataDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
			else
			{
				_dataDependencies = null;
			}
		}

		XElement IViewDataSerializable.WriteXElement()
		{
			List<XElement> list = new List<XElement>();
			string selectedItem = SelectedItem;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(selectedItem))
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("SelectedItem", selectedItem));
			}
			ObservableCollection<string> items = Items;
			if (items != null)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("Items", items.Select((string item) => new XElement("string", item)), new XAttribute("Count", items.Count)));
			}
			object variable = Variable;
			if (variable != null)
			{
				XElement xElement = CxvInternalHelpers.WriteDataToXElement(variable);
				if (xElement != null)
				{
					list.Add(new XElement("Variable", xElement));
				}
			}
			bool? isReadOnly = IsReadOnly;
			bool? isCalculated = IsCalculated;
			bool? isEntered = IsEntered;
			if (isReadOnly.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("IsReadOnly", isReadOnly));
			}
			if (isEntered.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("IsEntered", isEntered.Value));
			}
			if (isCalculated.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("IsCalculated", isCalculated.Value));
			}
			return new XElement(GetType().Name, list);
		}

		protected override void OnPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			base.OnPropertyChanged(propertyName);
			if (propertyName == "SelectedItem")
			{
				OnPropertyChanged("VariablePrompt");
			}
			if (propertyName == "Items")
			{
				OnPropertyChanged("OptionPrompts");
			}
		}

		private void variable_PropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.PropertyName == "CxsItemMoniker")
			{
				bool? isEntered = IsEntered;
				_items = null;
				_selectedItem = null;
				_controller.UpdateItems();
				if (isEntered != IsEntered)
				{
					OnPropertyChanged("IsEntered");
				}
			}
			if (e.PropertyName == "IsEntered")
			{
				OnPropertyChanged("IsEntered");
			}
		}

		public void Unload()
		{
			if (!IsUnloaded)
			{
				IsUnloaded = true;
				ResetVariable(clearValue: false);
			}
		}
	}
	public class CxvSpecPropertyData : CxvStatePropertyData, IViewDataSerializable, ICxvPropertyData
	{
		private CxvPropertyWatch _dataPathDependencies;

		private EventHandler _pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;

		private ICxvViewDataContainer _dataContainer;

		private ICxsDataWatch _canEnterWatch;

		private ICxsDataWatch _unfilledWatch;

		private CxvSpecOptions _specOptions;

		private List<CxvSpecPropertyDataItem> _specItems;

		private readonly Dictionary<string, string> _optionPrompts = new Dictionary<string, string>();

		public Dictionary<string, string> OptionPrompts => _optionPrompts;

		public IEnumerable<CxvSpecPropertyDataItem> Specs => _specItems;

		public bool IsUnloaded { get; set; }

		public CxvSpecPropertyData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvSpecProperty specProperty)
			: base(dataContainer, specProperty)
		{
			_dataContainer = dataContainer;
			if (specProperty != null)
			{
				_specOptions = specProperty.Options;
				_specItems = new List<CxvSpecPropertyDataItem>();
				for (int i = 0; i < specProperty.Count; i++)
				{
					_specItems.Add(new CxvSpecPropertyDataItem(this));
				}
			}
		}

		public void UpdateSelectedItem()
		{
			RefreshSpecSelections();
		}

		internal void UpdateItems()
		{
			ReconstructSpecOptions();
		}

		internal void OnSelectedItemChanged(CxvSpecPropertyDataItem spec)
		{
			string selectedItem = spec.SelectedItem;
			spec.ResetVariable(clearValue: true);
			spec.SilentlySetSelectedItemNR(selectedItem);
			foreach (CxvSpecPropertyDataItem specItem in _specItems)
			{
				if (specItem.PeekSelectedItem() == "")
				{
					specItem.SilentlySetSelectedItem(null);
				}
			}
			ReconstructSpecOptions();
		}

		internal object CreateSpecVariable(CxvSpecPropertyDataItem spec, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			CxvSpecOption cxvSpecOption = FindSpecOption(spec.SelectedItem);
			if (cxvSpecOption == null)
			{
				if (spec.Items == null || spec.Items.Count == 0)
				{
					return new CxvManualVariableData
					{
						IsReadOnly = true,
						IsCalculated = false,
						VariablePrompt = Resources.NoSpecPrompt,
						Freeze = true
					};
				}
				return new CxvManualVariableData
				{
					IsReadOnly = true,
					IsCalculated = false,
					Freeze = true
				};
			}
			return new CxvVariableProperty
			{
				DataPath = cxvSpecOption.DataPath,
				ExposeAs = cxvSpecOption.ExposeAs,
				BasisPath = cxvSpecOption.BasisPath
			}.GetPropertyValue(_dataContainer, dependencies);
		}

		private void ReconstructSpecOptions()
		{
			if (!AllItemsSet())
			{
				RefreshSpecSelections();
				return;
			}
			CxsDataProvider resolvedCxsProvider = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider;
			CxsCase cxsCase = resolvedCxsProvider?.Case;
			if (cxsCase == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_canEnterWatch != null)
			{
				_canEnterWatch.ValuesChanged -= _canEnterWatch_ValuesChanged;
			}
			List<ObservableCollection<string>> list = new List<ObservableCollection<string>>(_specItems.Count);
			for (int i = 0; i < _specItems.Count; i++)
			{
				list.Add(new ObservableCollection<string>());
			}
			List<string> list2 = new List<string>(_specOptions.Count);
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData2 = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData3 = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			foreach (CxvSpecOption specOption in _specOptions)
			{
				string text = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(specOption.DataPath, null);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					return;
				}
				list2.Add(text);
				cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(text, "value");
				cxsBatchRequestData2.AddRequest(text, "can_enter");
				cxsBatchRequestData3.AddRequest(text, "description");
			}
			object[] array = resolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData);
			int num = -1;
			foreach (CxvSpecPropertyDataItem specItem in _specItems)
			{
				num++;
				ObservableCollection<string> observableCollection = list[num];
				string text2 = specItem.PeekSelectedItem();
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					continue;
				}
				int num2 = FindSpecOptionIndex(text2);
				object[] array2 = cxsCase.BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData2);
				bool flag = false;
				if (array[num2] != null && array[num2].GetType() != typeof(DBNull))
				{
					flag = true;
					try
					{
						cxsCase.BatchSet(list2[num2], "pretend_entered", 0);
					}
					catch (CxsException)
					{
					}
				}
				object[] array3 = cxsCase.BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData2);
				if (flag)
				{
					try
					{
						cxsCase.BatchSet(list2[num2], "pretend_entered", 1);
					}
					catch (CxsException)
					{
					}
				}
				int num3 = -1;
				foreach (CxvSpecOption specOption2 in _specOptions)
				{
					num3++;
					bool? flag2 = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(array2[num3]);
					bool? flag3 = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(array3[num3]);
					bool? flag4 = null;
					if (flag2.HasValue || flag3.HasValue)
					{
						flag4 = (flag2 ?? false) || (flag3 ?? false);
					}
					if (num3 == num2)
					{
						if (!flag4.HasValue || flag4.Value)
						{
							observableCollection.Add(specOption2.DisplayLocalized);
						}
						else
						{
							specItem.SilentlySetSelectedItem(null);
						}
						continue;
					}
					bool flag5 = false;
					foreach (CxvSpecPropertyDataItem specItem2 in _specItems)
					{
						if (specItem2 != specItem && specItem2.SelectedItem == specOption2.DisplayLocalized)
						{
							int num4 = FindSpecOptionIndex(specItem2.SelectedItem);
							bool? flag6 = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(array2[num4]);
							if (!flag6.HasValue || flag6.Value)
							{
								flag5 = true;
							}
							break;
						}
					}
					if (!flag5 && (!flag4.HasValue || flag4.Value))
					{
						observableCollection.Add(specOption2.DisplayLocalized);
					}
				}
			}
			num = -1;
			List<string> list3 = new List<string>();
			foreach (CxvSpecPropertyDataItem specItem3 in _specItems)
			{
				num++;
				_ = list[num];
				string text3 = specItem3.PeekSelectedItem();
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3))
				{
					continue;
				}
				int num5 = FindSpecOptionIndex(text3);
				if (array[num5] != null && array[num5].GetType() != typeof(DBNull))
				{
					try
					{
						cxsCase.BatchSet(list2[num5], "pretend_entered", 0);
						list3.Add(list2[num5]);
					}
					catch (CxsException)
					{
					}
				}
			}
			object[] array4 = cxsCase.BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData3);
			int num6 = 0;
			foreach (CxvSpecOption specOption3 in _specOptions)
			{
				_optionPrompts[specOption3.DisplayLocalized] = CxvInternalHelpers.ValueToStringRemoveXml(array4[num6]);
				num6++;
			}
			foreach (string item in list3)
			{
				try
				{
					cxsCase.BatchSet(item, "pretend_entered", 1);
				}
				catch (CxsException)
				{
				}
			}
			num = -1;
			foreach (CxvSpecPropertyDataItem specItem4 in _specItems)
			{
				num++;
				specItem4.UpdateItems(list[num]);
			}
			if (_canEnterWatch == null)
			{
				_canEnterWatch = _dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
				foreach (CxvSpecOption specOption4 in _specOptions)
				{
					string text4 = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(specOption4.DataPath, null);
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
					{
						return;
					}
					_canEnterWatch.AddWatch(text4, "can_enter");
				}
				_ = _canEnterWatch.Values;
			}
			if (_canEnterWatch != null)
			{
				_canEnterWatch.ValuesChanged += _canEnterWatch_ValuesChanged;
			}
		}

		private void RefreshSpecSelections()
		{
			bool flag = AllItemsSet();
			if (flag)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsDataProvider resolvedCxsProvider = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider;
			if (resolvedCxsProvider?.Case == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			UpdateDataPathDependencies(null);
			CxvPropertyWatch cxvPropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
			foreach (CxvSpecOption specOption in _specOptions)
			{
				string path = CxvViewModel.ExpandDataPath(specOption.DataPath, _dataContainer, cxvPropertyWatch);
				object obj = resolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(path, "value", null);
				if (obj == null || obj.GetType() == typeof(DBNull))
				{
					continue;
				}
				bool flag2 = false;
				foreach (CxvSpecPropertyDataItem specItem in _specItems)
				{
					if (specOption.DisplayLocalized == specItem.PeekSelectedItem())
					{
						flag2 = true;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (!flag2)
				{
					foreach (CxvSpecPropertyDataItem specItem2 in _specItems)
					{
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(specItem2.PeekSelectedItem()))
						{
							specItem2.SilentlySetSelectedItem(specOption.DisplayLocalized);
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				flag = AllItemsSet();
				if (flag)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				foreach (CxvSpecOption specOption2 in _specOptions)
				{
					bool flag3 = false;
					foreach (CxvSpecPropertyDataItem specItem3 in _specItems)
					{
						if (specOption2.DisplayLocalized == specItem3.PeekSelectedItem())
						{
							flag3 = true;
							break;
						}
					}
					if (flag3)
					{
						if (specOption2 == _specOptions.Last())
						{
							foreach (CxvSpecPropertyDataItem specItem4 in _specItems)
							{
								if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(specItem4.PeekSelectedItem()))
								{
									specItem4.SilentlySetSelectedItem("");
									break;
								}
							}
						}
						flag = AllItemsSet();
						continue;
					}
					string path2 = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(specOption2.DataPath, null);
					bool? flag4 = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(resolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(path2, "can_enter", null));
					if (flag4.HasValue && !flag4.Value)
					{
						if (specOption2 == _specOptions.Last())
						{
							foreach (CxvSpecPropertyDataItem specItem5 in _specItems)
							{
								if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(specItem5.PeekSelectedItem()))
								{
									specItem5.SilentlySetSelectedItem("");
									break;
								}
							}
						}
						flag = AllItemsSet();
						continue;
					}
					foreach (CxvSpecPropertyDataItem specItem6 in _specItems)
					{
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(specItem6.PeekSelectedItem()))
						{
							specItem6.SilentlySetSelectedItem(specOption2.DisplayLocalized);
							break;
						}
					}
					flag = AllItemsSet();
					if (!flag)
					{
						continue;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				ReconstructSpecOptions();
				if (_unfilledWatch != null)
				{
					_unfilledWatch.Unload();
					_unfilledWatch = null;
				}
			}
			else if (_unfilledWatch == null)
			{
				_unfilledWatch = _dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
				foreach (CxvSpecOption specOption3 in _specOptions)
				{
					string text = CxvViewModel.ExpandDataPath(specOption3.DataPath, _dataContainer, null);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						_unfilledWatch.AddWatch(text, "value", null);
						_unfilledWatch.AddWatch(text, "can_enter", null);
					}
				}
				_ = _unfilledWatch.Values;
				_unfilledWatch.ValuesChanged += _unfilledWatch_ValuesChanged;
			}
			UpdateDataPathDependencies(cxvPropertyWatch);
		}

		private void _unfilledWatch_ValuesChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			ReconstructSpecOptions();
		}

		private bool AllItemsSet()
		{
			foreach (CxvSpecPropertyDataItem specItem in _specItems)
			{
				if (specItem.PeekSelectedItem() == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		private int FindSpecOptionIndex(string specDisplay)
		{
			int num = -1;
			foreach (CxvSpecOption specOption in _specOptions)
			{
				num++;
				if (specOption.DisplayLocalized == specDisplay)
				{
					return num;
				}
			}
			return -1;
		}

		private CxvSpecOption FindSpecOption(string specDisplay)
		{
			foreach (CxvSpecOption specOption in _specOptions)
			{
				if (specOption.DisplayLocalized == specDisplay)
				{
					return specOption;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private void UpdateDataPathDependencies(CxvPropertyWatch dataPathDependencies)
		{
			if (dataPathDependencies == _dataPathDependencies)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_dataPathDependencies != null)
			{
				if (_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler != null)
				{
					_dataPathDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= _pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				}
				_dataPathDependencies.Clear();
				_dataPathDependencies = null;
			}
			if (dataPathDependencies == null || dataPathDependencies.IsEmpty)
			{
				return;
			}
			_dataPathDependencies = dataPathDependencies;
			if (_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler == null)
			{
				_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler = delegate
				{
					ResetDataPath();
				};
			}
			_dataPathDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += _pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
		}

		private void ResetDataPath()
		{
			UpdateDataPathDependencies(null);
			foreach (CxvSpecPropertyDataItem specItem in _specItems)
			{
				if (specItem.PeekSelectedItem() == "" || specItem.Variable == null)
				{
					specItem.SilentlySetSelectedItemNR(null);
				}
				if (!(specItem.Variable is CxvVariableData { Value: not null }))
				{
					specItem.SilentlySetSelectedItemNR(null);
				}
			}
			RefreshSpecSelections();
			ReconstructSpecOptions();
		}

		public void RefreshOptions()
		{
			ReconstructSpecOptions();
		}

		XElement IViewDataSerializable.WriteXElement()
		{
			List<object> list = new List<object>();
			foreach (CxvSpecPropertyDataItem spec in Specs)
			{
				list.Add(CxvInternalHelpers.WriteDataToXElement(spec));
			}
			list.Add(new XAttribute("Count", Specs.Count()));
			Dictionary<string, string> optionPrompts = OptionPrompts;
			if (optionPrompts != null)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("OptionPrompts", optionPrompts.Select(delegate(KeyValuePair<string, string> item)
				{
					XName name = "string";
					KeyValuePair<string, string> keyValuePair = item;
					string key = keyValuePair.Key;
					keyValuePair = item;
					return new XElement(name, key + ", " + keyValuePair.Value);
				}), new XAttribute("Count", optionPrompts.Count)));
			}
			return new XElement(GetType().Name, list);
		}

		public void Unload()
		{
			if (IsUnloaded)
			{
				return;
			}
			UpdateDataPathDependencies(null);
			if (_canEnterWatch != null)
			{
				_canEnterWatch.ValuesChanged -= _canEnterWatch_ValuesChanged;
				_canEnterWatch.Unload();
			}
			foreach (CxvSpecPropertyDataItem specItem in _specItems)
			{
				specItem.Unload();
			}
			_specItems.Clear();
			IsUnloaded = true;
		}

		private void _canEnterWatch_ValuesChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			foreach (CxvSpecPropertyDataItem specItem in _specItems)
			{
				if (specItem.PeekSelectedItem() == "" || specItem.Variable == null)
				{
					specItem.SilentlySetSelectedItemNR(null);
				}
				if (specItem.Variable is CxvVariableData { Value: null, IsReadOnly: not false })
				{
					specItem.SilentlySetSelectedItemNR(null);
				}
			}
			RefreshSpecSelections();
			ReconstructSpecOptions();
		}
	}
	public class CxvCustomTooltipData : CxvVariableData
	{
		private ICxsDataWatch _tooltipWatch;

		private EventHandler _tooltipWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;

		public override Dictionary<string, string> OptionPrompts
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.Property is CxvCustomTooltipProperty cxvCustomTooltipProperty && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(cxvCustomTooltipProperty.ObjectType) && cxvCustomTooltipProperty.ObjectType.Contains("Streams.[Value]") && base.ValueOptions != null)
				{
					Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
					{
						foreach (string valueOption in base.ValueOptions)
						{
							string streamPath = CxvCustomTooltipHelper.ResolveDataPath(cxvCustomTooltipProperty.ObjectType, valueOption);
							string longNameForStream = GetLongNameForStream(streamPath);
							dictionary.Add(valueOption, longNameForStream);
						}
						return dictionary;
					}
				}
				return base.OptionPrompts;
			}
		}

		public CxvCustomTooltipData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvVariableProperty property)
			: base(dataContainer, property)
		{
		}

		private void UpdateTooltipWatch(ICxsDataWatch tooltipWatch)
		{
			if (tooltipWatch == _tooltipWatch)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_tooltipWatch != null && _tooltipWatch_ValuesChangedHandler != null)
			{
				_tooltipWatch.ValuesChanged -= _tooltipWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;
				_tooltipWatch.Unload();
				_tooltipWatch = null;
			}
			if (tooltipWatch == null || tooltipWatch.IsEmpty)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_tooltipWatch_ValuesChangedHandler == null)
			{
				_tooltipWatch_ValuesChangedHandler = delegate
				{
					OnPropertyChanged("OptionPrompts");
					OnPropertyChanged("VariablePrompt");
				};
			}
			_tooltipWatch = tooltipWatch;
			_tooltipWatch.ValuesChanged += _tooltipWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;
		}

		protected override string GetCustomedTooltipText()
		{
			CxvCustomTooltipProperty cxvCustomTooltipProperty = base.Property as CxvCustomTooltipProperty;
			if (base.Value == null || base.Value.Equals(string.Empty) || cxvCustomTooltipProperty == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			string objectType = cxvCustomTooltipProperty.ObjectType;
			string text = null;
			string text2 = null;
			string text3 = null;
			string text4 = null;
			string text5 = null;
			string text6 = null;
			objectType = CxvCustomTooltipHelper.LocalizeObjectType(base.DataPath);
			text2 = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cxvCustomTooltipProperty.SubObjectTypePath)) ? CxvCustomTooltipHelper.ResolveDataPath(cxvCustomTooltipProperty.SubObjectTypePath, base.Value.ToString()) : base.DataPath);
			if (text2.Contains("Top.appModelV8.Properties.Parameters.Binary Interaction") || text2.Contains("Top.appModelV8.Properties.Parameters.Electrolyte Pair"))
			{
				return "";
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(cxvCustomTooltipProperty.DescriptionPath))
			{
				text3 = CxvCustomTooltipHelper.ResolveDescriptionPath(base.DataPath);
			}
			else if (cxvCustomTooltipProperty.DescriptionPath.Contains("Streams.[Value]") && base.Value.ToString().Contains("."))
			{
				text3 = CxvCustomTooltipHelper.ResolveDataPath(cxvCustomTooltipProperty.DescriptionPath, base.Value.ToString());
				text3 = CxvCustomTooltipHelper.ResolveDescriptionPath(text3);
			}
			else
			{
				text3 = CxvCustomTooltipHelper.ResolveDescriptionPath(cxvCustomTooltipProperty.DescriptionPath).Replace("[Value]", base.Value.ToString());
			}
			text5 = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(cxvCustomTooltipProperty.StatusPath)) ? CxvCustomTooltipHelper.ResolveDataPath(cxvCustomTooltipProperty.StatusPath, base.Value.ToString()) : base.DataPath);
			try
			{
				ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = base.DataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
				cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text3, "value");
				cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text2, "type");
				cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text5, "status");
				UpdateTooltipWatch(cxsDataWatch);
				object[] values = cxsDataWatch.Values;
				text4 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(values[0]);
				text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(values[1]);
				text6 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(values[2]);
				if (text.Equals("Real") || text.Equals("Integer") || text.Equals("Text") || text.Equals("Leaf"))
				{
					text = CxvCustomTooltipHelper.LocalizeDataType(text);
				}
				if (text6.Equals("Calculated") || text6.Equals("Not calculated") || text6.Equals("Fixed") || text6.Equals("Default") || text6.Equals("Missing") || text6.Equals("Specified"))
				{
					text6 = CxvCustomTooltipHelper.LocalizeDataType1(text6);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			text = CxvCustomTooltipHelper.LocalizeSubobjectType(text);
			return objectType + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) ? string.Empty : (" (" + text + ")")) + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4) ? string.Empty : ("\n" + text4)) + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text6) ? string.Empty : ("\n" + text6));
		}

		private string GetLongNameForStream(string streamPath)
		{
			string text = CxvCustomTooltipHelper.LocalizeObjectType2(streamPath);
			string path = CxvCustomTooltipHelper.ResolveDescriptionPath(streamPath);
			string strSubobjectType = null;
			string text2 = null;
			string text3 = null;
			try
			{
				text2 = base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(path, "value", string.Empty) as string;
				strSubobjectType = base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(streamPath, "type", string.Empty) as string;
				text3 = base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(streamPath, "status", string.Empty) as string;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			strSubobjectType = CxvCustomTooltipHelper.LocalizeSubobjectType(strSubobjectType);
			return text + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(strSubobjectType) ? string.Empty : (" (" + strSubobjectType + ")")) + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2) ? string.Empty : ("\n" + text2)) + (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3) ? string.Empty : ("\n" + text3));
		}
	}
	public class CxvStringSetIndexBasisData : CxvIndexBasisBaseData
	{
		private ICxvViewDataContainer _dataContainer;

		private CxvStringSetIndexBasis _indexBasis;

		public override int Count
		{
			get
			{
				if (_indexBasis != null)
				{
					if (_indexBasis.Items != null)
					{
						return _indexBasis.Items.Length;
					}
					return 0;
				}
				return 0;
			}
		}

		protected ICxvViewDataContainer DataContainer
		{
			get
			{
				return _dataContainer;
			}
			set
			{
				_dataContainer = value;
			}
		}

		public CxvStringSetIndexBasis IndexBasis
		{
			get
			{
				return _indexBasis;
			}
			set
			{
				_indexBasis = value;
			}
		}

		public CxvStringSetIndexBasisData(CxvStringSetIndexBasis indexBasis, ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer)
		{
			_indexBasis = indexBasis;
			_dataContainer = dataContainer;
		}

		public override object GetIndexSubstitution(int index, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (index < 0 || index >= Count)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return _indexBasis.Items[index];
		}
	}
	public class CxvTabItemPropertyData : CxvStatePropertyData, ITabItemPropertyData, IStatePropertyData, IPropertyData, INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private readonly CxvTabItemProperty _property;

		public bool? Entered => GetPropertyWatchedValue("Entered", GetIsEntered) as bool?;

		public bool? Complete => GetPropertyWatchedValue("Complete", GetIsComplete) as bool?;

		public bool? Accessible => GetPropertyWatchedValue("Accessible", GetIsAccessible) as bool?;

		public override string VariablePrompt
		{
			get
			{
				if (_property.AccessibleDataPaths.Count >= 1 && !(Accessible ?? true))
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(DisabledPrompt))
					{
						return DisabledPrompt;
					}
					return CxvInternalHelpers.ValueToStringRemoveXml(GetDataAttributeValue("VariablePrompt", _property.AccessibleDataPaths[0], "description"));
				}
				return base.VariablePrompt;
			}
		}

		public string CompletionMessages
		{
			get
			{
				ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = base.DataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
				foreach (string completeDataPath in _property.CompleteDataPaths)
				{
					string text = base.DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(completeDataPath);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "tabstate", "complete");
					}
				}
				object[] values = cxsDataWatch.Values;
				cxsDataWatch = base.DataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
				string text2 = null;
				if (values != null)
				{
					int num = 0;
					object[] array = values;
					for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
					{
						bool? flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(array[i]);
						if (flag.HasValue && !flag.Value)
						{
							string text3 = base.DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.CompleteDataPaths[num]);
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3) && (text3.Contains("CC_Nodes") || text3.Contains("CC Nodes")))
							{
								cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text3, "completion_message");
							}
						}
						num++;
					}
					array = cxsDataWatch.Values;
					for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
					{
						string text4 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[i]);
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
						{
							text2 = ((text2 != null) ? (text2 + text4) : text4);
						}
					}
				}
				return text2;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			base.OnPropertyChanged(propertyName);
			if (propertyName == "Accessible")
			{
				RaisePropertyChanged("VariablePrompt");
			}
		}

		public CxvTabItemPropertyData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvTabItemProperty property)
			: base(dataContainer, property)
		{
			_property = property;
		}

		protected virtual object GetIsEntered(CxvPropertyWatch watcher)
		{
			CxvLogicalTestCollection tabStateLogicalTestCollection = CxvTabStateProperty.GetTabStateLogicalTestCollection(_property.EnteredDataPaths, CxvTabStateOperator.Entered);
			CxvLogicalOperator unionOperator = CxvTabStateProperty.GetUnionOperator(CxvTabStateOperator.Entered);
			return tabStateLogicalTestCollection.EvaluateConditions(unionOperator, base.DataContainer, watcher);
		}

		private object GetIsComplete(CxvPropertyWatch watcher)
		{
			CxvLogicalTestCollection tabStateLogicalTestCollection = CxvTabStateProperty.GetTabStateLogicalTestCollection(_property.CompleteDataPaths, CxvTabStateOperator.Complete);
			CxvLogicalOperator unionOperator = CxvTabStateProperty.GetUnionOperator(CxvTabStateOperator.Complete);
			return tabStateLogicalTestCollection.EvaluateConditions(unionOperator, base.DataContainer, watcher);
		}

		private object GetIsAccessible(CxvPropertyWatch watcher)
		{
			CxvLogicalTestCollection tabStateLogicalTestCollection = CxvTabStateProperty.GetTabStateLogicalTestCollection(_property.AccessibleDataPaths, CxvTabStateOperator.Accessible);
			CxvLogicalOperator unionOperator = CxvTabStateProperty.GetUnionOperator(CxvTabStateOperator.Accessible);
			return tabStateLogicalTestCollection.EvaluateConditions(unionOperator, base.DataContainer, watcher);
		}
	}
	public class CxvVariableData : CxvStatePropertyData, ICxvEditableObject, IEditableObject, ICxvPropertyData, IViewDataSerializable, IQuickValueReadable, IQuickValueEditable
	{
		private object _value;

		private string _valueOptionsString;

		private string _optionPromptsString;

		private bool _canDefineNewId;

		private bool _inNew;

		private ObservableCollection<string> _valueOptions;

		private ObservableCollection<string> _convertedValueOptions;

		private Dictionary<string, string> _optionPrompts;

		private bool _needRefresh;

		private bool _needRefreshUI;

		private string _description;

		private object _upperLimitValue;

		private object _lowerLimitValue;

		private string _units;

		private string _unitsOptionsString;

		private ObservableCollection<string> _unitsOptions;

		private int? _unitRow;

		private string _basis;

		private string _basisOptionsString;

		private ObservableCollection<string> _basisOptions;

		private ObservableCollection<string> _localizedBasisOptions;

		private bool? _IsReadOnly;

		private bool? _isEntered;

		private bool? _IsEntered;

		private bool? _isCalculated;

		private bool? _IsCalculated;

		private bool? _isDefaultUnit;

		private bool _inEditMode;

		private string _focusProperty;

		private CxsDisplayedCase? _convertCase;

		private string _alternatePath;

		private Dictionary<string, object> _changedProperties;

		private ICxsDataWatch _watch;

		private EventHandler _dataWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;

		private string[] aryOriginalOptions;

		private CxvVariableTypeCode? _exposeAsResolved;

		private bool _isLeaf = true;

		private ICxsDataWatch _exposeAsDataWatch;

		private EventHandler _exposeAsDependencies_EventHandler;

		private string _dimension;

		private bool? _readOnlyResolved;

		private ICxsDataWatch _readOnlyDataWatch;

		private EventHandler _isReadOnlyDependencies_EventHandler;

		private string _dataPathResolved;

		private string _basisPathResolved;

		private string _optionsPathResolved;

		private bool _unresolvedPath;

		private CxvPropertyWatch _dataPathDependencies;

		private EventHandler _pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;

		private CxvVariableProperty _property;

		private ICxvViewDataContainer _dataContainer;

		private double? _upperlimit;

		private double? _lowerlimit;

		private IHappDataAccessor _happDataAccessor;

		public string Label => _property.LabelLocalized;

		public object Value
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureData();
				if (_property != null && _property.Converter != null)
				{
					IValueConverter valueConverter = _property.ValueConverter(_dataContainer);
					if (valueConverter != null)
					{
						return valueConverter.Convert(_value, typeof(object), null, null);
					}
				}
				if (_value is string)
				{
					string text;
					if (_valueOptions != null)
					{
						int num = _valueOptions.IndexOf(_value.ToString());
						if (_convertedValueOptions != null && num < _convertedValueOptions.Count)
						{
							text = ((num == -1) ? _value.ToString() : _convertedValueOptions[num]);
						}
						else
						{
							string resourceOptionKey = CxvInternalHelpers.GetResourceOptionKey(_dataContainer, _dataPathResolved, _value.ToString());
							text = CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeString(_dataContainer, _value.ToString(), resourceOptionKey, "ComboItem");
						}
					}
					else
					{
						text = _value.ToString();
						text = LocalizeSpecialText(text);
					}
					if (_convertCase.HasValue && _convertCase.Value != CxsDisplayedCase.None && _value != null)
					{
						if (_convertCase.Value == CxsDisplayedCase.Lowercase)
						{
							return CultureInfo.CurrentCulture.TextInfo.ToTitleCase(text.ToLower(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture));
						}
						return text.ToUpper(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
					}
					return text;
				}
				return _value;
			}
			set
			{
				string svalue = value as string;
				if (svalue != null && ValueOptions is IEnumerable<string> source)
				{
					string text = source.FirstOrDefault((string x) => string.Equals(x, svalue, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase));
					if (text != null)
					{
						value = text;
					}
				}
				if (!object.Equals(_value, value) && ShouldCancelPropertyChanging("Value", _value, value))
				{
					Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action<string>(OnPropertyChanged), "Value");
					return;
				}
				if (IsReadOnly || (_exposeAsResolved.HasValue && _exposeAsResolved.Value == CxvVariableTypeCode.Id && string.IsNullOrEmpty(value as string)))
				{
					Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action<string>(OnPropertyChanged), "Value");
					return;
				}
				object newValue = value;
				if (newValue is string)
				{
					if (_convertedValueOptions == null)
					{
						_convertedValueOptions = new ObservableCollection<string>();
						if (_valueOptions != null && _dataContainer != null)
						{
							foreach (string valueOption in _valueOptions)
							{
								string resourceOptionKey = CxvInternalHelpers.GetResourceOptionKey(_dataContainer, _dataPathResolved, valueOption);
								_ = string.Empty;
								string text2;
								if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(resourceOptionKey))
								{
									text2 = CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeString(_dataContainer, valueOption, resourceOptionKey, "ComboItem");
									CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeOptionPrompt(_dataContainer, valueOption, resourceOptionKey + "_prompt", "ComboItemPrompt");
									if (_convertCase.HasValue && _convertCase.Value != CxsDisplayedCase.None)
									{
										text2 = ((_convertCase.Value == CxsDisplayedCase.Lowercase) ? CultureInfo.CurrentCulture.TextInfo.ToTitleCase(text2.ToLower(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture)) : text2.ToUpper(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture));
									}
								}
								else
								{
									text2 = valueOption;
								}
								if (!_convertedValueOptions.Contains(text2))
								{
									_convertedValueOptions.Add(text2);
								}
							}
						}
					}
					if (_convertedValueOptions != null && _convertedValueOptions.Contains(newValue.ToString()))
					{
						newValue = _valueOptions[_convertedValueOptions.IndexOf(newValue.ToString())];
					}
				}
				if (_value == newValue || ValueCompare(_value, newValue))
				{
					return;
				}
				if (ValueType == typeof(double) && newValue != null && newValue.GetType() != typeof(double))
				{
					string text3 = newValue.ToString();
					string systemDecimalSymbol = CxvInternalHelpers.GetSystemDecimalSymbol();
					if (systemDecimalSymbol != ".")
					{
						text3 = text3.Replace(".", systemDecimalSymbol);
					}
					if (!double.TryParse(text3, out var result))
					{
						Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action<string>(OnPropertyChanged), "Value");
						return;
					}
					newValue = result;
				}
				if (ValueType == typeof(int) && newValue != null && newValue.GetType() != typeof(int))
				{
					if (!int.TryParse(newValue.ToString(), out var result2))
					{
						Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action<string>(OnPropertyChanged), "Value");
						return;
					}
					newValue = result2;
				}
				if (ValueType == typeof(string) && newValue != null && _exposeAsResolved == CxvVariableTypeCode.IdWithOptions && !_canDefineNewId && _valueOptions != null && !_valueOptions.Contains(newValue))
				{
					Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action<string>(OnPropertyChanged), "Value");
					return;
				}
				if (_property != null && _property.ValueValidator != null && !_property.ValueValidator(newValue, _dataContainer))
				{
					Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action<string>(OnPropertyChanged), "Value");
					return;
				}
				object oldValue = _value;
				if (_property != null && _property.Converter != null && (newValue is string || (_property.CallConverterEvenWhenValueIsNull && newValue == null)))
				{
					IValueConverter valueConverter = _property.ValueConverter(_dataContainer);
					if (valueConverter != null)
					{
						newValue = valueConverter.ConvertBack(newValue, typeof(object), null, null);
					}
				}
				_value = newValue;
				if (_canDefineNewId && CxsConverter.ValueToString(_value) == Resources.CreateNewMenuItem)
				{
					Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
					{
						OnSetProperty("Value", oldValue, newValue);
						if (!_inEditMode && _value != newValue && !ValueCompare(_value, newValue))
						{
							Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action<string>(OnPropertyChanged), "Value");
						}
					});
					return;
				}
				OnSetProperty("Value", oldValue, newValue);
				Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					if (!_inEditMode && _value != newValue && !ValueCompare(_value, newValue))
					{
						Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action<string>(OnPropertyChanged), "Value");
					}
				});
			}
		}

		public object RawValue
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureData();
				return _value;
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable ValueOptions
		{
			get
			{
				if (_dataContainer == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				EnsureData();
				if (_convertedValueOptions == null)
				{
					_convertedValueOptions = new ObservableCollection<string>();
				}
				if (_valueOptions != null)
				{
					bool flag = _convertCase.HasValue && _convertCase.Value != CxsDisplayedCase.None;
					string descriptionFromDataWatch = GetDescriptionFromDataWatch(_dataPathResolved);
					foreach (string valueOption in _valueOptions)
					{
						string text = valueOption;
						string value = string.Empty;
						string empty = string.Empty;
						string resourceOptionKey = CxvInternalHelpers.GetResourceOptionKey(_dataContainer, _dataPathResolved, valueOption);
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(resourceOptionKey))
						{
							text = CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeString(_dataContainer, valueOption, resourceOptionKey, "ComboItem");
							if (flag)
							{
								text = ((_convertCase.Value == CxsDisplayedCase.Lowercase) ? CultureInfo.CurrentCulture.TextInfo.ToTitleCase(text.ToLower(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture)) : text.ToUpper(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture));
							}
							if (_property.UseVariableDescriptionForPrompts)
							{
								value = descriptionFromDataWatch;
							}
							else
							{
								empty = resourceOptionKey + "_prompt";
								value = CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeOptionPrompt(_dataContainer, valueOption, empty, "ComboItemPrompt");
							}
						}
						if (!_convertedValueOptions.Contains(text))
						{
							_convertedValueOptions.Add(text);
						}
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
						{
							if (_optionPrompts == null)
							{
								_optionPrompts = new Dictionary<string, string>();
							}
							_optionPrompts[text] = value;
						}
					}
					if (_property != null && (_property.Sorted ?? false) && _convertedValueOptions != null)
					{
						return _convertedValueOptions.OrderBy((string i) => i);
					}
					return _convertedValueOptions;
				}
				if (_property.AllowEmptyOptions ?? false)
				{
					_valueOptions = new ObservableCollection<string>();
				}
				return _valueOptions;
			}
		}

		public virtual Dictionary<string, string> OptionPrompts => _optionPrompts;

		public string Units
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureData();
				if (base.IsEnabled.HasValue && !base.IsEnabled.Value && Value == null && base.Property is CxvVariableProperty { HideUnitWhenNullValueAndDisabled: not false })
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _units;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(_units != value) || ValueCompare(_units, value))
				{
					return;
				}
				string units = _units;
				_units = value;
				if (_isLeaf && _value != null && _isCalculated.HasValue && !_isCalculated.Value)
				{
					if (!OnSetProperty("Value", _value, _value))
					{
						_units = units;
					}
					else if (!_inEditMode)
					{
						OnPropertyChanged("Units");
					}
				}
				else
				{
					OnSetProperty("Units", units, value);
				}
				if (!_inEditMode && _units != value && !ValueCompare(_units, value))
				{
					Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action<string>(OnPropertyChanged), "Units");
				}
			}
		}

		public ObservableCollection<string> UnitsOptions
		{

		public int UnitRow
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_unitRow.HasValue)
				{
					EnsureDataPath();
					object value = base.DataContainer?.ResolvedCxsProvider?.Case?.BatchGet(DataPath, "unit_row");
					_unitRow = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(value);
				}
				return _unitRow.GetValueOrDefault();
			}
		}

		public string Basis
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureData();
				if (_basis == null)
				{
					return _basis;
				}
				string text = CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeString(_dataContainer, _basis, "BasisOptions_" + CxvInternalHelpers.ReplaceSpecialChars(_basis));
				return CultureInfo.CurrentCulture.TextInfo.ToTitleCase(text.ToLower(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture));
			}
			set
			{
				string text;
				if (value != null && _localizedBasisOptions != null)
				{
					int num = _localizedBasisOptions.IndexOf(value);
					text = ((-1 != num) ? _basisOptions[num] : value.ToUpper(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture));
				}
				else
				{
					text = value.ToUpper(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
				}
				if (_basis != value && !ValueCompare(_basis, text))
				{
					string basis = _basis;
					_basis = text;
					OnSetProperty("Basis", basis, text);
					if (!_inEditMode && _basis != value && !ValueCompare(_basis, text))
					{
						Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action<string>(OnPropertyChanged), "Basis");
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public ObservableCollection<string> BasisOptions => _localizedBasisOptions;

		public override string VariablePrompt
		{
			get
			{
				if (_property == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.VariablePromptLocalized))
				{
					return _property.VariablePromptLocalized;
				}
				switch (EnsureExposeAs())
				{
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.IdWithOptions:
				{
					string text5 = CxvInternalHelpers.ValueToStringRemoveXml(GetDataAttributeValue("VariablePrompt", CxsPath.ParentPath(EnsureDataPath()), "id_description"));
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text5))
					{
						return GetCustomedTooltipText();
					}
					return text5;
				}
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.Id:
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.IdTwoLevel:
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.IdThreeLevel:
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.RealId:
				{
					string text2 = EnsureDataPath();
					string text3 = CxvInternalHelpers.ValueToStringRemoveXml(GetDataAttributeValue("VariablePrompt", text2, "id_description"));
					bool flag = false;
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2) && text2.StartsWith("Top.appModelV8.Components.UNIFAC-Groups.Input.GROUPNO.#"))
					{
						string text4 = "Top.appModelV8.Components.UNIFAC-Groups.Input.GROUPNO.#";
						text4 = text2.Substring(text4.Length - 1);
						if (!text4.Contains("."))
						{
							flag = true;
						}
						if (flag)
						{
							text3 = Resources.UNIFACGroups_Group_ID_Prompt;
						}
					}
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3))
					{
						return GetCustomedTooltipText();
					}
					return text3;
				}
				default:
				{
					if (_description != null)
					{
						return _description;
					}
					string text = CxvInternalHelpers.ValueToStringRemoveXml(GetDataAttributeValue("VariablePrompt", EnsureDataPath(), "description"));
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						return text;
					}
					return GetCustomedTooltipText();
				}
				}
			}
		}

		public double? UpperLimit

			get

				if (!_upperlimit.HasValue && _exposeAsResolved.HasValue && _exposeAsResolved.Value != CxvVariableTypeCode.Id && _exposeAsResolved.Value != CxvVariableTypeCode.IdThreeLevel && _exposeAsResolved.Value != CxvVariableTypeCode.IdTwoLevel && _exposeAsResolved.Value != CxvVariableTypeCode.IdWithOptions && _exposeAsResolved.Value != CxvVariableTypeCode.RealId)
				{

					{
						_upperlimit = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(_upperLimitValue);
					}
					else
					{
						_upperlimit = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(GetDataAttributeValue("UpperLimit", EnsureDataPath(), "upper_limit"));
					}
				}
				return _upperlimit;
			}
		}

		public object HelpFile
		{
			get
			{
				CxvVariableTypeCode cxvVariableTypeCode = EnsureExposeAs();
				string text = (((uint)(cxvVariableTypeCode - 6) > 4u) ? CxsConverter.ValueToString(GetDataAttributeValue("HelpFile", EnsureDataPath(), "help_file")) : CxsConverter.ValueToString(GetDataAttributeValue("HelpFile", EnsureDataPath(), "id_help_file")));
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					return null;
				}
				return text.TrimEnd();
			}
		}

		public object HelpId
		{
			get
			{
				CxvVariableTypeCode cxvVariableTypeCode = EnsureExposeAs();
				int num = (((uint)(cxvVariableTypeCode - 6) > 4u) ? (CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(GetDataAttributeValue("HelpId", EnsureDataPath(), "help_id")) ?? 0) : (CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(GetDataAttributeValue("HelpId", EnsureDataPath(), "id_help_id")) ?? 0));
				if (num == 0)
				{
					return null;
				}
				if (num < 0)
				{
					return -num;
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		public object QuickValue
		{
			get
			{
				string dataAttributeResolved = _property.DataAttributeResolved;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(dataAttributeResolved))
				{
					if (string.Equals(dataAttributeResolved, "value", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) || string.Equals(dataAttributeResolved, "display_name", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
					{
						IHappDataNode happDataNode = FindHappDataNode();
						if (happDataNode != null)
						{
							if (string.Equals(dataAttributeResolved, "value", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
							{
								return TryConvertValue(happDataNode.Value);
							}
							if (string.Equals(dataAttributeResolved, "display_name", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
							{
								return happDataNode.Name;
							}
						}
					}
					if (_dataContainer != null)
					{
						CxsDataProvider resolvedCxsProvider = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider;
						if (resolvedCxsProvider != null)
						{
							CxsCase cxsCase = resolvedCxsProvider.Case;
							if (cxsCase != null)
							{
								string text = EnsureDataPath();
								if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
								{
									return TryConvertValue(cxsCase.BatchGet(text, dataAttributeResolved));
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		private IHappDataAccessor ResolvedHappDataAccessor => _happDataAccessor ?? (_happDataAccessor = _dataContainer?.UnityContainer?.TryResolve<IHappDataAccessor>());

		public CxvVariableData(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvVariableProperty property)
			: base(dataContainer, property)
		{
			if (property == null)
			{
				throw new NullReferenceException("property cannot be null.");
			}
			_dataContainer = dataContainer;
			_property = property;
			if (property.DisplayedCase.HasValue && property.DisplayedCase.Value != CxsDisplayedCase.None)
			{
				_convertCase = property.DisplayedCase;
			}
		}

		private string LocalizeSpecialText(string strValue)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(strValue))
			{
				return strValue;
			}
			if (_dataPathResolved != null && _dataPathResolved.EndsWith(".Output.U-HOT-SIDE"))
			{
				if (strValue.ToUpper() == "TUBE")
				{
					strValue = CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeString(_dataContainer, strValue, "ShellTube_TUBE");
				}
				if (strValue.ToUpper() == "SHELL")
				{
					strValue = CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeString(_dataContainer, strValue, "ShellTube_Shell");
				}
			}
			return strValue;
		}

		private bool ValueCompare(object o1, object o2)
		{
			bool flag = o1 == null || o1 == DBNull.Value;
			bool flag2 = o2 == null || o2 == DBNull.Value;
			if (flag && flag2)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if ((!flag && o1.GetType() == typeof(string)) || (!flag2 && o2.GetType() == typeof(string)))
			{
				bool flag3 = flag || (o1 is string value && string.IsNullOrEmpty(value));
				bool flag4 = flag2 || (o2 is string value2 && string.IsNullOrEmpty(value2));
				if (flag3 && flag4)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (flag3 || flag4)
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			if (flag || flag2)
			{
				return false;
			}
			Type type = o1.GetType();
			if (type == o2.GetType())
			{
				if (type == typeof(double))
				{
					return (double)o1 == (double)o2;
				}
				if (type == typeof(int))
				{
					return (int)o1 == (int)o2;
				}
				if (type == typeof(string))
				{
					return (string)o1 == (string)o2;
				}
				if (type == typeof(bool))
				{
					return (bool)o1 == (bool)o2;
				}
				if (o1 is IComparable comparable)
				{
					return comparable.CompareTo(o2) == 0;
				}
				return o1 == o2;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private string GetDescriptionFromDataWatch(string path)
		{
			ICxsDataWatch exposeAsDataWatch = _exposeAsDataWatch;
			object value;
			if (exposeAsDataWatch != null)
			{
				value = exposeAsDataWatch.Values[2];
			}
			else
			{
				exposeAsDataWatch = _dataContainer?.CreateCxsWatch();
				exposeAsDataWatch.AddWatch(path, "description");
				value = exposeAsDataWatch.Values[0];
				exposeAsDataWatch.Unload();
				exposeAsDataWatch = null;
			}
			return CxvInternalHelpers.ValueToStringRemoveXml(value);
		}

		private void ResetUnitRow()
		{
			_unitRow = null;
			OnPropertyChanged("UnitRow");
		}

		public void UnsetReadOnly()
		{
			_IsReadOnly = null;
			OnPropertyChanged("IsReadOnly");
		}

		public void BeginEdit()
		{
			_inEditMode = true;
			_needRefreshUI = false;
		}

		public void CancelEdit()
		{
			_inEditMode = false;
			if (_changedProperties == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Dictionary<string, object> changedProperties = _changedProperties;
			_changedProperties = null;
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, object> item in changedProperties)
			{
				switch (item.Key)
				{
				case "Value":
					_value = item.Value;
					break;
				case "Units":
					_units = item.Value as string;
					break;
				case "Basis":
					_basis = item.Value as string;
					break;
				}
			}
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, object> item2 in changedProperties)
			{
				OnPropertyChanged(item2.Key);
			}
		}

		public void EndEdit()
		{
			bool flag = true;
			if (_changedProperties != null && _property != null)
			{
				CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
				if (_changedProperties.ContainsKey("Basis"))
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dataPathResolved))
					{
						cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(_dataPathResolved, "basis", null, _basis);
						if (_property.ExtraDataPaths != null)
						{
							foreach (string extraDataPath in _property.ExtraDataPaths)
							{
								string text = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(extraDataPath);
								if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
								{
									cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(text, "basis", null, _basis);
								}
							}
						}
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_basisPathResolved))
					{
						cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(_basisPathResolved, "value", null, _basis);
					}
				}
				if (_changedProperties.ContainsKey("Units"))
				{
					cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(_dataPathResolved, _property.UnitAttributeResolved, null, _units);
					if (_property.ExtraDataPaths != null)
					{
						foreach (string extraDataPath2 in _property.ExtraDataPaths)
						{
							string text2 = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(extraDataPath2);
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
							{
								cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(text2, _property.UnitAttributeResolved, null, _units);
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if (_changedProperties.ContainsKey("Value"))
				{
					if (_canDefineNewId && CxsConverter.ValueToString(_value) == Resources.CreateNewMenuItem)
					{
						if (_inNew)
						{
							_inNew = false;
							return;
						}
						_inNew = true;
						ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = _dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
						if (cxsDataWatch == null)
						{
							_inNew = false;
							return;
						}
						cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(_dataPathResolved, "default_name");
						cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(_dataPathResolved, "create_id_string");
						object[] array = cxsDataWatch.Values ?? new object[2]
						{
							string.Empty,
							string.Empty
						};
						if (_property.ShowDefaultNewItemDialog ?? true)
						{
							GetUserInputEventArgs e = new GetUserInputEventArgs
							{
								Title = _property.CreateNewPromptLocalized
							};
							if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.Title))
							{
								e.Title = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, Resources.CreateNewNameTitle);
							}
							e.Message = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[1]);
							if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.Message))
							{
								e.Message = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, Resources.CreateNewNamePrompt);
							}
							e.InputValue = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[0]);
							e.InputMask = 2;
							_dataContainer.GetUserInput(e);
							if (!e.DialogResult.HasValue || !e.DialogResult.Value)
							{
								CancelEdit();
								_inNew = false;
								return;
							}
							_value = CxsConverter.ValueToString(e.InputValue);
						}
						else
						{
							GetCustomUserInputEventArgs e2 = new GetCustomUserInputEventArgs();
							e2.InputValue = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[0]);
							_dataContainer.GetCustomUserInput(e2);
							if (!(e2.DialogResult ?? false))
							{
								CancelEdit();
								_inNew = false;
								return;
							}
							_value = CxsConverter.ValueToString(e2.InputValue);
						}
					}
					if (_value == null || (_valueOptions != null && _valueOptions.Contains(_value)))
					{
						_needRefresh = true;
					}
					cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(EnsureDataPath(), _property.DataAttributeResolved, _units, _value);
					if (_property.ExtraDataPaths != null)
					{
						foreach (string extraDataPath3 in _property.ExtraDataPaths)
						{
							string text3 = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(extraDataPath3);
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3))
							{
								cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(text3, "value", _units, _value);
							}
						}
					}
				}
				Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = _changedProperties;
				_changedProperties = null;
				if (cxsBatchRequestData.RequestsCount > 0)
				{
					try
					{
						flag = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchSet(cxsBatchRequestData, out var cxsUpdateException);
						bool flag2 = _needRefresh;
						if (!flag2 && cxsUpdateException != null && cxsUpdateException.InnerException?.Message == "refresh needed")
						{
							flag2 = true;
						}
						if (flag2)
						{
							RefreshData();
						}
					}
					catch (CxsException)
					{
						flag = false;
					}
					if (!flag)
					{
						_changedProperties = new Dictionary<string, object>(dictionary);
						dictionary = null;
						if (_needRefreshUI)
						{
							refreshUIWhenChangeFailed();
							_needRefreshUI = false;
						}
						else
						{
							CancelEdit();
						}
						_inNew = false;
					}
				}
				if (dictionary != null)
				{
					foreach (string key in dictionary.Keys)
					{
						if (key != null)
						{
							OnPropertyChanged(key);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			_inEditMode = false;
			if (!flag)
			{
				throw new CxsException("BatchSet failed");
			}
		}

		private void refreshUIWhenChangeFailed()
		{
			if (_changedProperties == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (string key in _changedProperties.Keys)
			{
				if (key != null)
				{
					OnPropertyChanged(key);
				}
				_changedProperties = null;
			}
		}

		protected virtual bool OnSetProperty(string name, object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			bool flag = !_inEditMode;
			bool result = true;
			try
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					BeginEdit();
				}
				if (_changedProperties == null)
				{
					_changedProperties = new Dictionary<string, object>();
					_changedProperties[name] = oldValue;
				}
				else if (!_changedProperties.ContainsKey(name))
				{
					_changedProperties[name] = oldValue;
				}
				if (!flag || !CanEndFakeEdit())
				{
					OnPropertyChanged(name);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					CancelEdit();
				}
				return false;
			}
			finally
			{
				if (flag && CanEndFakeEdit())
				{
					try
					{
						EndEdit();
					}
					catch (CxsException)
					{
						result = false;
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private bool CanEndFakeEdit()
		{
			if (EnsureExposeAs() == CxvVariableTypeCode.Real)
			{
				if (!IsReadOnly && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_focusProperty))
				{
					return _focusProperty == "Basis";
				}
				return true;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public void EnsureData()
		{
			if (_watch == null && _dataContainer != null)
			{
				RefreshData();
			}
		}

		public void RefreshData()
		{
			if (IsUnloaded)
			{
				return;
			}
			_needRefresh = false;
			CxvVariableTypeCode cxvVariableTypeCode = EnsureExposeAs();
			if (_watch == null && _dataContainer != null)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(EnsureDataPath()))
				{
					return;
				}
				ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = _dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
				if (cxsDataWatch == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				switch (cxvVariableTypeCode)
				{
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.Real:
					AddRealDataToWatch(cxsDataWatch);
					break;
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.String:
					AddStringDataToWatch(cxsDataWatch);
					break;
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.Integer:
					AddIntegerDataToWatch(cxsDataWatch);
					break;
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.Boolean:
					AddBooleanDataToWatch(cxsDataWatch);
					break;
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.Id:
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.RealId:
					AddIdDataToWatch(cxsDataWatch);
					break;
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.IdWithOptions:
					AddIdWithOptionsDataToWatch(cxsDataWatch);
					break;
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.IdTwoLevel:
					AddIdDataToWatch(cxsDataWatch, "2");
					break;
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.IdThreeLevel:
					AddIdDataToWatch(cxsDataWatch, "3");
					break;
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.Unknown:
					AddUnkownDataToWatch(cxsDataWatch);
					break;
				}
				UpdateWatch(cxsDataWatch);
			}
			if (_watch != null)
			{
				switch (cxvVariableTypeCode)
				{
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.Real:
					RefreshRealData();
					break;
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.String:
					RefreshStringData();
					break;
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.Integer:
					RefreshIntegerData();
					break;
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.Boolean:
					RefreshBooleanData();
					break;
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.Id:
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.IdTwoLevel:
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.IdThreeLevel:
					RefreshIdData();
					break;
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.RealId:
					RefreshRealIdData();
					break;
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.IdWithOptions:
					RefreshIdWithOptionsData();
					break;
				case CxvVariableTypeCode.Unknown:
					RefreshUnknownData();
					break;
				}
			}
			_needRefreshUI = true;
		}

		private void UpdateWatch(ICxsDataWatch watch)
		{
			if (watch == _watch)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_watch != null && _dataWatch_ValuesChangedHandler != null)
			{
				_watch.ValuesChanged -= _dataWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;
				_watch.Unload();
				_watch = null;
			}
			if (watch == null || watch.IsEmpty)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_dataWatch_ValuesChangedHandler == null)
			{
				_dataWatch_ValuesChangedHandler = delegate
				{
					RefreshData();
				};
			}
			_watch = watch;
			_watch.ValuesChanged += _dataWatch_ValuesChangedHandler;
		}

		private void AddRealDataToWatch(ICxsDataWatch watch)
		{
			string text = EnsureDataPath();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_basisPathResolved))
				{
					watch.AddWatch(text, "basis_options");
					watch.AddWatch(text, "basis");
					watch.AddWatch(text, "basis");
				}
				else
				{
					watch.AddWatch(_basisPathResolved, "option_list");
					watch.AddWatch(_basisPathResolved, "value");
					watch.AddWatch(text, "basis");
				}
				watch.AddWatch(text, "uom_options");
				watch.AddWatch(text, _property.UnitAttributeResolved);
				watch.AddWatch(text, _property.DataAttributeResolved);
			}
		}

		private void RefreshRealData()
		{
			object[] array = _watch.Values;
			if (array == null)
			{
				array = new object[6]
				{
					string.Empty,
					string.Empty,
					string.Empty,
					string.Empty,
					string.Empty,
					null
				};
			}
			RefreshBasisOptions(CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[0]), notify: true);
			RefreshBasis(CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[1]), CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[2]), notify: true);
			RefreshUnitsOptions(CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[3]), notify: true);
			RefreshUnits(CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[4]), notify: true);
			RefreshValue(CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(array[5]), notify: true);
			ResetUnitRow();
		}

		private void AddStringDataToWatch(ICxsDataWatch watch)
		{
			string text = EnsureDataPath();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return;
			}
			string dataAttributeResolved = _property.DataAttributeResolved;
			string text2 = _property.OptionsAttribute;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_optionsPathResolved))
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					text2 = "option_list";
				}
				watch.AddWatch(_optionsPathResolved, text2);
				watch.AddWatch(_optionsPathResolved, "option_prompts");
			}
			else
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					text2 = ((dataAttributeResolved == "value") ? "option_list" : (dataAttributeResolved + "_options"));
				}
				watch.AddWatch(text, text2);
				watch.AddWatch(text, "option_prompts");
			}
			watch.AddWatch(text, "user_define_id");
			watch.AddWatch(text, dataAttributeResolved);
		}

		private void RefreshStringData()
		{
			object[] array = _watch.Values ?? new object[3]
			{
				string.Empty,
				string.Empty,
				string.Empty
			};
			if (_property != null && _property.AllowNew.HasValue)
			{
				_canDefineNewId = _property.AllowNew.Value;
			}
			else
			{
				bool? flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(array[2]);
				_canDefineNewId = flag.HasValue && flag.Value;
			}
			string optionsString = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[0]);
			string optionPomptsString = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[1]);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(optionsString) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(optionPomptsString))
			{
				string[] array2 = optionsString.Split('\n');
				string[] array3 = optionPomptsString.Split('\n');
				if (array2 != null && array2.Length != 0)
				{
					int num = -1;
					for (int i = 0; i < array2.Length; i++)
					{
						if (string.Compare(array2[i], "PSValve") == 0)
						{
							num = i;
							break;
						}
					}
					if (num != -1 && array3 != null && array3.Length != 0 && array3.Length == array2.Length)
					{
						optionsString = string.Empty;
						optionPomptsString = string.Empty;
						for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
						{
							if (j != num)
							{
								optionsString = optionsString + array2[j] + "\n";
								optionPomptsString = optionPomptsString + array3[j] + "\n";
							}
						}
						optionsString.TrimEnd('\n');
						optionPomptsString.TrimEnd('\n');
					}
				}
			}
			CxvInternalHelpers.ReviseOptionAndOptionPrompt(ref optionsString, ref optionPomptsString);
			RefreshValueOptions(optionsString, notify: false);
			RefreshOptionPrompts(optionPomptsString, notify: false);
			RefreshValue(array[3], notify: false);
			OnPropertyChanged("ValueOptions");
			OnPropertyChanged("OptionPrompts");
			OnPropertyChanged("Value");
		}

		private void AddIntegerDataToWatch(ICxsDataWatch watch)
		{
			string text = EnsureDataPath();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				watch.AddWatch(text, _property.DataAttributeResolved);
			}
		}

		{
			object[] values = _watch.Values;
			int? num = null;
			if (values != null)
			{
				num = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(values[0]);
			}
			RefreshValue(num, notify: true);
		}

				watch.AddWatch(text, _property.DataAttributeResolved);

		}

			bool? flag = null;
			if (values != null)

				flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(values[0]);

		}

					watch.AddWatch(text, _property.DataAttributeResolved);
				}
				string path = CxsPath.ParentPath(text);
				watch.AddWatch(path, "child_lower_limit");
				watch.AddWatch(path, "child_upper_limit");
			}
		}
