				watch.AddWatch(path, "child_id_prompts");
			}
		}

		private void RefreshIdData()
		{
			object[] values = _watch.Values;
			string value = string.Empty;
			if (values != null)
			{
				value = CxsConverter.ValueToString(values[0]);
			}
			int? num = null;
			if (values != null)
			{
				num = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(values[1]);
			}
			int? num2 = null;
			if (values != null)
			{
				num2 = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(values[2]);
			}
			if (num.HasValue)
			{
				RefreshLowerLimit(num);
			}
			if (num2.HasValue)
			{
				RefreshUpperLimit(num2);
			}
			RefreshValue(value, notify: true);
		}

		private void RefreshRealIdData()
		{
			object[] values = _watch.Values;
			double? num = null;
			if (values != null)
			{
				num = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(values[0]);
			}
			double? lower = null;
			if (values != null)
			{
				lower = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(values[1]);
			}
			double? upper = null;
			if (values != null)
			{
				upper = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(values[2]);
			}
			if (lower.HasValue)
			{
				RefreshLowerLimit(lower);
			}
			if (upper.HasValue)
			{
				RefreshUpperLimit(upper);
			}
			RefreshValue(num, notify: true);
		}

		private void RefreshIdWithOptionsData()
		{
			object[] values = _watch.Values;
			string value = string.Empty;
			if (values != null)
			{
				value = CxsConverter.ValueToString(values[0]);
			}
			string optionsString = string.Empty;
			if (values != null)
			{
				optionsString = CxsConverter.ValueToString(values[1]);
			}
			string optionPomptsString = string.Empty;
			if (values != null)
			{
				optionPomptsString = CxsConverter.ValueToString(values[4]);
			}
			int? num = null;
			if (values != null)
			{
				num = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(values[2]);
			}
			int? num2 = null;
			if (values != null)
			{
				num2 = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(values[3]);
			}
			if (num.HasValue)
			{
				RefreshLowerLimit(num);
			}
			if (num2.HasValue)
			{
				RefreshUpperLimit(num2);
			}
			CxvInternalHelpers.ReviseOptionAndOptionPrompt(ref optionsString, ref optionPomptsString);
			RefreshValueOptions(optionsString, notify: false);
			if (values != null)
			{
				RefreshOptionPrompts(optionPomptsString, notify: false);
			}
			RefreshValue(value, notify: false);
			OnPropertyChanged("OptionPrompts");
			OnPropertyChanged("ValueOptions");
			OnPropertyChanged("Value");
		}

		private void AddUnkownDataToWatch(ICxsDataWatch watch)
		{
			string text = EnsureDataPath();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_basisPathResolved))
				{
					watch.AddWatch(text, "basis_options");
					watch.AddWatch(text, "basis");
				}
				else
				{
					watch.AddWatch(_basisPathResolved, "option_list");
					watch.AddWatch(_basisPathResolved, "value");
				}
			}
		}

		private void RefreshUnknownData()
		{
			object[] array = _watch.Values ?? new object[5]
			{
				string.Empty,
				string.Empty,
				null,
				null,
				null
			};
			RefreshBasisOptions(CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[0]), notify: true);
			RefreshBasis(CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[1]), null, notify: true);
		}

		private void RefreshValue(object value, bool notify)
		{
			if (_value != value || value == null)
			{
				_value = value;
				if (notify)
				{
					OnPropertyChanged("Value");
				}
			}
		}

		private void RefreshValueOptions(string optionsString, bool notify)
		{
			_inNew = false;
			if (!(optionsString != _valueOptionsString) && optionsString != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			_valueOptionsString = optionsString;
			ObservableCollection<string> observableCollection = null;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(optionsString))
			{
				observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<string>();
				aryOriginalOptions = optionsString.Split('\n');
				string[] array = aryOriginalOptions;
				foreach (string item in array)
				{
					if (!observableCollection.Contains(item))
					{
						observableCollection.Add(item);
					}
				}
			}
			if (_canDefineNewId && optionsString != null)
			{
				if (observableCollection == null)
				{
					observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<string>();
				}
				observableCollection.Add(Resources.CreateNewMenuItem);
			}
			_valueOptions = observableCollection;
			_convertedValueOptions = null;
			if (notify)
			{
				OnPropertyChanged("ValueOptions");
			}
		}

		private void RefreshOptionPrompts(string optionPromptsString, bool notify)
		{
			if (!(optionPromptsString != _optionPromptsString) && optionPromptsString != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			_optionPromptsString = optionPromptsString;
			Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = null;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(optionPromptsString))
			{
				dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
				string[] array = optionPromptsString.Split('\n');
				if (aryOriginalOptions != null)
				{
					for (int i = 0; i < aryOriginalOptions.Length; i++)
					{
						if (!dictionary.ContainsKey(array[i]))
						{
							dictionary[aryOriginalOptions[i]] = array[i];
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (_canDefineNewId && optionPromptsString != null)
			{
				if (dictionary == null)
				{
					dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
				}
				dictionary[Resources.CreateNewMenuItem] = Resources.CreateNewMenuItemPrompt;
			}
			if (dictionary != null && _convertCase.HasValue)
			{
				if (_convertedValueOptions == null)
				{
					_optionPrompts = dictionary;
				}
				else
				{
					for (int j = 0; j < _valueOptions.Count; j++)
					{
						if (dictionary.ContainsKey(_valueOptions[j]) && !dictionary.ContainsKey(_convertedValueOptions[j]))
						{
							dictionary.Add(_convertedValueOptions[j], dictionary[_valueOptions[j]]);
						}
					}
					_optionPrompts = dictionary;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				_optionPrompts = dictionary;
			}
			if (notify)
			{
				OnPropertyChanged("OptionPrompts");
			}
		}

		private void RefreshUnits(string units, bool notify)
		{
			if (_units != units || units == null)
			{
				_units = units;
				if (notify)
				{
					OnPropertyChanged("Units");
				}
			}
		}

		private void RefreshUnitsOptions(string optionsString, bool notify)
		{
			if (!(optionsString != _unitsOptionsString) && optionsString != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			_unitsOptionsString = optionsString;
			ObservableCollection<string> observableCollection = null;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(optionsString))
			{
				observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<string>();
				string[] array = optionsString.Split('\n');
				foreach (string item in array)
				{
					observableCollection.Add(item);
				}
			}
			_unitsOptions = observableCollection;
			if (notify)
			{
				OnPropertyChanged("UnitsOptions");
			}
		}

		private void RefreshBasis(string basis, string fallbackBasis, bool notify)
		{
			if (_basis != basis || basis == null)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(basis) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(fallbackBasis))
				{
					_basis = fallbackBasis;
				}
				else
				{
					_basis = basis;
				}
				if (notify)
				{
					OnPropertyChanged("Basis");
				}
			}
		}

		private void RefreshBasisOptions(string optionsString, bool notify)
		{
			if (!(optionsString != _basisOptionsString) && optionsString != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			_basisOptionsString = optionsString;
			ObservableCollection<string> observableCollection = null;
			ObservableCollection<string> observableCollection2 = null;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(optionsString))
			{
				observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<string>();
				observableCollection2 = new ObservableCollection<string>();
				string[] array = optionsString.Split('\n');
				foreach (string text in array)
				{
					observableCollection.Add(text);
					string text2 = CxvInternalHelpers.LocalizeString(_dataContainer, text, "BasisOptions_" + CxvInternalHelpers.ReplaceSpecialChars(text));
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
					{
						observableCollection2.Add(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture.TextInfo.ToTitleCase(text2.ToLower(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture)));
					}
				}
			}
			_basisOptions = observableCollection;
			_localizedBasisOptions = observableCollection2;
			if (notify)
			{
				OnPropertyChanged("BasisOptions");
			}
		}

		private void ResetAllData()
		{
			UpdateWatch(null);
			_lowerlimit = null;
			_upperlimit = null;
			_description = null;
			_lowerLimitValue = null;
			_upperLimitValue = null;
			RefreshUnitsOptions(null, notify: false);
			RefreshUnits(null, notify: false);
			RefreshBasisOptions(null, notify: false);
			RefreshBasis(null, null, notify: false);
			RefreshValueOptions(null, notify: false);
			RefreshValue(null, notify: true);
			OnPropertyChanged("ValueOptions");
			OnPropertyChanged("Basis");
			OnPropertyChanged("BasisOptions");
			OnPropertyChanged("Units");
			OnPropertyChanged("UnitsOptions");
			OnPropertyChanged("LowerLimit");
			OnPropertyChanged("UpperLimit");
			ResetUnitRow();
		}

		private CxvVariableTypeCode EnsureExposeAs()
		{
			if (_exposeAsDataWatch == null && _dataContainer != null)
			{
				RefreshExposeAs();
			}
			if (!_exposeAsResolved.HasValue)
			{
				return CxvVariableTypeCode.Unknown;
			}
			return _exposeAsResolved.Value;
		}

		private void RefreshExposeAs()
		{
			if (IsUnloaded)
			{
				return;
			}
			UpdateExposeAsDataWatch(null);
			CxvVariableTypeCode cxvVariableTypeCode = _property.ExposeAs;
			if (cxvVariableTypeCode == CxvVariableTypeCode.Unknown)
			{
				if (_exposeAsDataWatch == null && _dataContainer != null)
				{
					string text = EnsureDataPath();
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						return;
					}
					ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = _dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
					if (cxsDataWatch == null)
					{
						return;
					}
					cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "type");
					cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "dimension");
					cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "description");
					cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "upper_limit");
					cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "lower_limit");
					cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "underlying_path");
					UpdateExposeAsDataWatch(cxsDataWatch);
				}
				if (_exposeAsDataWatch != null)
				{
					object[] values = _exposeAsDataWatch.Values;
					if (values != null)
					{
						string text2 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(values[0]);
						_dimension = CxsConverter.ValueToString(values[1]);
						if (values[2] != null)
						{
							string text3 = CxvInternalHelpers.ValueToStringRemoveXml(values[2]);
							if (!CxvHelpers.ValuesEquivalent(_description, text3))
							{
								_description = text3;
								OnPropertyChanged("VariablePrompt");
							}
						}
						if (values[3] != null && !CxvHelpers.ValuesEquivalent(_upperLimitValue, values[3]))
						{
							_upperlimit = null;
							_upperLimitValue = values[3];
							OnPropertyChanged("UpperLimit");
						}
						if (values[4] != null && !CxvHelpers.ValuesEquivalent(_lowerLimitValue, values[4]))
						{
							_lowerlimit = null;
							_lowerLimitValue = values[4];
							OnPropertyChanged("LowerLimit");
						}
						if (values[5] != null)
						{
							_alternatePath = CxsConverter.ValueToString(values[5]);
						}
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
						{
							switch (text2.ToUpperInvariant())
							{
							case "REAL":
								cxvVariableTypeCode = CxvVariableTypeCode.Real;
								break;
							case "STRING":
							case "TEXT":
								cxvVariableTypeCode = CxvVariableTypeCode.String;
								break;
							case "INTEGER":
								cxvVariableTypeCode = CxvVariableTypeCode.Integer;
								break;
							case "BOOLEAN":
							case "ENUMBOOLEAN":
								cxvVariableTypeCode = CxvVariableTypeCode.Boolean;
								break;
							case "ARRAY":
								if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dimension))
								{
									cxvVariableTypeCode = CxvVariableTypeCode.Real;
								}
								_isLeaf = false;
								break;
							}
							if (cxvVariableTypeCode == CxvVariableTypeCode.Unknown && string.Compare(text2, 0, "Enum", 0, 4, StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
							{
								cxvVariableTypeCode = CxvVariableTypeCode.String;
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (cxvVariableTypeCode != _exposeAsResolved)
			{
				CxvVariableTypeCode? exposeAsResolved = _exposeAsResolved;
				_exposeAsResolved = cxvVariableTypeCode;
				if (exposeAsResolved.HasValue)
				{
					ResetAllData();
					OnPropertyChanged("ValueType");
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateExposeAsDataWatch(ICxsDataWatch dataWatch)
		{
			if (_exposeAsDataWatch == dataWatch)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_exposeAsDataWatch != null)
			{
				if (_exposeAsDependencies_EventHandler != null)
				{
					_exposeAsDataWatch.ValuesChanged -= _exposeAsDependencies_EventHandler;
				}
				_exposeAsDataWatch.Unload();
				_exposeAsDataWatch = null;
			}
			if (dataWatch == null || dataWatch.IsEmpty)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_exposeAsDependencies_EventHandler == null)
			{
				_exposeAsDependencies_EventHandler = delegate
				{
					RefreshExposeAs();
				};
			}
			_exposeAsDataWatch = dataWatch;
			_exposeAsDataWatch.ValuesChanged += _exposeAsDependencies_EventHandler;
		}

		private void ResetExposeAs()
		{
			UpdateExposeAsDataWatch(null);
			_exposeAsResolved = null;
			_dimension = null;
			ResetAllData();
			OnPropertyChanged("ValueType");
		}

		private bool EnsureIsReadOnly()
		{
			if (_readOnlyDataWatch == null && _dataContainer != null)
			{
				RefreshIsReadOnly();
			}
			if (_property != null && (_property.ReadOnlyIfProxy ?? false) && !(_readOnlyResolved ?? false))
			{
				return CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(GetDataAttributeValue("IsProxy", _dataPathResolved, "is_proxy")) ?? false;
			}
			if (!_readOnlyResolved.HasValue)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return _readOnlyResolved.Value;
		}

		private void RefreshIsReadOnly()
		{
			if (IsUnloaded)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool? flag = _property.IsReadOnly;
			bool? flag2 = _property.IsEntered;
			bool? flag3 = _property.IsCalculated;
			bool? flag4 = null;
			if (!flag.HasValue)
			{
				if (_readOnlyDataWatch == null && _dataContainer != null)
				{
					string text = EnsureDataPath();
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						return;
					}
					ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = _dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
					if (cxsDataWatch == null)
					{
						return;
					}
					if (_property.ExposeAs == CxvVariableTypeCode.Id || _property.ExposeAs == CxvVariableTypeCode.RealId)
					{
						cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "can_enter_id");
						cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "user_entered_id");
						cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "calculated");
					}
					else if (_property.ExposeAs == CxvVariableTypeCode.IdWithOptions)
					{
						cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "can_enter_id");
						cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "user_entered_id");
						cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "calculated");
					}
					else
					{
						cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "can_enter");
						cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "user_entered");
						cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "calculated");
						cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "is_unit_default");
					}
					UpdateIsReadOnlyDataWatch(cxsDataWatch);
				}
				if (_readOnlyDataWatch != null)
				{
					object[] values = _readOnlyDataWatch.Values;
					if (values == null)
					{
						flag = true;
					}
					else
					{
						bool? flag5 = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(values[0]);
						flag = !flag5.HasValue || !flag5.Value;
						if (!flag2.HasValue)
						{
							flag2 = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(values[1]);
						}
						if (!flag3.HasValue)
						{
							flag3 = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(values[2]);
							if (!flag3.HasValue && flag.HasValue && !flag.Value && (CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(GetDataAttributeValue("IsProxy", _dataPathResolved, "is_proxy")) ?? false))
							{
								flag3 = false;
							}
						}
						if (values.Length > 3)
						{
							flag4 = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(values[3]);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (_property.IsReadOnly.HasValue)
			{
				flag = _property.IsReadOnly.Value;
			}
			if (flag != _readOnlyResolved)
			{
				_readOnlyResolved = flag;
				OnPropertyChanged("IsReadOnly");
			}
			if (flag2 != _isEntered)
			{
				_isEntered = flag2;
				OnPropertyChanged("IsEntered");
			}
			if (flag3 != _isCalculated)
			{
				_isCalculated = flag3;
				OnPropertyChanged("IsCalculated");
			}
			if (flag4 != _isDefaultUnit)
			{
				_isDefaultUnit = flag4;
				OnPropertyChanged("IsDefaultUnit");
			}
		}

		private void UpdateIsReadOnlyDataWatch(ICxsDataWatch dataWatch)
		{
			if (dataWatch == _readOnlyDataWatch)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_readOnlyDataWatch != null)
			{
				if (_isReadOnlyDependencies_EventHandler != null)
				{
					_readOnlyDataWatch.ValuesChanged -= _isReadOnlyDependencies_EventHandler;
				}
				_readOnlyDataWatch.Unload();
				_readOnlyDataWatch = null;
			}
			if (dataWatch == null || dataWatch.IsEmpty)
			{
				return;
			}
			_readOnlyDataWatch = dataWatch;
			if (_isReadOnlyDependencies_EventHandler == null)
			{
				_isReadOnlyDependencies_EventHandler = delegate
				{
					RefreshIsReadOnly();
				};
			}
			dataWatch.ValuesChanged += _isReadOnlyDependencies_EventHandler;
		}

		private void ResetReadOnly()
		{
			if (!IsUnloaded)
			{
				UpdateIsReadOnlyDataWatch(null);
				_readOnlyResolved = null;
				OnPropertyChanged("IsReadOnly");
			}
		}

		private string EnsureDataPath()
		{
			if (IsUnloaded)
			{
				return _dataPathResolved;
			}
			if ((string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dataPathResolved) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.DataPath)) || (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_basisPathResolved) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.BasisPath)) || (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_optionsPathResolved) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.OptionsPath)))
			{
				ResolveDataPath();
			}
			return _dataPathResolved;
		}

		private void ResolveDataPath()
		{
			if (!IsUnloaded && (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dataPathResolved) || !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_basisPathResolved) || !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_optionsPathResolved) || !_unresolvedPath))
			{
				UpdateDataPathDependencies(null);
				CxvPropertyWatch cxvPropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.DataPath))
				{
					_dataPathResolved = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.DataPath, cxvPropertyWatch);
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.BasisPath))
				{
					_basisPathResolved = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.BasisPath, cxvPropertyWatch);
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.OptionsPath))
				{
					_optionsPathResolved = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.OptionsPath, cxvPropertyWatch);
				}
				UpdateDataPathDependencies(cxvPropertyWatch);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dataPathResolved) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(_basisPathResolved) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(_optionsPathResolved))
				{
					_unresolvedPath = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateDataPathDependencies(CxvPropertyWatch dataPathDependencies)
		{
			if (dataPathDependencies == _dataPathDependencies)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_dataPathDependencies != null)
			{
				if (_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler != null)
				{
					_dataPathDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= _pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				}
				_dataPathDependencies.Clear();
				_dataPathDependencies = null;
			}
			if (dataPathDependencies == null || dataPathDependencies.IsEmpty)
			{
				return;
			}
			_dataPathDependencies = dataPathDependencies;
			if (_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler == null)
			{
				_pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler = delegate
				{
					ResetDataPath();
				};
			}
			_dataPathDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += _pathDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
		}

		private void ResetDataPath()
		{
			UpdateDataPathDependencies(null);
			_dataPathResolved = null;
			_basisPathResolved = null;
			_optionsPathResolved = null;
			_unresolvedPath = false;
			ResetReadOnly();
			UpdateDataAttributeValue("VariablePrompt");
			UpdateDataAttributeValue("DisabledPrompt");
			ResetExposeAs();
			OnPropertyChanged("CxsItemMoniker");
		}

		public void Unload()
		{
			_property = null;
			_dataContainer = null;
			UpdateDataPathDependencies(null);
			UpdateExposeAsDataWatch(null);
			UpdateIsReadOnlyDataWatch(null);
			UpdateWatch(null);
		}

		XElement IViewDataSerializable.WriteXElement()
		{
			List<XElement> list = new List<XElement>();
			object value = Value;
			if (value != null)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("Value", value.ToString()));
			}
			if (ValueOptions is ObservableCollection<string> observableCollection)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("ValueOptions", observableCollection.Select((string vo) => new XElement("string", vo)), new XAttribute("Count", observableCollection.Count)));
			}
			string units = Units;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(units))
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("Units", units));
			}
			ObservableCollection<string> unitsOptions = UnitsOptions;
			if (unitsOptions != null)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("UnitsOptions", unitsOptions.Select((string uo) => new XElement("string", uo)), new XAttribute("Count", unitsOptions.Count)));
			}
			string basis = Basis;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(basis))
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("Basis", basis));
			}
			ObservableCollection<string> basisOptions = BasisOptions;
			if (basisOptions != null)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("BasisOptions", basisOptions.Select((string bo) => new XElement("string", bo)), new XAttribute("Count", basisOptions.Count)));
			}
			Dictionary<string, string> optionPrompts = OptionPrompts;
			if (optionPrompts != null)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("OptionPrompts", optionPrompts.Select(delegate(KeyValuePair<string, string> item)
				{
					XName name = "string";
					KeyValuePair<string, string> keyValuePair = item;
					string key = keyValuePair.Key;
					keyValuePair = item;
					return new XElement(name, key + ", " + keyValuePair.Value);
				}), new XAttribute("Count", optionPrompts.Count)));
			}
			string label = Label;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(label))
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("Label", label));
			}
			Type valueType = ValueType;
			if (valueType != null)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("ValueType", valueType));
			}
			string dimension = Dimension;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(dimension))
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("Dimension", dimension));
			}
			double? lowerLimit = LowerLimit;
			if (lowerLimit.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("LowerLimit", lowerLimit.Value));
			}
			double? upperLimit = UpperLimit;
			if (upperLimit.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("UpperLimit", upperLimit.Value));
			}
			bool isReadOnly = IsReadOnly;
			bool? isCalculated = IsCalculated;
			bool? isEntered = IsEntered;
			list.Add(new XElement("IsReadOnly", isReadOnly));
			if (isEntered.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("IsEntered", isEntered.Value));
			}
			if (isCalculated.HasValue)
			{
				list.Add(new XElement("IsCalculated", isCalculated.Value));
			}
			return new XElement(GetType().Name, list);
		}

		protected virtual string GetCustomedTooltipText()
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_property.ToolTipTextLocalized))
			{
				return _property.ToolTipTextLocalized;
			}
			return null;
		}

		private void RefreshUpperLimit(double? upper)
		{
			if (!ValueCompare(upper, _upperlimit))
			{
				_upperlimit = upper;
				OnPropertyChanged("UpperLimit");
			}
		}

		private void RefreshLowerLimit(double? lower)
		{
			if (!ValueCompare(lower, _lowerlimit))
			{
				_lowerlimit = lower;
				OnPropertyChanged("LowerLimit");
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			if (string.Equals(propertyName, "Value", StringComparison.InvariantCulture))
			{
				base.OnPropertyChanged("QuickValue");
			}
			base.OnPropertyChanged(propertyName);
		}

		object IQuickValueReadable.GetQuickValue(out bool? canEnter, out bool? isDefaulted)
		{
			string dataAttributeResolved = _property.DataAttributeResolved;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(dataAttributeResolved))
			{
				if (string.Equals(dataAttributeResolved, "value", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) || string.Equals(dataAttributeResolved, "display_name", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					IHappDataNode happDataNode = FindHappDataNode();
					if (happDataNode != null)
					{
						canEnter = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(happDataNode.GetAttributeValue(HappDataNodeAttribute.Enterable));
						if (canEnter == true)
						{
							isDefaulted = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(happDataNode.GetAttributeValue(HappDataNodeAttribute.ValueDefaulted));
						}
						else
						{
							isDefaulted = false;
						}
						if (string.Equals(dataAttributeResolved, "value", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
						{
							return TryConvertValue(happDataNode?.Value);
						}
						if (string.Equals(dataAttributeResolved, "display_name", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
						{
							return happDataNode.Name;
						}
					}
				}
				if (_dataContainer != null)
				{
					CxsDataProvider resolvedCxsProvider = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider;
					if (resolvedCxsProvider != null)
					{
						CxsCase cxsCase = resolvedCxsProvider.Case;
						if (cxsCase != null)
						{
							string text = EnsureDataPath();
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
							{
								canEnter = (isDefaulted = null);
								return TryConvertValue(cxsCase.BatchGet(text, dataAttributeResolved));
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			canEnter = (isDefaulted = null);
			return null;
		}

		void IQuickValueEditable.QuickUpdate(object value)
		{
			string dataAttributeResolved = _property.DataAttributeResolved;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(dataAttributeResolved))
			{
				return;
			}
			object value2 = TryConvertValueBack(value);
			if (string.Equals(dataAttributeResolved, "value", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				IHappDataNode happDataNode = FindHappDataNode();
				if (happDataNode != null)
				{
					happDataNode.Value = value2;
					return;
				}
			}
			if (_dataContainer == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsDataProvider resolvedCxsProvider = _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider;
			if (resolvedCxsProvider == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsCase cxsCase = resolvedCxsProvider.Case;
			if (cxsCase != null)
			{
				string text = EnsureDataPath();
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					cxsCase.BatchSet(text, dataAttributeResolved, value2);
				}
			}
		}

		private object TryConvertValue(object value)
		{
			if (value != null && _property != null && _property.Converter != null)
			{
				IValueConverter valueConverter = _property.ValueConverter(_dataContainer);
				if (valueConverter != null)
				{
					return valueConverter.Convert(value, typeof(object), null, null);
				}
			}
			return value;
		}

		private object TryConvertValueBack(object newValue)
		{
			if (_property != null && _property.Converter != null && (newValue is string || (_property.CallConverterEvenWhenValueIsNull && newValue == null)))
			{
				IValueConverter valueConverter = _property.ValueConverter(_dataContainer);
				if (valueConverter != null)
				{
					return valueConverter.ConvertBack(newValue, typeof(object), null, null);
				}
			}
			return newValue;
		}

		private IHappDataNode FindHappDataNode()
		{
			return ResolvedHappDataAccessor?.GetHappNode(_dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(_property.DataPath));
		}
	}
	public class CxvVectorPropertyData : CxvStatePropertyData, IBindingList, IList, ICollection, IEnumerable, INotifyPropertyChanged, INotifyCollectionChanged, ITypedList, ICxvPropertyData, IViewDataSerializable
	{
		internal class Enumerator : IEnumerator
		{
			private CxvVectorPropertyData _data;

			private int _index;

			public object Current
			{
				get
				{
					if (_index < 0 || _index >= _data.Count)
					{
						throw new InvalidOperationException();
					}
					return _data[_index];
				}
			}

			internal Enumerator(CxvVectorPropertyData data)
			{
				_data = data;
				_index = -1;
			}

			public bool MoveNext()
			{
				_index++;
				if (_index >= 0)
				{
					return _index < _data.Count;
				}
				return false;
			}

			public void Reset()
			{
				_index = -1;
			}
		}

		internal class ItemContext : ICxvViewDataContainer
		{
			private EventHandler _valueDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;

			private EventHandler _keyDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;

			internal int _index;

			internal object _indexKey;

			internal CxvVectorPropertyData _vector;

			internal CxvPropertyWatch _valueDependencies;

			internal CxvPropertyWatch _keyDependencies;

			internal object _value;

			public IUnityContainer UnityContainer
			{
				get
				{
					if (_vector._dataContainer == null)
					{
						return null;
					}
					return _vector._dataContainer.UnityContainer;
				}
			}

			public CxsDataProvider ResolvedCxsProvider => _vector._dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider;

			internal string IndexParameterName
			{
				get
				{
					string text = _vector._vectorProperty.IndexBasis.Name;
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						text = "row";
					}
					return text;
				}
			}

			public object IndexKey
			{
				get
				{
					if (_indexKey != null && _indexKey == DBNull.Value)
					{
						CxvPropertyWatch cxvPropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
						_indexKey = _vector.GetIndexSubstitution(_index, cxvPropertyWatch);
						UpdateKeyDependencies(cxvPropertyWatch);
					}
					return _indexKey;
				}
			}

			public object Value
			{
				get
				{
					if (_value != null && _value == DBNull.Value)
					{
						CxvPropertyWatch cxvPropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
						if (_vector._vectorProperty.ItemType != null)
						{
							IUnityContainer unityContainer = _vector._dataContainer.UnityContainer;
							if (unityContainer != null)
							{
								_value = unityContainer.Resolve(_vector._vectorProperty.ItemType);
							}
						}
						else
						{
							_value = _vector._vectorProperty.ItemTemplate.GetPropertyValue(this, cxvPropertyWatch);
						}
						if (_value is CxvViewModel cxvViewModel)
						{
							cxvViewModel.ParentVectorInfo = (Vector: _vector, RowIndex: _index);
							cxvViewModel.Parent = _vector._dataContainer;
							cxvViewModel.NotifyUpdates = false;
							cxvViewModel.ResolveUpdateParameter(IndexParameterName, IndexKey, localOnly: true);
							cxvViewModel.UpdateData();
						}
						UpdateValueDependencies(cxvPropertyWatch);
					}
					return _value;
				}
			}

			private EventHandler valueDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler
			{
				get
				{
					if (_valueDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler == null)
					{
						_valueDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler = delegate
						{
							ResetValue();
						};
					}
					return _valueDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				}
			}

			private EventHandler keyDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler
			{
				get
				{
					if (_keyDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler == null)
					{
						_keyDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler = delegate
						{
							ResetIndexKey();
						};
					}
					return _keyDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				}
			}

			public event EventHandler<SubPropertyDataChangingEventArgs> SubPropertyDataChanging;

			public event EventHandler<SubPropertyDataChangedEventArgs> SubPropertyDataChanged;

			internal ItemContext(CxvVectorPropertyData vector, int index)
			{
				_vector = vector;
				_index = index;
				_indexKey = DBNull.Value;
				_value = DBNull.Value;
			}

			public object ResolveResource(string key)
			{
				if (_vector._dataContainer == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _vector._dataContainer.ResolveResource(key);
			}

			public object ResolveResource(string key, string resourceType)
			{
				if (_vector._dataContainer == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _vector._dataContainer.ResolveResource(key, resourceType);
			}

			public ICxsDataWatch CreateCxsWatch()
			{
				return _vector._dataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
			}

			public string ExpandDataPath(string dataPath, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
			{
				return CxvViewModel.ExpandDataPath(dataPath, this, dependencies);
			}

			public object ResolveParameter(string parameter, object defaultValue, bool localOnly, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
			{
				if (string.Compare(parameter, IndexParameterName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					return IndexKey;
				}
				return _vector._dataContainer.ResolveParameter(parameter, defaultValue, localOnly, dependencies);
			}

			public void ResolveUpdateParameter(string parameter, object value, bool localOnly)
			{
				if (string.Compare(parameter, IndexParameterName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException("index parameter is read only");
				}
				if (_vector._dataContainer == null)
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException("Unable to resolve the parameter");
				}
				_vector._dataContainer.ResolveUpdateParameter(parameter, value, localOnly);
			}

			public object ResolveViewPropertyPath(string path, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
			{
				if (_vector._dataContainer == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _vector._dataContainer.ResolveViewPropertyPath(path, dependencies);
			}

			public void ResolveUpdateViewPropertyPath(string path, object value)
			{
				if (_vector._dataContainer == null)
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException("Unable to resolve the property path");
				}
				_vector._dataContainer.ResolveUpdateViewPropertyPath(path, value);
			}

			public void ShowMessageBox(ShowMessageBoxEventArgs args)
			{
				if (_vector._dataContainer != null)
				{
					_vector._dataContainer.ShowMessageBox(args);
				}
			}

			public void GetUserInput(GetUserInputEventArgs args)
			{
				if (_vector._dataContainer != null)
				{
					_vector._dataContainer.GetUserInput(args);
				}
			}

			public void GetCustomUserInput(GetCustomUserInputEventArgs args)
			{
				if (_vector._dataContainer != null)
				{
					_vector._dataContainer.GetCustomUserInput(args);
				}
			}

			public void OnSubPropertyDataChanged(CxvPropertyDeclaration pd, string subPropertyName)
			{
				_vector.OnItemPropertyChanged(new CxvVectorItemPropertyChangedEventArgs(_index, pd.Metadata, subPropertyName));
				this.SubPropertyDataChanged?.Invoke(this, new SubPropertyDataChangedEventArgs(pd?.Metadata, subPropertyName));
				if (_vector._dataContainer != null)
				{
					_vector._dataContainer.OnSubPropertyDataChanged(pd, subPropertyName);
				}
			}

			public void OnSubPropertyDataChanging(SubPropertyDataChangingEventArgs args)
			{
				CxvVectorItemPropertyChangingEventArgs e = new CxvVectorItemPropertyChangingEventArgs(_index, args.Property, args.SubPropertyName, args.OldValue, args.NewValue);
				_vector.OnItemPropertyChanging(e);
				args.Cancel = e.Cancel;
				if (!args.Cancel)
				{
					this.SubPropertyDataChanging?.Invoke(this, args);
				}
			}

			public void ResetValue()
			{
				object value = _value;
				_value = DBNull.Value;
				UpdateValueDependencies(null);
				if (_vector.HaveCollectionChangedHandlers)
				{
					object value2 = Value;
					_vector.OnCollectionChanged(new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace, value, value2));
				}
			}

			public void ResetIndexKey()
			{
				_indexKey = DBNull.Value;
				ResetValue();
			}

			private void UpdateValueDependencies(CxvPropertyWatch newDependencies)
			{
				if (newDependencies != _valueDependencies)
				{
					if (_valueDependencies != null)
					{
						_valueDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= valueDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
						_valueDependencies.Clear();
						_valueDependencies = null;
					}
					if (newDependencies != null && !newDependencies.IsEmpty)
					{
						_valueDependencies = newDependencies;
						_valueDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += valueDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
					}
				}
			}

			private void UpdateKeyDependencies(CxvPropertyWatch newDependencies)
			{
				if (newDependencies != _keyDependencies)
				{
					if (_keyDependencies != null)
					{
						_keyDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= keyDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
						_keyDependencies.Clear();
						_keyDependencies = null;
					}
					if (newDependencies != null && !newDependencies.IsEmpty)
					{
						_keyDependencies = newDependencies;
						_keyDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += keyDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
					}
				}
			}

			internal void Unload()
			{
				if (_vector != null)
				{
					_vector = null;
					if (_value is CxvViewModel cxvViewModel)
					{
						cxvViewModel.Unload();
					}
					_value = null;
					_indexKey = null;
					UpdateKeyDependencies(null);
					UpdateValueDependencies(null);
				}
			}
		}

		public int IndexOf(object value)
		{
			foreach (int key in _itemDictionary.Keys)
			{
				if (_itemDictionary[key] == value)
				{
					return key;
				}
			}
			int num = -1;
			IEnumerator enumerator2 = GetEnumerator();
			try
			{
				while (enumerator2.MoveNext())
				{
					object current2 = enumerator2.Current;
					num++;
					if (current2 == value)
					{
						return num;
					}
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				IDisposable disposable = enumerator2 as IDisposable;
				if (disposable != null)
				{
					disposable.Dispose();
				}
			}
			return -1;
		}

		public PropertyDescriptorCollection GetItemProperties(PropertyDescriptor[] listAccessors)
		{
			if (listAccessors != null && listAccessors.Length != 0)
			{
				Type propertyType = listAccessors[listAccessors.Length - 1].PropertyType;
				if (propertyType != null)
				{
					Type elementType = propertyType.GetElementType();
					if (elementType != null)
					{
						return TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(elementType);
					}
				}
			}
			object obj = EnsureDataItem(0);
			if (obj == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(obj);
		}

		public string GetListName(PropertyDescriptor[] listAccessors)
		{
			string text = "";
			if (listAccessors != null)
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				foreach (PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor in listAccessors)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(propertyDescriptor.PropertyType.Name);
					stringBuilder.Append("_");
				}
				return stringBuilder.ToString().TrimEnd('_');
			}
			return GetType().Name;
		}

		private void ResetIndexBasis()
		{
			if (_indexBasis != null)
			{
				_indexBasis = null;
				_indexBasis.CountChanged -= _indexBasis_CountChangedHandler;
			}
			ResetItems();
			OnPropertyChanged("Count");
			OnCollectionChanged(new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset));
			OnListChanged(new ListChangedEventArgs(ListChangedType.Reset, 0));
		}

		private void RefreshIndexBasis()
		{
			if (_indexBasisDependencies != null)
			{
				_indexBasisDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged -= indexBasisDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
				_indexBasisDependencies = null;
			}
			CxvPropertyWatch cxvPropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
			_indexBasis = _vectorProperty.IndexBasis.CreateData(_dataContainer, cxvPropertyWatch);
			if (_indexBasis != null)
			{
				_indexBasis.CountChanged += indexBasis_CountChangedHandler;
			}
			if (!cxvPropertyWatch.IsEmpty)
			{
				_indexBasisDependencies = cxvPropertyWatch;
				_indexBasisDependencies.PropertyDependencyChanged += indexBasisDependencies_PropertyDependencyChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		private object EnsureDataItem(int index)
		{
			ItemContext value = null;
			if (!_itemDictionary.TryGetValue(index, out value))
			{
				value = new ItemContext(this, index);
				_itemDictionary[index] = value;
			}
			return value.Value;
		}

		protected object GetIndexSubstitution(int index, CxvPropertyWatch dependencies)
		{
			if (_indexBasis == null)
			{
				RefreshIndexBasis();
			}
			if (_indexBasis == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return _indexBasis.GetIndexSubstitution(index, dependencies);
		}

		private void ResetItems()
		{
			foreach (ItemContext value in _itemDictionary.Values)
			{
				value.Unload();
			}
			_itemDictionary.Clear();
		}

		public void Unload()
		{
			if (_dataContainer != null)
			{
				ResetItems();
				if (_indexBasis != null)
				{
					_indexBasis.Unload();
				}
				IsUnloaded = true;
			}
		}

		XElement IViewDataSerializable.WriteXElement()
		{
			List<object> list = new List<object>();
			for (int i = 0; i < Count; i++)
			{
				list.Add(CxvInternalHelpers.WriteDataToXElement(this[i]));
			}
			list.Add(new XAttribute("Count", Count));
			return new XElement(GetType().Name, list);
		}

	public class CxvVectorItemPropertyChangingEventArgs : EventArgs
	{

		public CxvPropertyBase Property { get; }

		public string SubPropertyName { get; }

		public object OldValue { get; }

		public object NewValue { get; }

		public bool Cancel { get; set; }

		public CxvVectorItemPropertyChangingEventArgs(int index, CxvPropertyBase property, string subPropertyName, object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			Index = index;
			Property = property ?? throw new ArgumentNullException("property");
			SubPropertyName = subPropertyName ?? throw new ArgumentNullException("subPropertyName");
			OldValue = oldValue;
			NewValue = newValue;
		}
	}
	public class CxvVectorItemPropertyChangedEventArgs
	{
		public int Index { get; }

		public CxvPropertyBase Property { get; }

		public string SubPropertyName { get; }

		public CxvVectorItemPropertyChangedEventArgs(int index, CxvPropertyBase property, string subPropertyName)
		{
			Index = index;
			Property = property ?? throw new ArgumentNullException("property");
			SubPropertyName = subPropertyName ?? throw new ArgumentNullException("subPropertyName");
		}
	}

	public class CxvDataAccessor
	{
		private readonly ICxvViewDataContainer _dataContainer;

		private IHappDataAccessor _happDataAccessor;

		private IHappDataAccessor HappAccessor => _happDataAccessor ?? (_happDataAccessor = _dataContainer.UnityContainer.TryResolve<IHappDataAccessor>());

		public CxsCase CurrentCase => _dataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider?.Case;

		public CxvDataAccessor(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer)
		{
			_dataContainer = dataContainer ?? throw new ArgumentNullException("dataContainer");
		}

		public IHappDataNode FindHappNode(string dataPath)
		{
			string cxsPath = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(dataPath);
			return HappAccessor?.GetHappNode(cxsPath);
		}

		public object ReadData(string dataPath, string dataAttribute, string unit = null)
		{
			string text = _dataContainer.ExpandDataPath(dataPath);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return CurrentCase?.BatchGet(text, dataAttribute, unit);
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void PublishDataChangedEvent()
		{
			_dataContainer.UnityContainer?.TryResolve<IEventAggregator>()?.GetEvent<CxsCaseDataUpdatedEvent>().Publish(CxsCaseDataUpdatedEventArgs.Empty);
		}
	}

	public class CxvDescriptionPropertyDataV2 : CxvStatePropertyDataV2, IViewDataSerializable
	{
		private bool? _truncate;

		private static readonly List<string> _objPaths = new List<string>
		{
			"Blocks", "Streams", "Utilities", "Flowsheeting Options.Calculator", "Flowsheeting Options.Design-Spec", "Flowsheeting Options.Transfer", "Flowsheeting Options.Balance", "Flowsheeting Options.Pres-Relief", "Flowsheeting Options.Measurement", "Model Analysis Tools.Sensitivity",
			"Model Analysis Tools.Optimization", "Model Analysis Tools.Data-Fit.Regression", "Model Analysis Tools.Contraint", "Convergence.Convergence", "Convergence.Sequence", "EO Configuration.Objective", "EO Configuration.Ports", "Controllers", "Sensors", "Properties.Analysis",
			"Components.Assay/Blend", "Components.PetroCharacterization.Generation", "Components.PetroCharacterization.Analysis Options", "Properties.Estimation", "Properties.Regression"
		};

		private CxvDescriptionProperty DescProperty => base.Property as CxvDescriptionProperty;

		public bool? IsEntered => true;

		public bool IsReadOnly => DescProperty.IsReadOnly == true;

		public bool? IsCalculated => DescProperty.IsCalculated == true;

		public override string VariablePrompt
		{
			get
			{
				IHappDataNode happDataNode = base.DataAccessor.FindHappNode(DescProperty.DataPath);
				if (happDataNode != null)
				{
					return CxvInternalHelpers.ValueToStringRemoveXml(happDataNode.GetAttributeValue(HappDataNodeAttribute.Prompt) as string);
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		public string Value
		{
			get
			{
				if (IsPropertyValueEmpty("Value"))
				{
					string text = GetText(DescProperty.DataPath);
					SetPropertyValue(text, raisePropertyChanged: false, "Value");
				}
				return GetPropertyValue<string>("Value");
			}
			set
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Value) || !string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
				{
					string a = TrimLines(Value);
					string text = TrimLines(value);
					if (!string.Equals(a, text, StringComparison.CurrentCulture))
					{
						SetPropertyValue(text, raisePropertyChanged: false, "Value");
						SetText(DescProperty.DataPath, text);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private string TrimLines(string text)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return text.Replace("\r\n", "\n").Replace('\r', '\n');
			}
			return text;
		}

		public CxvDescriptionPropertyDataV2(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvDescriptionProperty property)
			: base(dataContainer, property)
		{
		}

		private Dictionary<string, string> ExistingIds(string path)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(path))
			{
				return null;
			}
			string text = CxsPath.LeafId(path);
			string text2 = null;
			switch (text)
			{
			case "Blocks":
			case "Utilities":
				text2 = CxsPath.ParentPath(path);
				break;
			case "Regression":
				text2 = CxsPath.ParentPath(CxsPath.ParentPath(CxsPath.ParentPath(path)));
				break;
			default:
				text2 = CxsPath.ParentPath(CxsPath.ParentPath(path));
				break;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
			{
				return null;
			}
			CxsBatchRequestData cxsBatchRequestData = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			foreach (string objPath in _objPaths)
			{
				cxsBatchRequestData.AddRequest(new CxsBatchRequest(CxsPath.CombinePaths(text2, objPath), "child_ids"));
			}
			object[] array = null;
			try
			{
				array = base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(cxsBatchRequestData);
			}
			catch (CxsException)
			{
			}
			if (array == null || array.Count() == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				string[] array2 = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(array[i]);
				if (array2 == null || array2.Count() == 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				string[] array3 = array2;
				foreach (string key in array3)
				{
					if (!dictionary.ContainsKey(key))
					{
						dictionary.Add(key, _objPaths[i]);
					}
				}
			}
			return dictionary;
		}

		private string GetErrorObjectsDescription(string s, bool isBatchConvergence, Dictionary<string, string> rootObjects)
		{
			string[] array = s.Trim().Split(' ');
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			if (array != null)
			{
				string text = "Top.appModelV8.Blocks";
				string[] array2 = array;
				foreach (string text2 in array2)
				{
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (text2.Contains('.'))
					{
						string[] array3 = text2.Trim().Split('.');
						string text3 = text;
						for (int j = 0; j < array3.Length - 1; j++)
						{
							text3 += ".";
							text3 += array3[j];
							text3 += ".Data.Blocks";
						}
						Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = ExistingIds(text3);
						string text4 = array3[array3.Length - 1];
						if (dictionary.ContainsKey(text4))
						{
							string recordType = GetRecordType(text3 + "." + text4);
							string value = $"<ErrorObjects Type=\"{dictionary[text4]}\" RecordType=\"{recordType}\">{text2}</ErrorObjects>\t";
							stringBuilder.Append(value);
						}
						else if (isBatchConvergence && text4.StartsWith("$"))
						{
							string value2 = string.Format("<ErrorObjects Type=\"{0}\" RecordType=\"{1}\">{2}</ErrorObjects>\t", "Convergence.Batch-Options", "Convergence.Batch-Options", text2);
							stringBuilder.Append(value2);
						}
					}
					else if (rootObjects.ContainsKey(text2))
					{
						string recordType2 = GetRecordType(text + "." + text2);
						string value3 = $"<ErrorObjects Type=\"{rootObjects[text2]}\" RecordType=\"{recordType2}\">{text2}</ErrorObjects>\t";
						stringBuilder.Append(value3);
					}
					else
					{
						if (!isBatchConvergence || !text2.StartsWith("$"))
						{
							return null;
						}
						string value4 = string.Format("<ErrorObjects Type=\"{0}\" RecordType=\"{1}\">{2}</ErrorObjects>\t", "Convergence.Batch-Options", "Convergence.Batch-Options", text2);
						stringBuilder.Append(value4);
					}
				}
				return stringBuilder.ToString();
			}
			return null;
		}

		private string GetRecordType(string dataPath)
		{
			return base.DataAccessor.FindHappNode(dataPath)?.GetAttributeValue(HappDataNodeAttribute.RecordType) as string;
		}

		private string GetText(string dataPath)
		{
			List<string> list = InternalGetLines(dataPath);
			if (list != null && list.Count > 0)
			{
				string text = null;
				string text2 = null;
				Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = null;
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				bool flag = false;
				foreach (string item in list)
				{
					bool flag2 = false;
					if (IsReadOnly)
					{
						bool isBatchConvergence = text == "The following Batch Convergence blocks were" && (text2 == "completed with errors:" || text2 == "completed with warnings:");
						if (text2 != null && text2.EndsWith("CHECK DATA BANK NAMES FOR SPELLING.") && item.EndsWith(" DATA BANK NAME"))
						{
							flag = true;
						}
						if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(item))
						{
							flag = false;
						}
						if (!flag && (text2 == null || (!text2.EndsWith("never executed:") && !text2.EndsWith("FOLLOWING STAGES:"))))
						{
							if (dictionary == null)
							{
								dictionary = ExistingIds("Top.appModelV8.Blocks");
							}
							string errorObjectsDescription = GetErrorObjectsDescription(item, isBatchConvergence, dictionary);
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(errorObjectsDescription))
							{
								stringBuilder.AppendLine(errorObjectsDescription);
								flag2 = true;
							}
						}
					}
					if (!flag2)
					{
						if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(item))
						{
							stringBuilder.AppendLine();
						}
						else
						{
							stringBuilder.AppendLine(item);
						}
					}
					text = text2;
					text2 = item;
				}
				stringBuilder = stringBuilder.Remove(stringBuilder.Length - 1, 1);
				return stringBuilder.ToString();
			}
			return string.Empty;
		}

		private void SetText(string dataPath, string value)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			IHappDataNode happDataNode = base.DataAccessor.FindHappNode(dataPath);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
			{
				string[] array = value.Replace("\r\n", "\n").Replace('\r', '\n').Split('\n');
				int num = array.Length;
				if (DescProperty.LineLimit.HasValue && num > DescProperty.LineLimit.Value)
				{
					if (!_truncate.HasValue)
					{
						ShowMessageBoxEventArgs e = new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
						{
							Title = base.DataContainer.ResolvedCxsProvider.GetI18nApplicationName(),
							Message = string.Format(Resources.TruncateWarning, DescProperty.LineLimit.Value, array.Length),
							MsgBoxButton = MessageBoxButton.YesNo,
							MsgBoxImage = MessageBoxImage.Exclamation,
							DialogResult = MessageBoxResult.Yes
						};
						base.DataContainer.UnityContainer.TryResolve<IEventAggregator>().GetEvent<ShowMessageBoxEvent>().Publish(e);
						_truncate = e.DialogResult == MessageBoxResult.Yes;
					}
					if (_truncate.Value)
					{
						num = DescProperty.LineLimit.Value;
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				SaveLines(happDataNode, array, num);
			}
			else
			{
				happDataNode.ClearChildren();
			}
			base.DataAccessor.PublishDataChangedEvent();
			if (flag)
			{
				OnPropertyChanged("Value");
			}
		}

		private List<string> InternalGetLines(string dataPath)
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			IHappDataNode happDataNode = base.DataAccessor.FindHappNode(dataPath);
			if (happDataNode != null)
			{
				foreach (IHappDataNode child in happDataNode.Children)
				{
					list.Add(child.Value as string);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		private void SaveLines(IHappDataNode node, string[] lines, int length)
		{
			List<IHappDataNode> list = node.Children.ToList();
			for (int num = list.Count - 1; num >= length; num--)
			{
				node.RemoveChild(num);
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < length; i++)
			{
				string value = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(lines[i]) ? " " : lines[i]);
				if (i < list.Count)
				{
					list[i].Value = value;
				}
				else
				{
					node.AppendNewChild().Value = value;
				}
			}
		}

		XElement IViewDataSerializable.WriteXElement()
		{
			string value = Value;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
			{
				return new XElement(GetType().Name, new XElement("Value", value));
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public abstract class CxvPropertyDataV2 : IPropertyData, INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private readonly Dictionary<string, object> _propertyValues = new Dictionary<string, object>();

		private CxvDataAccessor _dataAccessor;

		protected ICxvViewDataContainer DataContainer { get; }

		public CxvPropertyBase Property { get; }

		protected CxvDataAccessor DataAccessor => _dataAccessor ?? (_dataAccessor = new CxvDataAccessor(DataContainer));

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		protected CxvPropertyDataV2(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvPropertyBase property)
		{
			DataContainer = dataContainer ?? throw new ArgumentNullException("dataContainer");
			Property = property ?? throw new ArgumentNullException("property");
			DataContainer.UnityContainer?.TryResolve<IEventAggregator>()?.GetEvent<ViewDataRefreshQueryEvent>().Subscribe(OnViewDataRefreshQueryEvent);
		}

		protected virtual void OnPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			this.PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
			if (Property.PropertyDeclaration != null)
			{
				DataContainer.OnSubPropertyDataChanged(Property.PropertyDeclaration, propertyName);
			}
		}

		protected T GetPropertyValue<T>([CallerMemberName] string propertyName = null)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(propertyName) && _propertyValues.ContainsKey(propertyName))
			{
				object obj = _propertyValues[propertyName];
				if (obj is T)
				{
					return (T)obj;
				}
			}
			return default(T);
		}

		protected void SetPropertyValue<T>(T value, bool raisePropertyChanged = false, [CallerMemberName] string propertyName = null)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(propertyName))
			{
				_propertyValues[propertyName] = value;
				if (raisePropertyChanged)
				{
					OnPropertyChanged(propertyName);
				}
			}
		}

		protected bool IsPropertyValueEmpty([CallerMemberName] string propertyName = null)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(propertyName))
			{
				return !_propertyValues.ContainsKey(propertyName);
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected virtual void OnViewDataRefreshQueryEvent(ViewDataRefreshQueryEventArgs args)
		{
			List<string> list = _propertyValues.Keys.ToList();
			_propertyValues.Clear();
			foreach (string item in list)
			{
				OnPropertyChanged(item);
			}
		}
	}
	public abstract class CxvStatePropertyDataV2 : CxvPropertyDataV2, IStatePropertyData, IPropertyData, INotifyPropertyChanged, IStateData
	{
		private bool? _isEnabled;

		protected CxvStatePropertyBase StateProperty => base.Property as CxvStatePropertyBase;

		public bool? IsEnabled
		{
			get
			{
				if (_isEnabled.HasValue)
				{
					return _isEnabled;
				}
				if (IsPropertyValueEmpty("IsEnabled"))
				{
					bool? value = CxvLogicalTestCollection.EvaluateConditions(base.DataContainer, CxvLogicalOperator.And, StateProperty.IsEnabledCondition);
					SetPropertyValue(value, raisePropertyChanged: false, "IsEnabled");
				}
				return GetPropertyValue<bool?>("IsEnabled");
			}
			set
			{
				_isEnabled = value;
			}
		}

		public bool? IsVisible
		{
			get
			{
				if (IsPropertyValueEmpty("IsVisible"))
				{
					bool? value = CxvLogicalTestCollection.EvaluateConditions(base.DataContainer, CxvLogicalOperator.And, StateProperty.IsVisibleCondition);
					SetPropertyValue(value, raisePropertyChanged: false, "IsVisible");
				}
				return GetPropertyValue<bool?>("IsVisible");
			}
		}

		public virtual string DisabledPrompt
		{
			get
			{
				if (StateProperty.DisabledPrompt != null)
				{
					return StateProperty.DisabledPrompt;
				}
				if (IsPropertyValueEmpty("DisabledPrompt") && StateProperty.IsEnabledCondition != null)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(StateProperty.IsEnabledCondition.DataPath))
					{
						if (CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(base.DataAccessor.ReadData(StateProperty.IsEnabledCondition.DataPath, "tabstate", "complete")) != true)
						{
							string value = CxvInternalHelpers.ValueToStringRemoveXml(base.DataAccessor.ReadData(StateProperty.IsEnabledCondition.DataPath, "description"));
							SetPropertyValue(value, raisePropertyChanged: false, "DisabledPrompt");
						}
					}
					else if (StateProperty.IsEnabledCondition.SubConditions != null)
					{
						foreach (CxvLogicalTest subCondition in StateProperty.IsEnabledCondition.SubConditions)
						{
							if (CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(base.DataAccessor.ReadData(subCondition.DataPath, "tabstate", "complete")) != true)
							{
								string value2 = CxvInternalHelpers.ValueToStringRemoveXml(base.DataAccessor.ReadData(subCondition.DataPath, "description"));
								SetPropertyValue(value2, raisePropertyChanged: false, "DisabledPrompt");
								break;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				return GetPropertyValue<string>("DisabledPrompt");
			}
		}

		public virtual string VariablePrompt => string.Empty;

		protected CxvStatePropertyDataV2(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvStatePropertyBase property)
			: base(dataContainer, property)
		{
		}
	}
	public abstract class CxvEnablePromptPropertyDataV2 : CxvStatePropertyDataV2
	{
		public override string DisabledPrompt
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.StateProperty.DisabledPrompt != null)
				{
					return base.StateProperty.DisabledPrompt;
				}
				if (IsPropertyValueEmpty("DisabledPrompt") && base.StateProperty.IsEnabledCondition != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.StateProperty.IsEnabledCondition.DataPath))
				{
					string value = CxvInternalHelpers.ValueToStringRemoveXml(base.DataAccessor.ReadData(base.StateProperty.IsEnabledCondition.DataPath, "description"));
					SetPropertyValue(value, raisePropertyChanged: false, "DisabledPrompt");
				}
				return GetPropertyValue<string>("DisabledPrompt");
			}
		}

		protected CxvEnablePromptPropertyDataV2(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvStatePropertyBase property)
			: base(dataContainer, property)
		{
		}
	}
	public class CxvTabItemPropertyDataV2 : CxvStatePropertyDataV2, ITabItemPropertyData, IStatePropertyData, IPropertyData, INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private CxvTabItemProperty TabItemProperty => base.Property as CxvTabItemProperty;

		public bool? Entered
		{
			get
			{
				if (IsPropertyValueEmpty("Entered"))
				{
					CxvLogicalTestCollection tabStateLogicalTestCollection = CxvTabStateProperty.GetTabStateLogicalTestCollection(TabItemProperty.EnteredDataPaths, CxvTabStateOperator.Entered);
					CxvLogicalOperator unionOperator = CxvTabStateProperty.GetUnionOperator(CxvTabStateOperator.Entered);
					bool? value = tabStateLogicalTestCollection.EvaluateConditions(unionOperator, base.DataContainer, null);
					SetPropertyValue(value, raisePropertyChanged: false, "Entered");
				}
				return GetPropertyValue<bool?>("Entered");
			}
		}

		public bool? Complete
		{
			get
			{
				if (IsPropertyValueEmpty("Complete"))
				{
					CxvLogicalTestCollection tabStateLogicalTestCollection = CxvTabStateProperty.GetTabStateLogicalTestCollection(TabItemProperty.CompleteDataPaths, CxvTabStateOperator.Complete);
					CxvLogicalOperator unionOperator = CxvTabStateProperty.GetUnionOperator(CxvTabStateOperator.Complete);
					bool? value = tabStateLogicalTestCollection.EvaluateConditions(unionOperator, base.DataContainer, null);
					SetPropertyValue(value, raisePropertyChanged: false, "Complete");
				}
				return GetPropertyValue<bool?>("Complete");
			}
		}

		public bool? Accessible
		{
			get
			{
				if (IsPropertyValueEmpty("Accessible"))
				{
					CxvLogicalTestCollection tabStateLogicalTestCollection = CxvTabStateProperty.GetTabStateLogicalTestCollection(TabItemProperty.AccessibleDataPaths, CxvTabStateOperator.Accessible);
					CxvLogicalOperator unionOperator = CxvTabStateProperty.GetUnionOperator(CxvTabStateOperator.Accessible);
					bool? value = tabStateLogicalTestCollection.EvaluateConditions(unionOperator, base.DataContainer, null);
					SetPropertyValue(value, raisePropertyChanged: false, "Accessible");
				}
				return GetPropertyValue<bool?>("Accessible");
			}
		}

		public override string VariablePrompt
		{
			get
			{
				if (IsPropertyValueEmpty("VariablePrompt") && TabItemProperty.AccessibleDataPaths.Count >= 1 && !(Accessible ?? true))
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(DisabledPrompt))
					{
						SetPropertyValue(DisabledPrompt, raisePropertyChanged: false, "VariablePrompt");
					}
					else
					{
						SetPropertyValue(CxvInternalHelpers.ValueToStringRemoveXml(base.DataAccessor.ReadData(TabItemProperty.AccessibleDataPaths[0], "description")), raisePropertyChanged: false, "VariablePrompt");
					}
				}
				return GetPropertyValue<string>("VariablePrompt");
			}
		}

		public string CompletionMessages
		{
			get
			{
				if (IsPropertyValueEmpty("CompletionMessages"))
				{
					ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = base.DataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
					foreach (string completeDataPath in TabItemProperty.CompleteDataPaths)
					{
						string text = base.DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(completeDataPath);
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
						{
							cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text, "tabstate", "complete");
						}
					}
					object[] values = cxsDataWatch.Values;
					cxsDataWatch = base.DataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
					string text2 = null;
					if (values != null)
					{
						int num = 0;
						object[] array = values;
						for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
						{
							bool? flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(array[i]);
							if (flag.HasValue && !flag.Value)
							{
								string text3 = base.DataContainer.ExpandDataPath(TabItemProperty.CompleteDataPaths[num]);
								if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3) && (text3.Contains("CC_Nodes") || text3.Contains("CC Nodes")))
								{
									cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(text3, "completion_message");
								}
							}
							num++;
						}
						array = cxsDataWatch.Values;
						for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
						{
							string text4 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[i]);
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
							{
								text2 = ((text2 != null) ? (text2 + text4) : text4);
							}
						}
					}
					SetPropertyValue(text2, raisePropertyChanged: false, "CompletionMessages");
				}
				return GetPropertyValue<string>("CompletionMessages");
			}
		}

		public CxvTabItemPropertyDataV2(ICxvViewDataContainer dataContainer, CxvTabItemProperty property)
			: base(dataContainer, property)
		{
		}
	}

namespace AspenTech.AspenCxs.CxsViewModel.Internal
{
	public static class CxvInternalHelpers
	{
		internal static string LocalizeString(ICxvViewDataContainer container, string defaultValue, string resourceKey)
		{
			string result = defaultValue;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(resourceKey))
			{
				object obj = container.ResolveResource(resourceKey);
				if (obj != null)
				{
					string text = obj.ToString();
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						result = text;
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal static string LocalizeString(ICxvViewDataContainer container, string defaultValue, string resourceKey, string resourceType)
		{
			string result = defaultValue;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(resourceKey))
			{
				object obj = container.ResolveResource(resourceKey, resourceType);
				if (obj != null)
				{
					string text = obj.ToString();
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						result = text;
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static string GetSystemDecimalSymbol()
		{
			return 0.1.ToString().Replace("0", string.Empty).Replace("1", string.Empty);
		}

		internal static void ReviseOptionAndOptionPrompt(ref string optionsString, ref string optionPomptsString)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(optionsString))
			{
				return;
			}
			string[] array = optionsString.Split('\n');
			string[] array2 = null;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(optionPomptsString))
			{
				array2 = new string[array.Length];
				for (int i = 0; i < array2.Length; i++)
				{
					array2[i] = "";
				}
			}
			else
			{
				array2 = optionPomptsString.Split('\n');
			}
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
			if (array != null && array2 != null)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < array.Length; j++)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(array[j]))
					{
						list.Add(array[j]);
						string item = ((array2.Length > j) ? array2[j] : null);
						list2.Add(item);
					}
				}
			}
			if (list.Count > 0 && list.Count != array.Length)
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				for (int k = 0; k < list.Count - 1; k++)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(array[k]);
					stringBuilder.Append("\n");
				}
				stringBuilder.Append(list[list.Count - 1]);
				optionsString = stringBuilder.ToString();
				stringBuilder.Clear();
				for (int l = 0; l < list2.Count - 1; l++)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(list2[l]);
					stringBuilder.Append("\n");
				}
				stringBuilder.Append(list2[list2.Count - 1]);
				optionPomptsString = stringBuilder.ToString();
			}
		}

		internal static string LocalizeOptionPrompt(ICxvViewDataContainer container, string defaultValue, string resourceKey, string resourceType)
		{
			string result = string.Empty;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(resourceKey) && container != null)
			{
				object obj = container.ResolveResource(resourceKey, resourceType);
				if (obj != null)
				{
					string text = obj.ToString();
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						result = text;
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal static string GetResourceOptionKey(ICxvViewDataContainer container, string path)
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(path) && container != null)
			{
				try
				{
					empty = GetRecDefName(path, container);
					int num = path.LastIndexOf("Input");
					string[] array = path.Substring(num + 6).Split('.');
					if (array != null && array.Length != 0)
					{
						text = ReplaceSpecialChars(empty + "_" + array[0]);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return text.ToLower();
			}
			return string.Empty;
		}

		internal static string GetResourceOptionKey(ICxvViewDataContainer container, string path, string value)
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(path))
			{
				try
				{
					empty = GetRecDefName(path, container);
					int num = path.LastIndexOf("Input");
					string[] array = path.Substring(num + 6).Split('.');
					if (array != null && array.Length != 0)
					{
						text = ReplaceSpecialChars(empty + "_" + array[0] + "_" + value);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return text.ToLower();
			}
			return string.Empty;
		}
