		private static string GetRecordTypePath(List<string> keyNodesName, string path)
		{
			if (keyNodesName != null && keyNodesName.Count > 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(path))
			{
				string[] array = path.Split('.');
				for (int num = array.Length - 1; num >= 0; num--)
				{
					bool flag = false;
					for (int num2 = keyNodesName.Count - 1; num2 >= 0; num2--)
					{
						int num3 = keyNodesName.Count - 1 - num2;
						if (num - num3 < 0 || !array[num - num3].Equals(keyNodesName[num2]))
						{
							break;
						}
						if (num2 == 0)
						{
							flag = true;
						}
					}
					if (flag)
					{
						int num4 = 0;
						for (int i = 0; i <= num + 1; i++)
						{
							num4 += array[i].Length;
						}
						num4 += num;
						return path.Substring(0, num4 + 1);
					}
				}
			}
			return string.Empty;
		}

		public static string ReplaceSpecialChars(string str)
		{
			return Regex.Replace(str, "[^a-zA-Z0-9]", "_");
		}

		public static bool IsCxvPropertyDataUnloaded(CxvPropertyData pd)
		{
			if (pd is ICxvPropertyData { IsUnloaded: not false })
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static XElement WriteDataToXElement(object data)
		{
			if (data == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			XElement xElement = null;
			if (data is IViewDataSerializable viewDataSerializable)
			{
				xElement = viewDataSerializable.WriteXElement();
				if (data is IStateData stateData)
				{
					List<XElement> list = new List<XElement>();
					bool? isEnabled = stateData.IsEnabled;
					bool? isVisible = stateData.IsVisible;
					string disabledPrompt = stateData.DisabledPrompt;
					string variablePrompt = stateData.VariablePrompt;
					if (isEnabled.HasValue)
					{
						list.Add(new XElement("IsEnabled", isEnabled.Value));
					}
					if (isVisible.HasValue)
					{
						list.Add(new XElement("IsVisible", isVisible.Value));
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(disabledPrompt))
					{
						list.Add(new XElement("DisabledPrompt", disabledPrompt));
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(variablePrompt))
					{
						list.Add(new XElement("VariablePrompt", variablePrompt));
					}
					if (list.Count > 0)
					{
						if (xElement == null)
						{
							xElement = new XElement(data.GetType().Name, list);
						}
						else
						{
							xElement.Add(list);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return xElement;
		}

		internal static string ValueToStringRemoveXml(object value)
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(value);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			text = text.Replace("&amp;", "&");
			text = text.Replace("&gt;", ">");
			text = text.Replace("&lt;", "<");
			text = text.Replace("&quot;", "\"");
			text = text.Replace(" \n", " ");
			return text.Replace("\n", " ");
		}
	}
	internal static class CxvDataAttributeResolver
	{
		private class DependencyItem
		{
			public object Value { get; set; }

			public ICxsDataWatch Watcher { get; set; }

			public EventHandler Handler { get; set; }
		}

		private static readonly Dictionary<CxvPropertyData, Dictionary<string, DependencyItem>> _dependencies = new Dictionary<CxvPropertyData, Dictionary<string, DependencyItem>>();

		public static void UnloadPropertyDataInViewModel(CxvViewModel model)
		{
			List<CxvPropertyData> list = new List<CxvPropertyData>();
			foreach (KeyValuePair<CxvPropertyData, Dictionary<string, DependencyItem>> dependency in _dependencies)
			{
				if (dependency.Key.DataContainer == model)
				{
					list.Add(dependency.Key);
				}
			}
			foreach (CxvPropertyData item in list)
			{
				RemovePropertyData(item);
			}
		}

		public static object ResolvePropertyValue(CxvPropertyData pd, string propertyName, string dataPath, string attributeName)
		{
			if (pd == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("pd");
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(propertyName))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("propertyName cannot be null or empty.");
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(attributeName))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("attributeName cannot be null or empty.");
			}
			DependencyItem di = GetDependencyItem(pd, propertyName);
			if (di.Handler == null)
			{
				di.Handler = delegate
				{
					RefreshAttributeValue(pd, propertyName, dataPath, attributeName, di);
				};
			}
			if (di.Watcher == null)
			{
				RefreshAttributeValue(pd, propertyName, dataPath, attributeName, di);
			}
			return di.Value;
		}

		public static void UpdatePropertyValue(CxvPropertyData pd, string propertyName)
		{
			if (pd == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("pd");
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(propertyName))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("propertyName cannot be null or empty.");
			}
			if (!CxvInternalHelpers.IsCxvPropertyDataUnloaded(pd))
			{
				DependencyItem dependencyItem = GetDependencyItem(pd, propertyName);
				UpdateVariableAttributeWatch(dependencyItem, null);
				dependencyItem.Value = null;
				pd.RaisePropertyChanged(propertyName);
			}
		}

		private static void RefreshAttributeValue(CxvPropertyData pd, string propertyName, string dataPath, string attributeName, DependencyItem di)
		{
			if (CxvInternalHelpers.IsCxvPropertyDataUnloaded(pd))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (di.Watcher == null)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(dataPath))
				{
					return;
				}
				ICxsDataWatch cxsDataWatch = pd.DataContainer.CreateCxsWatch();
				if (cxsDataWatch == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				cxsDataWatch.AddWatch(dataPath, attributeName);
				UpdateVariableAttributeWatch(di, cxsDataWatch);
			}
			object obj = null;
			if (di.Watcher != null)
			{
				object[] values = di.Watcher.Values;
				if (values != null)
				{
					obj = values[0];
				}
			}
			if (di.Value != obj)
			{
				di.Value = obj;
				pd.RaisePropertyChanged(propertyName);
			}
		}

		private static void UpdateVariableAttributeWatch(DependencyItem di, ICxsDataWatch watcher)
		{
			if (watcher != di.Watcher)
			{
				if (di.Watcher != null)
				{
					di.Watcher.ValuesChanged -= di.Handler;
					di.Watcher.Unload();
					di.Watcher = null;
				}
				if (watcher != null && !watcher.IsEmpty)
				{
					di.Watcher = watcher;
					di.Watcher.ValuesChanged += di.Handler;
				}
			}
		}

		public static void RemovePropertyData(CxvPropertyData pd)
		{
			if (!_dependencies.ContainsKey(pd))
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, DependencyItem> item in _dependencies[pd])
			{
				DependencyItem value = item.Value;
				if (value.Watcher != null)
				{
					value.Watcher.ValuesChanged -= value.Handler;
					value.Watcher.Unload();
					value.Watcher = null;
				}
			}
			_dependencies.Remove(pd);
		}

		private static DependencyItem GetDependencyItem(CxvPropertyData pd, string propertyName)
		{
			if (!_dependencies.ContainsKey(pd))
			{
				_dependencies[pd] = new Dictionary<string, DependencyItem>();
			}
			if (!_dependencies[pd].ContainsKey(propertyName))
			{
				_dependencies[pd][propertyName] = new DependencyItem();
			}
			return _dependencies[pd][propertyName];
		}
	}
	internal static class CxvPropertyValueResolver
	{
		private class DependencyItem
		{
			public object Value { get; set; }

			public CxvPropertyWatch Watcher { get; set; }

			public EventHandler Handler { get; set; }
		}

		private static readonly Dictionary<CxvPropertyData, Dictionary<string, DependencyItem>> _dependencies = new Dictionary<CxvPropertyData, Dictionary<string, DependencyItem>>();

		public static object ResolvePropertyValue(CxvPropertyData pd, string propertyName, Func<CxvPropertyWatch, object> propertyValueFunc)
		{
			if (pd == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("pd");
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(propertyName))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("propertyName cannot be null or empty.");
			}
			if (propertyValueFunc == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("propertyValueFunc");
			}
			DependencyItem dependencyItem = GetDependencyItem(pd, propertyName);
			if (dependencyItem.Handler == null)
			{
				dependencyItem.Handler = delegate
				{
					UpdatePropertyValue(pd, propertyName);
				};
			}
			if (dependencyItem.Value == null && !CxvInternalHelpers.IsCxvPropertyDataUnloaded(pd))
			{
				CxvPropertyWatch cxvPropertyWatch = new CxvPropertyWatch();
				object value = propertyValueFunc(cxvPropertyWatch);
				UpdatePropertyWatcher(dependencyItem, cxvPropertyWatch);
				dependencyItem.Value = value;
			}
			if (dependencyItem.Watcher == null)
			{
				dependencyItem.Handler = null;
				RemoveDependencyItem(pd, propertyName);
			}
			return dependencyItem.Value;
		}

		public static void UpdatePropertyValue(CxvPropertyData pd, string propertyName)
		{
			if (pd == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("pd");
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(propertyName))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("propertyName cannot be null or empty.");
			}
			if (!CxvInternalHelpers.IsCxvPropertyDataUnloaded(pd))
			{
				DependencyItem dependencyItem = GetDependencyItem(pd, propertyName);
				UpdatePropertyWatcher(dependencyItem, null);
				dependencyItem.Value = null;
				pd.RaisePropertyChanged(propertyName);
			}
		}

		private static void UpdatePropertyWatcher(DependencyItem di, CxvPropertyWatch watcher)
		{
			if (di.Watcher != watcher)
			{
				if (di.Watcher != null)
				{
					di.Watcher.PropertyDependencyChanged -= di.Handler;
					di.Watcher.Clear();
					di.Watcher = null;
				}
				if (watcher != null && !watcher.IsEmpty)
				{
					di.Watcher = watcher;
					di.Watcher.PropertyDependencyChanged += di.Handler;
				}
			}
		}

		private static DependencyItem GetDependencyItem(CxvPropertyData pd, string propertyName)
		{
			if (!_dependencies.ContainsKey(pd))
			{
				_dependencies[pd] = new Dictionary<string, DependencyItem>();
			}
			if (!_dependencies[pd].ContainsKey(propertyName))
			{
				_dependencies[pd][propertyName] = new DependencyItem();
			}
			return _dependencies[pd][propertyName];
		}

		public static void RemovePropertyData(CxvPropertyData pd)
		{
			if (!_dependencies.ContainsKey(pd))
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, DependencyItem> item in _dependencies[pd])
			{
				DependencyItem value = item.Value;
				if (value.Watcher != null)
				{
					value.Watcher.PropertyDependencyChanged -= value.Handler;
					value.Watcher.Clear();
					value.Watcher = null;
				}
			}
			_dependencies.Remove(pd);
		}

		private static void RemoveDependencyItem(CxvPropertyData pd, string propertyName)
		{
			if (!_dependencies.ContainsKey(pd))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_dependencies[pd].ContainsKey(propertyName))
			{
				DependencyItem dependencyItem = _dependencies[pd][propertyName];
				if (dependencyItem.Watcher != null)
				{
					dependencyItem.Watcher.PropertyDependencyChanged -= dependencyItem.Handler;
					dependencyItem.Handler = null;
				}
				_dependencies[pd].Remove(propertyName);
			}
			if (_dependencies[pd].Count == 0)
			{
				_dependencies.Remove(pd);
			}
		}
	}
	internal interface ICxvPropertyData
	{
		bool IsUnloaded { get; }

		void Unload();
	}
	internal class VMCustomTypeDescriptor : ICustomTypeDescriptor
	{
		private class PropertyDescriptorComparer : IEqualityComparer<PropertyDescriptor>
		{
			public bool Equals(PropertyDescriptor p1, PropertyDescriptor p2)
			{
				return p1 == p2;
			}

			public int GetHashCode(PropertyDescriptor p)
			{
				return p?.GetHashCode() ?? 0;
			}
		}

		private const PropertyFilterOptions _anySet = PropertyFilterOptions.SetValues | PropertyFilterOptions.UnsetValues;

		private const PropertyFilterOptions _anyValid = PropertyFilterOptions.Valid;

		private ICustomTypeDescriptor _parent;

		private Type _objectType;

		private object _instance;

		private static Hashtable _typeProperties = new Hashtable();

		private static Dictionary<PropertyDescriptor, VMStaticPropertyDescriptor> _propertyMap = new Dictionary<PropertyDescriptor, VMStaticPropertyDescriptor>(new PropertyDescriptorComparer());

		internal VMCustomTypeDescriptor(ICustomTypeDescriptor parent, Type objectType, object instance)
		{
			_parent = parent;
			_objectType = objectType;
			_instance = instance;
		}

		public string GetComponentName()
		{
			return _parent.GetComponentName();
		}

		public PropertyDescriptorCollection GetProperties()
		{
			return GetProperties(null);
		}

		public PropertyDescriptorCollection GetProperties(Attribute[] attributes)
		{
			CxvViewModel cxvViewModel = null;
			PropertyFilterOptions propertyFilterOptions = PropertyFilterOptions.SetValues | PropertyFilterOptions.Valid;
			if (attributes != null)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < attributes.Length; i++)
				{
					if (attributes[i] is PropertyFilterAttribute propertyFilterAttribute)
					{
						propertyFilterOptions = propertyFilterAttribute.Filter;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			switch (propertyFilterOptions)
			{
			case PropertyFilterOptions.None:
			case PropertyFilterOptions.Invalid:
				return PropertyDescriptorCollection.Empty;
			case PropertyFilterOptions.SetValues:
				if (_instance == null)
				{
					return PropertyDescriptorCollection.Empty;
				}
				cxvViewModel = (CxvViewModel)TypeDescriptor.GetAssociation(_objectType, _instance);
				break;
			default:
				cxvViewModel = null;
				break;
			}
			PropertyDescriptorCollection propertyDescriptorCollection = _typeProperties[_objectType] as PropertyDescriptorCollection;
			if (propertyDescriptorCollection == null)
			{
				propertyDescriptorCollection = CreateProperties();
				lock (_typeProperties)
				{
					_typeProperties[_objectType] = propertyDescriptorCollection;
				}
			}
			if ((propertyFilterOptions & (PropertyFilterOptions.SetValues | PropertyFilterOptions.UnsetValues)) != (PropertyFilterOptions.SetValues | PropertyFilterOptions.UnsetValues) && (propertyFilterOptions & PropertyFilterOptions.Valid) != PropertyFilterOptions.Valid)
			{
				List<PropertyDescriptor> list = null;
				int count = propertyDescriptorCollection.Count;
				for (int j = 0; j < count; j++)
				{
					PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor = propertyDescriptorCollection[j];
					if (!(propertyDescriptor.ShouldSerializeValue(cxvViewModel) ^ ((propertyFilterOptions & (PropertyFilterOptions.SetValues | PropertyFilterOptions.UnsetValues)) == PropertyFilterOptions.UnsetValues)))
					{
						if (list == null)
						{
							list = new List<PropertyDescriptor>(count);
							for (int k = 0; k < j; k++)
							{
								list.Add(propertyDescriptorCollection[k]);
							}
						}
					}
					else
					{
						list?.Add(propertyDescriptor);
					}
				}
				if (list != null)
				{
					propertyDescriptorCollection = new PropertyDescriptorCollection(list.ToArray(), readOnly: true);
				}
			}
			return propertyDescriptorCollection;
		}

		public AttributeCollection GetAttributes()
		{
			return _parent.GetAttributes();
		}

		public string GetClassName()
		{
			return _parent.GetClassName();
		}

		public TypeConverter GetConverter()
		{
			TypeConverter converter = _parent.GetConverter();
			if (converter.GetType().IsPublic)
			{
				return converter;
			}
			return new TypeConverter();
		}

		public EventDescriptor GetDefaultEvent()
		{
			return _parent.GetDefaultEvent();
		}

		public PropertyDescriptor GetDefaultProperty()
		{
			return _parent.GetDefaultProperty();
		}

		public object GetEditor(Type editorBaseType)
		{
			return _parent.GetEditor(editorBaseType);
		}

		public EventDescriptorCollection GetEvents()
		{
			return _parent.GetEvents();
		}

		public EventDescriptorCollection GetEvents(Attribute[] attributes)
		{
			return _parent.GetEvents(attributes);
		}

		public object GetPropertyOwner(PropertyDescriptor property)
		{
			return _parent.GetPropertyOwner(property);
		}

		internal static void ClearCache()
		{
			lock (_propertyMap)
			{
				_propertyMap.Clear();
			}
			lock (_typeProperties)
			{
				_typeProperties.Clear();
			}
		}

		private PropertyDescriptorCollection CreateProperties()
		{
			PropertyDescriptorCollection properties = _parent.GetProperties();
			List<PropertyDescriptor> list = new List<PropertyDescriptor>(properties.Count);
			for (int i = 0; i < properties.Count; i++)
			{
				PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor = properties[i];
				CxvPropertyDeclaration cxvPropertyDeclaration = null;
				VMStaticPropertyDescriptor value = null;
				bool flag = false;
				lock (_propertyMap)
				{
					flag = _propertyMap.TryGetValue(propertyDescriptor, out value);
				}
				if (flag && value != null)
				{
					cxvPropertyDeclaration = CxvPropertyDeclaration.FromName(propertyDescriptor.Name, _objectType);
					if (cxvPropertyDeclaration != value.PropertyDeclaration)
					{
						value = null;
					}
					else if (value.Metadata != cxvPropertyDeclaration.Metadata)
					{
						value = null;
					}
				}
				if (value == null)
				{
					if (cxvPropertyDeclaration != null || typeof(CxvViewModel).IsAssignableFrom(propertyDescriptor.ComponentType))
					{
						if (cxvPropertyDeclaration == null)
						{
							cxvPropertyDeclaration = CxvPropertyDeclaration.FromName(propertyDescriptor.Name, _objectType);
						}
						if (cxvPropertyDeclaration != null)
						{
							value = new VMStaticPropertyDescriptor(propertyDescriptor, cxvPropertyDeclaration);
						}
					}
					if (!flag)
					{
						lock (_propertyMap)
						{
							_propertyMap[propertyDescriptor] = value;
						}
					}
				}
				if (value != null)
				{
					propertyDescriptor = value;
				}
				list.Add(propertyDescriptor);
			}
			return new PropertyDescriptorCollection(list.ToArray(), readOnly: true);
		}
	}
	internal sealed class VMTypeDescriptionProvider : TypeDescriptionProvider
	{
		public VMTypeDescriptionProvider()
			: base(TypeDescriptor.GetProvider(typeof(CxvViewModel)))
		{
			TypeDescriptor.Refreshed += delegate(RefreshEventArgs args)
			{
				if (args.TypeChanged != null && typeof(CxvViewModel).IsAssignableFrom(args.TypeChanged))
				{
					VMCustomTypeDescriptor.ClearCache();
				}
			};
		}

		public override IDictionary GetCache(object instance)
		{
			if (instance is CxvViewModel cxvViewModel)
			{
				return cxvViewModel.GetDescriptionProviderCache(createIfMissing: true);
			}
			return base.GetCache(instance);
		}

		public override ICustomTypeDescriptor GetTypeDescriptor(Type objectType, object instance)
		{
			return new VMCustomTypeDescriptor(base.GetTypeDescriptor(objectType, instance), objectType, instance);
		}
	}
	internal sealed class VMStaticPropertyDescriptor : PropertyDescriptor
	{
		private Type _componentType;

		private CxvPropertyDeclaration _dp;

		private PropertyDescriptor _property;

		public override Type ComponentType => _componentType;

		public override bool IsReadOnly
		{
			get
			{
				bool flag = _dp.IsReadOnly;
				if (!flag)
				{
					flag = Attributes.Contains(ReadOnlyAttribute.Yes);
				}
				return flag;
			}
		}

		public CxvPropertyDeclaration PropertyDeclaration => _dp;

		public CxvPropertyBase Metadata => _dp.Metadata;

		public override Type PropertyType => _dp.PropertyType;

		public override bool SupportsChangeEvents => true;

		internal VMStaticPropertyDescriptor(PropertyDescriptor property, CxvPropertyDeclaration dp)
			: base(dp.Name, null)
		{
			_property = property;
			_dp = dp;
			_componentType = property.ComponentType;
		}

		public override bool CanResetValue(object component)
		{
			return ShouldSerializeValue(component);
		}

		protected override AttributeCollection CreateAttributeCollection()
		{
			MergeAttributes();
			return base.CreateAttributeCollection();
		}

		public override object GetValue(object component)
		{
			return FromObj(component).GetValue(_dp);
		}

		public override void SetValue(object component, object value)
		{
			FromObj(component).SetValue(_dp, value);
		}

		public override void ResetValue(object component)
		{
			FromObj(component).ClearValue(_dp);
		}

		public override bool ShouldSerializeValue(object component)
		{
			return FromObj(component).ShouldSerializePropertyValue(_dp);
		}

		public override void AddValueChanged(object component, EventHandler handler)
		{
			CxvViewModel cxvViewModel = FromObj(component);
			if (cxvViewModel != null)
			{
				cxvViewModel.AddPropertyDeclarationValueChanged(_dp, handler);
			}
			else if (_property != null)
			{
				_property.AddValueChanged(component, handler);
			}
		}

		public override void RemoveValueChanged(object component, EventHandler handler)
		{
			CxvViewModel cxvViewModel = FromObj(component);
			if (cxvViewModel != null)
			{
				cxvViewModel.RemovePropertyDeclarationValueChanged(_dp, handler);
			}
			else if (_property != null)
			{
				_property.RemoveValueChanged(component, handler);
			}
		}

		private void MergeAttributes()
		{
			AttributeCollection attributeCollection = null;
			if (_property != null)
			{
				attributeCollection = _property.Attributes;
			}
			List<Attribute> list = new List<Attribute>((attributeCollection == null) ? 1 : (attributeCollection.Count + 1));
			bool flag = false;
			foreach (Attribute item in attributeCollection)
			{
				if (item is ReadOnlyAttribute readOnlyAttribute)
				{
					flag = readOnlyAttribute.IsReadOnly;
				}
				else
				{
					list.Add(item);
				}
			}
			if (flag | _dp.IsReadOnly)
			{
				list.Add(new ReadOnlyAttribute(isReadOnly: true));
			}
			Attribute[] array = list.ToArray();
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length / 2; i++)
			{
				int num = array.Length - i - 1;
				Attribute attribute2 = array[i];
				array[i] = array[num];
				array[num] = attribute2;
			}
			AttributeArray = array;
		}

		private static CxvViewModel FromObj(object component)
		{
			CxvViewModel cxvViewModel = component as CxvViewModel;
			if (cxvViewModel == null)
			{
				cxvViewModel = TypeDescriptor.GetAssociation(typeof(CxvViewModel), component) as CxvViewModel;
			}
			return cxvViewModel;
		}
	}
	internal interface IViewDataSerializable
	{
		XElement WriteXElement();
	}
}
namespace AspenTech.AspenCxs.CxsViewModel.Converters
{
	public class CxvIndexToRowConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			string text = value as string;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) || text[0] != '#' || text.Length < 2)
			{
				return "";
			}
			int num = System.Convert.ToInt32(text.Substring(1), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
			return (num + 1).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (!(value is string { Length: >=1 } text))
			{
				return "";
			}
			int num = System.Convert.ToInt32(text, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
			return "#" + (num - 1).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
		}
	}
}

	public class GetUserInputEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		public string Title { get; set; }

		public string Message { get; set; }

		public Type InputType { get; set; }

		public ReadOnlyCollection<object> InputOptions { get; set; }

		public object InputValue { get; set; }

		public bool? DialogResult { get; set; }

		public int InputMask { get; set; }

		public int? MaxTextLength { get; set; }
	}
	public class GetUserInputEvent : CompositePresentationEvent<GetUserInputEventArgs>
	{
	}
	public class GetCustomUserInputEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		public enum CustomAction
		{
			NewUtility
		}

		public string Title { get; set; }

		public object InputValue { get; set; }

		public bool? DialogResult { get; set; }

		public CustomAction CustomUserAction { get; set; }

		public object OtherInput { get; set; }

		public Dictionary<string, object> Parameters { get; set; }
	}
	public class GetCustomUserInputEvent : CompositePresentationEvent<GetCustomUserInputEventArgs>
	{
	}
	public class ShowMessageBoxEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		public string Title { get; set; }

		public string Message { get; set; }

		public MessageBoxResult DialogResult { get; set; }

		public MessageBoxButton MsgBoxButton { get; set; }

		public MessageBoxImage MsgBoxImage { get; set; }

		public ShowMessageBoxEventArgs()
		{
			DialogResult = MessageBoxResult.None;
		}
	}
	public class ShowMessageBoxEvent : CompositePresentationEvent<ShowMessageBoxEventArgs>
	{
	}
	public class ShowMMMessageBoxEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		public string Title { get; set; }

		public string Message { get; set; }

		public int? DialogResult { get; set; }

		public int? MsgBoxButton { get; set; }

		public int? MsgBoxImage { get; set; }

		public ShowMMMessageBoxEventArgs()
		{
			DialogResult = null;
		}
	}
	public class ShowMMMessageBoxEvent : RoutedCompositePresentationEvent<ShowMMMessageBoxEventArgs>
	{
		public ShowMMMessageBoxEvent()
		{
			base.RoutingStrategy = CompositeEventRoutingStrategy.FirstOfParentsThenChildren;
		}
	}
}
